<script>
'use strict';
/* =========================================================
   Pomocné funkce
   ========================================================= */
const $=(s,el=document)=>el.querySelector(s);
const esc=s=>String(s??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const cap=s=>s?s[0].toUpperCase()+s.slice(1):s;
const uid=()=>Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,9);
const pad=n=>String(n).padStart(2,'0');
const plural=(n,a,b,c)=>{ if(!Number.isInteger(n)) return c; n=Math.abs(n); return n===1?a:(n>=2&&n<=4?b:c); };
const fmtN=(n,d=0)=>Number(n).toLocaleString('cs-CZ',{maximumFractionDigits:d});
const DAY=864e5;
const s2d=s=>new Date(s+'T00:00:00Z');
const d2s=d=>d.toISOString().slice(0,10);
const addD=(s,n)=>d2s(new Date(s2d(s).getTime()+n*DAY));
const diffD=(a,b)=>Math.round((s2d(b)-s2d(a))/DAY);
const isoDow=s=>(s2d(s).getUTCDay()+6)%7+1;
const MONTHS=['leden','únor','březen','duben','květen','červen','červenec','srpen','září','říjen','listopad','prosinec'];
const MON_SH=['led','úno','bře','dub','kvě','čvn','čvc','srp','zář','říj','lis','pro'];
const DOW=['','Po','Út','St','Čt','Pá','So','Ne'];
const DOW_L=['','pondělí','úterý','středa','čtvrtek','pátek','sobota','neděle'];
const fmt=s=>{const d=s2d(s);return `${d.getUTCDate()}. ${d.getUTCMonth()+1}.`};
const fmtY=s=>`${fmt(s)} ${s.slice(0,4)}`;
const fmtR=(a,b)=>a===b?fmtY(a):(a.slice(0,4)===b.slice(0,4)?`${fmt(a)}–${fmtY(b)}`:`${fmtY(a)}–${fmtY(b)}`);
const isoWeek=s=>{const d=s2d(s);const day=(d.getUTCDay()+6)%7;d.setUTCDate(d.getUTCDate()-day+3);const y=d.getUTCFullYear();const f=new Date(Date.UTC(y,0,4));return 1+Math.round(((d-f)/DAY-3+((f.getUTCDay()+6)%7))/7);};
const today=(()=>{const n=new Date();return `${n.getFullYear()}-${pad(n.getMonth()+1)}-${pad(n.getDate())}`})();
const todayY=+today.slice(0,4);

/* =========================================================
   Kalendář: státní svátky ČR + školní prázdniny Libereckého kraje
   Zdroj prázdnin: MŠMT, Organizace školního roku 2025/26, 2026/27, 2027/28
   (jarní prázdniny pro okresy Liberec, Jablonec n. N., Česká Lípa, Semily)
   ========================================================= */
function easter(y){const a=y%19,b=Math.floor(y/100),c=y%100,d=Math.floor(b/4),e=b%4,f=Math.floor((b+8)/25),g=Math.floor((b-f+1)/3),h=(19*a+b-d-g+15)%30,i=Math.floor(c/4),k=c%4,l=(32+2*e+2*i-h-k)%7,m=Math.floor((a+11*h+22*l)/451),mo=Math.floor((h+l-7*m+114)/31),da=((h+l-7*m+114)%31)+1;return d2s(new Date(Date.UTC(y,mo-1,da)));}
function stateHolidays(y){
  const e=easter(y),p=s=>`${y}-${s}`;
  return {[p('01-01')]:'Nový rok, Den obnovy samostatného českého státu',[addD(e,-2)]:'Velký pátek',[addD(e,1)]:'Velikonoční pondělí',[p('05-01')]:'Svátek práce',[p('05-08')]:'Den vítězství',[p('07-05')]:'Den slovanských věrozvěstů Cyrila a Metoděje',[p('07-06')]:'Den upálení mistra Jana Husa',[p('09-28')]:'Den české státnosti',[p('10-28')]:'Den vzniku samostatného československého státu',[p('11-17')]:'Den boje za svobodu a demokracii',[p('12-24')]:'Štědrý den',[p('12-25')]:'1. svátek vánoční',[p('12-26')]:'2. svátek vánoční'};
}

function yearDays(y){if(YD[y])return YD[y];const a=[];for(let d=`${y}-01-01`;d<=`${y}-12-31`;d=addD(d,1))a.push(d);return YD[y]=a;}
function events(){
  const ev=[];
  for(let y=YEARS[0];y<=YEARS[YEARS.length-1]+1;y++) Object.entries(HOLI(y)).forEach(([d,n])=>ev.push({from:d,to:d,label:n,kind:'hol'}));
  SCHOOL.forEach(s=>ev.push({from:s.from,to:s.to,label:s.name,kind:'sch'}));
  return ev.sort((a,b)=>a.from.localeCompare(b.from));
}
const upcoming=(from,n)=>events().filter(e=>e.to>=from).slice(0,n);

const specName=id=>SPEC[id]?SPEC[id].name:id;

const scopeSites=(site=S.site)=>site==='all'?SITES.map(s=>s.id):[site];
const KINDS={or:'Operační sál',ext:'Externí sál',pacu:'Dospávací pokoj'};

function roomCounts(rs){const o=rs.filter(r=>r.kind!=='ext'&&r.kind!=='pacu').length,e=rs.filter(r=>r.kind==='ext').length,p=rs.filter(r=>r.kind==='pacu').length,a=[];
  if(o)a.push(`${o} ${plural(o,'sál','sály','sálů')}`);if(e)a.push(`${e} ${plural(e,'externí sál','externí sály','externích sálů')}`);if(p)a.push(`${p} ${plural(p,'dospávací pokoj','dospávací pokoje','dospávacích pokojů')}`);return a.join(' · ')||'prázdná';}
function mainSpec(room){const c={};tplFlat(D.template[room.id]).forEach(s=>{if(!s)return;if(s.a&&s.a.s)c[s.a.s]=(c[s.a.s]||0)+1;if(s.split&&s.b&&s.b.s)c[s.b.s]=(c[s.b.s]||0)+1;});return Object.keys(c).sort((a,b)=>c[b]-c[a])[0]||null;}
function roomColor(room){const c=room.color;if(c&&c.startsWith('spec:'))return (SPEC[c.slice(5)]||{}).c||'#A9B4B9';if(c)return c;if(room.kind==='pacu')return '#7FBFB1';const m=mainSpec(room);return m&&SPEC[m]?SPEC[m].c:'#A9B4B9';}
function colorLabel(room){const c=room.color;if(c&&c.startsWith('spec:'))return 'podle oboru '+specName(c.slice(5));if(c)return 'vlastní barva';const m=mainSpec(room);return 'automaticky'+(m?' – '+specName(m):'');}
const tmin=t=>{const [h,m]=String(t||'0:0').split(':').map(Number);return (h||0)*60+(m||0);};
const fT=t=>String(t||'').replace(/^0(\d)/,'$1');
const fH=H=>`${fT(H.from)}–${fT(H.to)}`;
const fHs=H=>{const q=t=>t.endsWith(':00')?String(+t.slice(0,2)):fT(t);return `${q(H.from)}–${q(H.to)}`;};
const sameH=(a,b)=>!!a&&!!b&&a.from===b.from&&a.to===b.to&&a.split===b.split;
const validH=H=>!!H&&!!H.from&&!!H.split&&!!H.to&&tmin(H.from)<tmin(H.split)&&tmin(H.split)<tmin(H.to);
const roomHours=room=>room.hours&&validH(room.hours)?room.hours:D.hours;
function hoursFor(room,ds){const dh=D.dayHours[room.id+'|'+ds];if(dh&&validH(dh))return {...dh,src:'day'};if(room.hours&&validH(room.hours))return {...room.hours,src:'room'};return {...D.hours,src:'def'};}
const halfT=(H,i)=>i===0?`${fT(H.from)}–${fT(H.split)}`:`${fT(H.split)}–${fT(H.to)}`;
const halfMin=(H,i)=>i===0?tmin(H.split)-tmin(H.from):tmin(H.to)-tmin(H.split);
const hoursInputs=(p,H)=>[['from','Začátek'],['split','Polovina dne'],['to','Konec']].map(([k,t])=>`<div class="fld"><label for="${p}-${k}">${t}</label><input class="in" type="time" step="900" id="${p}-${k}" data-ch="${p}" data-k="${k}" value="${H[k]||''}"></div>`).join('');
function roomBadges(r){return (r.hours&&validH(r.hours)&&!sameH(r.hours,D.hours)?`<span class="kb hrs" title="Pracovní doba ${fH(r.hours)}">${fHs(r.hours)}</span>`:'')+roomBadge1(r);}
function roomBadge1(r){if(r.kind==='pacu')return `<span class="kb pacu" title="Dospávací pokoj · ${r.beds||0} lůžek">DP·${r.beds||0}</span>`;if(r.kind==='ext')return `<span class="kb ext" title="Externí sál · ${anLabel(r.an)}">EXT</span>`;return `<span class="anb" title="Vybavení pro anestezii">${anLabel(r.an)}</span>`;}

/* =========================================================
   Výpočetní jádro: co se reálně děje na sále v daný den
   ========================================================= */
function blockFor(rid,ds){return D.blocks.find(b=>b.roomIds.includes(rid)&&ds>=b.from&&ds<=b.to)||null;}
function reqFor(sid,ds,half,site,roomId){return D.requests.find(q=>(q.status||'ok')==='ok'&&q.specId===sid&&ds>=q.from&&ds<=q.to&&(q.site==='all'||q.site===site)&&(q.part==='full'||(q.part==='am'&&half===0)||(q.part==='pm'&&half===1))&&(roomId==null||(()=>{const st=reqSet(q,ds,half);return st==='all'||st.has(roomId);})()))||null;}
function inSeason(se,ds){const md=ds.slice(5);return se.from<=se.to?(md>=se.from&&md<=se.to):(md>=se.from||md<=se.to);}
function seasonFor(ds){if(SEAC.has(ds))return SEAC.get(ds);const se=(D.seasons||[]).find(x=>x.active&&inSeason(x,ds))||null;SEAC.set(ds,se);return se;}
function tplOf(ds){const se=seasonFor(ds);return se?se.template:D.template;}
const parity=ds=>isoWeek(ds)%2?'l':'s';
const PAR_N={l:'lichý',s:'sudý'};
function tplPick(sl,ds){return sl&&sl.alt?(sl[parity(ds)]||null):(sl||null);}
function tplVars(sl){return sl&&sl.alt?[[sl.l||null,.5],[sl.s||null,.5]]:[[sl||null,1]];}
function tplFlat(t){return Object.values(t||{}).flatMap(sl=>sl&&sl.alt?[sl.l,sl.s]:[sl]).filter(Boolean);}
const DOW_A=['','pondělí','úterý','středu','čtvrtek','pátek','sobotu','neděli'],DOW_G=['','n','n','f','m','m','f','f'];
function kazdy(iso,p){const g=DOW_G[iso],k={n:'každé',f:'každou',m:'každý'}[g],a=p?{l:{n:'liché',f:'lichou',m:'lichý'},s:{n:'sudé',f:'sudou',m:'sudý'}}[p][g]+' ':'';return `${k} ${a}${DOW_A[iso]}`;}
function baseSlot(room,ds){const di=DI(ds);if(di.weekend||di.holiday)return null;return tplPick((tplOf(ds)[room.id]||{})[di.iso]||null,ds);}
function R(room,ds){
  if(room.kind==='pacu') return Rp(room,ds);
  const k=room.id+'|'+ds; let r=RC.get(k); if(r) return r;
  const di=DI(ds); const ov=Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(D.overrides,k); let slot,src;
  if(ov){slot=D.overrides[k];src='override';}
  else if(di.weekend){slot=null;src='weekend';}
  else if(di.holiday){slot=null;src='holiday';}
  else {slot=tplPick((tplOf(ds)[room.id]||{})[di.iso]||null,ds);src='template';}
  const halves=slot?[slot.a||null,slot.split?(slot.b||null):(slot.a||null)]:[null,null];
  r={room,ds,di,src,slot,block:blockFor(room.id,ds),h:[null,null],issues:[],lost:0,holLost:0,note:D.notes[k]||''};
  if(src==='holiday'){const t=tplPick((tplOf(ds)[room.id]||{})[di.iso],ds);if(t)r.holLost=t.split?((t.a?1:0)+(t.b?1:0)):2;}
  const planned=!!(halves[0]||halves[1]);
  if(r.block&&!(ov&&planned)){r.lost=halves.filter(Boolean).length;RC.set(k,r);return r;}
  if(r.block) r.issues.push({k:'block',t:`Sál je omezen (${r.block.type.toLowerCase()}), přesto je naplánován`});
  let anBad=false;
  for(let i=0;i<2;i++){
    const b=halves[i]; if(!b) continue;
    const h={s:b.s,an:b.an,released:false,req:null};
    const q=reqFor(b.s,ds,i,room.site,room.id);
    if(q){ if(ov){ if(!r.issues.some(x=>x.k==='req')) r.issues.push({k:'req',t:`${specName(b.s)}: ${q.type.toLowerCase()} (${PARTS[q.part].toLowerCase()})`}); } else {h.released=true;h.req=q;} }
    if(!h.released&&!anOk(room,b.an)) anBad=true;
    r.h[i]=h;
  }
  if(anBad) r.issues.push({k:'an',t:`Sál je vybaven jen pro ${room.an==='LA'?'lokální':'celkovou'} anestezii`});
  if(ov&&planned){ if(di.holiday) r.issues.push({k:'hol',t:`Naplánováno ve státní svátek (${di.holiday})`}); else if(di.weekend) r.issues.push({k:'wknd',t:'Naplánováno o víkendu'}); }
  RC.set(k,r); return r;
}
/* dospávací pokoj: otevřeno dopoledne / odpoledne, kontrola proti provozu jeho skupiny */
function pacuSlot(room,ds){
  const k=room.id+'|'+ds,di=DI(ds),ov=Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(D.overrides,k);let slot,src;
  if(ov){slot=D.overrides[k];src='override';}else if(di.weekend){slot=null;src='weekend';}else if(di.holiday){slot=null;src='holiday';}else{slot=tplPick((tplOf(ds)[room.id]||{})[di.iso]||null,ds);src='template';}
  const m=(slot&&slot.pacu)||null,block=blockFor(room.id,ds);
  let open=[m==='full'||m==='am',m==='full'||m==='pm'];const lost=block&&!ov?open.filter(Boolean).length:0;
  if(block&&!ov) open=[false,false];
  return {slot,src,ov,block,open,di,lost};
}
function groupLoad(gid,ds){
  const L=[0,0],C=[0,0]; if(!gid) return {L,C};
  for(const room of D.rooms){ if(!room.active||room.kind==='pacu'||gOf(room)!==gid) continue; const r=R(room,ds);
    r.h.forEach((h,i)=>{if(h&&!h.released){L[i]++;if(h.an==='CA')C[i]++;}}); }
  return {L,C};
}
function Rp(room,ds){
  const k=room.id+'|'+ds; let r=RC.get(k); if(r) return r;
  const p=pacuSlot(room,ds);
  r={room,ds,di:p.di,src:p.src,slot:p.slot,block:p.block,h:[null,null],open:p.open,pacu:true,issues:[],lost:p.lost,holLost:0,note:D.notes[k]||''};
  if(p.block&&p.ov&&(p.open[0]||p.open[1])) r.issues.push({k:'block',t:`Pokoj je omezen (${p.block.type.toLowerCase()}), přesto je otevřen`});
  const gid=gOf(room);
  if(gid){
    const pac=D.rooms.filter(x=>x.active&&x.kind==='pacu'&&gOf(x)===gid);
    if(pac[0]&&pac[0].id===room.id){
      const g=groupLoad(gid,ds),opens=pac.map(x=>x.id===room.id?p.open:pacuSlot(x,ds).open);
      const miss=[0,1].filter(i=>g.L[i]>0&&!opens.some(o=>o[i]));
      if(miss.length){const n=Math.max(...miss.map(i=>g.L[i]));r.issues.push({k:'pacu',t:`Skupina ${gName(gid)} operuje ${miss.length===2?'celý den':miss[0]===0?'dopoledne':'odpoledne'} (${n} ${plural(n,'sál','sály','sálů')}), dospávací pokoj je zavřený`});}
    }
  }
  if(p.ov&&(p.open[0]||p.open[1])){if(p.di.holiday)r.issues.push({k:'hol',t:`Otevřeno ve státní svátek (${p.di.holiday})`});else if(p.di.weekend)r.issues.push({k:'wknd',t:'Otevřeno o víkendu'});}
  RC.set(k,r); return r;
}
function SD(site,ds){
  const k=site+'|'+ds; let v=SC.get(k); if(v) return v;
  const di=DI(ds),n=D.staff.norm; const a={d:0,n:0,s:0},p={d:0,n:0,s:0};
  for(const room of D.rooms){ if(!room.active||room.site!==site) continue; const r=R(room,ds);
    if(room.kind==='pacu'){const st=room.staff||{doc:0,nurse:0};[a,p].forEach((acc,i)=>{if(r.open[i]){acc.d+=+st.doc||0;acc.n+=+st.nurse||0;}});continue;}
    [a,p].forEach((acc,i)=>{const h=r.h[i]; if(h&&!h.released){const q=n[h.an]||{};acc.d+=+q.doc||0;acc.n+=+q.nurse||0;acc.s+=+q.scrub||0;}}); }
  const c=D.staff.cap[site]||{},hol=!!di.school,up=x=>Math.ceil(x-1e-9);
  v={doc:up(Math.max(a.d,p.d)),nur:up(Math.max(a.n,p.n)),scr:up(Math.max(a.s,p.s)),capDoc:+(hol?c.docH:c.doc)||0,capNur:+(hol?c.nurseH:c.nurse)||0,capScr:+(hol?c.scrubH:c.scrub)||0,hol,h:[{doc:up(a.d),nur:up(a.n),scr:up(a.s)},{doc:up(p.d),nur:up(p.n),scr:up(p.s)}]};
  v.def=v.doc>v.capDoc||v.nur>v.capNur||v.scr>v.capScr;
  SC.set(k,v); return v;
}
function SDsum(sites,ds){const o={doc:0,nur:0,scr:0,capDoc:0,capNur:0,capScr:0,def:false};for(const s of sites){const v=SD(s,ds);for(const f of ['doc','nur','scr','capDoc','capNur','capScr'])o[f]+=v[f];if(v.def)o.def=true;}return o;}
function K(site=S.site){
  const key=S.year+'|'+site; if(KC.has(key)) return KC.get(key);
  const sites=scopeSites(site),days=yearDays(S.year),rooms=D.rooms.filter(r=>r.active&&sites.includes(r.site));
  let hrs=0,ca=0,la=0,rel=0,lost=0,pd=0,pn=0,ps=0,pdD='',pnD='',sd=0,sn=0,ss=0,wd=0,def=0;
  for(const ds of days){
    for(const room of rooms){const r=R(room,ds);lost+=r.lost;let H=null;r.h.forEach((h,i)=>{if(!h)return;if(h.released){rel++;return;}if(h.an==='CA')ca++;else la++;H=H||hoursFor(room,ds);hrs+=halfMin(H,i)/60;});}
    const st=SDsum(sites,ds); if(st.doc||st.nur||st.scr){wd++;sd+=st.doc;sn+=st.nur;ss+=st.scr;}
    if(st.doc>pd){pd=st.doc;pdD=ds;} if(st.nur>pn){pn=st.nur;pnD=ds;} if(st.scr>ps)ps=st.scr; if(st.def)def++;
  }
  const v={hrs,roomDays:(ca+la)/2,ca,la,caPct:ca+la?Math.round(ca*100/(ca+la)):0,rel,lost,pd,pn,ps,pdD,pnD,sd,sn,ss,avgD:wd?sd/wd:0,avgN:wd?sn/wd:0,avgS:wd?ss/wd:0,wd,def};
  KC.set(key,v); return v;
}
function ISSUES(){
  const key='iss|'+S.year; if(KC.has(key)) return KC.get(key);
  const out=[],days=yearDays(S.year);
  for(const room of D.rooms){ if(!room.active) continue;
    for(const ds of days){const r=R(room,ds);r.issues.forEach(i=>out.push({k:i.k,sev:(i.k==='hol'||i.k==='wknd')?'warn':'bad',roomId:room.id,ds,t:i.t}));} }
  for(const s of SITES){
    let run=null; const flush=()=>{if(run){out.push(run);run=null;}};
    for(const ds of days){const v=SD(s.id,ds);
      if(v.def){ if(run&&diffD(run.to,ds)>7) flush();
        if(!run) run={k:'staff',sev:'bad',site:s.id,from:ds,to:ds,n:0,md:0,cd:v.capDoc,mn:0,cn:v.capNur,ms:0,cs:v.capScr,dd:false,nd:false,xd:false};
        run.to=ds;run.n++;run.md=Math.max(run.md,v.doc);run.mn=Math.max(run.mn,v.nur);run.ms=Math.max(run.ms,v.scr);
        run.cd=Math.min(run.cd,v.capDoc);run.cn=Math.min(run.cn,v.capNur);run.cs=Math.min(run.cs,v.capScr);
        if(v.doc>v.capDoc)run.dd=true; if(v.nur>v.capNur)run.nd=true; if(v.scr>v.capScr)run.xd=true; } }
    flush();
  }
  (D.claims||[]).forEach(c=>{if(c.status==='pending'&&c.d.slice(0,4)===String(S.year))out.push({k:'claim',sev:'warn',c,from:c.d,to:c.d});});
  D.requests.forEach(q=>{if(q.status==='pending'&&q.to>=`${S.year}-01-01`&&q.from<=`${S.year}-12-31`)out.push({k:'pend',sev:'warn',q,from:q.from,to:q.to});});
  out.sort((a,b)=>(a.from||a.ds).localeCompare(b.from||b.ds));
  KC.set(key,out); return out;
}

const NAV=[['Provoz',[['home','Přehled','home'],['kal','Kalendář provozu','month'],['map','Operační sály 3D','cube'],['mine','Pohled oboru','user'],['timeline','Časová osa','tl']]],

function sideHTML(){
  const n=ISSUES().length,lab=S.mini?'Rozbalit menu':'Sbalit menu',pend=D.requests.filter(q=>q.status==='pending'&&(S.role!=='spec'||q.specId===S.roleSpec)).length,cl=(D.claims||[]).filter(c=>c.status==='pending').length;
  return `<div class="brand"><div class="logo">RO</div><div class="bt"><b>Rozpis operativy</b><small>Krajská nemocnice Liberec</small></div><button class="collapse" data-act="side" aria-label="${lab}" aria-expanded="${!S.mini}" title="${lab} (klávesa [ )">${ico(S.mini?'expand':'collapse')}</button></div>
  <button class="sbox" data-act="cmd" title="Hledat sály, obory, data a akce (Ctrl/⌘ + K)">${ico('search')}<span>Hledat…</span><kbd>⌘K</kbd></button>
  <div class="quick"><button class="tile" data-act="bulk" title="Hromadná úprava">${ico('layers')}<span>Hromadná<br>úprava</span></button><button class="tile" data-act="newReq" title="Nový požadavek">${ico('req')}<span>Nový<br>požadavek</span></button></div>
  <nav class="navs" aria-label="Hlavní navigace">${navList().map(([lab,items])=>`<div class="sec"><div class="nav-label"><i></i>${lab}</div>${items.map(([v,t,ic])=>`<button class="ni${S.view===v?' on':''}" data-act="go" data-v="${v}" title="${t}"${S.view===v?' aria-current="page"':''}>${ico(ic)}<span>${t}</span>${v==='alerts'&&n?`<em class="badge">${n}</em>`:''}${v==='requests'&&pend?`<em class="badge" title="Čeká na schválení">${pend}</em>`:''}${v==='free'&&cl&&isCoord()?`<em class="badge" title="Žádosti o volnou kapacitu">${cl}</em>`:''}</button>`).join('')}</div>`).join('')}</nav>
  <div class="side-foot"><div class="foot-row"><div class="rolew"><button class="who" data-act="rolePop" aria-expanded="${!!S.rolePop}" title="${esc(roleLabel())} – klepnutím přepnete roli">${ico('user')}<div><b>${esc(roleLabel())}</b><small>${S.role==='spec'?'požadavky ke schválení':S.role==='mgmt'?'jen čtení':'COS Liberec · editor'}</small></div></button>${S.rolePop?rolePopHTML():''}</div></div>
  <button class="linkbtn" data-act="help" title="Klávesové zkratky (?)">${ico('key')}<span>Klávesové zkratky</span></button>
  <button class="linkbtn${S.confirm==='reset'?' warn':''}" data-act="resetDemo" title="Obnovit ukázková data">${ico('reset')}<span>${S.confirm==='reset'?'Klepněte znovu – přepíše vaše úpravy':'Obnovit ukázková data'}</span></button></div>`;
}
function topHTML(){
  const n=ISSUES().length;
  return `<div class="years" role="tablist" aria-label="Plánovací rok">${YEARS.map(y=>`<button class="yr${S.year===y?' on':''}" role="tab" aria-selected="${S.year===y}" data-act="year" data-v="${y}">${y}</button>`).join('')}<span class="yr-note">${S.year>todayY?'plánovací rok':S.year===todayY?'aktuální rok':''}</span></div>
  <div class="top-r"><button class="btn sm srch" data-act="cmd" title="Hledat sály, obory, data a akce (Ctrl/⌘ + K)">${ico('search')}<span>Hledat</span><kbd>⌘K</kbd></button><button class="btn sm undo" data-act="undo"${UNDO.length?'':' disabled'} title="Vrátit poslední změnu (Ctrl/⌘ + Z)">${ico('undo')}<span>Zpět</span></button><button class="icon-btn thm" data-act="theme" data-v="${S.theme==='dark'?'light':'dark'}" aria-label="${S.theme==='dark'?'Přepnout na světlý vzhled':'Přepnout na tmavý vzhled'}" title="${S.theme==='dark'?'Světlý vzhled':'Tmavý vzhled'}">${ico(S.theme==='dark'?'sun':'moon')}</button><span class="status"><i></i>${isCoord()?'Lokální data · bez databáze':S.role==='spec'?esc(roleLabel())+' · požadavky':'Vedení · jen čtení'}</span><button class="btn-dark" data-act="bulk">${ico('plus')}Hromadná úprava</button><button class="icon-btn" data-act="go" data-v="alerts" aria-label="Upozornění (${n})">${ico('bell')}${n?`<b>${n>99?'99+':n}</b>`:''}</button></div>`;
}
function printMeta(){
  if(S.view==='kal')return `${S.bc.mode==='year'?S.year:cap(MONTHS[+bcInit().m.slice(5,7)-1])+' '+S.bc.m.slice(0,4)} · ${S.bc.site==='all'||!SITE[S.bc.site]?'všechna pracoviště':SITE[S.bc.site].name}`;
  if(S.view==='home')return `Týden ${isoWeek(wtWeek())} · ${S.wt.site==='all'||!SITE[S.wt.site]?'všechna pracoviště':SITE[S.wt.site].name}`;
  if(S.view==='wait')return `${S.year} · využití ${S.wlUtil} % · cíl ${S.wlTarget} týdnů`;
  if(S.view==='occ')return `${occLabel()} · ${S.occ.site==='all'?'všechna pracoviště':SITE[S.occ.site].name}`;
  if(S.view==='asanace')return `${S.year} · ${asLabel(asTarget())}`;
  if(S.view==='quota')return `${S.year} · ${S.qSite==='all'||!S.qSite?'všechna pracoviště':SITE[S.qSite].name}`;
  if(S.view==='home')return `stav k ${fmtY(today)}`;
  const scope=S.view==='staff'?(S.staffSite==='all'?'všechna pracoviště':SITE[S.staffSite].name):(S.site==='all'?'všechna pracoviště':SITE[S.site].name);
  if(S.view!=='timeline') return `${S.year} · ${S.view==='staff'||S.view==='timeline'?scope:'KNL'}`;
  const f=[periodLabel(),scope];
  if(S.kind!=='all') f.push({or:'jen sály',ext:'jen externí sály',pacu:'jen dospávací pokoje'}[S.kind]);
  if(S.an!=='all') f.push('anestezie '+S.an);
  if(S.specs.size) f.push('obory: '+[...S.specs].map(specName).join(', '));
  if(S.hidden.size) f.push(`skryto ${S.hidden.size} místností`);
  return f.join(' · ');
}
function ph(eb,t,right=''){
  const nx=upcoming(today,1)[0];
  return `<div class="print-h"><div><b>Rozpis operativy · Krajská nemocnice Liberec</b><span>${esc(t)}</span></div><div>${esc(printMeta())} · vytištěno ${fmtY(today)}</div></div>
  <div class="ph"><div><div class="eyebrow"><i></i>${eb}</div><h1>${t}</h1></div><div class="ph-r">${right||`<div class="today">Dnes · ${DOW[isoDow(today)].toLowerCase()} ${fmtY(today)}${nx?`<span><i></i>${nx.from===nx.to?fmt(nx.from):fmt(nx.from)+'–'+fmt(nx.to)} · ${esc(nx.label)}</span>`:''}</div>`}<button class="btn sm prt" data-act="print" title="Vytisknout tuto sekci">${ico('printer')}Tisk</button></div></div>`;
}
function kpi(label,num,unit,sub,color){return `<div class="kpi"${color?` style="--kc:${color}"`:''}><div class="lab"><i></i>${label}</div><div class="num">${num}${unit?`<small>${unit}</small>`:''}</div><div class="sub">${sub}</div></div>`;}
const empty=t=>`<div class="empty">${t}</div>`;

/* =========================================================
   Pohled: Časová osa
   ========================================================= */
function tlRange(){
  const y=S.year;
  if(S.zoom==='year') return [`${y}-01-01`,`${y}-12-31`];
  const a=s2d(S.anchor),m=a.getUTCMonth();
  if(S.zoom==='quarter'){const q=Math.floor(m/3);return [d2s(new Date(Date.UTC(y,q*3,1))),d2s(new Date(Date.UTC(y,q*3+3,0)))];}
  if(S.zoom==='month') return [d2s(new Date(Date.UTC(y,m,1))),d2s(new Date(Date.UTC(y,m+1,0)))];
  const mon=addD(S.anchor,-(isoDow(S.anchor)-1)); return [mon,addD(mon,6)];
}
function periodLabel(){
  const [a,b]=tlRange(),y=S.year;
  if(S.zoom==='year') return `Celý rok ${y}`;
  if(S.zoom==='quarter'){const q=Math.floor((+a.slice(5,7)-1)/3);return `Q${q+1} · ${MONTHS[q*3]}–${MONTHS[q*3+2]}`;}
  if(S.zoom==='month') return `${cap(MONTHS[+a.slice(5,7)-1])} ${y}`;
  return `Týden ${isoWeek(a)} · ${fmtR(a,b)}`;
}
function vTimeline(){
  const k=K(),iss=ISSUES();
  const sites=[['all','Vše'],...SITES.map(s=>[s.id,s.name])];
  const shown=D.rooms.filter(r=>r.active&&!S.hidden.has(r.id)).length,total=D.rooms.filter(r=>r.active).length;
  const scope=S.site==='all'?'všechna pracoviště':SITE[S.site].name;
  return ph('Plánování · '+scope,'Časová osa operačního provozu')+`
  <div class="kpis">
    ${kpi('Plánované sálodny '+S.year,fmtN(k.roomDays,1),'',`${fmtN(k.hrs)} sálových hodin · ${fmtN(k.ca/2,1)} CA · ${fmtN(k.la/2,1)} LA`)}
    ${kpi('Celková anestezie',k.caPct,'%','podíl půldenních bloků v CA','#86A0C2')}
    ${kpi('Anesteziologové',k.pd,'max / den',`Ø ${fmtN(k.avgD,1)} · celkem ${fmtN(k.sd)} lékař‑dnů`,'#76AFAA')}
    ${kpi('Anest. sestry',k.pn,'max / den',`Ø ${fmtN(k.avgN,1)} · celkem ${fmtN(k.sn)} sestra‑dnů`,'#A496CB')}
    ${kpi('Sálové sestry',k.ps,'max / den',`Ø ${fmtN(k.avgS,1)} · celkem ${fmtN(k.ss)} sestra‑dnů`,'#CF95A6')}
    ${kpi('Upozornění',iss.length,'',`${k.def} ${plural(k.def,'den','dny','dnů')} s nedostatkem personálu`,iss.length?'var(--bad)':'var(--ok)')}
  </div>
  <div class="card filters">
    <div class="frow">
      <div class="seg dark" role="group" aria-label="Měřítko">${Object.entries(ZL).map(([z,t])=>`<button data-act="zoom" data-v="${z}" class="${S.zoom===z?'on':''}">${t}</button>`).join('')}</div>
      <div class="period"><button class="icon-btn sm" data-act="nav" data-v="-1" aria-label="Předchozí období">${ico('cl')}</button><b>${periodLabel()}</b><button class="icon-btn sm" data-act="nav" data-v="1" aria-label="Další období">${ico('cr')}</button>${S.zoom!=='year'&&today.slice(0,4)==String(S.year)?`<button class="btn sm ghost" data-act="toToday">Dnes</button>`:''}</div>
      <span class="sp"></span>
      <span class="flab">Pracoviště</span><div class="seg">${sites.map(([v,t])=>`<button data-act="site" data-v="${v}" class="${S.site===v?'on':''}">${t}</button>`).join('')}</div>
      <span class="flab">Anestezie</span><div class="seg">${[['all','Vše'],['CA','CA'],['LA','LA']].map(([v,t])=>`<button data-act="anf" data-v="${v}" class="${S.an===v?'on':''}" ${v!=='all'?`title="${v==='CA'?'Celková':'Lokální'} anestezie"`:''}>${t}</button>`).join('')}</div>
      <span class="flab">Místnosti</span><div class="seg">${[['all','Vše'],['or','Sály'],['ext','Externí'],['pacu','Dospávací']].map(([v,t])=>`<button data-act="kindf" data-v="${v}" class="${S.kind===v?'on':''}">${t}</button>`).join('')}</div><span class="flab">Barvy</span><div class="seg">${[['spec','Podle oboru'],['room','Podle sálu']].map(([v,t])=>`<button data-act="colorBy" data-v="${v}" class="${S.colorBy===v?'on':''}">${t}</button>`).join('')}</div><div class="popw"><button class="btn sm" data-act="pop" aria-expanded="${S.pop}">Výběr <small>${shown}/${total}</small></button>${S.pop?popRooms():''}</div><button class="chip${S.asOn?' on':''}" data-act="asOn" title="Vyznačit navržené asanační dny">${ico('spark')}Asanace</button>
    </div>
    <div class="frow"><span class="flab">Obory</span><button class="chip${S.specs.size?'':' on'}" data-act="specAll">Všechny</button>${D.specs.map(s=>`<button class="chip${S.specs.has(s.id)?' on':''}" data-act="spec" data-v="${s.id}" style="--c:${s.c}"><i></i>${esc(s.name)}</button>`).join('')}</div>
  </div>
  <div class="card tl-card"><div class="tl-scroll" id="tlscroll"><div id="tl"></div></div>
    ${S.zoom==='year'?`<div class="legend ty-lg"><span class="lg"><i class="t1"></i>Barva = hlavní obor týdne, sytost = obsazenost</span><span class="lg"><i class="t2"></i>Dva obory v týdnu</span><span class="lg"><i class="t3"></i>Sál mimo provoz (omezení)</span><span class="lg"><i class="t4"></i>Část týdne omezena</span><span class="lg"><i class="t5"></i>Uvolněno požadavkem oboru</span><span class="lg"><i class="t6"></i>Státní svátek v týdnu</span><span class="lg"><i class="t7"></i>Školní prázdniny</span><span class="lg"><i class="t8"></i>Nedostatek personálu</span><span class="lg hint-dd">Klepnutím na dlaždici otevřete detail týdne</span>`:`<div class="legend">
      <span class="lg"><i class="l-ca"></i>Celková anestezie (CA)</span><span class="lg"><i class="l-la"></i>Lokální anestezie (LA)</span>
      <span class="lg"><i class="l-half"></i>Půlený den · dopoledne / odpoledne</span><span class="lg"><i class="l-rel"></i>Uvolněno požadavkem oboru</span>
      <span class="lg"><i class="l-x"></i>Omezení sálu</span><span class="lg"><i class="l-pacu"></i>Dospávací pokoj otevřen</span><span class="lg"><i class="l-hol"></i>Státní svátek</span>
      <span class="lg"><i class="l-sch"></i>Školní prázdniny (Liberecký kraj)</span><span class="lg"><i class="l-dot"></i>Konflikt</span>${(D.seasons||[]).some(x=>x.active)?'<span class="lg"><i class="l-sea"></i>Sezónní šablona</span>':''}${S.zoom==='month'||S.zoom==='week'?'<span class="lg hint-dd">Přetažením blok přesunete, na obsazený den vyměníte, s Alt/⌥ zkopírujete</span>':''}${S.asOn?`<span class="lg"><i class="l-as"></i>Navržený asanační den · ${esc(asLabel(asTarget()))}</span>`:''}`}
    </div></div>`;
}
function popRooms(){
  return `<div class="pop" role="dialog" aria-label="Výběr místností"><div class="pop-h"><b>Zobrazené místnosti</b><span><button class="linkbtn" data-act="roomsAll"><span>Vše</span></button><button class="linkbtn" data-act="roomsNone"><span>Nic</span></button></span></div>
  ${SITES.map(s=>{const rs=D.rooms.filter(r=>r.site===s.id&&r.active);if(!rs.length)return '';
    return `<div class="pop-g">${s.name}</div>${siteGroups(s.id).map(gid=>{const gr=sortRooms(rs.filter(r=>gOf(r)===gid));if(!gr.length)return '';const key=gid||'none:'+s.id,allOn=gr.every(r=>!S.hidden.has(r.id));
      return `<label class="cbx grp"><input type="checkbox" id="hg-${esc(key.replace(':','-'))}" data-ch="hideGroup" data-g="${esc(key)}"${allOn?' checked':''}><span><b>${gid?esc(gName(gid)):'Bez skupiny'}</b></span></label>${gr.map(r=>`<label class="cbx sub"><input type="checkbox" id="hv-${r.id}" data-ch="hideRoom" data-id="${r.id}"${S.hidden.has(r.id)?'':' checked'}><span><code>${esc(r.code)}</code> ${esc(r.name)}</span></label>`).join('')}`;}).join('')}`;}).join('')}</div>`;
}
const ZMIN={year:2.4,quarter:12,month:30,week:116};
const RH={year:17,quarter:26,month:40,week:60};
let lastTL='',PRINTING=false,lastViewKey='',lastIss=-1,lastKpiKey='';
const RM=()=>{try{return matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;}catch(e){return false;}};
function countUp(root){
  if(RM()) return;
  root.querySelectorAll('.kpi .num').forEach(el=>{const tn=el.firstChild;if(!tn||tn.nodeType!==3)return;const txt=tn.textContent,val=parseFloat(txt.replace(/[\s\u00a0]/g,'').replace(',','.'));if(!isFinite(val)||val===0)return;
    const dec=txt.includes(',')?1:0,t0=performance.now(),dur=750;
    const step=t=>{const q=Math.min(1,(t-t0)/dur),e=1-Math.pow(1-q,3);tn.textContent=q<1?fmtN(val*e,dec):txt;if(q<1)requestAnimationFrame(step);};requestAnimationFrame(step);});
}
/* ---------- časová osa · roční přehled po týdnech ---------- */
function tyWeeks(){const y=S.year;let m=addD(`${y}-01-04`,-(isoDow(`${y}-01-04`)-1));const out=[];while(+addD(m,3).slice(0,4)===y){out.push(m);m=addD(m,7);}return out;}
function tyAgg(room,mon){const o={cap:0,pl:0,blk:0,rel:0,hol:0,sp:{},open:0,days:0,ca:0,la:0,pacu:room.kind==='pacu'};
  for(let i=0;i<5;i++){const d=addD(mon,i),di=DI(d);if(di.holiday){o.hol++;continue;}o.days++;const r=R(room,d);
    if(o.pacu){if(r.block&&!(r.src==='override'&&(r.open[0]||r.open[1]))){o.blk+=2;o.cap+=2;continue;}r.open.forEach(x=>{o.cap++;if(x)o.open++;});continue;}
    if(r.block&&!(r.src==='override'&&(r.h[0]||r.h[1]))){o.blk+=2;o.cap+=2;continue;}
    for(let j=0;j<2;j++){const h=r.h[j];o.cap++;if(!h)continue;if(h.released){o.rel++;continue;}if(dimH(h))continue;o.pl++;o.sp[h.s]=(o.sp[h.s]||0)+1;if(h.an==='CA')o.ca++;else o.la++;}}
  return o;}
function tyTile(room,mon,wk){const a=tyAgg(room,mon),td=today>=mon&&today<=addD(mon,6),lab=`${esc(room.code)} · týden ${isoWeek(mon)} (${fmt(mon)}–${fmt(addD(mon,4))})`;let cls='ty-t'+(td?' td':''),st='',mk='',tip=[];
  if(!a.days){cls+=' hol';tip.push('státní svátky – bez provozu');}
  else if(a.blk>=a.cap){cls+=' blk';const b=blockFor(room.id,mon)||blockFor(room.id,addD(mon,2))||blockFor(room.id,addD(mon,4));tip.push(`omezení${b?' – '+b.type.toLowerCase():''}`);}
  else if(a.pacu){const u=a.cap?a.open/a.cap:0;if(u)st=`background:color-mix(in srgb,var(--dot) ${Math.round(18+u*62)}%,var(--raised))`;tip.push(`otevřeno ${a.open} z ${a.cap} půldnů`);}
  else{const top=Object.entries(a.sp).sort((x,y)=>y[1]-x[1]),u=a.cap?a.pl/a.cap:0;
    if(top.length){const c1=S.colorBy==='room'?roomColor(room):(SPEC[top[0][0]]||{}).c||'#999',k=Math.round(30+u*70);
      if(top[1]&&top[1][1]/a.pl>=.3&&S.colorBy!=='room'){const c2=(SPEC[top[1][0]]||{}).c||'#999',p=Math.round(top[0][1]/(top[0][1]+top[1][1])*100);st=`background:linear-gradient(180deg,color-mix(in srgb,${c1} ${k}%,var(--raised)) 0 ${p}%,color-mix(in srgb,${c2} ${k}%,var(--raised)) ${p}% 100%)`;}
      else st=`background:color-mix(in srgb,${c1} ${k}%,var(--raised))`;
      if(wk>=34)mk+=`<b>${esc(top[0][0])}</b>`;
      tip.push(top.map(([s,n])=>`${s} ${n}`).join(' · ')+` půlbloků`,`obsazenost ${Math.round(u*100)} %`);if(a.la)tip.push(`LA ${a.la} · CA ${a.ca}`);}
    else tip.push('bez programu');
    if(a.blk){mk+='<i class="k"></i>';tip.push(`omezení ${a.blk} ${plural(a.blk,'půlblok','půlbloky','půlbloků')}`);}}
  if(a.rel){mk+='<i class="r"></i>';tip.push(`uvolněno požadavky ${a.rel}`);}
  if(a.hol&&a.days)mk+='<i class="h"></i>',tip.push('obsahuje státní svátek');
  return `<button class="${cls}"${st?` style="${st}"`:''} data-act="tyWeek" data-d="${mon}" title="${lab} · ${esc(tip.join(' · '))}">${mk}</button>`;}
function drawTimelineYear(host,sc){const W=PRINTING?1040:(sc.clientWidth||900),LW=PRINTING?170:(W<560?120:240),wks=tyWeeks(),n=wks.length,cw=PRINTING?(W-LW-1)/n:Math.max(18,(W-LW-8)/n),cols=`${LW}px repeat(${n},${cw}px)`,total=Math.ceil(LW+n*cw+8);
  let h=`<div class="tl ty" style="width:${total}px">`;
  h+=`<div class="tl-r ty-hd" style="grid-template-columns:${cols}"><div class="tl-lab">Měsíc</div>`;for(let i=0;i<n;){const m=addD(wks[i],3).slice(0,7);let j=i;while(j<n&&addD(wks[j],3).slice(0,7)===m)j++;const w=(j-i)*cw,mi=+m.slice(5)-1;h+=`<div class="ty-m" style="grid-column:span ${j-i}">${w>70?cap(MONTHS[mi]):MON_SH[mi]}</div>`;i=j;}h+='</div>';
  h+=`<div class="tl-r ty-hd ty-wk" style="grid-template-columns:${cols}"><div class="tl-lab">Týden</div>${wks.map((m,i)=>{const td=today>=m&&today<=addD(m,6);return `<div class="ty-w${td?' td':''}">${cw>=22||i%2===0||td?isoWeek(m):''}</div>`;}).join('')}</div>`;
  h+=`<div class="tl-r ty-hd ty-band" style="grid-template-columns:${cols}"><div class="tl-lab">Svátky · prázdniny</div>${wks.map(m=>{let hol=[],sch=null;for(let i=0;i<7;i++){const di=DI(addD(m,i));if(di.holiday&&!di.weekend)hol.push(di.holiday);if(di.school&&!sch)sch=di.school;}return `<div class="ty-b${sch?' sch':''}" title="${esc([...hol,sch?sch.name:''].filter(Boolean).join(' · '))}">${hol.length?'<i></i>':''}</div>`;}).join('')}</div>`;
  if(AS_MARK)h+=`<div class="tl-r ty-hd ty-band" style="grid-template-columns:${cols}"><div class="tl-lab" title="Vhodnost pro asanaci · ${esc(AS_MARK.label)}">Asanace · ${esc(AS_MARK.label)}</div>${wks.map(m=>{let best=0,rec=false;for(let i=0;i<5;i++){const d=addD(m,i),a=asDay(d,AS_MARK.ids);if(a&&!a.full)best=Math.max(best,a.score);if(AS_MARK.days.has(d))rec=true;}return `<div class="ty-a${rec?' rec':''}" title="Týden ${isoWeek(m)}${rec?' · doporučený termín asanace':''}"${best?` style="background:color-mix(in srgb,var(--ok) ${Math.round(20+best*.75)}%,transparent)"`:''}></div>`;}).join('')}</div>`;
  const sites=scopeSites(),kindOk=r=>S.kind==='all'||(r.kind||'or')===S.kind;let rows=0;
  for(const sid of sites){const siteRooms=D.rooms.filter(r=>r.active&&r.site===sid&&!S.hidden.has(r.id)&&kindOk(r));if(!siteRooms.length)continue;
    h+=`<div class="tl-gr site"><div class="tl-g"><b>${SITE[sid].name}</b><small>${roomCounts(siteRooms)}</small></div></div>`;
    for(const gid of siteGroups(sid)){const rs=sortRooms(siteRooms.filter(r=>gOf(r)===gid));if(!rs.length)continue;
      h+=`<div class="tl-gr grp"><div class="tl-g"><b>${gid?esc(gName(gid)):'Bez skupiny'}</b><small>${roomCounts(rs)}</small></div></div>`;
      for(const room of rs){rows++;h+=`<div class="tl-r ty-room k-${room.kind||'or'}" style="grid-template-columns:${cols}" data-room="${room.id}"><div class="tl-lab" title="${esc(room.name)} · ${esc(room.code)}"><i class="rdot" style="--c:${roomColor(room)}"></i><b>${esc(room.name)}</b><code>${esc(room.code)}</code><span class="bdg">${roomBadges(room)}</span></div>${wks.map(m=>tyTile(room,m,cw)).join('')}</div>`;}}}
  if(!rows)h+=`<div class="tl-empty" style="width:${Math.min(total,W)}px">Žádný sál není vybrán. Upravte výběr v nabídce Sály.</div>`;
  h+=`<div class="tl-gr site"><div class="tl-g"><b>Personál</b><small>nejvytíženější den týdne · ${sites.length>1?'všechna pracoviště':SITE[sites[0]].name}</small></div></div>`;
  [['doc','Anesteziologové','capDoc'],['nur','Anest. sestry','capNur'],['scr','Sálové sestry','capScr']].forEach(([k,lab,ck])=>{h+=`<div class="tl-r ty-room ty-st" style="grid-template-columns:${cols}"><div class="tl-lab"><b>${lab}</b></div>`+wks.map(m=>{let r=0,bad=0,worst=null;for(let i=0;i<5;i++){const d=addD(m,i),di=DI(d);if(di.holiday)continue;sites.forEach(st=>{const v=SD(st,d),rr=v[ck]?v[k]/v[ck]:v[k]?9:0;if(v[k]>v[ck])bad++;if(rr>=r){r=rr;worst={d,n:v[k],c:v[ck],st};}});}
      const cl=!r?'z':bad?'r4':r>=.9?'r3':r>=.7?'r2':'r1';return `<button class="ty-t st ${cl}${today>=m&&today<=addD(m,6)?' td':''}" data-act="tyWeek" data-d="${m}" title="${lab} · týden ${isoWeek(m)}${worst?` · nejvíc ${worst.n} z ${worst.c} (${DOW[isoDow(worst.d)]} ${fmt(worst.d)}${sites.length>1?' · '+SITE[worst.st].name:''})`:''}${bad?` · nedostatek ${bad}×`:''}">${bad&&cw>=20?`<b>${bad}</b>`:''}</button>`;}).join('')+'</div>';});
  h+='</div>';host.innerHTML=h;
  const tw=wks.findIndex(m=>today>=m&&today<=addD(m,6));if(tw>=0&&total>W)sc.scrollLeft=Math.max(0,LW+tw*cw-(W-LW)/3);else sc.scrollLeft=0;lastTL=null;}

function drawTimeline(keepLeft){
  const host=$('#tl'),sc=$('#tlscroll'); if(!host||!sc) return;
  const [from,to]=tlRange(); const days=[]; for(let d=from;d<=to;d=addD(d,1)) days.push(d);
  AS_MARK=S.asOn&&asTarget().length?asMark():null;
  if(S.zoom==='year'){drawTimelineYear(host,sc);return;}
  const n=days.length,W=PRINTING?1040:(sc.clientWidth||900),LW=PRINTING?150:(W<560?112:196);
  const cw=PRINTING?(W-LW-1)/n:Math.max(ZMIN[S.zoom],(W-LW-1)/n), cols=`${LW}px repeat(${n},${cw}px)`, total=Math.ceil(LW+n*cw);
  const z={year:'z-y',quarter:'z-q',month:'z-m',week:'z-w'}[S.zoom];
  const key=S.zoom+from+to+S.site+S.kind,fresh=false;
  let h=`<div class="tl ${z}" style="width:${total}px;--rh:${RH[S.zoom]}px">`;
  // měsíce
  h+=`<div class="tl-r tl-h" style="grid-template-columns:${cols}"><div class="tl-lab">${S.zoom==='week'?'Týden '+isoWeek(from):'Měsíc'}</div>`;
  for(let i=0;i<n;){const m=days[i].slice(0,7);let j=i;while(j<n&&days[j].slice(0,7)===m)j++;const w=(j-i)*cw,mi=+m.slice(5)-1;h+=`<div class="hm" style="grid-column:span ${j-i}">${w>78?cap(MONTHS[mi])+(S.zoom==='month'||S.zoom==='week'?' '+m.slice(0,4):''):w>26?MON_SH[mi]:''}</div>`;i=j;}
  h+='</div>';
  // svátky a prázdniny
  h+=`<div class="tl-r tl-band" style="grid-template-columns:${cols}"><div class="tl-lab">Svátky · prázdniny</div>`;
  days.forEach((d,i)=>{const di=DI(d);h+=`<div class="hb${di.school?' sch':''}${di.holiday?' hol':''}${seasonFor(d)?' sea':''}"${di.holiday||di.school?` data-bd="${d}"`:''}></div>`;});
  for(let i=0;i<n;){const s=DI(days[i]).school;if(!s){i++;continue;}let j=i;while(j<n&&DI(days[j]).school===s)j++;const w=(j-i)*cw;if(w>58)h+=`<span class="hsl" style="left:${LW+i*cw+5}px;max-width:${w-10}px">${esc(w>130?s.name:s.short)}</span>`;i=j;}
  h+='</div>';
  // vhodnost pro asanaci
  if(AS_MARK){h+=`<div class="tl-r tl-band as" style="grid-template-columns:${cols}"><div class="tl-lab" title="Vhodnost pro asanaci · ${esc(AS_MARK.label)}">Asanace · ${esc(AS_MARK.label)}</div>`;
    days.forEach(d=>{const a=asDay(d,AS_MARK.ids);if(!a){h+='<div class="ab"></div>';return;}h+=`<div class="ab${AS_MARK.days.has(d)?' rec':''}${a.full?' full':''}" data-ad="${d}"${a.full?'':` style="background:color-mix(in srgb,var(--ok) ${Math.round(26+a.score*.74)}%,transparent)"`}></div>`;});
    h+='</div>';}
  // dny
  if(cw>=16){h+=`<div class="tl-r tl-days" style="grid-template-columns:${cols}"><div class="tl-lab">Den</div>`;days.forEach(d=>{const di=DI(d);h+=`<div class="hd${di.weekend?' we':''}${di.holiday?' hol':''}${d===today?' td':''}${d.slice(8)==='01'?' m1':''}"><b>${+d.slice(8)}</b>${cw>=24?`<small>${DOW[di.iso]}</small>`:''}</div>`;});h+='</div>';}
  // místnosti: pracoviště → skupina → sály, externí sály, dospávací pokoje
  const sites=scopeSites(); let rows=0;
  const kindOk=r=>S.kind==='all'||(r.kind||'or')===S.kind;
  for(const sid of sites){
    const siteRooms=D.rooms.filter(r=>r.active&&r.site===sid&&!S.hidden.has(r.id)&&kindOk(r)); if(!siteRooms.length) continue;
    h+=`<div class="tl-gr site"><div class="tl-g"><b>${SITE[sid].name}</b><small>${roomCounts(siteRooms)}</small></div></div>`;
    for(const gid of siteGroups(sid)){
      const rs=sortRooms(siteRooms.filter(r=>gOf(r)===gid)); if(!rs.length) continue;
      h+=`<div class="tl-gr grp"><div class="tl-g"><b>${gid?esc(gName(gid)):'Bez skupiny'}</b><small>${roomCounts(rs)}</small></div></div>`;
      for(const room of rs){rows++;
        h+=`<div class="tl-r tl-room k-${room.kind||'or'}" style="grid-template-columns:${cols};--i:${rows}" data-room="${room.id}"><div class="tl-lab" title="${esc(room.name)} · ${esc(room.code)}${room.loc?' · '+esc(room.loc):''}"><i class="rdot" style="--c:${roomColor(room)}"></i><b>${esc(room.name)}</b><code>${esc(room.code)}</code><span class="bdg">${roomBadges(room)}</span></div>`;
        for(const d of days) h+=cellHTML(room,d);
        h+='</div>';
      }
    }
  }
  if(!rows) h+=`<div class="tl-empty" style="width:${Math.min(total,W)}px">Žádný sál není vybrán. Upravte výběr v nabídce Sály.</div>`;
  h+=staffRows(days,cols,sites);
  h+='</div>';
  host.innerHTML=h;
  if(keepLeft!=null&&lastTL===key) sc.scrollLeft=keepLeft;
  else if(today>=from&&today<=to&&total>W){const idx=diffD(from,today);sc.scrollLeft=Math.max(0,LW+idx*cw-(W-LW)/3);}
  else sc.scrollLeft=0;
  lastTL=key;
}
const dimH=h=>(S.specs.size&&!S.specs.has(h.s))||(S.an!=='all'&&h.an!==S.an);
function bk(h,part,col){
  const sp=SPEC[h.s]||{c:'#999',name:h.s};
  let inner='';
  if(S.zoom==='month') inner=`<span>${esc(h.s)}</span>`;
  else if(S.zoom==='week') inner=`<span>${esc(sp.name)}</span><em>${h.released?'uvolněno':h.an}</em>`;
  return `<i class="b ${part} ${h.an}${h.released?' rel':''}${dimH(h)?' dim':''}" style="--c:${col||sp.c}">${inner}</i>`;
}
function cellHTML(room,d){
  const r=R(room,d),di=r.di;
  let cls='c'+(di.holiday?' hol':di.weekend?' we':di.school?' sch':'');
  if(d.slice(8)==='01') cls+=' m1'; else if(di.iso===1) cls+=' w1';
  if(d===today) cls+=' td';
  if(AS_MARK&&AS_MARK.rooms.has(room.id)&&AS_MARK.days.has(d)) cls+=' rec';
  let inner='';
  const xb=()=>`<i class="x">${S.zoom==='week'?`<span>${esc(r.block.type)}</span>`:''}</i>`;
  if(r.pacu){
    const [o1,o2]=r.open;
    if(r.block&&!(r.src==='override'&&(o1||o2))) inner=xb();
    else{const lab=S.zoom==='month'?'<span>DP</span>':S.zoom==='week'?`<span>Otevřeno</span><em>${room.beds||0} l.</em>`:'';
      if(o1&&o2) inner=`<i class="b pacu full">${lab}</i>`; else{if(o1)inner+=`<i class="b pacu am">${lab}</i>`;if(o2)inner+=`<i class="b pacu pm">${lab}</i>`;}}
  } else {
    const planned=r.h[0]||r.h[1];
    if(r.block&&!(r.src==='override'&&planned)) inner=xb();
    else{
      const [a,b]=r.h,split=r.slot&&r.slot.split,col=S.colorBy==='room'?roomColor(room):null;
      if(!split&&a&&b&&a.released===b.released) inner=bk(a,'full',col);
      else{ if(a) inner+=bk(a,'am',col); if(b) inner+=bk(b,'pm',col); }
      if(!a&&!b&&S.zoom==='week'&&r.src==='holiday'&&r.holLost) inner=`<span class="ct">Svátek</span>`;
    }
  }
  if(r.issues.length) inner+='<b class="dot"></b>';
  if(S.zoom!=='year'&&D.dayHours[room.id+'|'+d]) inner+='<b class="tmk"></b>';
  const dg=isCoord()&&(S.zoom==='month'||S.zoom==='week')&&!r.pacu&&r.slot&&(r.h[0]||r.h[1])&&!(r.block&&r.src!=='override');
  return `<div class="${cls}" data-r="${room.id}" data-d="${d}"${dg?' draggable="true"':''}>${inner}</div>`;
}
function staffRows(days,cols,sites){
  const bars=S.zoom==='year'||S.zoom==='quarter';
  let h=`<div class="tl-gr site"><div class="tl-g"><b>Personál</b><small>potřeba / kapacita · ${sites.length>1?'součet všech pracovišť':SITE[sites[0]].name}</small></div></div>`;
  [['doc','Anesteziologové','capDoc'],['nur','Anest. sestry','capNur'],['scr','Sálové sestry','capScr']].forEach(([k,lab,ck])=>{
    const mx=Math.max(1,...days.map(d=>{const s=SDsum(sites,d);return Math.max(s[k],s[ck]);}));
    h+=`<div class="tl-r tl-st" style="grid-template-columns:${cols};--i:${30}"><div class="tl-lab"><b>${lab}</b><small>Σ ${sites.length>1?'KNL':SITE[sites[0]].name}</small></div>`;
    days.forEach(d=>{const s=SDsum(sites,d),v=s[k],c=s[ck],bad=v>c,di=DI(d);
      let cls='sc'+(di.holiday?' hol':di.weekend?' we':'')+(d.slice(8)==='01'?' m1':'')+(d===today?' td':'');
      let inner='';
      if(bars){ if(v) inner=`<i class="bar${bad?' bad':''}" style="height:${v/mx*86}%"></i>`; inner+=`<i class="cap" style="bottom:${c/mx*86}%"></i>`; }
      else if(v||(!di.weekend&&!di.holiday)) inner=`<span class="n${bad?' bad':''}">${v}</span><small>/${c}</small>`;
      h+=`<div class="${cls}" data-st="${k}" data-d="${d}">${inner}</div>`;});
    h+='</div>';
  });
  return h;
}

/* ---------- tooltip ---------- */
function tipHalf(lab,h,t){const s=SPEC[h.s];return `<div><i class="tsw" style="--c:${s?s.c:'#999'}"></i><span>${lab}${t?' '+t:''} · ${esc(s?s.name:h.s)} · ${h.an==='CA'?'celková':'lokální'}${h.released?` <em>(uvolněno – ${esc(h.req.type.toLowerCase())})</em>`:''}</span></div>`;}
function tipPacu(room,d){
  const r=R(room,d),di=r.di,L=[],[o1,o2]=r.open,st=room.staff||{doc:0,nurse:0},H=hoursFor(room,d);
  if(r.block&&!(r.src==='override'&&(o1||o2))) L.push(`<div><i class="tk k-x"></i><span>${esc(r.block.type)}${r.block.note?' – '+esc(r.block.note):''}</span></div>`);
  else L.push(`<div><span>${o1&&o2?'Otevřeno celý den · '+fH(H):o1?'Otevřeno dopoledne · '+halfT(H,0):o2?'Otevřeno odpoledne · '+halfT(H,1):'Zavřeno'}</span></div>`);
  L.push(`<div><span>${room.beds||0} ${plural(room.beds||0,'lůžko','lůžka','lůžek')} · sestry ${fmtN(st.nurse||0,1)}${st.doc?' · lékař '+fmtN(st.doc,1):''}</span></div>`);
  const gid=gOf(room); if(gid){const g=groupLoad(gid,d);L.push(`<div><span>Skupina ${esc(gName(gid))}: dopoledne ${g.L[0]}, odpoledne ${g.L[1]} ${plural(g.L[1],'sál','sály','sálů')}</span></div>`);}
  if(di.holiday) L.push(`<div><i class="tk k-hol"></i><span>${esc(di.holiday)}</span></div>`);
  if(di.school) L.push(`<div><i class="tk k-sch"></i><span>${esc(di.school.name)}</span></div>`);
  r.issues.forEach(i=>L.push(`<div><i class="tk k-bad"></i><span>${esc(i.t)}</span></div>`));
  if(r.note) L.push(`<div><span>„${esc(r.note)}“</span></div>`);
  return `<b>${esc(room.name)}</b> <span class="t2">${esc(room.code)} · ${SITE[room.site].name}</span><div class="t2">${cap(DOW_L[di.iso])} ${fmtY(d)}${r.src==='override'?' · ruční úprava':''}</div><div class="tlines">${L.join('')}</div>`;
}
function tipCell(rid,d){
  const room=ROOM[rid];if(!room)return '';
  if(room.kind==='pacu') return tipPacu(room,d);
  const H=hoursFor(room,d);const r=R(room,d),di=r.di,L=[];
  const planned=r.h[0]||r.h[1];
  if(r.block&&!(r.src==='override'&&planned)) L.push(`<div><i class="tk k-x"></i><span>${esc(r.block.type)}${r.block.note?' – '+esc(r.block.note):''}</span></div>`);
  else{const [a,b]=r.h,split=r.slot&&r.slot.split;
    if(!a&&!b) L.push(`<div><span>${r.src==='holiday'?'Státní svátek – bez plánované operativy':r.src==='weekend'?'Víkend – mimo plán':'Bez operativy'}</span></div>`);
    else if(!split&&a&&b&&a.released===b.released) L.push(tipHalf('Celý den',a,fH(H)));
    else{L.push(a?tipHalf('Dopoledne',a,halfT(H,0)):`<div><span>Dopoledne ${halfT(H,0)} · volno</span></div>`);L.push(b?tipHalf('Odpoledne',b,halfT(H,1)):`<div><span>Odpoledne ${halfT(H,1)} · volno</span></div>`);}}
  {const se=seasonFor(d);if(se)L.push(`<div><span>Sezónní šablona: ${esc(se.name)}</span></div>`);}
  if(H.src!=='def') L.push(`<div><span>Pracovní doba ${fH(H)} · ${H.src==='day'?'upraveno pro tento den':'vlastní pro sál'}</span></div>`);
  if(di.holiday) L.push(`<div><i class="tk k-hol"></i><span>${esc(di.holiday)}</span></div>`);
  if(di.school) L.push(`<div><i class="tk k-sch"></i><span>${esc(di.school.name)}</span></div>`);
  r.issues.forEach(i=>L.push(`<div><i class="tk k-bad"></i><span>${esc(i.t)}</span></div>`));
  if(r.note) L.push(`<div><span>„${esc(r.note)}“</span></div>`);
  return `<b>${esc(room.name)}</b> <span class="t2">${esc(room.code)} · ${SITE[room.site].name}${room.loc?' · '+esc(room.loc):''}</span><div class="t2">${cap(DOW_L[di.iso])} ${fmtY(d)}${r.src==='override'?' · ruční úprava':''}</div><div class="tlines">${L.join('')}</div>`;
}
function tipStaff(k,d){
  const [vk,ck,lab]=SF[k]||SF.doc,di=DI(d);
  let h=`<b>${lab}</b><div class="t2">${cap(DOW_L[di.iso])} ${fmtY(d)}${di.school?' · prázdninová kapacita':''}</div><div class="tlines">`;
  scopeSites().forEach(s=>{const v=SD(s,d),n=v[vk],c=v[ck];h+=`<div><span>${SITE[s].name}: <b class="${n>c?'tbad':''}">${n}</b> z ${c} · ${c>=n?'volno '+(c-n):'chybí '+(n-c)}</span></div>`;});
  return h+'</div>';
}
function tipBand(d){const di=DI(d);return `<b>${cap(DOW_L[di.iso])} ${fmtY(d)}</b><div class="tlines">${di.holiday?`<div><i class="tk k-hol"></i><span>${esc(di.holiday)}</span></div>`:''}${di.school?`<div><i class="tk k-sch"></i><span>${esc(di.school.name)}${di.school.note?' · '+esc(di.school.note):''}</span></div>`:''}</div>`;}
function tipChart(d){const di=DI(d),s=SDsum(scopeSites(S.staffSite),d),[vk,ck,lab]=SF[S.staffMode]||SF.doc,v=s[vk],c=s[ck];return `<b>${cap(DOW_L[di.iso])} ${fmtY(d)}</b><div class="tlines"><div><span>${lab}: potřeba <b class="${v>c?'tbad':''}">${v}</b> · kapacita ${c}${di.school?' (prázdniny)':''} · ${c>=v?'volno může čerpat '+(c-v):'<b class="tbad">chybí '+(v-c)+'</b>'}</span></div>${di.holiday?`<div><i class="tk k-hol"></i><span>${esc(di.holiday)}</span></div>`:''}</div>`;}
const tip=$('#tip');
function placeTip(e){const r=tip.getBoundingClientRect();let x=e.clientX+14,y=e.clientY+16;if(x+r.width>innerWidth-8)x=e.clientX-r.width-14;if(y+r.height>innerHeight-8)y=e.clientY-r.height-14;tip.style.left=Math.max(8,x)+'px';tip.style.top=Math.max(8,y)+'px';}
document.addEventListener('mouseover',e=>{
  const c=e.target.closest('#tl [data-d],#tl [data-bd],[data-cd],[data-ad],[data-oc],[data-orr]');
  if(!c){tip.hidden=true;return;}
  let html='';
  if(c.dataset.r) html=tipCell(c.dataset.r,c.dataset.d); else if(c.dataset.st) html=tipStaff(c.dataset.st,c.dataset.d);
  else if(c.dataset.bd) html=tipBand(c.dataset.bd); else if(c.dataset.cd) html=tipChart(c.dataset.cd); else if(c.dataset.ad) html=tipAs(c.dataset.ad); else if(c.dataset.oc) html=tipOcc(+c.dataset.oc); else if(c.dataset.orr) html=tipOccRoom(c.dataset.orr);
  if(!html){tip.hidden=true;return;}
  tip.innerHTML=html;tip.hidden=false;placeTip(e);
});
document.addEventListener('mousemove',e=>{if(!tip.hidden)placeTip(e);});
document.addEventListener('scroll',()=>{tip.hidden=true;placeDP();},true);

/* =========================================================
   Pohled: Operační sály (+ šablona + obory)
   ========================================================= */
function vRooms(){
  const segs=[['all','Vše'],...SITES.map(s=>[s.id,s.name])];
  return ph('Konfigurace','Sály a skupiny')+`<div class="stack">
  <section class="card"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Skupiny, sály a dospávací pokoje</div><p class="hint">Sály, externí sály i dospávací pokoje řaďte do skupin výběrem ve sloupci Skupina. Dospávací pokoj hlídá, zda je otevřený, když jeho skupina operuje.</p></div>
    ${SITES.map(siteBlock).join('')}</section>
  <section class="card"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Výchozí pracovní doba</div><p class="hint">Platí pro místnosti bez vlastní pracovní doby. Vlastní dobu nastavíte u místnosti ve sloupci Pracovní doba, výjimku pro konkrétní den klepnutím do časové osy.</p></div>
    <div class="tgrid" style="max-width:540px">${hoursInputs('defH',D.hours)}</div>
    <p class="hint" style="margin-top:10px">Dopoledne ${halfT(D.hours,0)} · odpoledne ${halfT(D.hours,1)} · vlastní pracovní dobu má ${D.rooms.filter(r=>r.hours&&validH(r.hours)).length} ${plural(D.rooms.filter(r=>r.hours&&validH(r.hours)).length,'místnost','místnosti','místností')}.</p>
  </section>
  ${seasonCard()}<section class="card" id="tpl-card"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>${S.tplEdit&&S.tplEdit!=='base'&&curSeason()?'Sezónní šablona · '+esc(curSeason().name):'Týdenní šablona provozu'}</div>${tplSel()}<div class="seg">${segs.map(([v,t])=>`<button data-act="tplSite" data-v="${v}" class="${S.tplSite===v?'on':''}">${t}</button>`).join('')}</div></div>
    <p class="hint" style="margin-bottom:12px">Šablona se opakuje po celý rok. Státní svátky ji automaticky přeruší, požadavky oborů uvolní jejich bloky. Den sálu lze půlit na dopoledne a odpoledne s různými obory, dospávací pokoj může být otevřený celý den nebo jen půlden. Výjimky pro konkrétní den nastavíte v časové ose.</p>
    <div class="ovx">${tplGrid()}</div></section>
  <section class="card"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Obory</div><span class="hint">Klepnutím na barvu změníte odstín v časové ose.</span></div>
    <div class="specs">${D.specs.map(specRow).join('')}</div>
    <div class="newspec"><input class="in" id="ns-name" placeholder="Název nového oboru" aria-label="Název nového oboru"><input class="in mono" id="ns-code" placeholder="KÓD" maxlength="4" aria-label="Kód oboru"><button class="btn" data-act="addSpec">${ico('plus')}Přidat obor</button></div>
  </section></div>`;
}
function siteBlock(s){
  const rs=D.rooms.filter(r=>r.site===s.id),groups=D.groups.filter(g=>g.site===s.id),loose=sortRooms(rs.filter(r=>!gOf(r)));
  return `<div class="site-block"><div class="site-h"><b>${s.name}</b><span class="hint">${roomCounts(rs)} · ${groups.length} ${plural(groups.length,'skupina','skupiny','skupin')}</span><button class="btn sm" data-act="addGroup" data-site="${s.id}">${ico('plus')}Nová skupina</button></div>
    ${groups.map(g=>groupCard(g,sortRooms(rs.filter(r=>gOf(r)===g.id)),s.id)).join('')}
    ${loose.length||!groups.length?groupCard(null,loose,s.id):''}
  </div>`;
}
const RR_HEAD='<div class="rr head"><span>Název</span><span>Kód</span><span>Typ</span><span>Skupina</span><span>Vybavení / kapacita</span><span>Pracovní doba</span><span>Provoz</span><span></span></div>';
function groupCard(g,rs,site){
  const id=g?g.id:null,ops=rs.filter(r=>r.kind!=='pacu'&&r.active).length,pac=rs.filter(r=>r.kind==='pacu'&&r.active).length;
  const add=k=>`<button class="btn sm ghost" data-act="addRoom" data-kind="${k}" ${id?`data-group="${id}"`:`data-site="${site}"`}>${ico('plus')}${k==='or'?'Sál':k==='ext'?'Externí sál':'Dospávací pokoj'}</button>`;
  return `<div class="gcard${g?'':' none'}"><div class="gcard-h">${g?`<input class="in" id="gn-${id}" data-ch="groupName" data-id="${id}" value="${esc(g.name)}" aria-label="Název skupiny">`:'<b>Bez skupiny</b>'}<span class="meta">${roomCounts(rs)}</span>${g&&ops&&!pac?'<span class="tag">bez dospávacího pokoje</span>':''}
    <div class="acts">${add('or')}${add('ext')}${add('pacu')}${g?`<button class="icon-btn sm${S.confirm==='group:'+id?' armed':''}" data-act="delGroup" data-id="${id}" aria-label="Zrušit skupinu">${S.confirm==='group:'+id?'Zrušit skupinu?':ico('trash')}</button>`:''}</div></div>
    ${rs.length?RR_HEAD+rs.map(roomRow).join(''):'<p class="hint">Skupina je prázdná. Přidejte místnost, nebo přesuňte existující výběrem skupiny.</p>'}
  </div>`;
}
function colorPop(r){
  const ms=mainSpec(r),cur=r.color||'',hex=roomColor(r);
  return `<div class="cpop" role="dialog" aria-label="Barva místnosti"><b>Barva: ${esc(r.name)}</b>
    <button class="cp-o${!cur?' on':''}" data-act="rcSet" data-id="${r.id}" data-v=""><i style="--c:${r.kind==='pacu'?'#7FBFB1':ms&&SPEC[ms]?SPEC[ms].c:'#A9B4B9'}"></i><span>Automaticky${r.kind==='pacu'?'':' podle hlavního oboru'+(ms?' ('+esc(specName(ms))+')':'')}</span></button>
    <span class="fl">Podle oboru</span>
    <div class="cp-grid">${D.specs.map(sp=>`<button class="cp-o${cur==='spec:'+sp.id?' on':''}" data-act="rcSet" data-id="${r.id}" data-v="spec:${sp.id}"><i style="--c:${sp.c}"></i><span>${esc(sp.name)}</span></button>`).join('')}</div>
    <span class="fl">Vlastní barva</span>
    <label class="cp-cust"><input type="color" id="rcc-${r.id}" data-ch="rcCustom" data-id="${r.id}" value="${/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(hex)?hex:'#a9b4b9'}"><span>${cur&&!cur.startsWith('spec:')?'Vlastní barva je nastavena':'Vyberte libovolný odstín'}</span></label>
  </div>`;
}
function hoursPop(r){
  const H=S.hDraft||{...roomHours(r)},own=!!(r.hours&&validH(r.hours)),g=gOf(r),ok=validH(H);
  return `<div class="cpop hpop" role="dialog" aria-label="Pracovní doba"><b>Pracovní doba: ${esc(r.name)}</b>
    <div class="tgrid">${hoursInputs('hpH',H)}</div>
    ${ok?`<p class="hint">Dopoledne ${halfT(H,0)} · odpoledne ${halfT(H,1)} · celkem ${fmtN((tmin(H.to)-tmin(H.from))/60,2)} h</p>`:'<p class="warn-t">Začátek musí být před polovinou dne a polovina před koncem.</p>'}
    <div class="rp-g" style="padding:0">${[['07:00','11:30','15:30','7–15:30'],['07:00','13:00','19:00','7–19 prodloužený'],['07:30','11:30','15:00','7:30–15'],['08:00','11:00','14:00','8–14']].map(([a,b,c,t])=>`<button class="chip sm" data-act="hPreset" data-v="${a}|${b}|${c}">${t}</button>`).join('')}</div>
    <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap"><button class="btn sm pri" data-act="hSave" data-id="${r.id}"${ok?'':' disabled'}>Uložit pro místnost</button>${g?`<button class="btn sm" data-act="hSaveGroup" data-id="${r.id}"${ok?'':' disabled'}>Celá skupina ${esc(gName(g))}</button>`:''}${own?`<button class="btn sm ghost" data-act="hReset" data-id="${r.id}">Výchozí ${fH(D.hours)}</button>`:''}</div>
  </div>`;
}
function groupOptions(r){
  return SITES.map(s=>`<optgroup label="${s.name}">${D.groups.filter(g=>g.site===s.id).map(g=>`<option value="${g.id}"${gOf(r)===g.id?' selected':''}>${esc(g.name)}</option>`).join('')}<option value="none:${s.id}"${!gOf(r)&&r.site===s.id?' selected':''}>Bez skupiny (${s.name})</option></optgroup>`).join('');
}
function roomRow(r){
  const k=r.kind||'or';let det='';
  if(k==='pacu'){const st=r.staff||{doc:0,nurse:0};
    det=`<label>Lůžka <input class="in num" type="number" min="0" max="60" step="1" id="pb-${r.id}" data-ch="pacuF" data-k="beds" data-id="${r.id}" value="${r.beds||0}"></label><label>Sestry <input class="in num" type="number" min="0" max="20" step="0.5" id="pn-${r.id}" data-ch="pacuF" data-k="nurse" data-id="${r.id}" value="${st.nurse||0}"></label><label>Lékaři <input class="in num" type="number" min="0" max="10" step="0.5" id="pd-${r.id}" data-ch="pacuF" data-k="doc" data-id="${r.id}" value="${st.doc||0}"></label>`;}
  else det=`<div class="seg sm" role="group" aria-label="Vybavení pro anestezii">${['CA+LA','CA','LA'].map(v=>`<button data-act="roomAn" data-id="${r.id}" data-v="${v}" class="${r.an===v?'on':''}" title="${v==='CA+LA'?'Celková i lokální':v==='CA'?'Jen celková':'Jen lokální'}">${v==='CA+LA'?'CA + LA':'jen '+v}</button>`).join('')}</div>${k==='ext'?`<input class="in loc" id="rl-${r.id}" data-ch="roomLoc" data-id="${r.id}" value="${esc(r.loc||'')}" placeholder="Umístění / oddělení" aria-label="Umístění externího sálu">`:''}`;
  return `<div class="rr${r.active?'':' off'}">
    <div class="rname cpw"><button class="sw sm${r.color?'':' auto'}" data-act="rcOpen" data-id="${r.id}" style="--c:${roomColor(r)}" aria-label="Barva místnosti: ${esc(colorLabel(r))}" title="Barva: ${esc(colorLabel(r))}"></button><input class="in" id="rn-${r.id}" data-ch="roomName" data-id="${r.id}" value="${esc(r.name)}" aria-label="Název">${S.cpop===r.id?colorPop(r):''}</div>
    <input class="in" id="rc-${r.id}" data-ch="roomCode" data-id="${r.id}" value="${esc(r.code)}" aria-label="Kód" maxlength="6">
    <select class="in" id="rk-${r.id}" data-ch="roomKind" data-id="${r.id}" aria-label="Typ místnosti">${Object.entries(KINDS).map(([v,t])=>`<option value="${v}"${k===v?' selected':''}>${t}</option>`).join('')}</select>
    <select class="in" id="rg-${r.id}" data-ch="roomGroup" data-id="${r.id}" aria-label="Skupina">${groupOptions(r)}</select>
    <div class="det">${det}</div>
    <div class="hpw"><button class="hbtn${r.hours&&validH(r.hours)?'':' def'}" data-act="hOpen" data-id="${r.id}" title="Pracovní doba – ${r.hours&&validH(r.hours)?'vlastní':'výchozí'}">${ico('clock')}${fH(roomHours(r))}</button>${S.hpop===r.id?hoursPop(r):''}</div>
    <label class="tog"><input type="checkbox" id="ra-${r.id}" data-ch="roomActive" data-id="${r.id}"${r.active?' checked':''}><span></span><em>${r.active?'v provozu':'mimo provoz'}</em></label>
    <button class="icon-btn sm${S.confirm==='room:'+r.id?' armed':''}" data-act="delRoom" data-id="${r.id}" aria-label="Odstranit">${S.confirm==='room:'+r.id?'Smazat?':ico('trash')}</button>
  </div>`;
}
function tpCell(slot){
  const one=(h,half,lab)=>{ if(!h) return `<span class="tp free${half?' h':''}">${half?`<i>${lab}</i> `:''}volno</span>`; const s=SPEC[h.s]||{c:'#999',name:h.s}; return `<span class="tp ${h.an}${half?' h':''}" style="--c:${s.c}">${half?`<i>${lab}</i>`:''}<b>${esc(h.s)}</b>${half?'':`<span>${esc(s.name)}</span>`}<em>${h.an}</em></span>`; };
  if(!slot) return one(null,false);
  if(!slot.split) return one(slot.a,false);
  return one(slot.a,true,'dop')+one(slot.b,true,'odp');
}
function tpPacu(slot,H=D.hours){const m=slot&&slot.pacu;if(!m)return '<span class="tp free">zavřeno</span>';return `<span class="tp pacu"><b>DP</b><span>${PARTS[m]}</span><em>${m==='full'?fH(H):m==='am'?halfT(H,0):halfT(H,1)}</em></span>`;}
function tplGrid(){
  const sites=S.tplSite==='all'?SITES.map(s=>s.id):[S.tplSite];
  const cse=curSeason();
  let h=`${cse?`<div class="note sch tpl-note">${ico('info')}<div>Upravujete sezónní šablonu <b>${esc(cse.name)}</b> (${fmtMD(cse.from)}–${fmtMD(cse.to)}${cse.active?'':', zatím neaktivní'}). Mimo toto období platí základní šablona.</div></div>`:''}<div class="tpl"><div class="th">Místnost</div>${[1,2,3,4,5].map(i=>`<div class="th">${DOW_L[i]}</div>`).join('')}`;
  for(const sid of sites){const all=D.rooms.filter(r=>r.site===sid);if(!all.length)continue;
    h+=`<div class="tpl-sg">${SITE[sid].name}</div>`;
    for(const gid of siteGroups(sid)){const rs=sortRooms(all.filter(r=>gOf(r)===gid));if(!rs.length)continue;
      h+=`<div class="tpl-g">${gid?esc(gName(gid)):'Bez skupiny'}<small>${roomCounts(rs)}</small></div>`;
      for(const r of rs){h+=`<div class="tpl-l${r.active?'':' off'}"><i class="rdot" style="--c:${roomColor(r)}"></i><b>${esc(r.name)}</b><code>${esc(r.code)}</code><span class="bdg">${roomBadges(r)}</span></div>`;
        for(let i=1;i<=5;i++){const sl=(curTpl()[r.id]||{})[i]||null,al=!!(sl&&sl.alt)&&r.kind!=='pacu';h+=`<button class="tc${al?' alt':''}" data-act="tpl" data-r="${r.id}" data-i="${i}" aria-label="${esc(r.name)}, ${DOW_L[i]}${al?', střídání lichý a sudý týden':''}">${r.kind==='pacu'?tpPacu(sl,roomHours(r)):al?tpAlt(sl):tpCell(sl)}</button>`;}}
    }
  }
  return h+`</div><p class="hint" style="margin-top:10px">Klepnutím na den upravíte šablonu. Den lze nastavit tak, aby se střídal v lichých (L) a sudých (S) týdnech. Tento týden je ${isoWeek(today)}. – ${PAR_N[parity(today)]}.</p>`;
}
function tpAlt(sl){return ['l','s'].map(p=>`<span class="tpa"><i title="${PAR_N[p]} týden">${p.toUpperCase()}</i><span class="tpa-c">${tpCell(sl[p]||null)}</span></span>`).join('');}
function specUse(id){let n=0;for(const t of Object.values(D.template))for(const v of Object.values(t))for(const [s,w] of tplVars(v)){if(!s)continue;if(s.a&&s.a.s===id)n+=w;if(s.split&&s.b&&s.b.s===id)n+=w;}return n;}
function specRow(s){
  const u=specUse(s.id),inUse=u||(D.seasons||[]).some(se=>JSON.stringify(se.template).includes('"s":"'+s.id+'"'))||D.requests.some(q=>q.specId===s.id)||Object.values(D.overrides).some(o=>o&&((o.a&&o.a.s===s.id)||(o.b&&o.b.s===s.id)));
  return `<div class="sr"><button class="sw" data-act="specColor" data-id="${s.id}" style="--c:${s.c}" aria-label="Změnit barvu oboru ${esc(s.name)}"></button><input class="in" id="sn-${s.id}" data-ch="specName" data-id="${s.id}" value="${esc(s.name)}" aria-label="Název oboru"><code>${s.id}</code><span class="hint">${fmtN(u,u%1?1:0)} ${plural(Math.ceil(u),'blok','bloky','bloků')}/týden</span><button class="icon-btn sm" data-act="delSpec" data-id="${s.id}" ${inUse?'disabled title="Obor je použit v plánu"':''} aria-label="Odstranit obor">${ico('trash')}</button></div>`;
}

/* =========================================================
   Pohled: Požadavky oborů
   ========================================================= */
const opt=(arr,v)=>arr.map(([k,t])=>`<option value="${esc(k)}"${k===v?' selected':''}>${esc(t)}</option>`).join('');
const RQ_ST={pending:'Čeká na schválení',ok:'Schváleno',rejected:'Zamítnuto'};
function rqDefault(){const sp=S.role==='spec'&&SPEC[S.roleSpec]?S.roleSpec:(D.specs[0]?D.specs[0].id:'');return {specId:sp,type:REQ_TYPES[0],from:`${S.year}-03-01`,to:`${S.year}-03-01`,part:'full',site:'all',scope:'all',cnt:1,rooms:[],note:'',status:S.role==='spec'?'pending':'ok'};}
function reqImpact(q){let n=0;for(let d=q.from;d<=q.to;d=addD(d,1))for(const room of D.rooms){if(!room.active)continue;const r=R(room,d);for(const h of r.h)if(h&&h.req===q)n++;}return n;}
function vRequests(){
  const d=S.rqDraft||(S.rqDraft=rqDefault());
  const list=D.requests.filter(q=>q.to>=`${S.year}-01-01`&&q.from<=`${S.year}-12-31`&&(S.role!=='spec'||q.specId===S.roleSpec)).sort((a,b)=>a.from.localeCompare(b.from));
  return ph('Plánování','Požadavky oborů')+`<div class="stack">
  <section class="card" id="rq-form"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>${S.rqEdit?'Upravit požadavek':'Nový požadavek na omezení operativy'}</div>${S.rqEdit?`<button class="btn sm ghost" data-act="rqCancel">Zrušit úpravu</button>`:''}</div>
    <div class="grid-f">
      <div class="fld"><label for="rq-spec">Obor</label><select class="in" id="rq-spec" data-f="rq.specId"${S.role==='spec'?' disabled':''}>${opt(D.specs.map(s=>[s.id,s.name]),d.specId)}</select></div>
      <div class="fld"><label for="rq-type">Důvod</label><select class="in" id="rq-type" data-f="rq.type">${opt(REQ_TYPES.map(t=>[t,t]),d.type)}</select></div>
      <div class="fld"><label for="rq-from">Od</label>${dateField('rq-from',d.from,'data-f="rq.from" data-role="from" data-pair="rq-to"')}</div>
      <div class="fld"><label for="rq-to">Do</label>${dateField('rq-to',d.to,'data-f="rq.to" data-role="to" data-pair="rq-from"')}</div>
      <div class="fld"><label for="rq-part">Rozsah dne</label><select class="in" id="rq-part" data-f="rq.part">${opt(Object.entries(PARTS),d.part)}</select></div>
      <div class="fld"><label for="rq-site">Pracoviště</label><select class="in" id="rq-site" data-f="rq.site">${opt([['all','Všechna pracoviště'],...SITES.map(s=>[s.id,s.name])],d.site)}</select></div>
      <div class="fld"><label for="rq-status">Stav</label><select class="in" id="rq-status" data-f="rq.status"${S.role==='spec'?' disabled':''}>${opt(Object.entries(RQ_ST),d.status||'ok')}</select></div>
    </div>
    <div id="rq-rooms">${rqRoomsHTML()}</div>
    <div class="fld"><label for="rq-note">Poznámka</label><input class="in" id="rq-note" data-f="rq.note" value="${esc(d.note)}" placeholder="Název akce, počet nepřítomných lékařů…"></div>
    ${impactBox('rq')}
    <div class="frm-foot"><p class="hint" id="rq-msg">Bloky oboru se v zadaném období automaticky uvolní a nezapočítají se do potřeby personálu. Uvolněný sál můžete v časové ose přidělit jinému oboru.</p><button class="btn pri" data-act="rqSave">${S.rqEdit?'Uložit změny':'Přidat požadavek'}</button></div>
  </section>
  ${rqListCard(list)}</div>`;
}
function rqListCard(list){
  const f=S.rqFilter||'all',cnt=k=>list.filter(q=>(q.status||'ok')===k).length,L=f==='all'?list:list.filter(q=>(q.status||'ok')===f);
  return `<section class="card"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Požadavky ${S.year}<span class="cnt">${list.length}</span></div>
    <div class="seg">${[['all','Vše',list.length],['pending','Čeká',cnt('pending')],['ok','Schválené',cnt('ok')],['rejected','Zamítnuté',cnt('rejected')]].map(([v,t,n])=>`<button data-act="rqFilter" data-v="${v}" class="${f===v?'on':''}">${t} <small>${n}</small></button>`).join('')}</div></div>
    ${L.length?`<div class="list">${L.map(q=>{const s=SPEC[q.specId]||{c:'#999',name:q.specId},st=q.status||'ok',dd=diffD(q.from,q.to)+1,c=st==='ok'?{n:reqImpact(q)}:st==='pending'?rqCalc(q,q.id):null;
      return `<div class="li${st==='rejected'?' rej':''}">
      <div class="when">${fmtR(q.from,q.to)}<small>${dd} ${plural(dd,'den','dny','dnů')} · ${PARTS[q.part].toLowerCase()}</small></div>
      <div><div class="l1"><span class="chip st" style="--c:${s.c}"><i></i>${esc(s.name)}</span><span class="tag">${esc(q.type)}</span><span class="tag">${q.site==='all'?'Všechna pracoviště':SITE[q.site].name}</span>${reqScopeTag(q)}<span class="tag ${st==='ok'?'ok':st==='pending'?'sch':'bad'}">${RQ_ST[st]}</span></div>${q.note?`<div class="l2">${esc(q.note)}</div>`:''}</div>
      <div class="act">${c?`<span class="imp">${st==='pending'?'po schválení uvolní':'uvolněno'} <b>${c.n}</b> ${plural(c.n,'půlblok','půlbloky','půlbloků')}</span>`:''}
        ${isCoord()&&st!=='ok'?`<button class="btn sm pri" data-act="rqSet" data-id="${q.id}" data-v="ok">${ico('check')}Schválit</button>`:''}${isCoord()&&st==='pending'?`<button class="btn sm" data-act="rqSet" data-id="${q.id}" data-v="rejected">Zamítnout</button>`:''}
        ${st==='ok'?plLinkBtn('q',q.id):''}<button class="btn sm" data-act="jump" data-d="${q.from}">Na ose</button>${isCoord()||(S.role==='spec'&&q.specId===S.roleSpec&&st==='pending')?`<button class="icon-btn sm" data-act="rqEdit" data-id="${q.id}" aria-label="Upravit">${ico('edit')}</button><button class="icon-btn sm" data-act="rqDel" data-id="${q.id}" aria-label="Odstranit">${ico('trash')}</button>`:''}</div>
    </div>`;}).join('')}</div>`:empty(f==='all'?`Pro rok ${S.year} zatím nejsou zadané žádné požadavky oborů.`:'V tomto stavu nejsou žádné požadavky.')}
  </section>`;
}

/* ---------- požadavky oborů: omezení jen části sálů ---------- */
function reqRoomsAt(sid,ds,half,site){const L=[];for(const room of D.rooms){if(!room.active||room.kind==='pacu'||(site&&site!=='all'&&room.site!==site))continue;const k=room.id+'|'+ds,ov=Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(D.overrides,k),sl=ov?D.overrides[k]:baseSlot(room,ds);if(!sl)continue;const h=half===0?sl.a:(sl.split?sl.b:sl.a);if(!h||h.s!==sid)continue;const o2=half===0?(sl.split?sl.b:sl.a):sl.a;L.push({room,blk:!!blockFor(room.id,ds),ov,both:!!(o2&&o2.s===sid)});}
  const sc={};L.forEach(o=>{sc[o.room.site]=(sc[o.room.site]||0)+1;});
  L.sort((a,b)=>(b.blk-a.blk)||(b.both-a.both)||((mainSpec(a.room)===sid)-(mainSpec(b.room)===sid))||(sc[b.room.site]-sc[a.room.site])||b.room.code.localeCompare(a.room.code,'cs',{numeric:true}));return L;}
function reqSet(q,ds,half){const sc=q.scope||'all';if(sc==='all')return 'all';const key='rqs|'+q.id+'|'+ds+'|'+half;let s=KC.get(key);if(s!==undefined)return s;
  s=sc==='rooms'?new Set(q.rooms||[]):new Set(reqRoomsAt(q.specId,ds,half,q.site).filter(o=>!o.ov).slice(0,Math.max(1,+q.cnt||1)).map(o=>o.room.id));KC.set(key,s);return s;}
function reqScopeTag(q){const sc=q.scope||'all';if(sc==='cnt'){const n=Math.max(1,+q.cnt||1);return `<span class="tag rq-sc">−${n} ${plural(n,'sál','sály','sálů')} denně</span>`;}if(sc==='rooms')return `<span class="tag rq-sc">jen ${esc((q.rooms||[]).map(id=>(ROOM[id]||{}).code||id).join(', '))}</span>`;return '';}
function rqRoomsData(d){const out={rooms:new Map(),max:0,maxAt:null,halves:0};if(!d||!SPEC[d.specId]||!d.from||!d.to||d.from>d.to)return out;const to=diffD(d.from,d.to)>120?addD(d.from,120):d.to,sc=d.scope||'all',ids=new Set(d.rooms||[]),N=Math.max(1,+d.cnt||1);
  for(let x=d.from;x<=to;x=addD(x,1))[0,1].forEach(i=>{if((d.part==='am'&&i!==0)||(d.part==='pm'&&i!==1))return;const L=reqRoomsAt(d.specId,x,i,d.site);if(!L.length)return;out.halves++;if(L.length>out.max){out.max=L.length;out.maxAt=[x,i];}
    const pick=sc==='cnt'?new Set(L.filter(o=>!o.ov).slice(0,N).map(o=>o.room.id)):null;
    L.forEach(({room,blk,ov})=>{let r=out.rooms.get(room.id);if(!r){r={room,n:0,blk:0,rel:0,dow:new Set()};out.rooms.set(room.id,r);}r.n++;if(blk)r.blk++;r.dow.add(DI(x).iso);
      if(!ov&&(sc==='all'||(sc==='rooms'&&ids.has(room.id))||(sc==='cnt'&&pick.has(room.id))))r.rel++;});});
  return out;}
function rqRoomsHTML(){const d=S.rqDraft;if(!d||!SPEC[d.specId]||!d.from||!d.to||d.from>d.to)return '';const X=rqRoomsData(d),list=[...X.rooms.values()].sort((a,b)=>a.room.code.localeCompare(b.room.code,'cs',{numeric:true})),sc=d.scope||'all',sp=SPEC[d.specId],N=Math.max(1,+d.cnt||1);
  if(!list.length)return `<div class="rq-rooms"><div class="rq-rh"><b>Sály oboru v termínu</b></div><p class="hint" style="margin:0">${esc(sp.name)} nemá v zadaném termínu${d.site==='all'?'':' na pracovišti '+SITE[d.site].name} žádné přidělené sály.</p></div>`;
  const ids=new Set(d.rooms||[]);
  const cards=list.map(r=>{const sel=sc==='rooms'&&ids.has(r.room.id),on=r.rel>0||sel,main=mainSpec(r.room)===d.specId,st=!on?'zůstává':r.rel>=r.n?'uvolní se':'částečně';
    return `<button type="button" class="rq-rm${on?' on':''}${sc==='rooms'?' pick':''}" data-act="rqRoom" data-v="${r.room.id}" style="--c:${roomColor(r.room)}"${sc==='rooms'?` aria-pressed="${sel}"`:''}><span class="rq-rt">${sc==='rooms'?`<s class="rq-ck${sel?' y':''}">${sel?ico('check'):''}</s>`:'<i></i>'}<b>${esc(r.room.code)}</b><span>${esc(r.room.name)}</span><em class="${on?'y':''}">${st}</em></span>
      <span class="rq-rd">${[1,2,3,4,5].map(i=>`<u class="${r.dow.has(i)?'y':''}">${DOW[i]}</u>`).join('')}</span>
      <span class="rq-rb"><u><i style="width:${r.n?Math.round(r.rel/r.n*100):0}%"></i></u><b>${r.rel}/${r.n}</b></span>
      <small>${r.rel?`uvolní ${r.rel} z ${r.n} ${plural(r.n,'půlbloku','půlbloků','půlbloků')}`:`${r.n} ${plural(r.n,'půlblok','půlbloky','půlbloků')} zůstává`}${main?' · hlavní sál oboru':''}${r.blk?` · ${r.blk}× omezení sálu`:''}${d.site==='all'?' · '+SITE[r.room.site].name:''}</small></button>`;}).join('');
  return `<div class="rq-rooms"><div class="rq-rh"><div><b>Sály oboru v termínu</b><small>${list.length} ${plural(list.length,'sál','sály','sálů')} · současně nejvíc ${X.max}${X.maxAt?` (${DOW[DI(X.maxAt[0]).iso].toLowerCase()} ${fmt(X.maxAt[0])} ${X.maxAt[1]?'odpoledne':'dopoledne'})`:''}</small></div>
    <div class="seg">${[['all','Všechny sály'],['cnt','O počet sálů'],['rooms','Vybrané sály']].map(([v,t])=>`<button type="button" data-act="rqScope" data-v="${v}" class="${sc===v?'on':''}">${t}</button>`).join('')}</div></div>
    ${sc==='cnt'?`<div class="rq-cnt"><span class="flab">Omezit o</span><div class="seg">${Array.from({length:Math.max(1,X.max)},(_,i)=>i+1).map(n=>`<button type="button" data-act="rqCnt" data-v="${n}" class="${N===n?'on':''}">${n} ${plural(n,'sál','sály','sálů')}</button>`).join('')}</div><span class="hint">Každý den obor přijde nejvýš o ${N} ${plural(N,'sál','sály','sálů')}. Přednostně se uvolní sály, které jsou ten den stejně omezené, sály s celodenním blokem, sály, kde obor není hlavní, a sály na pracovišti, kde má obor víc sálů. Konkrétní sály zvolíte v režimu Vybrané sály.</span></div>`:''}
    ${sc==='rooms'?`<p class="hint" style="margin:0 0 8px">Klepnutím vyberte sály, které obor v termínu uvolní. Ostatní sály oboru zůstanou v plánu.</p>`:''}
    <div class="rq-rl">${cards}</div></div>`;}
function rqRoomsUpd(){const r=$('#rq-rooms');if(r)r.innerHTML=rqRoomsHTML();updImpact('rq');}

/* =========================================================
   Návrh obsazení – provázání požadavků, omezení a plánu
   ========================================================= */
const PL_H=[[4,'4 týdny'],[8,'8 týdnů'],[13,'Čtvrtletí'],[26,'Půl roku'],[0,'Celý rok']];
function plInit(){const o=S.pl||(S.pl={h:8,site:'all',f:'all',src:null,pick:{},skip:false});if(!o.pick)o.pick={};return o;}
function plRange(){const o=plInit(),y0=`${S.year}-01-01`,y1=`${S.year}-12-31`,t=addD(today,1);let a=t>y0?t:y0;if(a>y1)a=y0;const b=o.h?addD(a,o.h*7-1):y1;return [a,b<y1?b:y1];}
const plKey=(rid,d,i)=>`${rid}|${d}|${i}`;
function plLinks(){if(!D.links||typeof D.links!=='object')D.links={};return D.links;}
function plSkips(){if(!D.planSkip||typeof D.planSkip!=='object')D.planSkip={};return D.planSkip;}
function plSrcOk(L,d,room){if(L.src==='req'){const q=D.requests.find(x=>x.id===L.id);return !!q&&(q.status||'ok')==='ok'&&d>=q.from&&d<=q.to;}const b=D.blocks.find(x=>x.id===L.id),od=L.od||d;return !!b&&od>=b.from&&od<=b.to&&b.roomIds.includes(L.from);}
function plLinkState(k,L){const [rid,d,ii]=k.split('|'),room=ROOM[rid];if(!room)return 'gone';const h=R(room,d).h[+ii];if(!h||h.s!==L.s||h.released)return 'gone';return plSrcOk(L,d,room)?'ok':'orphan';}
function planModel(){const o=plInit(),[from,to]=plRange(),key=`plan|${S.year}|${o.h}|${o.site}|${from}|${DV}`;if(KC.has(key))return KC.get(key);
  const sites=scopeSites(o.site),rooms=D.rooms.filter(r=>r.active&&r.kind!=='pacu'&&sites.includes(r.site)),Q={},W={},RA={},GA={},gk=r=>gOf(r)||'n:'+r.site;
  quotaModel(o.site).rows.forEach(r=>Q[r.s.id]=r.p);waitModel().rows.forEach(r=>W[r.s.id]=r);
  rooms.forEach(rm=>{const s=new Set();tplFlat(D.template[rm.id]).forEach(sl=>{if(sl.a&&sl.a.s)s.add(sl.a.s);if(sl.split&&sl.b&&sl.b.s)s.add(sl.b.s);});RA[rm.id]=s;const g=gk(rm);GA[g]=GA[g]||new Set();s.forEach(x=>GA[g].add(x));});
  const done=new Set();Object.entries(plLinks()).forEach(([k,L])=>{if(L.src==='blk'&&plLinkState(k,L)!=='gone')done.add(`${L.id}|${L.from}|${L.od}|${L.oi}`);});
  const free=new Map(),blk=[],rel=[],y1=`${S.year}-12-31`,ext=addD(to,28)<y1?addD(to,28):y1;
  for(let d=from;d<=ext;d=addD(d,1)){const di=DI(d);if(di.weekend||di.holiday)continue;
    for(const room of rooms){const r=R(room,d);
      if(r.block&&!(r.src==='override'&&(r.h[0]||r.h[1]))){if(d>to||!r.lost||!r.slot)continue;const sl=r.slot,hs=[sl.a||null,sl.split?(sl.b||null):(sl.a||null)];
        hs.forEach((h,i)=>{if(!h||!h.s||reqFor(h.s,d,i,room.site,room.id)||done.has(`${r.block.id}|${room.id}|${d}|${i}`))return;blk.push({t:'blk',id:'b|'+plKey(room.id,d,i),room,d,i,s:h.s,an:h.an,b:r.block});});continue;}
      freeAt(room,d).forEach((f,i)=>{if(!f)return;const h=r.h[i];free.set(plKey(room.id,d,i),{room,d,i,src:f.src,q:f.q,s:f.s,an:h&&h.an||null});});}}
  const taken=new Map();
  blk.forEach(x=>{let best=null,bs=-1;for(const [k,f] of free){if(f.d!==x.d||f.i!==x.i||taken.has(k))continue;const c=f.room;if(c.site!==x.room.site||(c.kind==='ext'&&x.room.kind!=='ext')||!anOk(c,x.an))continue;
      let sc=0;if(gOf(c)&&gOf(c)===gOf(x.room))sc+=4;if(RA[c.id]&&RA[c.id].has(x.s))sc+=2;if(f.src==='rel')sc+=1;if(sc>bs){bs=sc;best=k;}}
    if(!best){const lim=addD(x.d,21);for(const [k,f] of free){if(f.d<=x.d||f.d>lim||taken.has(k))continue;const c=f.room;if(c.site!==x.room.site||(c.kind==='ext'&&x.room.kind!=='ext')||!anOk(c,x.an)||(x.b.roomIds.includes(c.id)&&f.d<=x.b.to)||reqFor(x.s,f.d,f.i,c.site))continue;
        let sc=0;if(gOf(c)&&gOf(c)===gOf(x.room))sc+=4;if(RA[c.id]&&RA[c.id].has(x.s))sc+=2;if(f.i===x.i)sc+=1;sc-=diffD(x.d,f.d)/7;if(sc>bs){bs=sc;best=k;}}if(best)x.alt=true;}
    if(best){const f=free.get(best);taken.set(best,x);x.to=f;x.why=(x.alt?`náhradní termín ${DOW[DI(f.d).iso].toLowerCase()} ${fmt(f.d)} ${HALF_N[f.i].toLowerCase()} · `:'')+(gOf(f.room)&&gOf(f.room)===gOf(x.room)?'stejná skupina sálů':'stejné pracoviště');if(f.src==='rel')x.why+=` · uvolněno požadavkem ${f.s}`;}});
  for(const [k,f] of free){if(f.src!=='rel'||taken.has(k)||f.d>to)continue;const room=f.room,g=gk(room),an=f.an&&anOk(room,f.an)?f.an:frAn(room),cl=claimFor(room.id,f.d,f.i);
    const cands=D.specs.filter(sp=>sp.id!==f.s&&!reqFor(sp.id,f.d,f.i,room.site)&&(RA[room.id].has(sp.id)||GA[g].has(sp.id)||cl.some(c=>c.specId===sp.id))).map(sp=>{let sc=0;const why=[],w=W[sp.id],p=Q[sp.id];
        if(cl.some(c=>c.specId===sp.id)){sc+=6;why.push('obor o sál žádá');}
