    for(const c of pool){if(c===st.best)continue;if([st.best,...st.alts].every(x=>!x||Math.abs(diffD(x.from,c.from))>=3))st.alts.push(c);if(st.alts.length>=3)break;}
    if(st.best)prev=st.best.to;
    res.push(st);
  }
  AC.set(key,res);return res;
}
function asBaseline(ids,len){const key=`base|${S.year}|${len}|${ids.join(',')}`;if(AC.has(key))return AC.get(key);let sum=0,n=0;for(const d of yearDays(S.year)){if(d<=today)continue;const c=asCand(d,ids,len);if(c){sum+=c.lost;n++;}}const v=n?sum/n:0;AC.set(key,v);return v;}
function asMark(){const ids=asTarget(),days=new Set();asPlan().forEach(w=>{if(w.status==='open'&&w.best)w.best.days.forEach(d=>days.add(d));});return {ids,rooms:new Set(ids),days,label:asLabel(ids)};}
function asPush(c,ids){D.blocks.push({id:'b'+uid(),roomIds:[...ids],type:'Asanace',from:c.from,to:c.to,note:`Navrženo plánovačem asanací (vhodnost ${c.score}/100)`});}
function tipAs(ds){const ids=asTarget(),a=asDay(ds,ids),di=DI(ds);if(!a)return '';return `<b>Asanace · ${esc(asLabel(ids))}</b><div class="t2">${cap(DOW_L[di.iso])} ${fmtY(ds)} · vhodnost ${a.score}/100</div><div class="tlines">${a.parts.map(p=>`<div><span>${p.pts>0?'+'+p.pts:p.pts<0?p.pts:'·'} ${esc(p.t)}</span></div>`).join('')}${a.full?'<div><i class="tk k-x"></i><span>Místnosti jsou už omezené</span></div>':''}</div>`;}
function asWhy(parts){const seen=new Set();return parts.filter(p=>{if(seen.has(p.t))return false;seen.add(p.t);return true;}).map(p=>`<span class="tag ${p.k==='cap'||!p.pts?'':p.pts>0?'plus':'minus'}">${p.pts&&p.k!=='cap'?(p.pts>0?'+':'')+p.pts+' · ':''}${esc(p.t)}</span>`).join('');}
const scoreBar=s=>`<div class="score"><b>${s}</b><span class="sb"><i style="width:${s}%"></i></span><small>/ 100</small></div>`;
function asRow(w){
  if(w.status==='done')return `<div class="li"><div class="when">${esc(w.label)}<small>asanace naplánována</small></div><div><div class="l1">${w.existing.map(b=>`<span class="tag ok">${fmtR(b.from,b.to)} · ${esc(b.type)}</span>`).join('')}</div></div><div class="act"><button class="btn sm" data-act="jump" data-d="${w.existing[0].from}">Na ose</button></div></div>`;
  if(w.status==='past')return `<div class="li dim"><div class="when">${esc(w.label)}<small>období uplynulo</small></div><div class="l2">Navrhují se jen budoucí dny.</div><div></div></div>`;
  if(!w.best)return `<div class="li"><div class="when">${esc(w.label)}<small>bez návrhu</small></div><div class="l2">V tomto období není vhodný pracovní den. Zkontrolujte povolené dny v týdnu.</div><div></div></div>`;
  const c=w.best,part=w.existing.length?`<span class="tag ok">Část místností už má asanaci ${w.existing.map(b=>fmtR(b.from,b.to)).join(', ')}</span>`:'';
  return `<div class="li"><div class="when">${cap(DOW_L[isoDow(c.from)])}<br>${fmtR(c.from,c.to)}<small>${esc(w.label)}</small></div>
    <div>${scoreBar(c.score)}<div class="why">${asWhy(c.parts)}${part}</div>
    ${w.alts.length?`<div class="alts">Další vhodné dny:${w.alts.map(a=>`<button class="chip sm" data-act="asAlt" data-w="${w.i}" data-d="${a.from}">${DOW[isoDow(a.from)]} ${fmt(a.from)} · <b>${a.score}</b></button>`).join('')}</div>`:''}</div>
    <div class="act"><button class="btn sm pri" data-act="asAdd" data-w="${w.i}">${ico('plus')}Přidat omezení</button><button class="btn sm" data-act="asSelDay" data-d="${c.from}">Detail</button><button class="btn sm" data-act="jump" data-d="${c.from}">Na ose</button></div></div>`;
}
function asHeat(ids,rec){let h='<div class="hm-grid">';
  for(let m=0;m<12;m++){const first=`${S.year}-${pad(m+1)}-01`,nd=new Date(Date.UTC(S.year,m+1,0)).getUTCDate();
    h+=`<div class="hm-m"><b>${cap(MONTHS[m])}</b><div class="hm-d">${['Po','Út','St','Čt','Pá','So','Ne'].map(x=>`<span class="hm-w">${x}</span>`).join('')}`;
    for(let i=1;i<isoDow(first);i++)h+='<span class="hmc x"></span>';
    for(let i=1;i<=nd;i++){const d=`${S.year}-${pad(m+1)}-${pad(i)}`,di=DI(d);let cls='hmc'+(d===today?' td':'')+(d<=today?' past':'');
      if(di.holiday){h+=`<span class="${cls} hol" title="${esc(di.holiday)}">${i}</span>`;continue;}
      if(di.weekend){h+=`<span class="${cls} we">${i}</span>`;continue;}
      const a=asDay(d,ids);if(!a){h+=`<span class="${cls}">${i}</span>`;continue;}
      if(a.full)cls+=' blk';if(rec.has(d))cls+=' rec';if(S.as.sel===d)cls+=' sel';
      h+=`<button class="${cls}" data-act="asSelDay" data-d="${d}" data-ad="${d}" style="background:color-mix(in srgb,var(--ok) ${Math.round(a.score*.85)}%,var(--sunk))" aria-label="${fmtY(d)}, vhodnost ${a.score}">${i}</button>`;}
    h+='</div></div>';}
  return h+'</div>';}
function asDetail(ids,rec){const d=S.as.sel;
  if(!d)return `<aside class="card as-det"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Vybraný den</div><p class="hint">Klepněte na den v kalendáři vhodnosti a uvidíte, jak je hodnocen.</p></aside>`;
  const di=DI(d),a=asDay(d,ids);
  if(!a)return `<aside class="card as-det"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Vybraný den</div><h2>${cap(DOW_L[di.iso])} ${fmtY(d)}</h2><p class="hint">${di.holiday?esc(di.holiday)+' – mimo provoz.':'Víkend – mimo provoz.'}</p></aside>`;
  return `<aside class="card as-det"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Vybraný den</div>
    <div><h2>${cap(DOW_L[di.iso])} ${fmtY(d)}</h2><div class="tags" style="margin-top:8px">${rec.has(d)?'<span class="tag dark">Navržený termín</span>':''}${di.school?`<span class="tag sch">${esc(di.school.name)}</span>`:''}${a.full?'<span class="tag">Místnosti už jsou omezené</span>':''}</div></div>
    <div class="score lg"><b class="big">${a.score}</b><span class="sb"><i style="width:${a.score}%"></i></span><small>/ 100</small></div>
    <div class="pts">${a.parts.map(p=>`<div class="pt ${p.pts>0?'p':p.pts<0?'m':'z'}"><b>${p.pts>0?'+'+p.pts:p.pts<0?p.pts:'·'}</b><span>${esc(p.t)}</span></div>`).join('')}</div>
    ${d<=today?'<p class="hint">Den už uplynul.</p>':a.full?'':`<button class="btn pri" data-act="asAddDay" data-d="${d}">${ico('plus')}Přidat asanaci${S.as.len>1?' na 2 dny':''}</button>`}
    <button class="btn" data-act="jump" data-d="${d}">Zobrazit na časové ose</button></aside>`;}
function asSettings(ids,lbl){const o=S.as;
  return `<section class="card"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Co plánovat</div><label class="tog"><input type="checkbox" id="as-on" data-ch="asOnCh"${S.asOn?' checked':''}><span></span><em>Vyznačit v časové ose</em></label></div>
    <div class="fld" style="margin-bottom:14px"><span class="fl">Místnosti k asanaci · ${esc(lbl)}</span>${roomPicker(ids,'asRoom')}</div>
    <div class="as-row">
      <div class="fld"><span class="fl">Četnost</span><div class="seg">${Object.entries(AS_FREQ).map(([v,[t]])=>`<button data-act="asFreq" data-v="${v}" class="${o.freq===v?'on':''}">${t}</button>`).join('')}</div></div>
      <div class="fld"><span class="fl">Délka</span><div class="seg">${[[1,'1 den'],[2,'2 dny']].map(([v,t])=>`<button data-act="asLen" data-v="${v}" class="${o.len===v?'on':''}">${t}</button>`).join('')}</div></div>
      <div class="fld"><span class="fl">Povolené dny</span><div class="seg">${[1,2,3,4,5].map(i=>`<button data-act="asDow" data-v="${i}" class="${o.dows.includes(i)?'on':''}">${DOW[i]}</button>`).join('')}</div></div>
    </div>
    <p class="hint" style="margin-top:14px">Každý pracovní den dostane hodnocení 0–100. Kapacita až 40 bodů podle toho, kolik plánované operativy by se ztratilo. Požadavek oboru +15, most mezi svátkem a víkendem +30, období mezi svátky +25, den před nebo po svátku +15, nedostatek personálu +12, školní prázdniny +8, pátek +4, ruční plán −15. V každém období se vybere nejlépe hodnocený den s odstupem od předchozí asanace.</p>
  </section>`;}
function vAsanace(){
  const ids=asTarget(),lbl=asLabel(ids);
  if(!ids.length)return ph('Plánování','Návrh asanačních dnů',bpGoBtn())+asSettings(ids,lbl)+`<section class="card" style="margin-top:16px">${empty('Vyberte skupinu nebo místnosti, pro které chcete navrhnout asanační dny.')}</section>`;
  const P=asPlan(),open=P.filter(w=>w.status==='open'&&w.best),done=P.filter(w=>w.status==='done').length;
  const avg=open.length?Math.round(open.reduce((a,w)=>a+w.best.score,0)/open.length):0;
  const lost=open.reduce((a,w)=>a+w.best.lost,0),base=asBaseline(ids,S.as.len),save=open.length&&base>0?Math.max(0,Math.round((1-(lost/open.length)/base)*100)):0;
  const hol=open.filter(w=>w.best.parts.some(p=>p.k==='hol')).length,req=open.filter(w=>w.best.parts.some(p=>p.k==='req')).length;
  const rec=new Set();open.forEach(w=>w.best.days.forEach(d=>rec.add(d)));
  if(!S.as.sel||+S.as.sel.slice(0,4)!==S.year)S.as.sel=open[0]?open[0].best.from:null;
  return ph('Plánování · '+esc(lbl),'Návrh asanačních dnů')+`
  <div class="kpis k4">
    ${kpi('Navržené termíny',open.length,'',`${P.length} ${plural(P.length,'období','období','období')} · ${done} už ${done===1?'naplánováno':'naplánováno'}`,'var(--ok)')}
    ${kpi('Průměrná vhodnost',avg,'/ 100','požadavky oborů, svátky a personál','#76AFAA')}
    ${kpi('Ztráta operativy',lost,plural(lost,'půlblok','půlbloky','půlbloků'),save?`o ${save} % méně než průměrný den`:'v navržených termínech','#86A0C2')}
    ${kpi('Navazuje na svátek',hol,'×',`${req}× využit požadavek oboru`,'#CF95A6')}
  </div>
  <div class="as-grid"><div class="stack">
    ${asSettings(ids,lbl)}
    <section class="card"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Navržené termíny ${S.year}<span class="cnt">${open.length}</span></div>${open.length>1?`<button class="btn sm pri" data-act="asAddAll">${ico('plus')}Přidat všechny navržené</button>`:''}</div>
      <div class="list">${P.map(asRow).join('')}</div></section>
    <section class="card"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Vhodnost dnů v roce ${S.year}</div><div class="hm-leg"><span>méně vhodné</span><i class="hm-scale"></i><span>nejvhodnější</span><span class="lg"><i class="l-rec"></i>navržený termín</span><span class="lg"><i class="l-x"></i>už omezeno</span></div></div>
      ${asHeat(ids,rec)}</section>
  </div>${asDetail(ids,rec)}</div>`;
}
function blSuggest(d){
  const ids=(d.roomIds||[]).filter(id=>ROOM[id]&&ROOM[id].active);if(!ids.length)return '';
  let wd=0;if(d.from&&d.to&&d.from<=d.to)for(let x=d.from;x<=d.to&&wd<3;x=addD(x,1)){const di=DI(x);if(!di.weekend&&!di.holiday)wd++;}
  const len=wd>=2?2:1,start=`${S.year}-01-01`>today?`${S.year}-01-01`:addD(today,1),end=`${S.year}-12-31`;if(start>end)return '';
  const cs=[];for(let x=start;x<=end;x=addD(x,1)){const c=asCand(x,ids,len);if(c)cs.push(c);}
  cs.sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score||a.from.localeCompare(b.from));
  const out=[];for(const c of cs){if(out.every(o=>Math.abs(diffD(o.from,c.from))>5))out.push(c);if(out.length>=6)break;}
  if(!out.length)return '';
  return `<div class="fld" style="margin-bottom:12px"><span class="fl">Nejvhodnější termíny pro vybrané místnosti (${len} ${plural(len,'den','dny','dnů')}) · podle požadavků oborů, svátků a personálu</span><div class="rp-g" style="padding:0">${out.map(c=>`<button class="chip sm sug" data-act="blSug" data-from="${c.from}" data-to="${c.to}" title="${esc(c.parts.filter(p=>p.pts>0&&p.k!=='cap').map(p=>p.t).join(' · ')||'Nízká ztráta operativy')}">${DOW[isoDow(c.from)]} ${fmtR(c.from,c.to)} <b>${c.score}</b></button>`).join('')}<button class="chip sm" data-act="asFromBl">${ico('spark')}Celý plán asanací</button></div></div>`;
}

/* =========================================================
   Obsazenost sálů – animované přehledy
   ========================================================= */
const OZ={week:'Týden',month:'Měsíc',quarter:'Kvartál',year:'Rok',custom:'Vlastní'};
let OCC_M=null,PLAY=null;
function stopPlay(){if(PLAY){clearInterval(PLAY);PLAY=null;}S.occ.playing=false;}
function occAnchor(){const a=S.occ.anchor;if(!a||+a.slice(0,4)!==S.year)S.occ.anchor=S.year===todayY?today:`${S.year}-01-04`;return S.occ.anchor;}
function occRange(){const o=S.occ,y=S.year;
  if(o.zoom==='custom'){let a=o.from||`${y}-01-01`,b=o.to||`${y}-12-31`;if(a>b)[a,b]=[b,a];if(diffD(a,b)>740)b=addD(a,740);return [a,b];}
  if(o.zoom==='year')return [`${y}-01-01`,`${y}-12-31`];
  const an=occAnchor(),a=s2d(an),m=a.getUTCMonth(),yy=a.getUTCFullYear();
  if(o.zoom==='quarter'){const q=Math.floor(m/3);return [d2s(new Date(Date.UTC(yy,q*3,1))),d2s(new Date(Date.UTC(yy,q*3+3,0)))];}
  if(o.zoom==='month')return [d2s(new Date(Date.UTC(yy,m,1))),d2s(new Date(Date.UTC(yy,m+1,0)))];
  const mon=addD(an,-(isoDow(an)-1));return [mon,addD(mon,6)];}
function occLabel(){const [a,b]=occRange(),z=S.occ.zoom;
  if(z==='year')return `Rok ${S.year}`;
  if(z==='quarter'){const q=Math.floor((+a.slice(5,7)-1)/3);return `Q${q+1} ${a.slice(0,4)} · ${MONTHS[q*3]}–${MONTHS[q*3+2]}`;}
  if(z==='month')return `${cap(MONTHS[+a.slice(5,7)-1])} ${a.slice(0,4)}`;
  if(z==='week')return `Týden ${isoWeek(a)} · ${fmtR(a,b)}`;
  return fmtR(a,b);}
function occNext(dir){const z=S.occ.zoom,an=occAnchor(),a=s2d(an);let n;
  if(z==='week')n=addD(an,7*dir);else if(z==='month')n=d2s(new Date(Date.UTC(a.getUTCFullYear(),a.getUTCMonth()+dir,1)));else if(z==='quarter')n=d2s(new Date(Date.UTC(a.getUTCFullYear(),a.getUTCMonth()+3*dir,1)));else return null;
  return +n.slice(0,4)===S.year?n:null;}
function occMove(dir){const z=S.occ.zoom;if(z==='custom')return;
  if(z==='year'){if(YEARS.includes(S.year+dir)){setYear(S.year+dir);render();}return;}
  const an=occAnchor(),a=s2d(an),n=z==='week'?addD(an,7*dir):d2s(new Date(Date.UTC(a.getUTCFullYear(),a.getUTCMonth()+(z==='quarter'?3:1)*dir,1))),y=+n.slice(0,4);
  if(!YEARS.includes(y))return;
  if(y!==S.year){setYear(y);S.occ.anchor=n;render();return;}
  S.occ.anchor=n;occRefresh();}
function occRooms(){const o=S.occ,out=[];
  for(const s of SITES){if(o.site!=='all'&&o.site!==s.id)continue;
    for(const gid of siteGroups(s.id)){const key=gid||'none:'+s.id;if(o.groups.length&&!o.groups.includes(key))continue;
      sortRooms(D.rooms.filter(r=>r.active&&r.site===s.id&&r.kind!=='pacu'&&gOf(r)===gid)).forEach(r=>out.push({room:r,gk:key,site:s.id}));}}
  return out;}
function occModel(){
  const [from,to]=occRange(),list=occRooms(),days=[];for(let d=from;d<=to;d=addD(d,1))days.push(d);
  const n=days.length,unit=n<=31?'day':n<=124?'week':'month',bk=[],bmap=new Map();
  const bkey=d=>unit==='day'?d:unit==='week'?addD(d,-(isoDow(d)-1)):d.slice(0,7);
  days.forEach(d=>{const k=bkey(d);let b=bmap.get(k);if(!b){b={k,from:d,to:d,cap:0,pl:0,hol:false,we:false};bmap.set(k,b);bk.push(b);}b.to=d;const di=DI(d);if(di.holiday&&!di.weekend)b.hol=true;if(unit==='day'&&di.weekend)b.we=true;});
  const T={cap:0,pl:0,ca:0,la:0,rel:0,blk:0,free:0,hol:0,halves:0,wd:days.filter(d=>{const di=DI(d);return !di.weekend&&!di.holiday;}).length};
  const SP={};D.specs.forEach(s=>SP[s.id]=0);const WD=[0,1,2,3,4,5].map(()=>({cap:0,pl:0}));
  const RS=list.map(({room,gk,site})=>{const x={room,gk,site,cap:0,pl:0,rel:0,blk:0,free:0,sp:{}};
    for(const d of days){const di=DI(d),r=R(room,d),H=hoursFor(room,d),hh=[halfMin(H,0)/60,halfMin(H,1)/60],working=!di.weekend&&!di.holiday,planned=r.h[0]||r.h[1],b=bmap.get(bkey(d));
      if(!working){if(di.holiday&&!di.weekend&&r.holLost)T.hol+=r.holLost>=2?hh[0]+hh[1]:hh[0];if(!(r.src==='override'&&planned))continue;}
      if(r.block&&!(r.src==='override'&&planned)){const c=hh[0]+hh[1];x.cap+=c;x.blk+=c;b.cap+=c;if(di.iso<=5)WD[di.iso].cap+=c;continue;}
      for(let i=0;i<2;i++){const h=r.h[i],v=hh[i];
        if(working||h){x.cap+=v;b.cap+=v;if(di.iso<=5)WD[di.iso].cap+=v;}
        if(!h){if(working)x.free+=v;continue;}
        if(h.released){x.rel+=v;continue;}
        x.pl+=v;b.pl+=v;if(di.iso<=5)WD[di.iso].pl+=v;x.sp[h.s]=(x.sp[h.s]||0)+v;SP[h.s]=(SP[h.s]||0)+v;if(h.an==='CA')T.ca+=v;else T.la+=v;T.halves++;}}
    T.cap+=x.cap;T.pl+=x.pl;T.rel+=x.rel;T.blk+=x.blk;T.free+=x.free;return x;});
  return {from,to,days:n,unit,bk,T,SP,WD,RS};
}
const pctOf=(a,b)=>b?a/b*100:0;
/* ---------- Obsazenost: skupiny, denní mapa, sál × období, tabulka ---------- */
function occExt(M){if(M._x)return M._x;const bi=new Map();M.bk.forEach((b,i)=>{bi.set(b.k,i);});const unit=M.unit,bkey=d=>unit==='day'?d:unit==='week'?addD(d,-(isoDow(d)-1)):d.slice(0,7);
  const days=[];for(let d=M.from;d<=M.to;d=addD(d,1))days.push(d);const DY=new Map(days.map(d=>[d,{cap:0,pl:0,blk:0}])),G=new Map();
  M.RS.forEach(x=>{x.bb=M.bk.map(()=>({cap:0,pl:0,blk:0,rel:0,sp:{}}));x.ca=0;x.la=0;
    for(const d of days){const di=DI(d),r=R(x.room,d),H=hoursFor(x.room,d),hh=[halfMin(H,0)/60,halfMin(H,1)/60],working=!di.weekend&&!di.holiday,planned=r.h[0]||r.h[1],b=x.bb[bi.get(bkey(d))],dy=DY.get(d);
      if(!working&&!(r.src==='override'&&planned))continue;
      if(r.block&&!(r.src==='override'&&planned)){const c=hh[0]+hh[1];b.cap+=c;b.blk+=c;dy.cap+=c;dy.blk+=c;continue;}
      for(let i=0;i<2;i++){const h=r.h[i],v=hh[i];if(working||h){b.cap+=v;dy.cap+=v;}if(!h)continue;if(h.released){b.rel+=v;continue;}b.pl+=v;dy.pl+=v;b.sp[h.s]=(b.sp[h.s]||0)+v;if(h.an==='CA')x.ca+=v;else x.la+=v;}}
    let g=G.get(x.gk);if(!g){g={gk:x.gk,site:x.site,name:x.gk.startsWith('none:')?'Bez skupiny':gName(x.gk),cap:0,pl:0,rel:0,blk:0,n:0,sp:{}};G.set(x.gk,g);}g.cap+=x.cap;g.pl+=x.pl;g.rel+=x.rel;g.blk+=x.blk;g.n++;Object.entries(x.sp).forEach(([k,v])=>g.sp[k]=(g.sp[k]||0)+v);});
  M._x={days,DY,G:[...G.values()]};return M._x;}
const occCol=u=>`color-mix(in srgb,var(--dot) ${Math.round(10+Math.min(1,u)*82)}%,var(--sunk))`;
const occTxt=u=>u>.62?'#06122A':'var(--ink)';
function occTop(sp,tot,n=3){return Object.entries(sp).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]).slice(0,n).map(([k,v])=>({k,v,p:tot?v/tot*100:0,c:(SPEC[k]||{}).c||'#999',name:specName(k)}));}
function occGroupsHTML(M){const X=occExt(M);if(!X.G.length)return '';const C=2*Math.PI*36;
  return `<section class="card" id="occ-groups"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Skupiny sálů<span class="cnt">${X.G.length}</span></div><span class="hint">využití plánovatelné kapacity skupiny</span></div><div class="og">${X.G.map(g=>{const u=g.cap?g.pl/g.cap:0,top=occTop(g.sp,g.pl,4);
    return `<div class="og-c"><div class="og-r"><svg viewBox="0 0 84 84" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="42" cy="42" r="36" class="og-bg"/><circle cx="42" cy="42" r="36" class="og-fg" style="stroke-dasharray:${(Math.min(1,u)*C).toFixed(1)} ${C.toFixed(1)}"/></svg><b>${fmtN(u*100)}<small>%</small></b></div>
      <div class="og-i"><span>${esc(SITE[g.site]?SITE[g.site].name:'')}</span><b>${esc(g.name)}</b><em>${fmtN(g.pl)} z ${fmtN(g.cap)} h · ${g.n} ${plural(g.n,'sál','sály','sálů')}</em>
      <div class="og-s">${top.map(t=>`<i style="width:${t.p.toFixed(1)}%;background:${t.c}" title="${esc(t.name)} ${fmtN(t.v)} h"></i>`).join('')}</div><div class="og-l">${top.slice(0,3).map(t=>`<span><i style="background:${t.c}"></i>${esc(t.k)} ${fmtN(t.p)} %</span>`).join('')}</div>${g.rel||g.blk?`<small>${g.rel?`uvolněno ${fmtN(g.rel)} h`:''}${g.rel&&g.blk?' · ':''}${g.blk?`omezení ${fmtN(g.blk)} h`:''}</small>`:''}</div></div>`;}).join('')}</div></section>`;}
function occCalHTML(M){const X=occExt(M),start=addD(M.from,-(isoDow(M.from)-1)),wk=[];for(let d=start;d<=M.to;d=addD(d,7))wk.push(d);
  let h=`<div class="oc" style="--w:${wk.length};--cs:${wk.length<=6?40:wk.length<=14?26:16}px"><span></span>${wk.map((w,i)=>{const m=addD(w,3),lab=i===0||m.slice(5,7)!==addD(wk[i-1],3).slice(5,7)?MON_SH[+m.slice(5,7)-1]:'';return `<span class="oc-m">${lab}</span>`;}).join('')}`;
  for(let i=1;i<=5;i++){h+=`<span class="oc-d">${DOW[i]}</span>`;wk.forEach(w=>{const d=addD(w,i-1);if(d<M.from||d>M.to){h+='<i class="oc-c na"></i>';return;}const di=DI(d),v=X.DY.get(d);
      if(di.holiday){h+=`<i class="oc-c hol" title="${fmtY(d)} · ${esc(di.holiday)}"></i>`;return;}if(!v||!v.cap){h+=`<i class="oc-c z" title="${fmtY(d)} · bez kapacity"></i>`;return;}const u=v.pl/v.cap;
      h+=`<i class="oc-c${d===today?' td':''}${di.school?' sch':''}" style="background:${occCol(u)}" title="${DOW[i]} ${fmtY(d)} · využití ${fmtN(u*100)} % · ${fmtN(v.pl,1)} z ${fmtN(v.cap,1)} h${v.blk?` · omezení ${fmtN(v.blk,1)} h`:''}${di.school?' · '+esc(di.school.name):''}"></i>`;});}
  return `<section class="card" id="occ-cal"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Denní využití</div><span class="hint">každý čtvereček = pracovní den</span></div><div class="ovx">${h}</div></div>
    <div class="oc-lg"><span>méně</span>${[.1,.35,.6,.8,1].map(u=>`<i style="background:${occCol(u)}"></i>`).join('')}<span>více</span><em><i class="hol"></i>svátek</em><em><i class="sch"></i>prázdniny</em></div></section>`;}
function occHeatHTML(M){const X=occExt(M),n=M.bk.length,big=n<=18,blab=b=>M.unit==='day'?`${+b.from.slice(8)}.`:M.unit==='week'?`${isoWeek(b.from)}.`:MON_SH[+b.from.slice(5,7)-1];
  const tot=M.bk.map((b,i)=>M.RS.reduce((a,x)=>{a.cap+=x.bb[i].cap;a.pl+=x.bb[i].pl;return a;},{cap:0,pl:0}));let lastG='';
  let h=`<div class="oh" style="--n:${n}"><div class="oh-h l">${M.unit==='day'?'Den':M.unit==='week'?'Týden':'Měsíc'}</div>${M.bk.map(b=>{const di=M.unit==='day'?DI(b.from):null;return `<div class="oh-h${di&&(di.weekend||di.holiday)?' we':''}${today>=b.from&&today<=b.to?' td':''}">${blab(b)}${di?`<small>${DOW[di.iso]}</small>`:''}</div>`;}).join('')}<div class="oh-h r">Celkem</div>`;
  M.RS.forEach(x=>{if(x.gk!==lastG){lastG=x.gk;h+=`<div class="oh-g">${esc(SITE[x.site].name)} · ${x.gk.startsWith('none:')?'Bez skupiny':esc(gName(x.gk))}</div>`;}const U=x.cap?x.pl/x.cap:0;
    h+=`<div class="oh-l"><i class="rdot" style="--c:${roomColor(x.room)}"></i><b>${esc(x.room.code)}</b><span>${esc(x.room.name)}</span></div>`+x.bb.map((c,i)=>{const b=M.bk[i];if(!c.cap)return `<div class="oh-c z" title="${esc(x.room.code)} · bez kapacity"></div>`;if(c.blk>=c.cap)return `<div class="oh-c blk" title="${esc(x.room.code)} · omezení sálu"></div>`;
      const u=c.pl/c.cap,top=occTop(c.sp,c.pl,1)[0];return `<div class="oh-c${c.blk?' pb':''}${c.rel?' pr':''}" style="background:${occCol(u)};color:${occTxt(u)}${top&&big?`;--sc:${top.c}`:''}" title="${esc(x.room.code)} · ${M.unit==='day'?fmtY(b.from):fmtR(b.from,b.to)} · využití ${fmtN(u*100)} %${top?` · ${esc(top.name)}`:''}${c.rel?` · uvolněno ${fmtN(c.rel,1)} h`:''}${c.blk?` · omezení ${fmtN(c.blk,1)} h`:''}">${big?`${fmtN(u*100)}`:''}${top&&big?'<i></i>':''}</div>`;}).join('')+`<div class="oh-t"><u><i style="width:${Math.min(100,U*100).toFixed(1)}%"></i></u><b>${fmtN(U*100)} %</b></div>`;});
  const TU=M.T.cap?M.T.pl/M.T.cap:0;
  h+=`<div class="oh-l tot"><b>Celkem</b></div>${tot.map(t=>{const u=t.cap?t.pl/t.cap:0;return t.cap?`<div class="oh-c tot" style="background:${occCol(u)};color:${occTxt(u)}">${fmtN(u*100)}</div>`:'<div class="oh-c z"></div>';}).join('')}<div class="oh-t"><u><i style="width:${Math.min(100,TU*100).toFixed(1)}%"></i></u><b>${fmtN(TU*100)} %</b></div></div>`;
  return `<section class="card" id="occ-heat"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Mapa obsazení · sál × ${M.unit==='day'?'den':M.unit==='week'?'týden':'měsíc'}</div><span class="hint">sytost = využití kapacity${big?' · barevná tečka = hlavní obor':''} · šrafa = omezení sálu</span></div><div class="ovx">${h}</div></section>`;}
function occTableHTML(M){const X=occExt(M),srt=S.occ.sort||'util',rows=M.RS.slice();
  rows.sort(srt==='util'?(a,b)=>(b.cap?b.pl/b.cap:0)-(a.cap?a.pl/a.cap:0):srt==='free'?(a,b)=>(b.free+b.rel)-(a.free+a.rel):(a,b)=>a.room.code.localeCompare(b.room.code,'cs',{numeric:true}));
  const f=v=>fmtN(v);
  return `<section class="card" id="occ-table"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Tabulka sálů</div><div class="seg">${[['util','Podle využití'],['free','Podle volna'],['name','Podle kódu']].map(([v,t])=>`<button data-act="occSort" data-v="${v}" class="${srt===v?'on':''}">${t}</button>`).join('')}</div></div>
  <div class="ovx"><table class="tb ot"><thead><tr><th>Sál</th><th class="r">Kapacita</th><th class="r">Plánováno</th><th>Využití</th><th>CA / LA</th><th class="r">Uvolněno</th><th class="r">Omezení</th><th class="r">Volno</th><th>Hlavní obory</th><th>Průběh</th></tr></thead><tbody>
  ${rows.map((x,i)=>{const u=x.cap?x.pl/x.cap:0,ca=x.ca+x.la?x.ca/(x.ca+x.la)*100:0,top=occTop(x.sp,x.pl,3),mx=1;
    return `<tr><th><span class="ot-n">${srt!=='name'?`<em>${i+1}</em>`:''}<i class="rdot" style="--c:${roomColor(x.room)}"></i><b>${esc(x.room.code)}</b><span>${esc(x.room.name)}<small>${esc(SITE[x.site].name)} · ${x.gk.startsWith('none:')?'bez skupiny':esc(gName(x.gk))}</small></span></span></th>
      <td class="r">${f(x.cap)} h</td><td class="r"><b>${f(x.pl)} h</b></td>
      <td><div class="ot-u ${u>=.85?'hi':u>=.6?'mid':'lo'}"><u><i style="width:${Math.min(100,u*100).toFixed(1)}%"></i></u><b>${fmtN(u*100)} %</b></div></td>
      <td>${x.ca+x.la?`<div class="ot-cl"><u><i class="ca" style="width:${ca.toFixed(1)}%"></i><i class="la" style="width:${(100-ca).toFixed(1)}%"></i></u><small>${fmtN(ca)} / ${fmtN(100-ca)} %</small></div>`:'<span class="mut">—</span>'}</td>
      <td class="r${x.rel?' ot-rel':''}">${x.rel?f(x.rel)+' h':'—'}</td><td class="r${x.blk?' ot-blk':''}">${x.blk?f(x.blk)+' h':'—'}</td><td class="r">${x.free?f(x.free)+' h':'—'}</td>
      <td><div class="ot-sp">${top.length?top.map(t=>`<span style="--c:${t.c}" title="${esc(t.name)} · ${fmtN(t.v)} h"><i></i>${esc(t.k)}<small>${fmtN(t.p)} %</small></span>`).join(''):'<span class="mut">—</span>'}</div></td>
      <td><div class="ot-sl" title="Využití po ${M.unit==='day'?'dnech':M.unit==='week'?'týdnech':'měsících'}">${x.bb.map(c=>{const v=c.cap?c.pl/c.cap:0;return `<i class="${!c.cap?'nx':c.blk>=c.cap?'nb':''}" style="height:${c.cap?Math.max(8,Math.min(1,v)*100).toFixed(0):8}%"></i>`;}).join('')}</div></td></tr>`;}).join('')}
  </tbody></table></div></section>`;}

function occKpi(key,label,val,dec,unit,sub,color){return `<div class="kpi"${color?` style="--kc:${color}"`:''}><div class="lab"><i></i>${label}</div><div class="num"><span data-o="${key}" data-num="${val}" data-dec="${dec}">${fmtN(val,dec)}</span>${unit?`<small>${unit}</small>`:''}</div><div class="sub" data-o="${key}s" data-txt>${sub}</div></div>`;}
function vOcc(){
  const M=occModel(),o=S.occ,T=M.T,util=pctOf(T.pl,T.cap);OCC_M=M;
  const scope=o.site==='all'?SITES:SITES.filter(s=>s.id===o.site);
  const gks=[];scope.forEach(s=>siteGroups(s.id).forEach(gid=>{if(D.rooms.some(r=>r.active&&r.kind!=='pacu'&&r.site===s.id&&gOf(r)===gid))gks.push([gid||'none:'+s.id,gid?gName(gid):'Bez skupiny',s.name]);}));
  const [ra,rb]=occRange(),prog=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,pctOf(diffD(`${S.year}-01-01`,ra),diffD(`${S.year}-01-01`,`${S.year}-12-31`))));
  const ctl=`<div class="card filters" id="occ-ctl">
    <div class="frow">
      <div class="seg dark" role="group" aria-label="Období">${Object.entries(OZ).map(([z,t])=>`<button data-act="occZoom" data-v="${z}" class="${o.zoom===z?'on':''}">${t}</button>`).join('')}</div>
      ${o.zoom==='custom'?`<div class="dr"><label class="flab" for="occ-from">Od</label>${dateField('occ-from',ra,'data-occ="from" data-role="from" data-pair="occ-to"')}<label class="flab" for="occ-to">Do</label>${dateField('occ-to',rb,'data-occ="to" data-role="to" data-pair="occ-from"')}</div>`
        :`<div class="period"><button class="icon-btn sm" data-act="occNav" data-v="-1" aria-label="Předchozí období">${ico('cl')}</button><b>${occLabel()}</b><button class="icon-btn sm" data-act="occNav" data-v="1" aria-label="Další období">${ico('cr')}</button>${o.zoom!=='year'&&S.year===todayY?'<button class="btn sm ghost" data-act="occToday">Dnes</button>':''}</div>`}
      ${o.zoom!=='custom'&&o.zoom!=='year'?`<button class="btn sm play${o.playing?' on':''}" data-act="occPlay">${ico(o.playing?'pause':'play')}${o.playing?'Zastavit':'Přehrát rok'}</button>`:''}
      <span class="sp"></span>
      <span class="flab">Pracoviště</span><div class="seg">${[['all','Vše'],...SITES.map(s=>[s.id,s.name])].map(([v,t])=>`<button data-act="occSite" data-v="${v}" class="${o.site===v?'on':''}">${t}</button>`).join('')}</div>
    </div>
    <div class="frow"><span class="flab">Skupiny</span><button class="chip${o.groups.length?'':' on'}" data-act="occGrpAll">Všechny</button>${gks.map(([k,n,sn])=>`<button class="chip${o.groups.includes(k)?' on':''}" data-act="occGrp" data-v="${esc(k)}">${esc(n)}${o.site==='all'?` <small style="opacity:.85">${sn}</small>`:''}</button>`).join('')}</div>
  </div>`;
  const kp=`<div class="kpis k5" id="occ-kpi">
    ${occKpi('k1','Využití kapacity',util,0,'%',`${fmtN(T.pl)} z ${fmtN(T.cap)} sálových hodin`,'var(--dot)')}
    ${occKpi('k2','Plánované hodiny',T.pl,0,'h',`CA ${fmtN(T.ca)} h · LA ${fmtN(T.la)} h`,'#86A0C2')}
    ${occKpi('k3','Sálodny',T.halves/2,1,'',`${M.RS.length} ${plural(M.RS.length,'sál','sály','sálů')} · ${T.wd} ${plural(T.wd,'pracovní den','pracovní dny','pracovních dnů')}`,'#76AFAA')}
    ${occKpi('k4','Uvolněno požadavky',T.rel,0,'h',`${fmtN(pctOf(T.rel,T.cap),1)} % kapacity`,'#D6A06E')}
    ${occKpi('k5','Omezení sálů',T.blk,0,'h',`svátky ≈ ${fmtN(T.hol)} h mimo kapacitu`,'var(--ink-3)')}
  </div>`;
  const C=2*Math.PI*78,segs=[['ca','Plánováno · CA','var(--dot)',T.ca],['la','Plánováno · LA','color-mix(in srgb,var(--dot) 42%,var(--raised))',T.la],['rel','Uvolněno požadavky','#D6A06E',T.rel],['blk','Omezení sálů','var(--ink-3)',T.blk],['free','Volno ve šabloně','var(--line-2)',T.free]];
  const tot=segs.reduce((a,x)=>a+x[3],0);let acc=0;
  const circ=segs.map(([k,,c,v])=>{const len=tot?v/tot*C:0,el=`<circle class="dseg" data-o="d${k}" cx="100" cy="100" r="78" style="stroke:${c};stroke-dasharray:${len.toFixed(2)} ${C.toFixed(2)};stroke-dashoffset:${(-acc).toFixed(2)}"></circle>`;acc+=len;return el;}).join('');
  const donut=`<section class="card" id="occ-donut"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Využití kapacity</div></div><div class="donut">
    <div class="dn-w"><svg viewBox="0 0 200 200" aria-hidden="true"><circle class="trk" cx="100" cy="100" r="78"></circle><g transform="rotate(-90 100 100)">${circ}</g></svg>
      <div class="dn-c"><b><span data-o="dutil" data-num="${util}" data-dec="0">${fmtN(util)}</span><small>%</small></b><span>plánovaná operativa</span></div></div>
    <div class="dl-list">${segs.map(([k,t,c,v])=>`<div class="dl"><i style="background:${c}"></i><span>${t}</span><b><span data-o="dl${k}" data-num="${v}" data-dec="0">${fmtN(v)}</span> h</b><em data-o="dp${k}" data-txt>${fmtN(pctOf(v,tot))} %</em></div>`).join('')}</div>
  </div></section>`;
  const nb=M.bk.length,step=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(nb/(M.unit==='day'?16:M.unit==='week'?13:12)));
  const blab=b=>M.unit==='day'?`${+b.from.slice(8)}.`:M.unit==='week'?fmt(b.from):MON_SH[+b.from.slice(5,7)-1];
  let grid='';for(let v=0;v<=100;v+=25)grid+=`<div class="gl" style="bottom:${v}%"><span>${v} %</span></div>`;
  const trend=`<section class="card" id="occ-trend"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Využití po ${M.unit==='day'?'dnech':M.unit==='week'?'týdnech':'měsících'}</div><span class="hint">sloupec = plánováno z kapacity · přerušovaná čára = průměr</span></div>
    <div class="trd" style="--g:${nb>40?'2px':nb>20?'3px':'6px'}"><div class="trd-plot">${grid}
      <div class="trd-cols">${M.bk.map((b,i)=>`<div class="tr-c${b.hol?' hol':''}${today>=b.from&&today<=b.to?' td':''}" data-o="tc${i}" data-cls data-oc="${i}" style="--i:${i}"><i class="tr-cap" data-o="tk${i}" style="height:${b.cap?100:0}%"></i><i class="tr-v" data-o="tv${i}" style="height:${pctOf(b.pl,b.cap).toFixed(1)}%"></i></div>`).join('')}</div>
      <div class="tr-avg" data-o="tavg" style="bottom:${util.toFixed(1)}%"><span data-o="tavgt" data-txt>Ø ${fmtN(util)} %</span></div></div>
      <div class="trd-x">${M.bk.map((b,i)=>`<span data-o="tx${i}" data-txt>${i%step===0?blab(b):''}</span>`).join('')}</div></div></section>`;
  let lastG='';
  const rooms=`<section class="card" id="occ-rooms"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Obsazení sálů<span class="cnt">${M.RS.length}</span></div><span class="hint">barvy = obory · šrafy = uvolněno / omezení · klepnutím na obor vpravo ho zvýrazníte</span></div>
    ${M.RS.length?M.RS.map((x,i)=>{const id=x.room.id,cp=x.cap||1,u=pctOf(x.pl,x.cap);let g='';if(x.gk!==lastG){lastG=x.gk;g=`<div class="rb-g">${SITE[x.site].name} · ${x.gk.startsWith('none:')?'Bez skupiny':esc(gName(x.gk))}</div>`;}
      return g+`<div class="rb" data-orr="${id}" style="--i:${i}"><div class="rb-l"><i class="rdot" style="--c:${roomColor(x.room)}"></i><b>${esc(x.room.name)}</b><code>${esc(x.room.code)}</code></div><div class="rb-bar">${D.specs.map(sp=>`<i data-o="r${id}${sp.id}" style="width:${pctOf(x.sp[sp.id]||0,cp).toFixed(2)}%;background:${sp.c};opacity:${o.focus&&o.focus!==sp.id?.18:1}"></i>`).join('')}<i class="rel" data-o="r${id}rel" style="width:${pctOf(x.rel,cp).toFixed(2)}%"></i><i class="blk" data-o="r${id}blk" style="width:${pctOf(x.blk,cp).toFixed(2)}%"></i></div><div class="rb-p"><span data-o="r${id}p" data-num="${u}" data-dec="0">${fmtN(u)}</span> %</div></div>`;}).join(''):empty('Ve výběru nejsou žádné sály.')}
  </section>`;
  const mxs=Math.max(1,...D.specs.map(sp=>M.SP[sp.id]||0));
  const spec=`<section class="card" id="occ-spec"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Podíl oborů</div>${o.focus?`<button class="btn sm ghost" data-act="occFocus" data-v="${o.focus}">Zrušit zvýraznění</button>`:''}</div>
    ${D.specs.map(sp=>{const v=M.SP[sp.id]||0;return `<div class="sbr${o.focus===sp.id?' on':''}" data-o="sr${sp.id}" data-cls data-act="occFocus" data-v="${sp.id}" role="button" tabindex="0"><span class="sbr-l"><i style="background:${sp.c}"></i>${esc(sp.name)}</span><span class="sbr-t"><i data-o="sb${sp.id}" style="width:${pctOf(v,mxs).toFixed(1)}%;background:${sp.c};opacity:${v?1:.3}"></i></span><span class="sbr-v"><span data-o="sv${sp.id}" data-num="${v}" data-dec="0">${fmtN(v)}</span> h · <span data-o="sp${sp.id}" data-txt>${fmtN(pctOf(v,T.pl))} %</span></span></div>`;}).join('')}
  </section>`;
  const wd=`<section class="card" id="occ-wd"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Využití podle dne v týdnu</div></div><div class="wd">${[1,2,3,4,5].map(i=>{const w=M.WD[i],u=pctOf(w.pl,w.cap);return `<div class="wd-c"><b><span data-o="wv${i}" data-num="${u}" data-dec="0">${fmtN(u)}</span> %</b><div class="wd-t"><i data-o="wb${i}" style="height:${u.toFixed(1)}%"></i></div><span>${DOW_L[i]}</span></div>`;}).join('')}</div></section>`;
  const pg=`<div id="occ-prog"><div class="playbar" data-o="pbw" style="opacity:${o.playing?1:0}"><i data-o="pb" style="width:${prog.toFixed(1)}%"></i></div></div>`;
  return ph('Přehledy','Obsazenost sálů')+`<div id="occ-root" class="stack">${ctl}${pg}${kp}<div class="occ-top" style="margin-top:0">${donut}${trend}</div><div class="two">${rooms}<div class="stack">${spec}${wd}</div></div><div class="occ-mid">${occGroupsHTML(M)}${occCalHTML(M)}</div>${occHeatHTML(M)}${occTableHTML(M)}</div>`;
}
function tween(el,a,b,dec){if(RM()||a===b||!isFinite(a)){el.textContent=fmtN(b,dec);return;}cancelAnimationFrame(el._raf);const t0=performance.now(),dur=650;
  const step=t=>{const q=Math.min(1,(t-t0)/dur),e=1-Math.pow(1-q,3);el.textContent=fmtN(a+(b-a)*e,dec);if(q<1)el._raf=requestAnimationFrame(step);};el._raf=requestAnimationFrame(step);}
function morph(oldEl,newEl){
  const oo=[...oldEl.querySelectorAll('[data-o]')],nn=[...newEl.querySelectorAll('[data-o]')];
  if(oo.length!==nn.length||oo.some((x,i)=>x.dataset.o!==nn[i].dataset.o))return false;
  nn.forEach((n,i)=>{const x=oo[i],st=n.getAttribute('style')||'';if((x.getAttribute('style')||'')!==st)x.setAttribute('style',st);
    if(n.hasAttribute('data-cls')&&x.className!==n.className)x.className=n.className;
    if(n.hasAttribute('data-num')){const a=parseFloat(x.dataset.num)||0,b=parseFloat(n.dataset.num)||0;x.dataset.num=n.dataset.num;if(a!==b)tween(x,a,b,+n.dataset.dec||0);}
    else if(n.hasAttribute('data-txt')&&x.innerHTML!==n.innerHTML)x.innerHTML=n.innerHTML;});
  return true;}
function occRefresh(){
  if(S.view!=='occ'){render();return;}
  const root=$('#occ-root');if(!root){render();return;}
  if(!$('#dp').hidden)closeDP();
  const tpl=document.createElement('div');tpl.innerHTML=vOcc();const nr=tpl.querySelector('#occ-root');
  const oc=root.querySelector('#occ-ctl'),nc=nr.querySelector('#occ-ctl');if(oc&&nc)oc.replaceWith(nc);
  const op=$('#view .print-h'),np=tpl.querySelector('.print-h');if(op&&np)op.replaceWith(np);
  ['occ-groups','occ-cal','occ-heat','occ-table'].forEach(id=>{const a=root.querySelector('#'+id),b=nr.querySelector('#'+id);if(a&&b)a.replaceWith(b);});
  ['occ-prog','occ-kpi','occ-donut','occ-trend','occ-rooms','occ-spec','occ-wd'].forEach(id=>{const a=root.querySelector('#'+id),b=nr.querySelector('#'+id);if(!a||!b)return;
    if(!morph(a,b)){if(!RM())b.classList.add('occ-anim');a.replaceWith(b);setTimeout(()=>b.classList.remove('occ-anim'),1400);}});
  savePrefs();}
function tipOcc(i){const M=OCC_M;if(!M||!M.bk[i])return '';const b=M.bk[i],u=Math.round(pctOf(b.pl,b.cap));
  const t=M.unit==='day'?`${cap(DOW_L[isoDow(b.from)])} ${fmtY(b.from)}`:M.unit==='week'?`Týden ${isoWeek(b.from)} · ${fmtR(b.from,b.to)}`:`${cap(MONTHS[+b.from.slice(5,7)-1])} ${b.from.slice(0,4)}`;
  return `<b>${t}</b><div class="tlines"><div><span>Využití <b>${u} %</b></span></div><div><span>Plánováno ${fmtN(b.pl,1)} h z ${fmtN(b.cap,1)} h</span></div>${b.hol?'<div><i class="tk k-hol"></i><span>Obsahuje státní svátek</span></div>':''}${b.we&&!b.cap?'<div><span>Víkend – mimo provoz</span></div>':''}</div>`;}
function tipOccRoom(id){const x=OCC_M&&OCC_M.RS.find(r=>r.room.id===id);if(!x)return '';const u=Math.round(pctOf(x.pl,x.cap)),sp=Object.entries(x.sp).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]);
  return `<b>${esc(x.room.name)}</b> <span class="t2">${esc(x.room.code)} · ${SITE[x.site].name}</span><div class="t2">Využití ${u} % · ${fmtN(x.pl,1)} z ${fmtN(x.cap,1)} h</div><div class="tlines">${sp.map(([s,h])=>`<div><i class="tsw" style="--c:${(SPEC[s]||{}).c||'#999'}"></i><span>${esc(specName(s))} · ${fmtN(h,1)} h</span></div>`).join('')}${x.rel?`<div><span>Uvolněno požadavky · ${fmtN(x.rel,1)} h</span></div>`:''}${x.blk?`<div><i class="tk k-x"></i><span>Omezení · ${fmtN(x.blk,1)} h</span></div>`:''}${x.free?`<div><span>Volno ve šabloně · ${fmtN(x.free,1)} h</span></div>`:''}</div>`;}

/* =========================================================
   Personál – volno a nedostatek po týdnech
   ========================================================= */
const ROLES=[['doc','capDoc','Anesteziologové','#76AFAA'],['nur','capNur','Anest. sestry','#A496CB'],['scr','capScr','Sálové sestry','#CF95A6']];
function staffWeek(site,mon){const out={};ROLES.forEach(([k])=>out[k]={free:Infinity,def:0,days:0});
  for(let i=0;i<5;i++){const d=addD(mon,i),di=DI(d);if(di.holiday)continue;const v=SD(site,d);if(!v.doc&&!v.nur&&!v.scr)continue;
    ROLES.forEach(([k,ck])=>{const s=v[ck]-v[k],o=out[k];o.days++;o.free=Math.min(o.free,s);if(s<0)o.def=Math.max(o.def,-s);});}
  ROLES.forEach(([k])=>{if(!isFinite(out[k].free))out[k].free=0;});return out;}
function staffFree(sites,mon){const res={};ROLES.forEach(([k])=>res[k]={free:0,def:0,defSites:[],tip:[],days:0});
  sites.forEach(s=>{const w=staffWeek(s,mon);ROLES.forEach(([k])=>{const o=w[k],r=res[k];if(!o.days)return;r.days=Math.max(r.days,o.days);r.free+=Math.max(0,o.free);if(o.def){r.def+=o.def;r.defSites.push(SITE[s].name);}r.tip.push(`${SITE[s].name}: ${o.def?'chybí '+o.def:'volno '+Math.max(0,o.free)}`);});});
  return res;}
function leaveCard(){
  const sites=scopeSites(S.staffSite),y=S.year,jan=`${y}-01-01`;let mon=addD(jan,-(isoDow(jan)-1));
  if(y===todayY){const tm=addD(today,-(isoDow(today)-1));if(tm>mon)mon=tm;}
  const weeks=[];for(let m=mon;m<=`${y}-12-31`&&(S.staffWeeks==='all'||weeks.length<12);m=addD(m,7))weeks.push(m);
  if(!weeks.length)return '';
  const W=weeks.map(m=>({m,r:staffFree(sites,m)})),first=W[0].r,start=y===todayY?today:jan;
  const near={};ROLES.forEach(([k,ck])=>{for(const d of yearDays(y)){if(d<start)continue;if(sites.some(s=>{const v=SD(s,d);return v[k]>v[ck];})){near[k]=d;break;}}});
  const isCur=y===todayY&&W[0].m<=today,wl=isCur?'Tento týden':`Týden ${isoWeek(W[0].m)}`;
  const runs=ISSUES().filter(i=>i.k==='staff'&&sites.includes(i.site)&&i.to>=start).slice(0,5);
  const roleTxt=i=>{const p=[];if(i.dd)p.push(`anesteziologové ${i.md} z ${i.cd}`);if(i.nd)p.push(`anest. sestry ${i.mn} z ${i.cn}`);if(i.xd)p.push(`sálové sestry ${i.ms} z ${i.cs}`);return p.join(' · ');};
  return `<section class="card" style="margin-bottom:16px"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Nedostatek a možné volno</div><div class="seg">${[['12','12 týdnů'],['all','Celý rok']].map(([v,t])=>`<button data-act="staffWeeks" data-v="${v}" class="${S.staffWeeks===v?'on':''}">${t}</button>`).join('')}</div></div>
    <div class="lv">${ROLES.map(([k,,lab,c])=>{const o=first[k];return o.def?`<div class="lv-t bad" style="--kc:${c}"><span class="l"><i></i>${lab} · ${wl.toLowerCase()}</span><span class="n">chybí ${o.def}<small>${plural(o.def,'osoba','osoby','osob')}</small></span><span class="s">${esc(o.defSites.join(', '))} · navyšte kapacitu nebo omezte program</span></div>`
      :`<div class="lv-t" style="--kc:${c}"><span class="l"><i></i>${lab} · ${wl.toLowerCase()}</span><span class="n">${o.free}<small>může čerpat volno</small></span><span class="s">${near[k]?`nejbližší možný nedostatek ${DOW[isoDow(near[k])].toLowerCase()} ${fmtY(near[k])}`:'bez nedostatku do konce roku'}</span></div>`;}).join('')}</div>
    ${runs.map(i=>`<div class="note bad" style="margin-bottom:8px">${ico('warn')}<div><b>Možný nedostatek · ${SITE[i.site].name} · ${fmtR(i.from,i.to)}</b> (${i.n} ${plural(i.n,'den','dny','dnů')}) – ${roleTxt(i)}. <button class="linkbtn" data-act="jump" data-d="${i.from}" data-z="month" data-site="${i.site}"><span>Zobrazit na ose</span></button> <button class="linkbtn" data-act="fixOpen" data-site="${i.site}" data-from="${i.from}" data-to="${i.to}"><span>Navrhnout řešení</span></button></div></div>`).join('')}
    <div class="ovx"><table class="tb"><thead><tr><th>Týden</th><th>Termín</th>${ROLES.map(([,,lab])=>`<th>${lab}</th>`).join('')}<th></th></tr></thead><tbody>
    ${W.map(({m,r},idx)=>{const f=addD(m,4),wk=[0,1,2,3,4].map(i=>DI(addD(m,i))),sch=wk.map(x=>x.school).find(Boolean),hol=wk.filter(x=>x.holiday).length;
      return `<tr class="${idx===0&&isCur?'cur':''}"><th>${isoWeek(m)}</th><td>${fmtR(m,f)}${sch?` <span class="tag sch">${esc(sch.short)}</span>`:''}${hol?` <span class="tag hol">${hol} ${plural(hol,'svátek','svátky','svátků')}</span>`:''}</td>
        ${ROLES.map(([k])=>{const o=r[k];return `<td title="${esc(o.tip.join(' · '))}">${!o.days?'<span class="fp zero">mimo provoz</span>':o.def?`<span class="fp bad">chybí ${o.def}</span>${sites.length>1?`<small>${esc(o.defSites.join(', '))}</small>`:''}`:o.free?`<span class="fp ok">volno ${o.free}</span>`:'<span class="fp zero">bez rezervy</span>'}</td>`;}).join('')}
        <td><button class="btn sm" data-act="jump" data-d="${m}" data-z="week"${S.staffSite!=='all'?` data-site="${S.staffSite}"`:''}>Na ose</button></td></tr>`;}).join('')}
    </tbody></table></div>
    <p class="hint" style="margin-top:10px">Volno = kolik lidí může chybět celý týden, aniž by vznikl nedostatek (nejmenší rezerva mezi kapacitou a potřebou v pracovních dnech týdne). V týdnech školních prázdnin se počítá s prázdninovou kapacitou. ${sites.length>1?'Rezervy se sčítají po pracovištích, personál se mezi nimi nepřesouvá.':''}</p>
  </section>`;
}

/* =========================================================
   Historie změn a verze plánu
   ========================================================= */
const LKEY='knl-rozpis-log-v1',VKEY='knl-rozpis-versions-v1';
const loadArr=k=>{try{const v=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(k)||'[]');return Array.isArray(v)?v:[];}catch(e){return [];}};
let LOG=loadArr(LKEY),VERS=loadArr(VKEY);
function saveLog(){try{localStorage.setItem(LKEY,JSON.stringify(LOG.slice(0,200)));}catch(e){}}
function saveVers(){try{localStorage.setItem(VKEY,JSON.stringify(VERS));return true;}catch(e){return false;}}
function logAdd(msg){LOG.unshift({ts:Date.now(),msg:String(msg)});if(LOG.length>200)LOG.length=200;saveLog();}
function relTime(ts){const m=Math.round((Date.now()-ts)/60000);if(m<1)return 'právě teď';if(m<60)return `před ${m} min`;const dt=new Date(ts),n=new Date();
  if(dt.toDateString()===n.toDateString())return `dnes ${dt.getHours()}:${pad(dt.getMinutes())}`;return `${dt.getDate()}. ${dt.getMonth()+1}. ${dt.getHours()}:${pad(dt.getMinutes())}`;}
function statsNow(){const days=yearDays(S.year),sp={};let hrs=0,halves=0;
  for(const room of D.rooms){if(!room.active||room.kind==='pacu')continue;for(const d of days){const r=R(room,d);let H=null;for(let i=0;i<2;i++){const h=r.h[i];if(!h||h.released)continue;H=H||hoursFor(room,d);const v=halfMin(H,i)/60;hrs+=v;halves++;sp[h.s]=(sp[h.s]||0)+v;}}}
  return {year:S.year,hrs,days:halves/2,sp,blocks:D.blocks.length,reqs:D.requests.length,ovs:Object.keys(D.overrides).length,rooms:D.rooms.filter(r=>r.active).length,iss:ISSUES().length};}
function withData(data,fn){const keep=D;D=data;try{migrate();invalidate();return fn();}finally{D=keep;invalidate();}}
function verDiff(old){const res=[],by=a=>Object.fromEntries((a||[]).map(x=>[x.id,x]));
  const code=id=>(ROOM[id]||(old.rooms||[]).find(r=>r.id===id)||{code:id}).code;
  const db=b=>`${b.type} · ${b.roomIds.map(code).join(', ')} · ${fmtR(b.from,b.to)}`,dq=q=>`${specName(q.specId)} – ${q.type} · ${fmtR(q.from,q.to)}`;
  const cmp=(oa,ca,kind,desc)=>{const o=by(oa),c=by(ca);ca.forEach(x=>{if(!o[x.id])res.push(['+',kind,desc(x)]);else if(JSON.stringify(o[x.id])!==JSON.stringify(x))res.push(['~',kind,desc(x)]);});(oa||[]).forEach(x=>{if(!c[x.id])res.push(['−',kind,desc(x)]);});};
  cmp(old.blocks,D.blocks,'Omezení',db);cmp(old.requests,D.requests,'Požadavek',dq);
  cmp(old.rooms,D.rooms,'Místnost',r=>`${r.name} (${r.code})`);cmp(old.groups,D.groups,'Skupina',g=>g.name);
  const oo=old.overrides||{},ok=Object.keys(oo);let oa=0,orr=0,oc=0;Object.keys(D.overrides).forEach(k=>{if(!(k in oo))oa++;else if(JSON.stringify(oo[k])!==JSON.stringify(D.overrides[k]))oc++;});ok.forEach(k=>{if(!(k in D.overrides))orr++;});
  if(oa||orr||oc)res.push(['~','Ruční úpravy dnů',`${oa} přidáno · ${oc} změněno · ${orr} odebráno`]);
  let tc=0;const ot=old.template||{};new Set([...Object.keys(ot),...Object.keys(D.template)]).forEach(id=>{for(let i=1;i<=5;i++)if(JSON.stringify((ot[id]||{})[i]||null)!==JSON.stringify((D.template[id]||{})[i]||null))tc++;});
  if(tc)res.push(['~','Týdenní šablona',`${tc} ${plural(tc,'změněný den sálu','změněné dny sálů','změněných dnů sálů')}`]);
  if(JSON.stringify(old.staff)!==JSON.stringify(D.staff))res.push(['~','Personál','normativ nebo kapacita']);
  if(JSON.stringify(old.quotas||null)!==JSON.stringify(D.quotas||null)&&old.quotas)res.push(['~','Kvóty oborů','upravené hodiny']);
  return res;}
function verCompare(v){let old;try{old=JSON.parse(v.json);}catch(e){return '';}
  const cur=statsNow(),vs=withData(JSON.parse(v.json),statsNow),diff=verDiff(old);
  const specs=D.specs.filter(s=>(cur.sp[s.id]||0)||(vs.sp[s.id]||0));
  const dl=(a,b,dec=0,u='')=>{const d=b-a;return Math.abs(d)<.05?'beze změny':`<b class="${d>0?'dt-pos':'dt-neg'}">${d>0?'+':''}${fmtN(d,dec)}${u}</b>`;};
  return `<section class="card" style="margin-top:16px" id="ver-cmp"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Porovnání · ${esc(v.name)} → aktuální plán · rok ${S.year}</div><button class="btn sm ghost" data-act="verCmpClose">Zavřít porovnání</button></div>
    <div class="kpis k4">
      ${kpi('Plánované hodiny',fmtN(cur.hrs),'h',`verze ${fmtN(vs.hrs)} h · ${dl(vs.hrs,cur.hrs,0,' h')}`,'var(--dot)')}
      ${kpi('Sálodny',fmtN(cur.days,1),'',`verze ${fmtN(vs.days,1)} · ${dl(vs.days,cur.days,1)}`,'#86A0C2')}
      ${kpi('Omezení a požadavky',cur.blocks+cur.reqs,'',`verze ${vs.blocks+vs.reqs} · ${dl(vs.blocks+vs.reqs,cur.blocks+cur.reqs)}`,'#D6A06E')}
      ${kpi('Upozornění',cur.iss,'',`verze ${vs.iss} · ${dl(vs.iss,cur.iss)}`,cur.iss>vs.iss?'var(--bad)':'var(--ok)')}
    </div>
    <div class="two"><div class="ovx"><table class="tb"><thead><tr><th>Obor</th><th class="r">Verze h</th><th class="r">Aktuální h</th><th class="r">Rozdíl</th></tr></thead><tbody>
      ${specs.map(s=>{const a=vs.sp[s.id]||0,b=cur.sp[s.id]||0;return `<tr><th><span class="sbr-l"><i style="background:${s.c}"></i>${esc(s.name)}</span></th><td class="r">${fmtN(a)}</td><td class="r">${fmtN(b)}</td><td class="r">${dl(a,b)}</td></tr>`;}).join('')}
    </tbody></table></div>
    <div><div class="card-t" style="margin-bottom:10px"><i></i>Změny<span class="cnt">${diff.length}</span></div>
      ${diff.length?`<div class="list">${diff.slice(0,14).map(([t,k,x])=>`<div class="ver"><div><b>${t==='+'?'Přidáno':t==='−'?'Odebráno':'Změněno'} · ${esc(k)}</b><small>${esc(x)}</small></div><span class="tag ${t==='+'?'ok':t==='−'?'bad':''}">${t}</span></div>`).join('')}</div>${diff.length>14?`<p class="hint" style="margin-top:8px">a dalších ${diff.length-14} změn…</p>`:''}`:empty('Žádné rozdíly – aktuální plán odpovídá verzi.')}
    </div></div></section>`;}
function copyText(txt,okMsg){S.copyFallback=null;try{const pr=navigator.clipboard&&navigator.clipboard.writeText(txt);if(pr&&pr.then){pr.then(()=>toast(okMsg),()=>showCopy(txt));return;}}catch(e){}showCopy(txt);}
function showCopy(txt){S.copyFallback=txt;render();setTimeout(()=>{const t=$('#copy-fb');if(t){t.focus();t.select();t.scrollIntoView({block:'center'});}},60);toast('Schránka není dostupná – text je označený, zkopírujte ho Ctrl/⌘ + C');}
function impCheck(text){S.imp={text,ok:false};try{const o=JSON.parse(text);if(!(o&&o.v===2&&Array.isArray(o.rooms)&&Array.isArray(o.specs)&&o.template&&typeof o.template==='object'&&Array.isArray(o.blocks)&&Array.isArray(o.requests)&&o.staff))throw 0;
  S.imp.ok=true;S.imp.info=`${o.rooms.length} místností · ${(o.groups||[]).length} skupin · ${o.specs.length} oborů · ${o.blocks.length} omezení · ${o.requests.length} požadavků`;}catch(e){S.imp.err='Text není platná záloha Rozpisu operativy.';}}
function impInfo(){const m=S.imp;if(!m)return '';if(!m.ok)return note('bad','warn',esc(m.err||'Neplatná záloha'));
  return `<div class="note" style="margin-top:12px">${ico('info')}<div><b>Platná záloha:</b> ${esc(m.info)}. Načtením se nahradí celý aktuální plán (lze vrátit tlačítkem Zpět).<div style="margin-top:8px"><button class="btn sm pri" data-act="impApply">${S.confirm==='imp'?'Opravdu nahradit plán?':'Nahradit plán zálohou'}</button></div></div></div>`;}
function cellTxt(room,d){const r=R(room,d),di=r.di,planned=r.h[0]||r.h[1];
  if(r.block&&!(r.src==='override'&&planned))return r.block.type;if(!planned)return di.holiday?'svátek':'';
  const f=h=>!h?'—':h.released?'uvolněno':`${h.s} ${h.an}`,[a,b]=r.h;
  if(!(r.slot&&r.slot.split)&&a&&b&&a.released===b.released)return f(a);return `${f(a)} / ${f(b)}`;}
function rosterTSV(ym,site){const [y,m]=ym.split('-').map(Number),nd=new Date(Date.UTC(y,m,0)).getUTCDate(),days=[];for(let i=1;i<=nd;i++)days.push(`${ym}-${pad(i)}`);
  const rows=[['Pracoviště','Skupina','Sál','Kód',...days.map(d=>`${+d.slice(8)}. ${DOW[isoDow(d)]}`)].join('\t')];
  for(const s of SITES){if(site!=='all'&&s.id!==site)continue;for(const gid of siteGroups(s.id))for(const r of sortRooms(D.rooms.filter(x=>x.active&&x.site===s.id&&gOf(x)===gid&&x.kind!=='pacu')))
    rows.push([s.name,gid?gName(gid):'',r.name,r.code,...days.map(d=>cellTxt(r,d))].join('\t'));}
  return rows.join('\n');}
function rosterPreview(){const rows=rosterTSV(S.xMonth,S.xSite||'all').split('\n').map(r=>r.split('\t'));if(rows.length<2)return empty('Ve výběru nejsou žádné sály.');const cols=Math.min(rows[0].length,4+7);
  return `<table class="tb"><thead><tr>${rows[0].slice(2,cols).map(c=>`<th>${esc(c)}</th>`).join('')}</tr></thead><tbody>${rows.slice(1,7).map(r=>`<tr>${r.slice(2,cols).map((c,i)=>i===0?`<th>${esc(c)}</th>`:`<td>${esc(c)}</td>`).join('')}</tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table><p class="hint" style="margin-top:6px">Náhled prvních řádků a dnů · celkem ${rows.length-1} ${plural(rows.length-1,'sál','sály','sálů')} × ${rows[0].length-4} dní</p>`;}
function vData(){
  const cmp=S.verCmp&&VERS.find(v=>v.id===S.verCmp),months=Array.from({length:12},(_,i)=>`${S.year}-${pad(i+1)}`);
  if(!S.xMonth||+S.xMonth.slice(0,4)!==S.year)S.xMonth=`${S.year}-${(S.anchor||'').slice(5,7)||'01'}`;
  return ph('Systém','Verze a záloha')+`<div class="two">
    <section class="card"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Verze plánu<span class="cnt">${VERS.length}</span></div></div>
      <div class="row-in"><input class="in" id="ver-name" placeholder="Název verze, např. Návrh pro vedení" maxlength="80" aria-label="Název verze"><button class="btn pri" data-act="verSave">${ico('save')}Uložit verzi</button></div>
      <p class="hint" style="margin:10px 0 12px">Verze je snímek celého plánu. Můžete ji porovnat s aktuálním stavem nebo se k ní vrátit.</p>
      ${VERS.length?`<div class="list">${VERS.map(v=>`<div class="ver${S.verCmp===v.id?' on':''}"><div><b>${esc(v.name)}</b><small>${relTime(v.ts)} · ${fmtN(v.st.hrs)} h · ${v.st.blocks} omezení · ${v.st.reqs} požadavků · rok ${v.st.year}</small></div><div class="act"><button class="btn sm${S.verCmp===v.id?' pri':''}" data-act="verCmp" data-id="${v.id}">Porovnat</button><button class="btn sm${S.confirm==='verR:'+v.id?' warn':''}" data-act="verRestore" data-id="${v.id}">${S.confirm==='verR:'+v.id?'Opravdu obnovit?':'Obnovit'}</button><button class="icon-btn sm${S.confirm==='verD:'+v.id?' armed':''}" data-act="verDel" data-id="${v.id}" aria-label="Smazat verzi">${S.confirm==='verD:'+v.id?'Smazat?':ico('trash')}</button></div></div>`).join('')}</div>`:empty('Zatím žádná uložená verze.')}
    </section>
    <section class="card"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Historie změn<span class="cnt">${LOG.length}</span></div>${LOG.length?`<button class="btn sm ghost${S.confirm==='logClr'?' warn':''}" data-act="logClear">${S.confirm==='logClr'?'Klepněte znovu pro vymazání':'Vymazat historii'}</button>`:''}</div>
      ${LOG.length?`<div class="log">${LOG.slice(0,60).map(l=>`<div class="lg-r"><span title="${esc(new Date(l.ts).toLocaleString('cs-CZ'))}">${relTime(l.ts)}</span><div>${esc(l.msg)}</div></div>`).join('')}</div>`:empty('Změny plánu se zde začnou zapisovat od první úpravy.')}
    </section></div>
    ${cmp?verCompare(cmp):''}
    <div class="two" style="margin-top:16px">
    <section class="card"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Záloha dat</div></div>
      <p class="hint" style="margin-bottom:12px">Celý plán (sály, skupiny, šablona, požadavky, omezení, personál, kvóty) jako text. Uložte ho do souboru nebo pošlete kolegovi, který ho zde načte.</p>
      <div class="row-in">${DL?`<button class="btn" data-act="bkSave">${ico('save')}Stáhnout zálohu</button>`:''}<button class="btn" data-act="bkCopy">${ico('copy')}Zkopírovat zálohu</button><label class="btn" for="imp-file">${ico('upload')}Načíst ze souboru</label><input type="file" id="imp-file" accept=".json,.txt,application/json,text/plain" data-ch="impFile" hidden></div>
      <div class="fld" style="margin-top:12px"><label for="imp-text">Nebo vložte text zálohy</label><textarea class="in" id="imp-text" data-ch="impText" rows="3" placeholder="Vložte zkopírovanou zálohu a klepněte mimo pole">${esc(S.imp&&S.imp.text&&S.imp.text.length<4000?S.imp.text:'')}</textarea></div>
      ${impInfo()}
      ${S.copyFallback?`<div class="fld" style="margin-top:12px"><label for="copy-fb">Text ke zkopírování</label><textarea class="in" id="copy-fb" rows="4" readonly>${esc(S.copyFallback)}</textarea></div>`:''}
    </section>
    <section class="card"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Rozpis pro Excel</div></div>
      <p class="hint" style="margin-bottom:12px">Zkopíruje měsíční rozpis sálů jako tabulku. V Excelu nebo Google Tabulkách ji stačí vložit.</p>
      <div class="grid-f"><div class="fld"><label for="x-month">Měsíc</label><select class="in" id="x-month" data-ch="xMonth">${months.map(m=>`<option value="${m}"${m===S.xMonth?' selected':''}>${cap(MONTHS[+m.slice(5)-1])} ${m.slice(0,4)}</option>`).join('')}</select></div>
        <div class="fld"><label for="x-site">Pracoviště</label><select class="in" id="x-site" data-ch="xSite">${[['all','Všechna'],...SITES.map(s=>[s.id,s.name])].map(([v,t])=>`<option value="${v}"${(S.xSite||'all')===v?' selected':''}>${t}</option>`).join('')}</select></div></div>
      ${DL?`<button class="btn pri" data-act="xSave">${ico('save')}Stáhnout pro Excel</button><button class="btn" data-act="xCopy">${ico('copy')}Zkopírovat</button>`:`<button class="btn pri" data-act="xCopy">${ico('copy')}Zkopírovat rozpis</button>`}
      <div class="ovx" style="margin-top:14px">${rosterPreview()}</div>
    </section></div>`;
}

/* =========================================================
   Kvóty oborů
   ========================================================= */
function tplWeekly(site){const o={};for(const room of D.rooms){if(!room.active||room.kind==='pacu'||room.site!==site)continue;const H=roomHours(room);
  for(let i=1;i<=5;i++){for(const [sl,w] of tplVars((D.template[room.id]||{})[i])){if(!sl)continue;const hs=sl.split?[sl.a,sl.b]:[sl.a,sl.a];hs.forEach((h,j)=>{if(h&&h.s)o[h.s]=(o[h.s]||0)+w*halfMin(H,j)/60;});}}}return o;}
function quotas(){if(!D.quotas||typeof D.quotas!=='object'){D.quotas={};SITES.forEach(s=>{const w=tplWeekly(s.id);D.quotas[s.id]=Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(w).map(([k,v])=>[k,Math.round(v)]));});save();LASTJ=snap();}
  SITES.forEach(s=>{if(!D.quotas[s.id])D.quotas[s.id]={};});return D.quotas;}
function quotaModel(site){const key='quota|'+S.year+'|'+site;if(KC.has(key))return KC.get(key);
  const sites=scopeSites(site),Q=quotas(),days=yearDays(S.year),mon=Array.from({length:12},()=>({days:0,sp:{}})),yr={};
  const rooms=D.rooms.filter(r=>r.active&&r.kind!=='pacu'&&sites.includes(r.site));
  for(const d of days){const m=+d.slice(5,7)-1;mon[m].days++;for(const room of rooms){const r=R(room,d);let H=null;for(let i=0;i<2;i++){const h=r.h[i];if(!h||h.released)continue;H=H||hoursFor(room,d);const v=halfMin(H,i)/60;mon[m].sp[h.s]=(mon[m].sp[h.s]||0)+v;yr[h.s]=(yr[h.s]||0)+v;}}}
  const wY=days.length/7,rows=D.specs.map(s=>{const t=sites.reduce((a,x)=>a+(+Q[x][s.id]||0),0),a=(yr[s.id]||0)/wY;return {s,t,a,p:t?a/t*100:null,ms:mon.map(mm=>(mm.sp[s.id]||0)/(mm.days/7))};}).filter(r=>r.t||r.a>.05);
  const v={rows,T:rows.reduce((x,r)=>x+r.t,0),A:rows.reduce((x,r)=>x+r.a,0)};KC.set(key,v);return v;}
function qColor(p){if(p==null)return 'var(--sunk)';const dv=Math.min(40,Math.abs(p-100));if(p<90)return `color-mix(in srgb,var(--warn) ${Math.round(28+dv*1.6)}%,var(--sunk))`;if(p>110)return `color-mix(in srgb,#86A0C2 ${Math.round(32+dv*1.6)}%,var(--sunk))`;return 'color-mix(in srgb,var(--ok) 48%,var(--sunk))';}
function vQuota(){
  const site=S.qSite||'all',M=quotaModel(site),Q=quotas();
  const withT=M.rows.filter(r=>r.t).length,inN=M.rows.filter(r=>r.p!=null&&r.p>=90&&r.p<=110).length;
  const worst=M.rows.filter(r=>r.p!=null).sort((a,b)=>Math.abs(b.p-100)-Math.abs(a.p-100))[0];
  const seg=`<div class="seg">${[['all','Vše'],...SITES.map(s=>[s.id,s.name])].map(([v,t])=>`<button data-act="qSite" data-v="${v}" class="${site===v?'on':''}">${t}</button>`).join('')}</div>`;
  return ph('Přehledy · '+(site==='all'?'všechna pracoviště':SITE[site].name),'Kvóty oborů',seg)+`
  <div class="kpis k4">
    ${kpi('Kvóty celkem',fmtN(M.T),'h / týden',`${withT} ${plural(withT,'obor','obory','oborů')} s kvótou`,'#86A0C2')}
    ${kpi('Skutečnost',fmtN(M.A),'h / týden',`${fmtN(pctOf(M.A,M.T))} % kvót · průměr roku ${S.year}`,'var(--dot)')}
    ${kpi('V normě',inN,`z ${withT}`,'odchylka do ±10 %',inN===withT?'var(--ok)':'var(--warn)')}
    ${kpi('Největší odchylka',worst?fmtN(Math.abs(worst.p-100)):0,'%',worst?`${esc(worst.s.name)} · ${worst.p>=100?'nad':'pod'} kvótou`:'—',worst&&Math.abs(worst.p-100)>10?'var(--bad)':'var(--ok)')}
  </div>
  <section class="card"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Plnění kvót · ${S.year}</div>
    <div class="q-leg"><span><i style="background:${qColor(100)}"></i>v normě ±10 %</span><span><i style="background:${qColor(75)}"></i>pod kvótou</span><span><i style="background:${qColor(125)}"></i>nad kvótou</span>${site!=='all'?`<button class="btn sm ghost${S.confirm==='qReset'?' warn':''}" data-act="qReset">${S.confirm==='qReset'?'Klepněte znovu – přepíše kvóty':'Nastavit podle šablony'}</button>`:''}</div></div>
    ${M.rows.length?`<div class="ovx"><table class="qt"><thead><tr><th>Obor</th><th class="r">Kvóta h/týden</th><th class="r">Skutečnost</th><th>Plnění</th><th class="r">Odchylka</th><th>Stav</th><th>Měsíce · led–pro</th></tr></thead><tbody>
    ${M.rows.map(r=>{const st=r.p==null?['','bez kvóty']:r.p<90?['under','pod kvótou']:r.p>110?['over','nad kvótou']:['ok','v normě'],mx=Math.max(r.t,r.a,1)*1.15;
      return `<tr><th><span class="sbr-l"><i style="background:${r.s.c}"></i>${esc(r.s.name)}</span></th>
      <td class="r">${site==='all'?fmtN(r.t,1):`<input class="in num" type="number" min="0" max="400" step="0.5" id="q-${site}-${r.s.id}" data-ch="quota" data-site="${site}" data-k="${r.s.id}" value="${+Q[site][r.s.id]||0}" aria-label="Kvóta ${esc(r.s.name)}">`}</td>
      <td class="r">${fmtN(r.a,1)}</td>
      <td><div class="qbar"><i style="width:${(r.a/mx*100).toFixed(1)}%;background:${r.s.c}"></i>${r.t?`<b style="left:${(r.t/mx*100).toFixed(1)}%" title="Kvóta ${fmtN(r.t,1)} h/týden"></b>`:''}</div></td>
      <td class="r">${r.p==null?'—':`${r.p>=100?'+':'−'}${fmtN(Math.abs(r.p-100))} %`}</td>
      <td><span class="qs ${st[0]}">${st[1]}</span></td>
      <td><div class="qm">${r.ms.map((v,i)=>{const p=r.t?v/r.t*100:null;return `<i style="background:${qColor(p)}" title="${cap(MONTHS[i])}: ${fmtN(v,1)} h/týden${p!=null?` · ${fmtN(p)} % kvóty`:''}"></i>`;}).join('')}</div></td></tr>`;}).join('')}
    </tbody></table></div>`:empty('Na pracovišti nejsou plánované žádné obory.')}
    <p class="hint" style="margin-top:12px">Kvóta je dohodnutý týdenní čas oboru na sálech. Skutečnost jsou plánované hodiny po odečtení svátků, požadavků oborů a omezení sálů, v průměru na kalendářní týden. Černá značka v pruhu ukazuje kvótu. ${site==='all'?'Kvóty se upravují po pracovištích – přepněte na Liberec, Turnov nebo Frýdlant.':'Hodnotu kvóty upravíte přímo v tabulce.'}</p>
  </section>`;
}

/* =========================================================
   Přehled provozu
   ========================================================= */
function focusDay(){let d=today;for(let i=0;i<14;i++){const di=DI(d);if(!di.weekend&&!di.holiday)return d;d=addD(d,1);}return today;}
function hpChip(h){if(!h)return '<span class="hp off">volno</span>';if(h.released)return `<span class="hp off" title="Uvolněno – ${esc(h.req.type)}">${esc(h.s)} ↓</span>`;const s=SPEC[h.s]||{c:'#999'};return `<span class="hp ${h.an}" style="--c:${s.c}" title="${esc(specName(h.s))} · ${h.an==='CA'?'celková':'lokální'} anestezie">${esc(h.s)}</span>`;}
function siteToday(s,fd){
  const rooms=sortRooms(D.rooms.filter(r=>r.active&&r.site===s.id&&r.kind!=='pacu')),pac=D.rooms.filter(r=>r.active&&r.site===s.id&&r.kind==='pacu');let run=0;
  const rows=rooms.map(room=>{const r=R(room,fd),planned=r.h[0]||r.h[1];let chips;
    if(r.block&&!(r.src==='override'&&planned))chips=`<span class="hp x">${esc(r.block.type)}</span>`;
    else{const [a,b]=r.h,split=r.slot&&r.slot.split;if((a&&!a.released)||(b&&!b.released))run++;chips=(!split&&a&&b&&a.released===b.released)?hpChip(a):hpChip(a)+hpChip(b);}
    return `<button class="trw" data-act="openDay" data-r="${room.id}" data-d="${fd}" title="${esc(room.name)} · ${fH(hoursFor(room,fd))} · klepnutím upravíte"><span><code>${esc(room.code)}</code><b>${esc(room.name)}</b></span><span class="hps">${chips}${r.issues.length?'<i class="dotx" title="Konflikt"></i>':''}</span></button>`;}).join('');
  const v=SD(s.id,fd),po=pac.filter(p=>{const r=R(p,fd);return r.open&&(r.open[0]||r.open[1]);}).length;
  return `<section class="card"><div class="sc-h"><h3>${s.name}</h3><span>${run} z ${rooms.length} sálů v provozu${pac.length?` · dospávací pokoje ${po}/${pac.length}`:''}</span></div><div class="tr-list">${rows||'<p class="hint">Bez sálů.</p>'}</div>
    <div class="st3">${ROLES.map(([k,ck,lab])=>`<div>${lab}<b>${v[k]}<small> z ${v[ck]}</small></b><em class="${v[k]>v[ck]?'bad':''}">${v[ck]>=v[k]?'volno '+(v[ck]-v[k]):'chybí '+(v[k]-v[ck])}</em></div>`).join('')}</div></section>`;}
function weekStrip(fd){const mon=addD(fd,-(isoDow(fd)-1)),days=[0,1,2,3,4].map(i=>addD(mon,i));
  let h=`<div class="wk"><span></span>${days.map(d=>`<span class="h${d===today?' td':''}">${DOW[isoDow(d)]} ${fmt(d)}</span>`).join('')}`;
  SITES.forEach(s=>{const rooms=D.rooms.filter(r=>r.active&&r.site===s.id&&r.kind!=='pacu');h+=`<span class="s">${s.name}</span>`;
    days.forEach(d=>{const di=DI(d);if(di.holiday){h+=`<span class="wkc hol" title="${esc(di.holiday)}"><span>svátek</span></span>`;return;}
      let pl=0;rooms.forEach(room=>R(room,d).h.forEach(x=>{if(x&&!x.released)pl++;}));const u=rooms.length?pl/(rooms.length*2)*100:0,def=SD(s.id,d).def;
      h+=`<button class="wkc${def?' def':''}" data-act="jump" data-d="${d}" data-z="week" data-site="${s.id}" title="${s.name} · ${fmtY(d)} · obsazenost ${fmtN(u)} %${def?' · nedostatek personálu':''}"><i style="height:${u.toFixed(0)}%"></i><span>${fmtN(u)} %</span></button>`;});});
  return h+'</div>';}
function upcomingAll(from,n){const to=addD(from,n),ev=[];
  for(let y=+from.slice(0,4);y<=+to.slice(0,4);y++)Object.entries(HOLI(y)).forEach(([d,nm])=>{if(d>=from&&d<=to)ev.push({from:d,to:d,k:'hol',t:nm,tag:'Státní svátek'});});
  SCHOOL.forEach(x=>{if(x.to>=from&&x.from<=to)ev.push({from:x.from,to:x.to,k:'sch',t:x.name,tag:'Prázdniny'});});
  D.requests.forEach(q=>{if(q.to>=from&&q.from<=to)ev.push({from:q.from,to:q.to,k:'req',t:`${specName(q.specId)} – ${q.type.toLowerCase()}`,sub:q.note,tag:'Požadavek oboru'});});
  D.blocks.forEach(b=>{if(b.to>=from&&b.from<=to)ev.push({from:b.from,to:b.to,k:'blk',t:`${b.type} – ${b.roomIds.map(id=>(ROOM[id]||{}).code).filter(Boolean).join(', ')}`,sub:b.note,tag:'Omezení sálu'});});
  return ev.sort((a,b)=>a.from.localeCompare(b.from));}
function vHome(){
  const fd=focusDay(),isT=fd===today,iss=ISSUES().filter(i=>(i.to||i.ds)>=today),ev=upcomingAll(today,28);
  let run=0,tot=0,hrs=0,ca=0,la=0,fN=0,fS=0;
  SITES.forEach(s=>{D.rooms.filter(r=>r.active&&r.site===s.id&&r.kind!=='pacu').forEach(room=>{tot++;const r=R(room,fd);let on=false,H=null;r.h.forEach((h,i)=>{if(!h||h.released)return;on=true;H=H||hoursFor(room,fd);hrs+=halfMin(H,i)/60;if(h.an==='CA')ca++;else la++;});if(on)run++;});
    const v=SD(s.id,fd);fN+=Math.max(0,v.capNur-v.nur);fS+=Math.max(0,v.capScr-v.scr);});
  const tagc={hol:'hol',sch:'sch',req:'',blk:'dark'};
  const pendN=D.requests.filter(q=>q.status==='pending').length;
  return ph(`${isT?'Dnes':'Nejbližší pracovní den'} · ${DOW_L[isoDow(fd)]} ${fmtY(fd)}`,'Přehled provozu',`<button class="btn" data-act="go" data-v="kal">${ico('month')}Kalendář</button><button class="btn" data-act="board" title="Nástěnka pro obrazovku u sálů (B)">${ico('tv')}Nástěnka</button>`)+`
  <div class="kpis k4">
    ${kpi('Sály v provozu',run,`z ${tot}`,isT?'dnes':`${DOW_L[isoDow(fd)]} ${fmt(fd)}`,'var(--dot)')}
    ${kpi('Plánované hodiny',fmtN(hrs),'h',`${ca} ${plural(ca,'půlblok','půlbloky','půlbloků')} v CA · ${la} v LA`,'#86A0C2')}
    ${kpi('Volné sestry',fN+fS,'',`anesteziologické ${fN} · sálové ${fS}`,'#CF95A6')}
    ${kpi('Upozornění',iss.length,'',`od dnešního dne v roce ${S.year}`,iss.length?'var(--bad)':'var(--ok)')}
  </div>
  ${wtCard()}
  <div class="two" style="margin-top:16px">
    <section class="card"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Nadcházející události · 4 týdny<span class="cnt">${ev.length}</span></div></div>
      ${ev.length?`<div class="list">${ev.slice(0,10).map(e=>`<div class="li"><div class="when">${e.from===e.to?fmtY(e.from):fmtR(e.from,e.to)}<small>${cap(DOW_L[isoDow(e.from)])}</small></div><div><div class="l1"><span class="tag ${tagc[e.k]}">${e.tag}</span><b>${esc(e.t)}</b></div>${e.sub?`<div class="l2">${esc(e.sub)}</div>`:''}</div><div class="act"><button class="btn sm" data-act="jump" data-d="${e.from<today?today:e.from}" data-z="week">Na ose</button></div></div>`).join('')}</div>${ev.length>10?`<p class="hint" style="margin-top:8px">a dalších ${ev.length-10} událostí</p>`:''}`:empty('V příštích 4 týdnech nejsou žádné události.')}
    </section>
    <div class="stack">
      <section class="card"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Vyžaduje pozornost<span class="cnt">${iss.length}</span></div>${iss.length?'<button class="btn sm" data-act="go" data-v="alerts">Vše</button>':''}</div>
        ${iss.length?`<div class="list">${iss.slice(0,5).map(alertRow).join('')}</div>`:empty('Žádné konflikty ani nedostatky personálu.')}</section>
      <section class="card"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Poslední změny</div>${LOG.length?'<button class="btn sm" data-act="go" data-v="data">Historie</button>':''}</div>
        ${LOG.length?`<div class="log">${LOG.slice(0,6).map(l=>`<div class="lg-r"><span>${relTime(l.ts)}</span><div>${esc(l.msg)}</div></div>`).join('')}</div>`:'<p class="hint">Zatím žádné změny. Rychlé hledání otevřete klávesou / nebo Ctrl/⌘ + K.</p>'}</section>
    </div></div>`;
}

/* =========================================================
   Rychlé hledání (⌘K)
   ========================================================= */
const CMD={q:'',i:0,list:[]};
const nrm=x=>String(x||'').normalize('NFD').replace(/[̀-ͯ]/g,'').toLowerCase();
function goRoom(id){const r=ROOM[id];if(!r)return;if(!r.active){S.view='rooms';render();toast(`${r.name} je mimo provoz – upravíte ho v sekci Sály a skupiny`);return;}
  S.view='timeline';if(S.zoom==='year')S.zoom='month';if(S.site!=='all'&&S.site!==r.site)S.site=r.site;if(S.kind!=='all'&&S.kind!==(r.kind||'or'))S.kind='all';S.hidden.delete(id);render();
  requestAnimationFrame(()=>{const row=document.querySelector(`#tl .tl-room[data-room="${id}"]`);if(row){row.scrollIntoView({block:'center',behavior:RM()?'auto':'smooth'});row.classList.add('flash');setTimeout(()=>row.classList.remove('flash'),2000);}});}
function goGroup(gid){const g=D.groups.find(x=>x.id===gid);if(!g)return;S.view='timeline';S.site=g.site;S.kind='all';S.hidden=new Set(D.rooms.filter(r=>gOf(r)!==gid).map(r=>r.id));render();toast(`Zobrazena skupina ${g.name} – ostatní místnosti vrátíte ve Výběru`);}
function cmdSource(){const out=[],add=o=>out.push(o);
  navList().forEach(([,items])=>items.forEach(([v,t,ic])=>add({k:'Sekce',t,ic,run:()=>{S.view=v;render();scrollTo({top:0});}})));
  add({k:'Akce',t:'Hromadná úprava plánu',ic:'layers',run:()=>ACT.bulk()});
  add({k:'Akce',t:'Nový požadavek oboru',ic:'req',run:()=>ACT.newReq()});
  add({k:'Akce',t:'Nové omezení sálu',ic:'ban',run:()=>{S.view='blocks';S.blEdit=null;S.blDraft=blDefault();render();scrollTo({top:0});}});
  add({k:'Akce',t:'Navrhnout asanační dny',ic:'spark',run:()=>{S.view='asanace';render();scrollTo({top:0});}});
  add({k:'Akce',t:'Uložit verzi plánu',ic:'save',run:()=>{S.view='data';render();setTimeout(()=>{const e=$('#ver-name');if(e)e.focus();},60);}});
  add({k:'Akce',t:'Vrátit poslední změnu',ic:'undo',run:()=>ACT.undo()});
  add({k:'Akce',t:S.theme==='dark'?'Přepnout na světlý vzhled':'Přepnout na tmavý vzhled',ic:S.theme==='dark'?'sun':'moon',run:()=>ACT.theme({dataset:{v:S.theme==='dark'?'light':'dark'}})});
  add({k:'Akce',t:S.mini?'Rozbalit postranní menu':'Sbalit postranní menu',ic:S.mini?'expand':'collapse',run:()=>ACT.side()});
  add({k:'Akce',t:'Tisk aktuální sekce',ic:'printer',run:()=>ACT.print()});
  [['coord','Koordinátor operativy'],['spec','Primář oboru'],['mgmt','Vedení nemocnice (jen čtení)']].forEach(([v,t])=>add({k:'Role',t:`Přepnout roli: ${t}`,ic:'user',run:()=>ACT.role({dataset:{v}})}));
  add({k:'Akce',t:'Tisk omezení sálů a asanací za období',ic:'printer',run:()=>ACT.bpGo()});
  add({k:'Akce',t:'Nástěnka na velkou obrazovku',ic:'tv',hint:'B',run:()=>boardOpen()});
  add({k:'Akce',t:'Klávesové zkratky',ic:'key',hint:'?',run:()=>{S.help=true;renderModal();}});
  add({k:'Akce',t:'Sezónní šablony (letní provoz)',ic:'cal',run:()=>{S.view='rooms';render();setTimeout(()=>{const e=$('#se-card');if(e)e.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth',block:'start'});},60);}});
  {const pn=D.requests.filter(q=>q.status==='pending').length;if(pn)add({k:'Akce',t:`Požadavky čekající na schválení (${pn})`,ic:'req',run:()=>{S.view='requests';S.rqFilter='pending';render();}});}
  D.rooms.forEach(r=>add({k:'Místnost',t:r.name,sub:`${r.code} · ${KINDS[r.kind||'or']} · ${SITE[r.site].name}${gOf(r)?' · '+gName(r.group):''}`,sw:roomColor(r),hint:r.code,run:()=>goRoom(r.id)}));
  D.groups.forEach(g=>add({k:'Skupina',t:g.name,sub:SITE[g.site].name,ic:'layers',run:()=>goGroup(g.id)}));
  D.specs.forEach(sp=>add({k:'Obor',t:sp.name,sub:`${sp.id} · zobrazit v časové ose`,sw:sp.c,run:()=>{S.view='timeline';S.specs=new Set([sp.id]);render();scrollTo({top:0});}}));
  D.requests.forEach(q=>add({k:'Požadavek',t:`${specName(q.specId)} – ${q.type}`,sub:`${RQ_ST[q.status||'ok']} · ${fmtR(q.from,q.to)}${q.note?' · '+q.note:''}`,ic:'req',run:()=>ACT.jump({dataset:{d:q.from}})}));
  D.blocks.forEach(b=>add({k:'Omezení',t:`${b.type} – ${b.roomIds.map(id=>(ROOM[id]||{}).code).filter(Boolean).join(', ')}`,sub:`${fmtR(b.from,b.to)}${b.note?' · '+b.note:''}`,ic:'ban',run:()=>ACT.jump({dataset:{d:b.from}})}));
  return out;}
function cmdFilter(q){const all=cmdSource(),nq=nrm(q.trim());let out;
  if(!nq)out=all.filter(o=>o.k==='Sekce'||o.k==='Akce');
  else{const w=nq.split(/\s+/);out=all.map(o=>{const hay=nrm(o.t+' '+(o.sub||'')+' '+o.k);if(!w.every(x=>hay.includes(x)))return null;const tt=nrm(o.t);return {o,sc:(tt.startsWith(w[0])?0:tt.includes(w[0])?1:2)};}).filter(Boolean).sort((a,b)=>a.sc-b.sc).map(x=>x.o);}
  const iso=q.trim()?parseIn(q.trim()):null;
  if(iso)out.unshift({k:'Datum',t:`Přejít na ${DOW_L[isoDow(iso)]} ${fmtY(iso)}`,sub:[DI(iso).holiday,DI(iso).school&&DI(iso).school.name].filter(Boolean).join(' · ')||'týden v časové ose',ic:'cal',run:()=>ACT.jump({dataset:{d:iso,z:'week'}})});
  return out.slice(0,60);}
function hlq(t,q){const w=nrm(q.trim()).split(/\s+/)[0];if(!w)return esc(t);const i=nrm(t).indexOf(w);if(i<0)return esc(t);return esc(t.slice(0,i))+'<mark>'+esc(t.slice(i,i+w.length))+'</mark>'+esc(t.slice(i+w.length));}
function cmdList(){CMD.list=cmdFilter(CMD.q);if(CMD.i>=CMD.list.length)CMD.i=Math.max(0,CMD.list.length-1);const el=$('#cmd-list');if(!el)return;
  if(!CMD.list.length){el.innerHTML='<div class="cmd-e">Nic nenalezeno. Zkuste název sálu, obor, skupinu nebo datum (např. 21.4.2027).</div>';return;}
  let g='',h='';CMD.list.forEach((o,i)=>{if(o.k!==g){g=o.k;h+=`<div class="cmd-g">${esc(g)}</div>`;}
    h+=`<button class="cmd-it${i===CMD.i?' on':''}" data-cmd="run" data-i="${i}" role="option" aria-selected="${i===CMD.i}"><span class="ci">${o.sw?`<i style="--c:${o.sw}"></i>`:o.ic?ico(o.ic):''}</span><span><b>${hlq(o.t,CMD.q)}</b>${o.sub?`<small>${esc(o.sub)}</small>`:''}</span><em>${esc(o.hint||'')}</em></button>`;});
  el.innerHTML=h;const on=el.querySelector('.cmd-it.on');if(on)on.scrollIntoView({block:'nearest'});}
function cmdOpen(){CMD.q='';CMD.i=0;closeDP();tip.hidden=true;const h=$('#cmd');
  h.innerHTML=`<div class="mo-scrim" data-cmd="close"></div><div class="cmd" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="Rychlé hledání"><div class="cmd-in">${ico('search')}<input id="cmd-q" placeholder="Hledat sál, obor, skupinu, datum nebo akci…" autocomplete="off" spellcheck="false" aria-label="Hledat"><kbd>Esc</kbd></div><div class="cmd-list" id="cmd-list" role="listbox"></div><div class="cmd-f"><span><kbd>↑</kbd> <kbd>↓</kbd> výběr</span><span><kbd>Enter</kbd> otevřít</span><span>datum pište jako 21.4.2027</span></div></div>`;
  h.hidden=false;cmdList();const qi=$('#cmd-q');if(qi)qi.focus();}
function cmdClose(){const h=$('#cmd');h.hidden=true;h.innerHTML='';}
function cmdRun(i){const o=CMD.list[i];cmdClose();if(o)o.run();}
{const c=$('#cmd');
  c.addEventListener('input',e=>{if(e.target.id==='cmd-q'){CMD.q=e.target.value;CMD.i=0;cmdList();}});
  c.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{const n=CMD.list.length;
    if(e.key==='ArrowDown'||e.key==='ArrowUp'){e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();if(!n)return;CMD.i=(CMD.i+(e.key==='ArrowDown'?1:-1)+n)%n;cmdList();}
    else if(e.key==='Enter'){e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();cmdRun(CMD.i);}
    else if(e.key==='Escape'){e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();cmdClose();}});
  c.addEventListener('click',e=>{e.stopPropagation();const b=e.target.closest('[data-cmd]');if(!b)return;if(b.dataset.cmd==='close')cmdClose();else cmdRun(+b.dataset.i);});
  c.addEventListener('mousemove',e=>{const b=e.target.closest('.cmd-it');if(!b||+b.dataset.i===CMD.i)return;CMD.i=+b.dataset.i;c.querySelectorAll('.cmd-it.on').forEach(x=>x.classList.remove('on'));b.classList.add('on');});}

/* =========================================================
   Dopad požadavku a omezení
   ========================================================= */
function rqCalc(d,editId){let n=0,h=0;const rooms=new Set(),dd=new Set();
  if(!d||!SPEC[d.specId]||!d.from||!d.to||d.from>d.to||diffD(d.from,d.to)>400)return {n,h,rooms:0,days:0};
  const sc=d.scope||'all',ids=new Set(d.rooms||[]),N=Math.max(1,+d.cnt||1);
  for(let x=d.from;x<=d.to;x=addD(x,1))for(let i=0;i<2;i++){if((d.part==='am'&&i!==0)||(d.part==='pm'&&i!==1))continue;let L=reqRoomsAt(d.specId,x,i,d.site).filter(o=>!o.ov);
    if(sc==='cnt')L=L.slice(0,N);else if(sc==='rooms')L=L.filter(o=>ids.has(o.room.id));
    L.forEach(({room,blk})=>{if(blk)return;const q2=reqFor(d.specId,x,i,room.site,room.id);if(q2&&q2.id!==editId)return;n++;h+=halfMin(hoursFor(room,x),i)/60;rooms.add(room.id);dd.add(x);});}
  return {n,h,rooms:rooms.size,days:dd.size};}
function rqImpactHTML(){const d=S.rqDraft;if(!d||!SPEC[d.specId]||!d.from||!d.to||d.from>d.to||diffD(d.from,d.to)>400)return '';
  const st=d.status||'ok';if(st==='rejected')return `${ico('info')}<div>Zamítnutý požadavek plán nemění – bloky oboru zůstávají.</div>`;
  if((d.scope||'all')==='rooms'&&!(d.rooms||[]).length)return `${ico('info')}<div>Vyberte v přehledu sálů, které sály obor v termínu uvolní.</div>`;
  const c=rqCalc(d,S.rqEdit),verb=st==='pending'?'Po schválení uvolní':'Požadavek uvolní';
  return `${ico('info')}<div>${c.n?`${verb} <b>${c.n} ${plural(c.n,'půlblok','půlbloky','půlbloků')}</b> (${fmtN(c.h,1)} h) na ${c.rooms} ${plural(c.rooms,'sále','sálech','sálech')} v ${c.days} ${plural(c.days,'dni','dnech','dnech')}.${st==='pending'?' Do schválení zůstává plán beze změny.':' Uvolněný čas můžete v časové ose přidělit jinému oboru.'}`:`${esc(specName(d.specId))} nemá v zadaném termínu${d.site==='all'?'':' na pracovišti '+SITE[d.site].name} žádné plánované bloky – požadavek nic neuvolní.`}</div>`;}
function blImpactHTML(){const d=S.blDraft;if(!d||!d.roomIds.length||!d.from||!d.to||d.from>d.to||diffD(d.from,d.to)>400)return '';
  let n=0,h=0;const dd=new Set();
  for(let x=d.from;x<=d.to;x=addD(x,1))for(const id of d.roomIds){const room=ROOM[id];if(!room||!room.active||room.kind==='pacu')continue;const r=R(room,x),H=hoursFor(room,x);
    if(r.block){if(r.block.id===S.blEdit&&r.lost){n+=r.lost;h+=r.lost*(tmin(H.to)-tmin(H.from))/120;dd.add(x);}continue;}
    r.h.forEach((hh,i)=>{if(!hh||hh.released)return;n++;h+=halfMin(H,i)/60;dd.add(x);});}
  return `${ico('info')}<div>${n?`Omezení zruší <b>${n} ${plural(n,'půlblok','půlbloky','půlbloků')}</b> (${fmtN(h,1)} h) plánované operativy v ${dd.size} ${plural(dd.size,'dni','dnech','dnech')}.`:'Vybrané místnosti nemají v zadaném termínu žádnou plánovanou operativu – omezení provoz nezmění.'}</div>`;}
function impactBox(o){const x=o==='rq'?rqImpactHTML():blImpactHTML();return `<div class="impact" id="${o}-impact"${x?'':' hidden'}>${x}</div>`;}
