function updImpact(o){if(o!=='rq'&&o!=='bl')return;const el=$('#'+o+'-impact');if(!el)return;const x=o==='rq'?rqImpactHTML():blImpactHTML();el.innerHTML=x;el.hidden=!x;}

/* =========================================================
   Sezónní šablony
   ========================================================= */
const fmtMD=md=>{const [m,d]=String(md||'').split('-').map(Number);return m&&d?`${d}. ${m}.`:'';};
function curSeason(){return S.tplEdit&&S.tplEdit!=='base'?(D.seasons||[]).find(x=>x.id===S.tplEdit)||null:null;}
function curTpl(){const se=curSeason();return se?se.template:D.template;}
function tplName(){const se=curSeason();return se?`Sezónní šablona ${se.name}`:'Týdenní šablona';}
function tplSel(){const ss=D.seasons||[];if(!ss.length)return '';const cur=curSeason()?S.tplEdit:'base';
  return `<div class="seg" role="group" aria-label="Upravovaná šablona">${[['base','Základní'],...ss.map(x=>[x.id,x.name])].map(([v,t])=>`<button data-act="tplEdit" data-v="${v}" class="${cur===v?'on':''}">${esc(t)}</button>`).join('')}</div>`;}
function seDays(se){return yearDays(S.year).filter(d=>{const di=DI(d);return !di.weekend&&!di.holiday&&inSeason(se,d);}).length;}
function seDiff(se){let n=0;new Set([...Object.keys(D.template),...Object.keys(se.template)]).forEach(id=>{for(let i=1;i<=5;i++)if(JSON.stringify((D.template[id]||{})[i]||null)!==JSON.stringify((se.template[id]||{})[i]||null))n++;});return n?`${n} ${plural(n,'rozdíl','rozdíly','rozdílů')} proti základní šabloně`:'zatím shodná se základní šablonou';}
function seasonCard(){const ss=D.seasons||[];
  return `<section class="card" id="se-card"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Sezónní šablony<span class="cnt">${ss.length}</span></div><button class="btn sm" data-act="seAdd">${ico('plus')}Přidat sezónní šablonu</button></div>
    <p class="hint" style="margin-bottom:12px">Sezónní šablona každý rok v zadaném období nahradí základní týdenní šablonu, třeba při letním omezeném provozu. Svátky, požadavky oborů a omezení sálů platí dál.</p>
    ${ss.length?`<div class="list">${ss.map(se=>`<div class="ver${S.tplEdit===se.id?' on':''}"><div>
      <div class="se-f"><input class="in" id="se-n-${se.id}" data-ch="seName" data-id="${se.id}" value="${esc(se.name)}" aria-label="Název sezóny">
        <div class="dr"><label class="flab" for="se-from-${se.id}">Od</label>${dateField('se-from-'+se.id,`${S.year}-${se.from}`,`data-se="from" data-id="${se.id}"`)}<label class="flab" for="se-to-${se.id}">Do</label>${dateField('se-to-'+se.id,`${S.year}-${se.to}`,`data-se="to" data-id="${se.id}"`)}</div>
        <label class="tog"><input type="checkbox" id="se-a-${se.id}" data-ch="seActive" data-id="${se.id}"${se.active?' checked':''}><span></span><em>${se.active?'aktivní':'neaktivní'}</em></label></div>
      <small>Každý rok ${fmtMD(se.from)}–${fmtMD(se.to)} · ${seDays(se)} pracovních dnů v roce ${S.year} · ${seDiff(se)}</small></div>
      <div class="act"><button class="btn sm${S.tplEdit===se.id?' pri':''}" data-act="seEdit" data-id="${se.id}">${S.tplEdit===se.id?'Upravujete':'Upravit šablonu'}</button><button class="btn sm${S.confirm==='seCopy:'+se.id?' warn':''}" data-act="seCopy" data-id="${se.id}">${S.confirm==='seCopy:'+se.id?'Přepsat šablonu?':'Kopírovat ze základní'}</button><button class="icon-btn sm${S.confirm==='seDel:'+se.id?' armed':''}" data-act="seDel" data-id="${se.id}" aria-label="Smazat sezónu">${S.confirm==='seDel:'+se.id?'Smazat?':ico('trash')}</button></div></div>`).join('')}</div>`:empty('Zatím žádná sezónní šablona.')}
  </section>`;}

/* =========================================================
   Přetahování bloků v časové ose
   ========================================================= */
let DRAG=null;
function slotAt(room,ds){const r=R(room,ds);if(r.block&&!(r.src==='override'&&(r.h[0]||r.h[1])))return null;if(!r.slot)return null;
  const [a,b]=r.h.map(h=>h&&!h.released?{s:h.s,an:h.an}:null);if(!a&&!b)return null;if(!r.slot.split&&a&&b)return {split:false,a};return {split:true,a,b};}
function setSlot(room,ds,slot){const k=room.id+'|'+ds;if(same(slot,baseSlot(room,ds)))delete D.overrides[k];else D.overrides[k]=slot;}
function dropSlot(sr,sd,tr,td,copy){if(!isCoord()){denied();return;}if(sr===tr&&sd===td)return;const a=ROOM[sr],b=ROOM[tr];if(!a||!b||b.kind==='pacu')return;
  const sa=slotAt(a,sd);if(!sa){toast('Na zdrojovém dni není co přesunout');return;}
  const rb=R(b,td);if(rb.block&&!(rb.src==='override'&&(rb.h[0]||rb.h[1]))){toast(`${b.name} je ${fmt(td)} mimo provoz (${rb.block.type.toLowerCase()})`);return;}
  const sb=slotAt(b,td),hasB=sb&&((sb.a)||(sb.split&&sb.b));setSlot(b,td,sa);let msg;
  if(copy)msg=`Zkopírováno: ${a.code} ${fmt(sd)} → ${b.code} ${fmt(td)}`;
  else if(hasB){setSlot(a,sd,sb);msg=`Vyměněno: ${a.code} ${fmt(sd)} ↔ ${b.code} ${fmt(td)}`;}
  else{setSlot(a,sd,null);msg=`Přesunuto: ${a.code} ${fmt(sd)} → ${b.code} ${fmt(td)}`;}
  commit(msg);}
document.addEventListener('dragstart',e=>{const c=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('#tl .c[draggable="true"]');if(!c)return;DRAG={r:c.dataset.r,d:c.dataset.d,over:null};
  try{e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed='copyMove';e.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain',c.dataset.r+'|'+c.dataset.d);}catch(x){}c.classList.add('dragging');tip.hidden=true;document.body.classList.add('is-drag');});
document.addEventListener('dragover',e=>{if(!DRAG)return;const c=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('#tl .c[data-r]');if(!c){if(DRAG.over){DRAG.over.classList.remove('drop');DRAG.over=null;}return;}
  const room=ROOM[c.dataset.r];if(!room||room.kind==='pacu')return;e.preventDefault();try{e.dataTransfer.dropEffect=e.altKey?'copy':'move';}catch(x){}
  if(DRAG.over!==c){if(DRAG.over)DRAG.over.classList.remove('drop');c.classList.add('drop');DRAG.over=c;}});
document.addEventListener('drop',e=>{if(!DRAG)return;const c=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('#tl .c[data-r]');if(!c)return;e.preventDefault();const d=DRAG;DRAG=null;document.body.classList.remove('is-drag');dropSlot(d.r,d.d,c.dataset.r,c.dataset.d,e.altKey||e.ctrlKey||e.metaKey);});
document.addEventListener('dragend',()=>{document.querySelectorAll('#tl .dragging,#tl .drop').forEach(x=>x.classList.remove('dragging','drop'));document.body.classList.remove('is-drag');DRAG=null;});

/* =========================================================
   Návrh řešení nedostatku personálu
   ========================================================= */
function halfNeed(site,ds){const n=D.staff.norm,res=[0,1].map(()=>({doc:0,nur:0,scr:0,items:[]}));
  for(const room of D.rooms){if(!room.active||room.site!==site)continue;const r=R(room,ds);
    if(room.kind==='pacu'){const st=room.staff||{};[0,1].forEach(i=>{if(r.open&&r.open[i]){res[i].doc+=+st.doc||0;res[i].nur+=+st.nurse||0;}});continue;}
    r.h.forEach((h,i)=>{if(!h||h.released)return;const q=n[h.an]||{};res[i].doc+=+q.doc||0;res[i].nur+=+q.nurse||0;res[i].scr+=+q.scrub||0;res[i].items.push({room,i,h});});}
  return res;}
function quotaRatio(site,spec){const r=quotaModel(site).rows.find(x=>x.s.id===spec);return r&&r.p!=null?r.p:null;}
function fixPlan(site,from,to){const ratio={};quotaModel(site).rows.forEach(r=>ratio[r.s.id]=r.p==null?100:r.p);const KN={doc:'doc',nur:'nurse',scr:'scrub'},days=[];
  for(let d=from;d<=to&&days.length<15;d=addD(d,1)){const v=SD(site,d);if(!v.def)continue;const cap={doc:v.capDoc,nur:v.capNur,scr:v.capScr},acts=[];let ok=true;
    halfNeed(site,d).forEach(x=>{const need={doc:x.doc,nur:x.nur,scr:x.scr},over=()=>['doc','nur','scr'].filter(k=>Math.ceil(need[k]-1e-9)>cap[k]);if(!over().length)return;
      const cands=x.items.slice().sort((a,b)=>(ratio[b.h.s]||100)-(ratio[a.h.s]||100)||((a.h.an==='LA')-(b.h.an==='LA')));
      for(const c of cands){const o=over();if(!o.length)break;const q=D.staff.norm[c.h.an]||{};if(!o.some(k=>(+q[KN[k]]||0)>0))continue;need.doc-=+q.doc||0;need.nur-=+q.nurse||0;need.scr-=+q.scrub||0;acts.push({...c,d});}
      if(over().length)ok=false;});
    days.push({d,acts,ok,v});}
  return days;}
function applyFix(acts){const mp=new Map();acts.forEach(a=>{const k=a.room.id+'|'+a.d;let sl=mp.has(k)?mp.get(k):slotAt(a.room,a.d);if(!sl)return;
    if(!sl.split)sl={split:true,a:sl.a?{...sl.a}:null,b:sl.a?{...sl.a}:null};if(a.i===0)sl.a=null;else sl.b=null;if(!sl.a&&!sl.b)sl=null;mp.set(k,sl);});
  mp.forEach((sl,k)=>{const [rid,d]=k.split('|');setSlot(ROOM[rid],d,sl);});return mp.size;}
function fixHTML(){const f=S.fix,plan=fixPlan(f.site,f.from,f.to),acts=plan.flatMap(d=>d.acts),RN={doc:'anesteziologové',nur:'anest. sestry',scr:'sálové sestry'};
  return `<div class="mo-scrim" data-act="fixClose"></div><div class="mo" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="fx-t" style="width:min(760px,100%)">
    <div class="mo-h"><div><div class="eyebrow"><i></i>Návrh řešení · ${SITE[f.site].name}</div><h2 id="fx-t">Nedostatek personálu ${fmtR(f.from,f.to)}</h2></div><button class="icon-btn" data-act="fixClose" aria-label="Zavřít">${ico('x')}</button></div>
    <p class="hint">Návrh ruší co nejméně půlbloků: jen ty, které chybějící profesi skutečně uvolní, a přednostně u oborů, které jsou v roce ${S.year} nad svou kvótou. Druhou možností je navýšit kapacitu v sekci Personál.</p>
    ${plan.length?plan.map(d=>{const v=d.v,miss=[['doc','capDoc'],['nur','capNur'],['scr','capScr']].filter(([k,ck])=>v[k]>v[ck]).map(([k,ck])=>`${RN[k]} ${v[k]-v[ck]}`).join(' · ');
      return `<div class="fx-d"><div class="fx-h"><b>${cap(DOW_L[isoDow(d.d)])} ${fmtY(d.d)}</b><span class="tag bad">chybí ${miss}</span>${DI(d.d).school?`<span class="tag sch">${esc(DI(d.d).school.short)}</span>`:''}</div>
        ${d.acts.length?`<div class="fx-l">${d.acts.map(a=>{const sp=SPEC[a.h.s]||{c:'#999',name:a.h.s},q=quotaRatio(f.site,a.h.s);return `<div class="fx-a"><span class="hp ${a.h.an}" style="--c:${sp.c}">${esc(a.h.s)}</span><span><b>${a.i===0?'Dopoledne':'Odpoledne'} · ${esc(a.room.name)}</b> <code>${esc(a.room.code)}</code><small>${esc(sp.name)} · ${a.h.an==='CA'?'celková':'lokální'} anestezie${q!=null?` · obor na ${fmtN(q)} % kvóty`:''}</small></span><em>zrušit</em></div>`;}).join('')}</div>`:''}
        ${d.ok?'':`<p class="warn-t">${ico('warn')}Ani zrušením všech vhodných bloků se nedostatek nepokryje – je potřeba navýšit kapacitu.</p>`}</div>`;}).join(''):empty('V tomto období už nedostatek personálu není.')}
    <div class="mo-f"><span class="hint">${acts.length?`Celkem ${acts.length} ${plural(acts.length,'půlblok','půlbloky','půlbloků')} ve ${plan.length} ${plural(plan.length,'dni','dnech','dnech')}. Změnu lze vrátit tlačítkem Zpět.`:''}</span><div class="btns"><button class="btn ghost" data-act="fixStaff">Upravit kapacitu</button>${acts.length?`<button class="btn pri" data-act="fixApply">${ico('check')}Zrušit navržené bloky</button>`:''}</div></div></div>`;}

/* =========================================================
   Klávesové zkratky
   ========================================================= */
function helpHTML(){const k=(...a)=>a.map(x=>`<kbd>${x}</kbd>`).join(' + ');
  const rows=[[`${k('Ctrl/⌘','K')} nebo ${k('/')}`,'Rychlé hledání sálů, oborů, skupin, dat a akcí'],[`${k('←')} ${k('→')}`,'Předchozí / další období v časové ose a v obsazenosti'],[k('['),'Sbalit nebo rozbalit postranní menu'],[k('Ctrl/⌘','Z'),'Vrátit poslední změnu'],[k('B'),'Nástěnka pro obrazovku u sálů'],[k('Esc'),'Zavřít okno, kalendář, editor nebo nástěnku'],[k('Enter'),'Potvrdit zapsané datum'],['přetažení bloku','Přesun bloku na jiný den nebo sál (měsíc, týden), na obsazený den výměna'],[`${k('Alt/⌥')} + přetažení`,'Kopie bloku'],[k('?'),'Tato nápověda']];
  return `<div class="mo-scrim" data-act="helpClose"></div><div class="mo" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="kb-t" style="width:min(620px,100%)">
    <div class="mo-h"><div><div class="eyebrow"><i></i>Nápověda</div><h2 id="kb-t">Klávesové zkratky</h2></div><button class="icon-btn" data-act="helpClose" aria-label="Zavřít">${ico('x')}</button></div>
    <div>${rows.map(([a,b])=>`<div class="kb-r"><span>${a}</span><span>${b}</span></div>`).join('')}</div></div>`;}

/* =========================================================
   Nástěnka pro obrazovku u sálů
   ========================================================= */
let BOARD=null;
function boardOpen(){const h=$('#board');if(BOARD)return;BOARD={i:0,rot:true,t0:Date.now()};closeDP();cmdClose();tip.hidden=true;h.hidden=false;document.body.style.overflow='hidden';boardRender(true);BOARD.tick=setInterval(boardTick,1000);}
function boardClose(){const h=$('#board');if(BOARD)clearInterval(BOARD.tick);BOARD=null;h.hidden=true;h.innerHTML='';document.body.style.overflow='';try{if(document.fullscreenElement)document.exitFullscreen().catch(()=>{});}catch(e){}}
const hms=d=>`${pad(d.getHours())}:${pad(d.getMinutes())}:${pad(d.getSeconds())}`;
function boardTick(){if(!BOARD)return;const now=new Date(),c=$('#bd-clock');if(c)c.textContent=hms(now);
  if(BOARD.rot&&Date.now()-BOARD.t0>=15000){BOARD.i=(BOARD.i+1)%SITES.length;BOARD.t0=Date.now();boardRender(true);return;}
  const pb=$('#bd-rot');if(pb)pb.style.width=`${BOARD.rot?Math.min(100,(Date.now()-BOARD.t0)/150):0}%`;
  if(BOARD.isT){const m=now.getHours()*60+now.getMinutes()+now.getSeconds()/60;document.querySelectorAll('#board [data-bdp]').forEach(el=>{const [a,b]=el.dataset.bdp.split('|').map(Number);el.style.width=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,(m-a)/(b-a)*100))+'%';});}
  if(now.getSeconds()===0)boardRender();}
function boardRender(anim){const h=$('#board');if(!BOARD)return;
  const s=SITES[BOARD.i],fd=focusDay(),isT=fd===today,now=new Date(),nm=now.getHours()*60+now.getMinutes();BOARD.isT=isT;
  const rooms=sortRooms(D.rooms.filter(r=>r.active&&r.site===s.id&&r.kind!=='pacu'));
  const cards=rooms.map(room=>{const r=R(room,fd),H=hoursFor(room,fd),planned=r.h[0]||r.h[1];let cls='',stt='',body='';
    if(r.block&&!(r.src==='override'&&planned)){cls='x';body=`<div class="bd-sp">${esc(r.block.type)}</div>`;stt='mimo provoz';}
    else if(!planned||r.h.every(x=>!x||x.released)){cls='off';body=`<div class="bd-sp muted">${planned?'Uvolněno – požadavek oboru':'Bez programu'}</div>`;stt='volno';}
    else{const [a,b]=r.h,split=r.slot&&r.slot.split,hv=(!split&&a&&b&&a.released===b.released)?[{h:a,f:H.from,t:H.to,l:'Celý den'}]:[{h:a,f:H.from,t:H.split,l:'Dopoledne'},{h:b,f:H.split,t:H.to,l:'Odpoledne'}];
      body=hv.map(x=>{const fa=tmin(x.f),ta=tmin(x.t),act=isT&&nm>=fa&&nm<ta,done=isT&&nm>=ta,prog=act?(nm-fa)/(ta-fa)*100:done?100:0;
        if(!x.h)return `<div class="bd-row off"><span>${x.l} ${fT(x.f)}–${fT(x.t)}</span><b>volno</b></div>`;
        const sp=SPEC[x.h.s]||{c:'#999',name:x.h.s};
        return `<div class="bd-row${act?' now':''}${done?' done':''}" style="--c:${sp.c}"><span>${x.l} ${fT(x.f)}–${fT(x.t)}</span><b>${x.h.released?'Uvolněno':esc(sp.name)}</b>${x.h.released?'':`<em>${x.h.an}</em>`}<i class="bd-p"><i style="width:${prog.toFixed(1)}%"${isT?` data-bdp="${fa}|${ta}"`:''}></i></i></div>`;}).join('');
      const f0=tmin(H.from),t1=tmin(H.to);stt=!isT?`začátek ${fT(H.from)}`:nm<f0?`začátek ${fT(H.from)}`:nm>=t1?'program ukončen':'probíhá';}
    return `<div class="bd-c ${cls}" style="--c:${roomColor(room)}"><div class="bd-ch"><code>${esc(room.code)}</code><b>${esc(room.name)}</b><span class="bd-st${stt==='probíhá'?' live':''}">${stt}</span></div>${body}</div>`;}).join('');
  const v=SD(s.id,fd),nx=upcoming(today,1)[0],di=DI(fd);
  h.innerHTML=`<div class="bd"><header class="bd-h"><div class="bd-brand"><div class="logo">RO</div><div><b>Operační program</b><span>Krajská nemocnice Liberec</span></div></div>
    <div class="bd-tabs" role="tablist">${SITES.map((x,i)=>`<button data-bd="site" data-i="${i}" class="${i===BOARD.i?'on':''}" role="tab" aria-selected="${i===BOARD.i}">${x.name}</button>`).join('')}</div>
    <div class="bd-time"><b id="bd-clock">${hms(now)}</b><span>${cap(DOW_L[isoDow(today)])} ${fmtY(today)}</span></div>
    <div class="bd-ctl"><button class="btn sm" data-bd="rot">${BOARD.rot?ico('pause')+'Zastavit střídání':ico('play')+'Střídat pracoviště'}</button><button class="btn sm" data-bd="fs">Celá obrazovka</button><button class="icon-btn" data-bd="close" aria-label="Zavřít nástěnku">${ico('x')}</button></div></header>
    <div class="bd-rotbar"><i id="bd-rot" style="width:0%"></i></div>
    <div class="bd-sub"><h1>${s.name}</h1><span>${isT?'Dnešní operační program':'Program na '+DOW_L[isoDow(fd)]+' '+fmt(fd)}${di.school?' · '+esc(di.school.name):''}${seasonFor(fd)?' · '+esc(seasonFor(fd).name):''}</span></div>
    <div class="bd-grid${anim&&!RM()?' fade':''}">${cards||'<p class="hint">Na pracovišti nejsou sály.</p>'}</div>
    <footer class="bd-f">${ROLES.map(([k,ck,lab])=>`<div><span>${lab}</span><b class="${v[k]>v[ck]?'bad':''}">${v[k]} / ${v[ck]}</b></div>`).join('')}${nx?`<div><span>Nejbližší ${nx.kind==='hol'?'svátek':'prázdniny'}</span><b>${nx.from===nx.to?fmt(nx.from):fmt(nx.from)+'–'+fmt(nx.to)} ${esc(nx.label)}</b></div>`:''}</footer></div>`;}
{const bd=$('#board');bd.addEventListener('click',e=>{e.stopPropagation();const b=e.target.closest('[data-bd]');if(!b)return;const a=b.dataset.bd;
  if(a==='close')boardClose();
  else if(a==='site'){BOARD.i=+b.dataset.i;BOARD.t0=Date.now();boardRender(true);}
  else if(a==='rot'){BOARD.rot=!BOARD.rot;BOARD.t0=Date.now();boardRender();}
  else if(a==='fs'){try{const p=bd.requestFullscreen&&bd.requestFullscreen();if(p&&p.catch)p.catch(()=>toast('Celou obrazovku prohlížeč nepovolil – použijte F11'));}catch(x){toast('Celou obrazovku prohlížeč nepovolil – použijte F11');}}});}

/* =========================================================
   Operační sály 3D – plán nemocnice, trasy a transport
   ========================================================= */
const MCELL=2;
const MK={
  or:{n:'Operační sál',c:'#6FA8C7',def:[5,9]},
  pacu:{n:'Dospávací pokoj',c:'#5FB8A6',def:[8,7]},
  ward:{n:'Lůžková stanice',c:'#B3A3D3',def:[12,9]},
  icu:{n:'JIP / ARO',c:'#D98B8B',def:[10,8]},
  er:{n:'Urgentní příjem',c:'#E3A55A',def:[10,8]},
  staff:{n:'Zázemí personálu',c:'#97A7B2',def:[6,6]},
  hall:{n:'Chodba',c:'#D5DFE3',def:[12,2]},
  elev:{n:'Výtah',c:'#F2C14E',def:[3,3]},
  stair:{n:'Schodiště',c:'#B4C0C6',def:[3,4]},
  other:{n:'Místnost',c:'#C7D0D4',def:[6,6]}};
const MP_MODES=[['overview','Stav sálů'],['flow','Tok pacientů'],['route','Transport'],['staff','Personál'],['cmp','Areál']];
const MP_DESC={cmp:'Budovy areálu, heliporty a spojovací chodby. Vyberte převoz sanitkou nebo z heliportu a přehrajte ho – čas zahrnuje naložení, jízdu nebo chůzi chodbou a předání.',flow:'Animované šipky ukazují, odkud na sály míří pacienti podle plánu vybraného dne a kam pokračují na dospávací pokoje.',route:'Vyberte stanici a sál. Aplikace najde nejrychlejší cestu chodbami a výtahy a přehraje převoz lůžka.',staff:'Kolik personálu potřebuje každý sál podle normativu a odkud se na sály přesouvá ze zázemí.',overview:'Živý stav sálů podle plánu operativy: příprava, výkon, úklid a volno. Posuňte čas na liště v modelu nebo spusťte průběh celého dne.'};
function mapSeed(site){const it=[];let n=0;const add=(k,lv,x,y,w,h,o={})=>it.push({id:`m${site}${++n}`,k,lv,x,y,w,h,name:o.name||MK[k].n,...o});
  const base=(F,top)=>{F.forEach(f=>{add('hall',f.lv,1,13,46,2,{name:'Hlavní chodba'});add('stair',f.lv,25,9,3,4,{name:'Schodiště'});});add('elev',0,22,9,3,4,{name:'Výtah A',lv1:top});};
  if(site==='LB'){const F=[{lv:0,name:'1. NP · Příjem a diagnostika'},{lv:1,name:'2. NP · Lůžka chirurgických oborů'},{lv:2,name:'3. NP · Operační trakt PCHO'},{lv:3,name:'4. NP · COS NO a oční'},{lv:4,name:'5. NP · Lůžka ortopedie, neurochirurgie, urologie a gynekologie'}];
    base(F,4);add('elev',0,44,9,3,4,{name:'Výtah B – lůžkový',lv1:4});
    add('er',0,2,3,14,10,{name:'Urgentní příjem',beds:8});add('or',0,28,3,8,10,{room:'E1'});add('or',0,36,3,7,10,{room:'E2'});add('other',0,2,15,12,10,{name:'Hlavní vstup a recepce'});add('staff',0,16,15,8,10,{name:'Šatny a zázemí personálu'});add('other',0,28,15,18,10,{name:'Radiodiagnostika'});
    add('ward',1,2,3,18,10,{name:'Lůžková stanice Chirurgie',specs:['CHI','CEV'],beds:28});add('ward',1,28,3,15,10,{name:'Lůžková stanice Traumatologie',specs:['TRA'],beds:24});add('icu',1,2,15,14,10,{name:'JIP chirurgická',beds:10});add('ward',1,26,15,20,10,{name:'Lůžková stanice ORL, čelistní a plastická',specs:['ORL','MFC','PLA'],beds:26});
    ['P1','P2','P3','P4'].forEach((r,i)=>add('or',2,2+i*5,3,5,10,{room:r}));['P5','P6','P7'].forEach((r,i)=>add('or',2,28+i*5,3,5,10,{room:r}));
    add('pacu',2,24,15,14,10,{room:'DP1',beds:8});add('other',2,2,15,12,10,{name:'Sterilizace a přípravna'});add('staff',2,15,15,8,10,{name:'Zázemí personálu PCHO'});
    ['NO1','NO2','NO3'].forEach((r,i)=>add('or',3,2+i*6,3,6,10,{room:r}));add('pacu',3,2,15,12,10,{room:'DP2',beds:5});add('staff',3,15,15,7,9,{name:'Zázemí personálu COS NO'});add('or',3,28,3,6,10,{room:'OC1'});add('ward',3,34,3,9,10,{name:'Lůžková stanice Oční',specs:['OCN'],beds:14});add('icu',3,28,15,18,10,{name:'ARO',beds:12});
    add('ward',4,2,3,18,10,{name:'Lůžková stanice Ortopedie',specs:['ORT'],beds:30});add('ward',4,28,3,15,10,{name:'Lůžková stanice Neurochirurgie',specs:['NCH'],beds:20});add('ward',4,2,15,18,10,{name:'Lůžková stanice Urologie',specs:['URO'],beds:24});add('ward',4,26,15,20,10,{name:'Lůžková stanice Gynekologie',specs:['GYN'],beds:26});
    return {W:48,H:28,floors:F,items:it};}
  if(site==='TU'){const F=[{lv:0,name:'1. NP · Lůžková oddělení'},{lv:1,name:'2. NP · COS Turnov'}];base(F,1);
    add('ward',0,2,3,18,10,{name:'Lůžková stanice Chirurgie',specs:['CHI'],beds:24});add('ward',0,28,3,18,10,{name:'Lůžková stanice Ortopedie a urologie',specs:['ORT','URO'],beds:20});add('ward',0,2,15,16,10,{name:'Lůžková stanice Gynekologie',specs:['GYN'],beds:18});add('er',0,20,15,12,10,{name:'Příjem',beds:4});add('staff',0,34,15,10,8,{name:'Zázemí personálu'});
    ['T1','T2','T3'].forEach((r,i)=>add('or',1,2+i*6,3,6,10,{room:r}));add('pacu',1,2,15,12,9,{room:'DPT',beds:3});add('staff',1,15,15,7,8,{name:'Zázemí personálu COS'});add('icu',1,28,3,16,10,{name:'JIP',beds:8});
    return {W:48,H:28,floors:F,items:it};}
  if(site==='FR'){const F=[{lv:0,name:'1. NP · Lůžková oddělení'},{lv:1,name:'2. NP · COS Frýdlant'}];base(F,1);
    add('ward',0,2,3,18,10,{name:'Lůžková stanice Chirurgie',specs:['CHI'],beds:22});add('ward',0,28,3,16,10,{name:'Lůžková stanice Ortopedie',specs:['ORT'],beds:16});add('er',0,2,15,12,9,{name:'Příjem',beds:3});add('staff',0,16,15,8,8,{name:'Zázemí personálu'});
    add('or',1,2,3,7,10,{room:'F1'});add('or',1,9,3,6,10,{room:'F2'});add('pacu',1,2,15,10,8,{room:'DPF',beds:2});add('staff',1,14,15,7,7,{name:'Zázemí personálu COS'});
    return {W:48,H:28,floors:F,items:it};}
  const F=[{lv:0,name:'1. NP'}];add('hall',0,1,13,46,2,{name:'Hlavní chodba'});return {W:48,H:28,floors:F,items:it};}
function mapOf(site){if(!D.map||typeof D.map!=='object')D.map={};if(!D.map[site])D.map[site]=mapSeed(site);const M=D.map[site];
  if(!M.pos){M.pos={x:30,y:40};if(!M.blds)Object.assign(M,mpCampusSeed(site));}if(!M.bname)M.bname=site==='LB'?'Pavilon A – chirurgické obory':'Hlavní budova';if(site==='LB'&&!M._knl){M._knl=1;if(M.bname==='Hlavní budova')M.bname='Pavilon A – chirurgické obory';(M.blds||[]).forEach(b=>{if(b.id==='bLBG'&&b.name==='Pavilon G – interní obory')b.name='Pavilon B – interní obory';});(M.amb||[]).forEach(r=>{if(r.id==='aLB1'&&r.name==='Pavilon G → urgentní příjem')r.name='Pavilon B → urgentní příjem';});}['blds','amb','heli','links'].forEach(k=>{if(!Array.isArray(M[k]))M[k]=[];});if(!M.campus)M.campus={W:150,H:100};return M;}
const onFloor=(it,lv)=>it.k==='elev'?lv>=it.lv&&lv<=(it.lv1??it.lv):it.lv===lv;
const mpFloors=M=>[...M.floors].sort((a,b)=>a.lv-b.lv);
function mpFloorIdx(M){const o={};mpFloors(M).forEach((f,i)=>o[f.lv]=i);return o;}
const mpFloorName=(M,lv)=>{const f=M.floors.find(x=>x.lv===lv);return f?f.name.split(' · ')[0]:`${lv+1}. NP`;};
const mpItem=(M,id)=>M.items.find(i=>i.id===id)||null;
const mpCenter=it=>[it.x+it.w/2,it.y+it.h/2];
const clampN=(v,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,v));
function mpTitle(it,short){if((it.k==='or'||it.k==='pacu')&&it.room&&ROOM[it.room]){const r=ROOM[it.room];return short?r.code:`${r.code} · ${r.name}`;}const n=it.name||MK[it.k].n;return short?n.replace('Lůžková stanice ','LS ').replace('Zázemí personálu','Zázemí'):n;}
function mpSpecOn(it,d){if(it.k!=='or'||!it.room||!ROOM[it.room])return null;const r=R(ROOM[it.room],d);const h=r.h.find(x=>x&&!x.released);return h?h.s:null;}
function mpColor(it,d){if(it.k==='or'){const s=d?mpSpecOn(it,d):null;return s&&SPEC[s]?SPEC[s].c:'#9DA9AF';}if(it.k==='ward'&&it.specs&&it.specs[0]&&SPEC[it.specs[0]])return SPEC[it.specs[0]].c;return MK[it.k]?MK[it.k].c:'#C7D0D4';}
function mpWalk(M,lv){const g=new Uint8Array(M.W*M.H);for(const it of M.items){if(!onFloor(it,lv)||(it.k!=='hall'&&it.k!=='elev'))continue;for(let y=Math.max(0,it.y);y<Math.min(M.H,it.y+it.h);y++)for(let x=Math.max(0,it.x);x<Math.min(M.W,it.x+it.w);x++)g[y*M.W+x]=1;}return g;}
function mpBFS(M,lv,A,B){const W=M.W,H=M.H,g=mpWalk(M,lv),inR=(r,x,y)=>x>=r.x&&x<r.x+r.w&&y>=r.y&&y<r.y+r.h,prev=new Int32Array(W*H).fill(-2),q=new Int32Array(W*H);let qh=0,qt=0;
  for(let y=Math.max(0,A.y);y<Math.min(H,A.y+A.h);y++)for(let x=Math.max(0,A.x);x<Math.min(W,A.x+A.w);x++){const i=y*W+x;prev[i]=-1;q[qt++]=i;}
  const N=[[1,0],[-1,0],[0,1],[0,-1]];
  while(qh<qt){const c=q[qh++],x=c%W,y=(c-x)/W;if(inR(B,x,y)&&!inR(A,x,y)){const p=[];let k=c;while(k>=0){p.push([k%W,(k-k%W)/W]);k=prev[k];}p.reverse();let s=0;while(s<p.length-1&&inR(A,p[s+1][0],p[s+1][1]))s++;return p.slice(s);}
    for(const [dx,dy] of N){const nx=x+dx,ny=y+dy;if(nx<0||ny<0||nx>=W||ny>=H)continue;const ni=ny*W+nx;if(prev[ni]!==-2)continue;if(!(g[ni]||inR(B,nx,ny)))continue;prev[ni]=c;q[qt++]=ni;}}
  return null;}
function mpSimplify(P){if(P.length<3)return P;const o=[P[0]];for(let i=1;i<P.length-1;i++){const a=o[o.length-1],b=P[i],c=P[i+1];if((a[0]===b[0]&&b[0]===c[0])||(a[1]===b[1]&&b[1]===c[1]))continue;o.push(b);}o.push(P[P.length-1]);return o;}
function mpLeg(M,lv,A,B){const p=mpBFS(M,lv,A,B);let pts,nc=false;
  if(p)pts=[mpCenter(A),...p.map(([x,y])=>[x+.5,y+.5]),mpCenter(B)];else{nc=true;const [ax,ay]=mpCenter(A),[bx,by]=mpCenter(B);pts=[[ax,ay],[bx,ay],[bx,by]];}
  pts=mpSimplify(pts);let d=0;for(let i=1;i<pts.length;i++)d+=Math.abs(pts[i][0]-pts[i-1][0])+Math.abs(pts[i][1]-pts[i-1][1]);return {lv,pts,d:d*MCELL,nc};}
function mpRoute(M,a,b,opt){const C=D.mapCfg,sp=(opt&&opt.speed)||C.speed,ho=opt&&opt.handover!=null?opt.handover:C.handover,key=`mpr|${S.mp.site}|${a.id}|${b.id}|${sp}|${ho}`;if(KC.has(key))return KC.get(key);
  const fi=mpFloorIdx(M);let res;
  if(a.lv===b.lv&&a.k!=='elev'||a.k==='elev'&&onFloor(a,b.lv)||b.k==='elev'&&onFloor(b,a.lv)){const lv=a.k==='elev'?b.lv:a.lv,L=mpLeg(M,lv,a,b);res={legs:[L],dist:L.d,floors:0,elev:null,nc:L.nc,parts:{walk:L.d/sp,wait:0,ride:0,walk2:0,handover:ho}};}
  else{let best=null;for(const e of M.items){if(e.k!=='elev'||!onFloor(e,a.lv)||!onFloor(e,b.lv))continue;const A=mpLeg(M,a.lv,a,e),B=mpLeg(M,b.lv,e,b),fl=Math.abs((fi[a.lv]||0)-(fi[b.lv]||0)),t=(A.d+B.d)/sp+C.wait+fl*C.floor+(A.nc||B.nc?90:0);if(!best||t<best.t)best={t,A,B,e,fl};}
    if(best)res={legs:[best.A,best.B],dist:best.A.d+best.B.d,floors:best.fl,elev:best.e,nc:best.A.nc||best.B.nc,parts:{walk:best.A.d/sp,wait:C.wait,ride:best.fl*C.floor,walk2:best.B.d/sp,handover:ho}};
    else{const L=mpLeg(M,b.lv,{...a,lv:b.lv},b);res={legs:[L],dist:L.d,floors:Math.abs((fi[a.lv]||0)-(fi[b.lv]||0)),elev:null,nc:true,noElev:true,parts:{walk:L.d/sp,wait:0,ride:0,walk2:0,handover:ho}};}}
  const P=res.parts;res.t=P.walk+P.wait+P.ride+P.walk2+P.handover;KC.set(key,res);return res;}
function mpNear(M,from,kind,filter){let best=null;for(const it of M.items){if(it.k!==kind||it===from||(filter&&!filter(it)))continue;const r=mpRoute(M,it,from);if(!best||r.t<best.t)best={it,t:r.t};}return best?best.it:null;}
function mpNearWard(M,spec,or){const key=`mpnw|${S.mp.site}|${spec}|${or.id}`;if(KC.has(key))return KC.get(key);const w=mpNear(M,or,'ward',x=>(x.specs||[]).includes(spec));KC.set(key,w);return w;}
function mpPacuOf(M,or){const room=ROOM[or.room];const g=room&&gOf(room);return M.items.find(p=>p.k==='pacu'&&p.room&&ROOM[p.room]&&g&&gOf(ROOM[p.room])===g)||mpNear(M,or,'pacu');}
function mpOverlap(M,n,selfId){const out=[];for(const it of M.items){if(it.id===selfId)continue;const sameFl=n.k==='elev'||it.k==='elev'?(Math.max(n.lv,it.lv)<=Math.min(n.k==='elev'?(n.lv1??n.lv):n.lv,it.k==='elev'?(it.lv1??it.lv):it.lv)):it.lv===n.lv;if(!sameFl)continue;if(n.x<it.x+it.w&&n.x+n.w>it.x&&n.y<it.y+it.h&&n.y+n.h>it.y)out.push(it);}return out;}
const fmtMin=s=>{const m=Math.floor(s/60),x=Math.round(s%60);return `${m}:${pad(x>=60?59:x)}`;};
function mpInit(){const o=S.mp;if(!SITE[o.site])o.site='LB';if(o.tab==='map'&&o.site!=='LB')o.tab='3d';if(!mpBlds(o.site).some(b=>b.id===o.bld))o.bld=MP_MAIN;const M=mapCur(),XF=mpXfers(o.site);if(!XF.some(x=>x.id===o.cx))o.cx=XF[0]?XF[0].id:null;if(!MP_MODES.some(x=>x[0]===o.mode))o.mode='overview';let fd=focusDay();if(+fd.slice(0,4)!==S.year)fd=firstWork(S.year);if(!o.day||+o.day.slice(0,4)!==S.year)o.day=fd;
  if(!mpItem(M,o.from)){const w=M.items.find(i=>i.k==='ward'&&(i.specs||[]).includes('ORT'))||M.items.find(i=>i.k==='ward')||M.items.find(i=>i.k==='er');o.from=w?w.id:null;}
  if(!mpItem(M,o.to)){const t=M.items.find(i=>i.k==='or'&&i.room==='NO1')||M.items.find(i=>i.k==='or');o.to=t?t.id:null;}
  if(o.sel&&!mpItem(M,o.sel))o.sel=null;if(!o.reg)o.reg='std';
  if(o.floor==null){const c={};M.items.forEach(i=>{if(i.k==='or')c[i.lv]=(c[i.lv]||0)+1;});const b=Object.keys(c).sort((x,y)=>c[y]-c[x]||x-y)[0];o.floor=b!=null&&M.floors.length>1?String(b):'all';}
  if(o.floor!=='all'&&!M.floors.some(f=>f.lv===+o.floor))o.floor='all';
  if(o.t==null){const H=D.hours,t0=tmin(H.from),t1=tmin(H.to),n=m3Now();if(o.day===today&&n>=t0-30&&n<=t1){o.t=n;o.live=true;}else{o.t=t0+160;o.live=false;}}
  if(o.live&&o.day!==today)o.live=false;
  if(o.emFrom&&!mpItem(M,o.emFrom))o.emFrom=null;if(!o.emFrom){const e=M.items.find(i=>i.k==='er')||M.items.find(i=>i.k==='ward');o.emFrom=e?e.id:null;}if(o.emTo&&!mpItem(M,o.emTo))o.emTo=null;
  return o;}
function mpStats(site){const cur=site===S.mp.site,key='mps|'+site+'|'+(cur?mpCurId():MP_MAIN);if(KC.has(key))return KC.get(key);const M=cur?mapCur():mapOf(site),rooms=D.rooms.filter(r=>r.active&&r.site===site&&r.kind!=='pacu'),ors=M.items.filter(i=>i.k==='or'&&i.room&&ROOM[i.room]),placed=new Set(mpBlds(site).flatMap(b=>b.M.items).filter(i=>i.k==='or'&&i.room).map(i=>i.room));
  let sum=0,n=0,max=null;for(const it of ors){const t=D.template[it.room]||{};for(let i=1;i<=5;i++)for(const [sl,w] of tplVars(t[i])){if(!sl)continue;(sl.split?[sl.a,sl.b]:[sl.a,sl.a]).forEach(h=>{if(!h)return;const wd=mpNearWard(M,h.s,it);if(!wd)return;const r=mpRoute(M,wd,it);sum+=r.t*w;n+=w;if(!max||r.t>max.t)max={t:r.t,from:mpTitle(wd,true),to:mpTitle(it,true)};});}}
  const v={rooms:rooms.length,placed:rooms.filter(r=>placed.has(r.id)).length,missing:rooms.filter(r=>!placed.has(r.id)).map(r=>r.code),avg:n?sum/n:0,max,elev:M.items.filter(i=>i.k==='elev').length,wards:M.items.filter(i=>i.k==='ward').length};KC.set(key,v);return v;}
function mpFlows(M){const d=S.mp.day,out=new Map();
  for(const it of M.items){if(it.k!=='or'||!it.room||!ROOM[it.room])continue;const r=R(ROOM[it.room],d),sp=new Map();r.h.forEach(h=>{if(h&&!h.released)sp.set(h.s,(sp.get(h.s)||0)+1);});
    sp.forEach((n,s)=>{const w=mpNearWard(M,s,it);if(w)out.set(w.id+'>'+it.id,{a:w,b:it,c:(SPEC[s]||{}).c||'#888',n,s});});
    if(sp.size){const pc=mpPacuOf(M,it);if(pc)out.set(it.id+'>'+pc.id,{a:it,b:pc,c:'#33B79F',n:1,pacu:true});}}
  return [...out.values()];}
function mpStaffFlows(M){const d=S.mp.day,hi=S.mp.half==='pm'?1:0,N=D.staff.norm,out=[];
  for(const it of M.items){if(it.k!=='or'||!it.room||!ROOM[it.room])continue;const h=R(ROOM[it.room],d).h[hi];if(!h||h.released)continue;const q=N[h.an]||{},need={doc:+q.doc||0,nur:+q.nurse||0,scr:+q.scrub||0},sum=need.doc+need.nur+need.scr;
    const st=mpNear(M,it,'staff',x=>x.lv===it.lv)||mpNear(M,it,'staff');out.push({or:it,st,need,sum,h});}
  return out;}

/* ---------- stav sálů v čase ---------- */
const MST={free:{n:'Volný',c:'#25D07C'},op:{n:'Probíhá operace',c:'#3D8DFF'},prep:{n:'Příprava',c:'#FF9B30'},clean:{n:'Úklid',c:'#27D6EA'},emerg:{n:'Emergency',c:'#FF3C55'},off:{n:'Mimo provoz',c:'#5A6A85'}};
const MST_ORD=['free','op','prep','clean','emerg'];
const MP_REG=[['std','Standardní režim'],['emerg','Emergency'],['hyg','Hygiena']];
const hm=m=>{m=Math.max(0,Math.floor(m));return `${Math.floor(m/60)%24}:${pad(m%60)}`;};
function m3Now(){const n=new Date();return n.getHours()*60+n.getMinutes()+n.getSeconds()/60;}
function mpRnd(s){let h=2166136261;for(let i=0;i<s.length;i++)h=Math.imul(h^s.charCodeAt(i),16777619);h^=h>>>13;h=Math.imul(h,1274126177);return ((h^h>>>16)>>>0)/4294967296;}
function mpSched(it,d){const key=`mpsc|${it.id}|${it.room||''}|${d}`;if(KC.has(key))return KC.get(key);const room=it.room&&ROOM[it.room],o={segs:[],t0:0,t1:0,why:''};
  if(!room){o.why='nepropojeno s plánem';KC.set(key,o);return o;}
  const r=R(room,d),H=hoursFor(room,d),t0=tmin(H.from),tm=tmin(H.split),t1=tmin(H.to);Object.assign(o,{t0,t1,H});
  if(r.block&&!r.h.some(Boolean)){o.block=r.block;o.why=r.block.type;KC.set(key,o);return o;}
  if(!r.h.some(Boolean)&&(r.di.weekend||r.di.holiday)){o.closed=true;o.why=r.di.holiday?'státní svátek':'víkend';KC.set(key,o);return o;}
  const bl=[];[[t0,tm],[tm,t1]].forEach(([a,b],i)=>{const h=r.h[i],l=bl[bl.length-1];if(h&&!h.released&&l&&l.h&&!l.h.released&&l.h.s===h.s&&l.h.an===h.an)l.b=b;else bl.push({a,b,h});});
  for(const B of bl){if(!B.h||B.h.released){o.segs.push({a:B.a,b:B.b,k:'free',rel:B.h&&B.h.released?B.h.s:null});continue;}
    const s=B.h.s,an=B.h.an,ca=an!=='LA',P=ca?25:15,C=ca?20:12,MIN=20;let t=B.a,n=0;
    while(B.b-t>=P+C+MIN){const q=mpRnd(`${room.id}|${d}|${B.a}|${n}`);let du=Math.round(ca?65+q*85:30+q*45);const rest=B.b-(t+P+du+C);if(rest<0||rest<P+C+MIN)du=B.b-t-P-C;
      o.segs.push({a:t,b:t+P,k:'prep',s,an,n},{a:t+P,b:t+P+du,k:'op',s,an,n},{a:t+P+du,b:t+P+du+C,k:'clean',s,an,n});t+=P+du+C;n++;}
    if(t<B.b)o.segs.push({a:t,b:B.b,k:'free'});}
  KC.set(key,o);return o;}
function mpStat(it,d,t){const sc=mpSched(it,d);
  if(!sc.H)return {k:'off',sub:sc.why};
  if(sc.block)return {k:'off',sub:sc.block.type,blk:sc.block};
  if(sc.closed)return {k:'off',sub:sc.why};
  if(t<sc.t0)return {k:'off',sub:`provoz od ${hm(sc.t0)}`};
  if(t>=sc.t1)return {k:'off',sub:'po pracovní době'};
  const i=sc.segs.findIndex(x=>t>=x.a&&t<x.b),g=sc.segs[i]||{k:'free',a:t,b:sc.t1},nx=sc.segs.slice(i+1).find(x=>x.k!==g.k);let sub='';
  if(g.k==='op')sub=`${g.s} · ${g.an} · do ${hm(g.b)}`;else if(g.k==='prep')sub=`${g.s} · začátek ${hm(g.b)}`;else if(g.k==='clean')sub=`hotovo ${hm(g.b)}`;else sub=g.rel?`uvolněno · ${g.rel}`:nx&&nx.k==='prep'?`do ${hm(g.b)}`:'bez programu';
  return {...g,sub,i};}
function mpFreeIn(it,d,t){const st=mpStat(it,d,t);if(st.k==='free')return 0;if(st.k==='off')return st.blk||!it.room?Infinity:20;const sc=mpSched(it,d);if(st.k==='clean')return st.b-t;let j=st.i;while(j<sc.segs.length&&sc.segs[j].k!=='clean')j++;return (sc.segs[j]?sc.segs[j].b:st.b)-t;}
function mpEmerg(M,d,t){const o=S.mp,from=mpItem(M,o.emFrom)||M.items.find(i=>i.k==='er')||M.items.find(i=>i.k==='ward');if(!from)return null;
  const list=M.items.filter(i=>i.k==='or'&&i.room&&ROOM[i.room]).map(it=>{const w=mpFreeIn(it,d,t),r=mpRoute(M,from,it),tr=r.t/60;return {it,w,r,tr,ready:Math.max(tr,w),st:mpStat(it,d,t)};}).filter(x=>isFinite(x.w)).sort((a,b)=>a.ready-b.ready||a.tr-b.tr);
  const pick=(o.emTo&&list.find(x=>x.it.id===o.emTo))||list[0]||null;return {from,list,pick};}
function mpAutoFloor(a,b){if(!a||!b)return;const M=mapCur();if(M.floors.length<2)return;S.mp.floor=a.lv===b.lv&&a.k!=='elev'&&b.k!=='elev'?String(a.lv):'all';}
function mpEmView(){const o=S.mp,M=mapCur();if(o.reg!=='emerg')return;const e=mpEmerg(M,o.day,o.t);if(e&&e.pick)mpAutoFloor(e.from,e.pick.it);}
function mpRouteView(){const o=S.mp,M=mapCur();if(o.reg==='std'&&o.mode==='route')mpAutoFloor(mpItem(M,o.from),mpItem(M,o.to));}
function mpStatAll(M){const o=S.mp,d=o.day,t=o.t,res={};
  for(const it of M.items){if(it.k!=='or')continue;let st=mpStat(it,d,t);if(o.reg==='hyg'&&st.blk&&/asan/i.test(st.blk.type))st={...st,k:'clean',sub:'asanace · celý den'};res[it.id]=st;}
  if(o.reg==='emerg'){const e=mpEmerg(M,d,t);if(e&&e.pick){const p=res[e.pick.it.id];res[e.pick.it.id]={...p,k:'emerg',prev:p,sub:e.pick.w>.5?`připraven za ${Math.ceil(e.pick.w)} min`:'připraven pro akutní výkon'};}res._em=e;}
  return res;}
function mpTRange(M){const rs=M.items.filter(i=>i.k==='or'&&i.room&&ROOM[i.room]).map(i=>hoursFor(ROOM[i.room],S.mp.day));const a=rs.length?Math.min(...rs.map(h=>tmin(h.from))):tmin(D.hours.from),b=rs.length?Math.max(...rs.map(h=>tmin(h.to))):tmin(D.hours.to);return [Math.max(0,Math.floor((a-60)/30)*30),Math.min(1439,Math.ceil((b+60)/30)*30)];}

function m3Script(src){return new Promise((res,rej)=>{const s=document.createElement('script');s.src=src;s.onload=()=>res();s.onerror=()=>rej(new Error(src));document.head.appendChild(s);});}
function m3Load(){if(window.THREE&&M3.libs)return Promise.resolve();if(M3.loading)return M3.loading;
  M3.loading=(window.THREE?Promise.resolve():m3Script('https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/three.js/r128/three.min.js'))
    .then(()=>['shaders/CopyShader.js','shaders/LuminosityHighPassShader.js','postprocessing/EffectComposer.js','postprocessing/RenderPass.js','postprocessing/ShaderPass.js','postprocessing/UnrealBloomPass.js'].reduce((p,f)=>p.then(()=>m3Script(M3_LIB+f)),Promise.resolve()).catch(()=>{}))
    .then(()=>{M3.libs=true;},e=>{M3.loading=null;throw e;});
  return M3.loading;}
function m3Init(){if(M3.renderer)return true;if(M3.fail)return false;const T=THREE;let r;
  try{r=new T.WebGLRenderer({antialias:true,powerPreference:'high-performance'});}catch(e){M3.fail=true;return false;}
  M3.pr=Math.min(1.6,window.devicePixelRatio||1);r.setPixelRatio(M3.pr);r.shadowMap.enabled=true;r.shadowMap.type=T.PCFSoftShadowMap;r.setClearColor(0x050920,1);M3.renderer=r;
  const w=document.createElement('div');w.className='m3-wrap';w.appendChild(r.domElement);const lb=document.createElement('div');lb.className='m3-lbl';w.appendChild(lb);const hud=document.createElement('div');hud.className='m3-hud';hud.hidden=true;w.appendChild(hud);M3.wrap=w;M3.lbl=lb;M3.hud=hud;
  M3.camera=new T.PerspectiveCamera(30,1,.5,1600);M3.v3=new T.Vector3();M3.ray=new T.Raycaster();M3.orb={r:70,th:.5,ph:.95,tx:0,ty:6,tz:0};M3.goal={...M3.orb};M3.col={};
  if(T.EffectComposer&&T.RenderPass&&T.UnrealBloomPass&&T.ShaderPass&&T.CopyShader&&T.LuminosityHighPassShader){try{let rt;if(r.capabilities.isWebGL2&&T.WebGLMultisampleRenderTarget){rt=new T.WebGLMultisampleRenderTarget(8,8,{format:T.RGBAFormat});}
      const c=new T.EffectComposer(r,rt);c.setPixelRatio(M3.pr);M3.rp=new T.RenderPass(new T.Scene(),M3.camera);c.addPass(M3.rp);M3.bloom=new T.UnrealBloomPass(new T.Vector2(256,256),.9,.62,.5);c.addPass(M3.bloom);M3.comp=c;}catch(e){M3.comp=null;}}
  const c=r.domElement;c.style.touchAction='none';c.addEventListener('pointerdown',m3Down);c.addEventListener('pointermove',m3Move);c.addEventListener('pointerup',m3Up);c.addEventListener('pointercancel',m3Up);c.addEventListener('pointerleave',()=>{m3Tip(null);c.style.cursor='';});c.addEventListener('wheel',m3Wheel,{passive:false});c.addEventListener('contextmenu',e=>e.preventDefault());
  m3Theme();return true;}
function m3Mount(){const host=$('#m3-host');if(!host){m3Stop();return;}
  if(!window.THREE||!M3.libs){m3Load().then(()=>{if(S.view==='map'&&S.mp.tab==='3d')m3Mount();}).catch(()=>{const h=$('#m3-host');if(h)h.innerHTML='<div class="m3-load">3D knihovnu se nepodařilo načíst. Zkontrolujte připojení k internetu a obnovte stránku.</div>';});return;}
  if(!m3Init()){host.innerHTML='<div class="m3-load">3D zobrazení není v tomto prohlížeči dostupné (vyžaduje WebGL).</div>';return;}
  host.innerHTML='';host.appendChild(M3.wrap);M3.host=host;const bot=document.querySelector('.m3-bot');if(bot)bot.prepend(M3.hud);document.documentElement.classList.toggle('m3full',!!S.mp.full);if(M3.ro)M3.ro.disconnect();M3.ro=new ResizeObserver(()=>m3Size());M3.ro.observe(host);m3Size();m3Sync();
  if(!M3.raf){M3.lt=0;M3.raf=requestAnimationFrame(m3Loop);}}
function m3Stop(){document.documentElement.classList.remove('m3full');if(M3.raf){cancelAnimationFrame(M3.raf);M3.raf=0;}if(M3.ro){M3.ro.disconnect();M3.ro=null;}}
function m3Size(){if(!M3.host||!M3.renderer)return;const w=M3.host.clientWidth,h=M3.host.clientHeight;if(!w||!h)return;M3.w=w;M3.h=h;M3.renderer.setSize(w,h);if(M3.comp)M3.comp.setSize(w,h);M3.camera.aspect=w/h;M3.camera.updateProjectionMatrix();}
function m3Dispose(obj){if(!obj)return;const K=M3.G?M3.G.keep:new Set();obj.traverse(o=>{if(o.geometry&&!K.has(o.geometry))o.geometry.dispose();const m=o.material;if(m)(Array.isArray(m)?m:[m]).forEach(x=>{if(K.has(x))return;if(x.map&&!K.has(x.map))x.map.dispose();x.dispose();});});}

function m3Inst(g,geo,mat,list,shadow=true){if(!list.length)return null;const T=THREE,d=new T.Object3D(),im=new T.InstancedMesh(geo,mat,list.length);list.forEach((p,i)=>{d.position.set(p[0],p[1],p[2]);d.rotation.set(0,p[6]||0,0);d.scale.set(p[3]??1,p[4]??1,p[5]??1);d.updateMatrix();im.setMatrixAt(i,d.matrix);});im.castShadow=shadow;im.receiveShadow=true;g.add(im);return im;}
function m3Text(txt,h,op=.6,col='#d8ecff'){const T=THREE,f=88,c=document.createElement('canvas'),x=c.getContext('2d'),font=`600 ${f}px ${M3.G.FONT}`;x.font=font;const sp=f*.16,tw=[...txt].reduce((s,ch)=>s+x.measureText(ch).width+sp,0);c.width=Math.ceil(tw+f*.8);c.height=Math.ceil(f*1.5);
  if(M3.light&&col==='#d8ecff')col='#4b5b66';x.font=font;x.textBaseline='middle';x.fillStyle=col;x.shadowColor=M3.light?'rgba(255,255,255,0)':'rgba(70,170,255,.9)';x.shadowBlur=M3.light?0:f*.25;let px=f*.4;for(const ch of txt){x.fillText(ch,px,c.height/2);px+=x.measureText(ch).width+sp;}
  const t=new T.CanvasTexture(c);t.anisotropy=8;const m=new T.Mesh(M3.G.geo.plane,new T.MeshBasicMaterial({map:t,transparent:true,opacity:op,depthWrite:false}));m.scale.set(h*c.width/c.height,h,1);return m;}
function m3Sync(){m3Theme();const o=S.mp,M=mapCur();
  const bkey=[o.site,JSON.stringify(M),o.floor,o.ex,DV].join('|');if(bkey!==M3.bkey){M3.bkey=bkey;m3Base(M);M3.okey='';M3.lkey='';}
  const st=mpStatAll(M);M3.st=st;for(const id in M3.rooms)m3Paint(M3.rooms[id],st[id]||{k:'off',sub:''});
  const sig=o.reg==='emerg'?(st._em&&st._em.pick?`${st._em.pick.it.id}|${st._em.from.id}`:''):o.reg==='hyg'?Object.keys(st).filter(id=>id[0]!=='_'&&(st[id].k==='clean'||st[id].k==='prep')).join(','):'';
  const okey=[o.mode,o.reg,o.day,o.half,o.from,o.to,o.floor,o.ex,DV,sig,o.mode==='cmp'?o.cx+'|'+o.bld:''].join('|');if(okey!==M3.okey){M3.okey=okey;m3Ovl(M);}
  const cm=o.mode==='cmp'&&o.reg==='std',fk=o.site+'|'+o.bld+'|'+o.floor+'|'+cm;if(M3.fitKey!==fk){const first=!M3.fitKey;M3.fitKey=fk;m3Cam(cm?'cmp':'iso',first);}
  m3SelMark(M);m3Dom(M,st);}
function m3Base(M){const T=THREE,o=S.mp,G=M3.G,m=G.mat,gm=G.geo;if(M3.scene)m3Dispose(M3.scene);
  const LT=M3.light,sc=new T.Scene();sc.background=G.tex.bg;sc.fog=new T.FogExp2(LT?0xdce5e8:0x050920,LT?.0026:.0034);
  sc.add(new T.HemisphereLight(LT?0xffffff:0x87a5d8,LT?0xa9b8c0:0x04070d,LT?.78:.62));sc.add(new T.AmbientLight(LT?0xffffff:0x1c2d4c,LT?.34:.42));
  const key=new T.DirectionalLight(LT?0xffffff:0xd6e4ff,LT?.82:.58);key.position.set(-55,110,75);key.castShadow=true;const sh=key.shadow;sh.mapSize.set(2048,2048);Object.assign(sh.camera,{left:-46,right:46,top:46,bottom:-46,near:20,far:300});sh.bias=-.0005;sh.normalBias=.02;sc.add(key);
  const rim=new T.DirectionalLight(LT?0xcfe2ee:0x3f6fff,LT?.3:.4);rim.position.set(80,45,-90);sc.add(rim);
  const W=M.W,H=M.H,fl=mpFloors(M),fi=mpFloorIdx(M),FH=M3.FH*o.ex,fIdx=o.floor==='all'?null:fi[+o.floor];
  const gr=new T.Mesh(gm.plane,new T.MeshStandardMaterial({color:LT?0xd2dce1:0x050a14,roughness:.92,metalness:.1}));gr.scale.set(1600,1600,1);gr.rotation.x=-Math.PI/2;gr.position.y=-.42;gr.receiveShadow=true;sc.add(gr);
  const dt=G.tex.dots.clone();dt.needsUpdate=true;dt.wrapS=dt.wrapT=T.RepeatWrapping;dt.repeat.set(400,400);const dots=new T.Mesh(gm.plane,new T.MeshBasicMaterial({map:dt,transparent:true,opacity:LT?.22:.32,depthWrite:false}));dots.scale.set(1600,1600,1);dots.rotation.x=-Math.PI/2;dots.position.y=-.41;sc.add(dots);
  const ug=new T.Mesh(gm.plane,new T.MeshBasicMaterial({map:G.tex.radial,color:LT?0xffffff:0x1d58b0,transparent:true,opacity:LT?.75:.5,blending:m3B(),depthWrite:false}));ug.scale.set(W*2.6,H*3.4,1);ug.rotation.x=-Math.PI/2;ug.position.y=-.4;sc.add(ug);
  M3.scene=sc;if(M3.rp)M3.rp.scene=sc;M3.base=new T.Group();sc.add(M3.base);M3.selG=null;M3.labels=[];M3.lbl.innerHTML='';M3.pick=[];M3.rooms={};M3.elevs=[];
  const tile=G.tex.tile.clone();tile.needsUpdate=true;tile.wrapS=tile.wrapT=T.RepeatWrapping;tile.repeat.set((W+1)*1.5,(H+1)*1.5);const fm=m.floor.clone();fm.map=tile;if(fIdx==null){fm.transparent=true;fm.opacity=.72;fm.depthWrite=false;}
  fl.forEach(f=>{const idx=fi[f.lv];if(fIdx!=null&&idx>fIdx)return;const ghost=fIdx!=null&&idx<fIdx,g=new T.Group();g.position.y=idx*FH;M3.base.add(g);
    const slab=new T.Mesh(gm.box,ghost?m.slabG:fIdx==null?m.slabT:m.slab);slab.scale.set(W+1.4,.32,H+1.4);slab.position.y=-.17;slab.receiveShadow=!ghost;g.add(slab);
    const se=new T.LineSegments(gm.boxE,ghost?m.slabEG:m.slabE);se.scale.copy(slab.scale);se.position.copy(slab.position);g.add(se);
    const [fa,fb]=f.name.split(' · ');m3Label(`<b>${esc(fa)}</b>${fb?`<span>${esc(fb)}</span>`:''}`,new T.Vector3(-W/2-.9,idx*FH+.05,H/2+.9),'m3f'+(ghost?' gh':''),{lv:f.lv,kind:'floor'});
    if(ghost){const p=[];M.items.forEach(it=>{if(it.k==='elev'||it.k==='hall'||it.lv!==f.lv)return;const x0=it.x-W/2+.12,x1=it.x+it.w-W/2-.12,z0=it.y-H/2+.12,z1=it.y+it.h-H/2-.12,y=.04;p.push(x0,y,z0,x1,y,z0,x1,y,z0,x1,y,z1,x1,y,z1,x0,y,z1,x0,y,z1,x0,y,z0);});const geo=new T.BufferGeometry();geo.setAttribute('position',new T.Float32BufferAttribute(p,3));g.add(new T.LineSegments(geo,m.ghostE));return;}
    const fd=new T.Mesh(gm.plane,fm);fd.scale.set(W+1,H+1,1);fd.rotation.x=-Math.PI/2;fd.position.y=.004;fd.receiveShadow=true;g.add(fd);
    const walk=mpWalk(M,f.lv),posts=[],orFl=M.items.some(i=>i.k==='or'&&i.lv===f.lv);
    for(const it of M.items){if(it.k==='elev'||it.lv!==f.lv)continue;m3Item(g,it,M,walk,idx*FH,posts,orFl);}
    M.items.filter(i=>i.k==='elev'&&onFloor(i,f.lv)).forEach(e=>m3FloorTag(g,e,M,walk,orFl?(/lůžk/i.test(e.name)?'PŘÍJEM PACIENTA':e.name.toUpperCase()):e.name.toUpperCase()));
    m3Inst(g,gm.box,m.post,posts.map(([x,z,h])=>[x,h/2+.01,z,.06,h,.06]),false);
    M.items.filter(i=>i.k==='or'&&i.lv===f.lv&&M3.rooms[i.id]).sort((a,b)=>a.x-b.x||a.y-b.y).forEach((i,n)=>{if(n%2)M3.rooms[i.id].L.el.classList.add('alt');});});
  const topI=fIdx!=null?fIdx:fl.length-1;
  for(const it of M.items.filter(i=>i.k==='elev')){const i0=fi[it.lv],i1=Math.min(fi[it.lv1??it.lv]??i0,topI);if(i0==null||i0>topI)continue;const cx=it.x+it.w/2-W/2,cz=it.y+it.h/2-H/2,y0=i0*FH-.02,y1=i1*FH+1.75,hh=y1-y0;
    const s=new T.Mesh(gm.box,m.shaft);s.scale.set(it.w-.3,hh,it.h-.3);s.position.set(cx,y0+hh/2,cz);s.renderOrder=3;M3.base.add(s);const sl=new T.LineSegments(gm.boxE,m.shaftE);sl.scale.copy(s.scale);sl.position.copy(s.position);M3.base.add(sl);
    for(let i=i0;i<=i1;i++){const r=new T.LineSegments(gm.boxE,m.stopE);r.scale.set(it.w-.22,.02,it.h-.22);r.position.set(cx,i*FH+.05,cz);M3.base.add(r);}
    const cab=new T.Group(),cb=new T.Mesh(gm.box,m.cabin);cb.scale.set(it.w-.85,1.1,it.h-.85);cb.position.y=.58;cb.castShadow=true;cb.userData.id=it.id;cab.add(cb);const ce=new T.LineSegments(gm.boxE,m.cabE);ce.scale.copy(cb.scale);ce.position.copy(cb.position);cab.add(ce);const lt=new T.Mesh(gm.box,m.cabL);lt.scale.set(it.w-.78,.035,it.h-.78);lt.position.y=1.15;cab.add(lt);
    cab.position.set(cx,y0+.06,cz);M3.base.add(cab);M3.pick.push(cb);const ys=[];for(let i=i0;i<=i1;i++)ys.push(i*FH+.06);M3.elevs.push({it,cab,ys,y:ys[0],tgt:ys[0],hold:Math.random()*3,busy:false});
    m3Label(`<b>${esc(it.name)}</b><span>${esc(mpFloorName(M,it.lv))}–${esc(mpFloorName(M,it.lv1??it.lv))}</span>`,new T.Vector3(cx,y1+.35,cz),'m3e',{kind:'elev',id:it.id});}
  m3Campus(M);}
function mpDoorInfo(it,M,walk){const W=M.W,H=M.H,hallAt=(x,y)=>x>=0&&y>=0&&x<W&&y<H&&!!walk[y*W+x]&&!(x>=it.x&&x<it.x+it.w&&y>=it.y&&y<it.y+it.h);
  const sd={n:Array.from({length:it.w},(_,k)=>hallAt(it.x+k,it.y-1)),s:Array.from({length:it.w},(_,k)=>hallAt(it.x+k,it.y+it.h)),w:Array.from({length:it.h},(_,k)=>hallAt(it.x-1,it.y+k)),e:Array.from({length:it.h},(_,k)=>hallAt(it.x+it.w,it.y+k))};
  const o={main:null};let best=0;for(const k in sd){const c=sd[k],i=c.findIndex(Boolean);if(i<0){o[k]=null;continue;}const j=c.length-1-[...c].reverse().findIndex(Boolean),n=c.filter(Boolean).length;o[k]=(i+j+1)/2;if(n>best){best=n;o.main=k;}}return o;}
function m3FloorTag(g,it,M,walk,txt){const di=mpDoorInfo(it,M,walk),k=di.main;if(!k||!txt)return;const W=M.W,H=M.H,v=di[k];let x,z;
  if(k==='s'){x=it.x+v;z=it.y+it.h+1;}else if(k==='n'){x=it.x+v;z=it.y-1;}else if(k==='e'){x=it.x+it.w+1;z=it.y+v;}else{x=it.x-1;z=it.y+v;}
  const t=m3Text(txt,.46,.6);t.rotation.x=-Math.PI/2;if(k==='e'||k==='w')t.rotation.z=Math.PI/2;t.position.set(x-W/2,.03,z-H/2);t.renderOrder=4;g.add(t);}
function m3Hall(g,it,cx,cz){const T=THREE,G=M3.G,m=G.mat,gm=G.geo,hz=it.w>=it.h;
  const f=new T.Mesh(gm.plane,m.hall);f.scale.set(it.w,it.h,1);f.rotation.x=-Math.PI/2;f.position.set(cx,.012,cz);f.receiveShadow=true;g.add(f);
  const L=(hz?it.w:it.h)-.3;[-1,1].forEach(s=>{const b=new T.Mesh(gm.box,m.stripH);if(hz){b.scale.set(L,.02,.04);b.position.set(cx,.03,cz+s*(it.h/2-.06));}else{b.scale.set(.04,.02,L);b.position.set(cx+s*(it.w/2-.06),.03,cz);}g.add(b);});
  const n=Math.max(1,Math.floor(L/2.6)),list=[];for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const u=-((n-1)*2.6)/2+i*2.6;list.push(hz?[cx+u,1.66,cz,.95,.02,.09]:[cx,1.66,cz+u,.09,.02,.95]);}m3Inst(g,gm.box,m.ceil,list,false);}
function m3Item(g,it,M,walk,yB,posts,orFl){const T=THREE,G=M3.G,m=G.mat,gm=G.geo,W=M.W,H=M.H,cx=it.x+it.w/2-W/2,cz=it.y+it.h/2-H/2;
  if(it.k==='hall'){m3Hall(g,it,cx,cz);return;}
  const isOR=it.k==='or',wh=isOR?1.5:it.k==='stair'?.6:1.2,th=.05,di=mpDoorInfo(it,M,walk),spc=it.k==='ward'&&it.specs&&SPEC[it.specs[0]]?SPEC[it.specs[0]].c:null,bc=spc||MK[it.k].c;
  const tint=new T.Mesh(gm.plane,new T.MeshBasicMaterial({color:new T.Color(bc),transparent:true,opacity:isOR?.1:it.k==='ward'?.07:.045,blending:m3B(),depthWrite:false}));tint.scale.set(it.w-.1,it.h-.1,1);tint.rotation.x=-Math.PI/2;tint.position.set(cx,.02,cz);tint.userData.id=it.id;g.add(tint);M3.pick.push(tint);
  const glow=new T.Mesh(gm.plane,new T.MeshBasicMaterial({map:G.tex.radial,color:new T.Color(isOR?bc:spc||'#6f9bd6'),transparent:true,opacity:isOR?.5:it.k==='ward'?.14:.1,blending:m3B(),depthWrite:false}));glow.scale.set(it.w*1.05,it.h*1.05,1);glow.rotation.x=-Math.PI/2;glow.position.set(cx,.026,cz);g.add(glow);
  const frame=isOR?new T.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0x7fffc0,transparent:true,opacity:1}):m.frameN,doors=[];
  const wall=(len,horiz,x,z,door)=>{const seg=(a,b)=>{const L=b-a;if(L<=.05)return;const p=new T.Mesh(gm.box,m.glass);p.scale.set(horiz?L:th,wh,horiz?th:L);p.position.set(horiz?x-len/2+(a+b)/2:x,wh/2+.02,horiz?z:z-len/2+(a+b)/2);p.renderOrder=2;g.add(p);
      const n=Math.max(1,Math.round(L/2.4));for(let i=0;i<=n;i++){const u=a+L*i/n;posts.push(horiz?[x-len/2+u,z,wh]:[x,z-len/2+u,wh]);}};
    if(door==null){seg(0,len);return;}const dw=Math.min(1.5,len/3);seg(0,door-dw/2);seg(door+dw/2,len);doors.push({x:horiz?x-len/2+door:x,z:horiz?z:z-len/2+door,dw,horiz});};
  wall(it.w,true,cx,cz-it.h/2+th/2,di.n);wall(it.w,true,cx,cz+it.h/2-th/2,di.s);wall(it.h,false,cx-it.w/2+th/2,cz,di.w);wall(it.h,false,cx+it.w/2-th/2,cz,di.e);
  doors.forEach(d=>{[-1,1].forEach(s=>{const p=new T.Mesh(gm.box,frame);p.scale.set(.06,wh,.06);p.position.set(d.horiz?d.x+s*d.dw/2:d.x,wh/2+.02,d.horiz?d.z:d.z+s*d.dw/2);g.add(p);});const hd=new T.Mesh(gm.box,frame);hd.scale.set(d.horiz?d.dw:.06,.05,d.horiz?.06:d.dw);hd.position.set(d.x,wh-.02,d.z);g.add(hd);});
  const rect=(y,ins,mat)=>{const x0=cx-it.w/2+ins,x1=cx+it.w/2-ins,z0=cz-it.h/2+ins,z1=cz+it.h/2-ins,l=new T.Line(new T.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints([[x0,z0],[x1,z0],[x1,z1],[x0,z1],[x0,z0]].map(([a,b])=>new T.Vector3(a,y,b))),mat);g.add(l);return l;};
  const top=rect(wh+.03,th/2,isOR?new T.LineBasicMaterial({color:0x7fffc0,transparent:true,opacity:1}):m.topN),bot=isOR?rect(.035,.1,new T.LineBasicMaterial({color:0x7fffc0,transparent:true,opacity:.9})):spc?rect(.035,.1,new T.LineBasicMaterial({color:new T.Color(spc),transparent:true,opacity:.55})):null;
  const B=(mat,w,h,d,x,y,z,ry,cs=true)=>{const s=new T.Mesh(gm.box,mat);s.scale.set(w,h,d);s.position.set(x,y,z);if(ry)s.rotation.y=ry;s.castShadow=cs;s.receiveShadow=true;g.add(s);return s;};
  if(isOR){const vol=new T.Mesh(gm.box,G.vol());vol.scale.set(it.w-.16,wh,it.h-.16);vol.position.set(cx,wh/2+.02,cz);vol.renderOrder=5;g.add(vol);
    const swm=new T.MeshBasicMaterial({map:G.tex.radial,color:M3.light?0x1aa3b8:0x8ff6ff,transparent:true,opacity:M3.light?.6:.9,blending:m3B(),depthWrite:false}),sw=new T.Mesh(gm.plane,swm),alongZ=it.h>=it.w;sw.scale.set(alongZ?it.w-.3:.5,alongZ?.5:it.h-.3,1);sw.rotation.x=-Math.PI/2;sw.position.set(cx,.05,cz);sw.visible=false;g.add(sw);
    const side=di.main||'s',A=side==='n'||side==='s'?it.w:it.h,Dp=side==='n'||side==='s'?it.h:it.w,rm={it,code:it.room&&ROOM[it.room]?ROOM[it.room].code:mpTitle(it,true),k:'',ph:Math.random()*6.28,tint,glow,vol,top,bot,frame,lamps:[],scr:[],sw,swA:alongZ?'z':'x',swR:(alongZ?it.h:it.w)/2-.5,c0:alongZ?cz:cx,glowO:.5,volO:.3};
    const cl=new T.Group(),cf=m3Fig(m.scrubG,1.05);cl.add(cf);const ct=new T.Mesh(gm.box,m.cart);ct.scale.set(.32,.4,.24);ct.position.set(.32,.2,0);ct.castShadow=true;cl.add(ct);cl.position.set(cx,0,cz);cl.visible=false;g.add(cl);rm.cl=cl;
    const kit=m3OrKit(rm,A,Dp);kit.position.set(cx,0,cz);kit.rotation.y={s:0,n:Math.PI,e:Math.PI/2,w:-Math.PI/2}[side];g.add(kit);
    rm.L=m3Label('',new T.Vector3(cx,yB+wh+.3,cz),'m3o',{lv:it.lv,kind:'or',id:it.id});M3.rooms[it.id]=rm;return;}
  if(['ward','icu','pacu','er'].includes(it.k)){m3Beds(g,it,cx,cz,it.k!=='ward');const dw=Math.min(2.4,it.w/3);B(m.desk,dw,.5,.62,cx,.26,cz);B(m.strip2,dw,.02,.02,cx,.52,cz+.31,0,false);const sc=new T.Mesh(gm.plane,m.scrEcg);sc.scale.set(.42,.26,1);sc.position.set(cx,.72,cz-.12);sc.rotation.x=-.35;g.add(sc);}
  if(it.k==='staff'){const n=Math.max(2,Math.floor((it.w-1)/.62));m3Inst(g,gm.box,m.cab,Array.from({length:n},(_,i)=>[cx-it.w/2+.7+i*.6,.56,cz-it.h/2+.38,.54,1.08,.4]));m3Inst(g,gm.box,m.strip,Array.from({length:n},(_,i)=>[cx-it.w/2+.7+i*.6,1.11,cz-it.h/2+.6,.48,.02,.02]),false);
    const tb=new T.Mesh(gm.cyl,m.desk);tb.scale.set(1.5,.05,1.5);tb.position.set(cx,.6,cz+.6);tb.castShadow=true;g.add(tb);m3Inst(g,gm.box,m.chair,[0,1,2,3].map(i=>[cx+Math.cos(i*Math.PI/2)*1.05,.3,cz+.6+Math.sin(i*Math.PI/2)*1.05,.36,.42,.36]));}
  if(it.k==='stair'){m3Inst(g,gm.box,m.metalD,Array.from({length:7},(_,i)=>[cx,.06+i*.075,cz-it.h/2+.5+i*.45,it.w-.5,.12+i*.15,.45]).map(p=>{p[1]=p[4]/2;return p;}));m3Inst(g,gm.box,m.strip2,Array.from({length:7},(_,i)=>[cx,.13+i*.15,cz-it.h/2+.28+i*.45,it.w-.55,.015,.02]),false);}
  if(it.k==='other'){const nm=it.name||'';
    if(/steril/i.test(nm)){for(let i=0;i<3;i++){const x=cx-it.w/2+1.2+i*1.3;B(m.metal,1.0,1.0,.8,x,.5,cz-it.h/2+.6);B(m.led,.5,.04,.02,x,.88,cz-it.h/2+1.01,0,false);}m3Inst(g,gm.box,m.tray,[[cx+1,.62,cz+1,2.6,.04,.7],[cx-1.5,.62,cz+1.5,1.8,.04,.7]]);}
    else if(/radio|rtg|ct\b/i.test(nm)){const ring=new T.Mesh(gm.torus,m.ct);ring.position.set(cx-it.w/4,.98,cz);ring.rotation.y=Math.PI/2;ring.castShadow=true;g.add(ring);B(m.ct,1.9,.12,.5,cx-it.w/4+.9,.62,cz,Math.PI/2*0);B(m.desk,2.4,.5,.6,cx+it.w/4,.26,cz-it.h/2+.6);const s1=new T.Mesh(gm.plane,m.scrWall);s1.scale.set(.8,.45,1);s1.position.set(cx+it.w/4,.82,cz-it.h/2+.45);g.add(s1);}
    else if(/recep|vstup/i.test(nm)){B(m.desk,4,.55,.7,cx,.28,cz-it.h/2+1.6);B(m.strip,4,.02,.02,cx,.56,cz-it.h/2+1.96,0,false);m3Inst(g,gm.box,m.chair,Array.from({length:8},(_,i)=>[cx-2.8+(i%4)*1.1,.22,cz+1+Math.floor(i/4)*1.3,.7,.36,.5]));}
    else m3Inst(g,gm.box,m.cab,Array.from({length:Math.max(2,Math.floor(it.w/1.2))},(_,i)=>[cx-it.w/2+.8+i*1.1,.5,cz-it.h/2+.4,.9,1,.4]));}
  if(['pacu','other','staff','er','icu'].includes(it.k)&&orFl||it.k==='er'){const t=it.k==='pacu'?'ODCHOD NA DOSPÁVÁNÍ':it.k==='er'?'PŘÍJEM PACIENTA':/steril/i.test(it.name||'')?'CENTRÁLNÍ STERILIZACE':it.k==='staff'?'ZÁZEMÍ PERSONÁLU':it.k==='icu'?(it.name||'').toUpperCase():'';if(t)m3FloorTag(g,it,M,walk,t);}
  m3Label(`<b>${esc(mpTitle(it,true))}</b>${it.k==='ward'?`<span>${esc((it.specs||[]).join(' · '))}</span>`:it.beds?`<span>${it.beds} lůžek</span>`:''}`,new T.Vector3(cx,yB+wh+.25,cz),'m3i k-'+it.k,{lv:it.lv,kind:'room',id:it.id});}
function m3Beds(g,it,cx,cz,mon){const G=M3.G,m=G.mat,gm=G.geo,per=Math.max(1,Math.floor((it.w-1)/1.3)),rows=it.h>=6?2:1,want=Math.min(it.beds||per*rows,per*rows),F=[],Ma=[],Bl=[],Pi=[],Po=[],Sc=[];
  for(let i=0;i<want;i++){const r=i%rows,c=Math.floor(i/rows),x=cx-it.w/2+.95+c*1.3,s=r===0?1:-1,z=r===0?cz-it.h/2+.92:cz+it.h/2-.92;
    F.push([x,.2,z,.52,.16,1.04]);Ma.push([x,.31,z,.46,.07,.98]);Bl.push([x,.36,z+s*.13,.5,.03,.62]);Pi.push([x,.37,z-s*.36,.34,.06,.17]);
    if(mon){Po.push([x+.38,.55,z-s*.38,.03,1.1,.03]);Sc.push([x+.38,1.12,z-s*.34,.28,.19,1,s>0?0:Math.PI]);}}
  m3Inst(g,gm.box,m.bedF,F);m3Inst(g,gm.box,m.mattr,Ma);m3Inst(g,gm.box,m.blanket,Bl,false);m3Inst(g,gm.box,m.pillow,Pi,false);if(mon){m3Inst(g,gm.box,m.metal,Po,false);m3Inst(g,gm.plane,m.scrEcg,Sc,false);}}
function m3Fig(mat,s=1){const T=THREE,G=M3.G,g=new T.Group(),b=new T.Mesh(G.geo.cyl8,mat);b.scale.set(.22*s,.6*s,.2*s);b.position.y=.3*s;b.castShadow=true;g.add(b);const h=new T.Mesh(G.geo.sph,G.mat.skin);h.scale.setScalar(.17*s);h.position.y=.7*s;h.castShadow=true;g.add(h);const c=new T.Mesh(G.geo.sph,G.mat.cap);c.scale.set(.18*s,.09*s,.18*s);c.position.y=.76*s;g.add(c);return g;}

    const hg=new T.Group();hg.position.set(b.x,y,b.z);hg.rotation.set(tl*.35,0,tl);k.add(hg);const dm=new T.Mesh(gm.dome,m.lampBody);dm.scale.setScalar(s);dm.castShadow=true;hg.add(dm);const rg=new T.Mesh(gm.ring,m.lampOff);rg.scale.setScalar(s);rg.rotation.x=Math.PI/2;rg.position.y=-.05;hg.add(rg);const dc=new T.Mesh(gm.circ,m.lampOff);dc.scale.setScalar(.26*s);dc.rotation.x=Math.PI/2;dc.position.y=-.06;hg.add(dc);rm.lamps.push(rg,dc);});
  const ax=-.66*s,az=tz-.86*s;B(m.metalD,.46*s,.72,.38*s,ax,.42,az);B(m.metal,.52*s,.04,.44*s,ax,.8,az);B(m.dark,.36*s,.3,.28*s,ax,.97,az-.04);C(m.vapP,.045*s,.17,ax+.14*s,.9,az+.15*s,1);C(m.vapY,.045*s,.17,ax+.02*s,.9,az+.15*s,1);

  return k;}
function m3Paint(rm,st){const T=THREE,G=M3.G,o=S.mp,k=st.k||'off',c=M3.col[k]||(M3.col[k]=new T.Color(MST[k].c)),dim=o.reg==='emerg'?(k==='emerg'?1:.34):o.reg==='hyg'?(k==='clean'?1:.34):1;
  rm.st=st;if(rm.k!==k||rm.dim!==dim){rm.k=k;rm.dim=dim;const lc=c.clone().lerp(M3.white,.28);
    rm.tint.material.color.copy(c);rm.tint.material.opacity=(k==='off'?.04:.16)*dim;rm.glow.material.color.copy(c);rm.glowO=(k==='off'?.12:k==='free'?.5:.72)*dim*(M3.light?.62:1);rm.glow.material.opacity=rm.glowO;
    rm.vol.material.uniforms.uC.value.copy(c);rm.volO=(k==='off'?.07:k==='free'?.28:.42)*dim*(M3.light?.5:1);rm.vol.material.uniforms.uO.value=rm.volO;
    rm.top.material.color.copy(lc);rm.top.material.opacity=k==='off'?.4:Math.max(.5,dim);rm.bot.material.color.copy(lc);rm.bot.material.opacity=(k==='off'?.3:.9)*Math.max(.5,dim);rm.frame.color.copy(lc);rm.frame.opacity=k==='off'?.45:Math.max(.55,dim);
    const lit=k==='op'||k==='emerg',pre=k==='prep';rm.lamps.forEach(l=>l.material=lit?G.mat.lampOn:pre?G.mat.lampDim:G.mat.lampOff);rm.pool.visible=lit;rm.pat.visible=lit||pre;rm.scr.forEach(x=>x.m.material=k==='off'||k==='free'&&x.on!==G.mat.scrWall?G.mat.scrOff:x.on);rm.sw.visible=k==='clean';rm.cl.visible=k==='clean';rm.crew.visible=lit||pre;rm.crew.children.forEach((f,i)=>f.visible=lit||i===3);}
  const html=`<b><span>SÁL </span>${esc(rm.code)}</b><em style="--c:${MST[k].c}"><i></i>${MST[k].n}</em>${st.sub?`<small>${esc(st.sub)}</small>`:''}${st.b!=null&&st.a!=null&&['op','prep','clean'].includes(st.k)?`<u style="--c:${MST[k].c}"><i style="width:${clampN((S.mp.t-st.a)/Math.max(1,st.b-st.a)*100,0,100).toFixed(0)}%"></i></u>`:''}`;if(rm.L.h!==html){rm.L.h=html;rm.L.el.firstChild.innerHTML=html;}
  rm.L.el.classList.toggle('em',k==='emerg');}
function m3Anim(t){for(const id in M3.rooms){const r=M3.rooms[id],k=r.k;let f=1;if(k==='emerg')f=.5+.8*Math.abs(Math.sin(t*3.2));else if(k==='prep')f=.86+.18*Math.sin(t*2.2+r.ph);else if(k==='op')f=.95+.06*Math.sin(t*1.2+r.ph);else if(k==='clean')f=.9+.12*Math.sin(t*3+r.ph);
  r.vol.material.uniforms.uO.value=r.volO*f;r.glow.material.opacity=r.glowO*(.82+.18*f);if(k==='emerg'){r.top.material.opacity=.45+.55*Math.abs(Math.sin(t*3.2));r.frame.opacity=r.top.material.opacity;}
  if(r.sw.visible){const u=Math.sin(t*.9+r.ph)*r.swR;if(r.swA==='z'){r.sw.position.z=r.c0+u;r.cl.position.z=r.c0+u-.35;}else{r.sw.position.x=r.c0+u;r.cl.position.x=r.c0+u-.35;}}
  if(r.crew.visible)r.crew.children.forEach(f=>{f.rotation.y=Math.sin(t*.8+f.userData.ph)*.35;f.position.y=Math.abs(Math.sin(t*1.6+f.userData.ph))*.012;});
  const hv=M3.hov===id;if(r.hv!==hv){r.hv=hv;r.top.material.opacity=hv?1:r.k==='off'?.4:Math.max(.5,r.dim);r.tint.material.opacity=(r.k==='off'?.04:.16)*r.dim*(hv?2.2:1);}}}
function m3SelMark(M){const T=THREE,o=S.mp;if(M3.selG){M3.scene.remove(M3.selG);m3Dispose(M3.selG);M3.selG=null;}const it=mpItem(M,o.sel);if(!it||it.k==='hall')return;const fi=mpFloorIdx(M),idx=it.k==='elev'?null:fi[it.lv];if(idx==null)return;if(o.floor!=='all'&&idx>fi[+o.floor])return;

function m3Pts(M,legs){const T=THREE,fi=mpFloorIdx(M),FH=M3.FH*S.mp.ex,W=M.W,H=M.H,P=[];legs.forEach(L=>L.pts.forEach(([x,y])=>{const v=new T.Vector3(x-W/2,(fi[L.lv]||0)*FH+.42,y-H/2);const l=P[P.length-1];if(!l||l.distanceTo(v)>.01)P.push(v);}));return P;}
function m3Curve(P){const T=THREE,cp=new T.CurvePath();for(let i=0;i<P.length-1;i++)cp.add(new T.LineCurve3(P[i],P[i+1]));return cp;}
function m3Tube(cp,color,rad,speed){if(S.mp.floor==='all')rad*=1.7;const T=THREE,G=M3.G,len=cp.getLength(),seg=Math.max(24,Math.round(len*3)),c=new T.Color(color),tex=G.tex.dash.clone();tex.needsUpdate=true;tex.wrapS=T.RepeatWrapping;tex.repeat.set(Math.max(1,len/2.2),1);
  M3.ovl.add(new T.Mesh(new T.TubeGeometry(cp,seg,rad*.42,6,false),new T.MeshBasicMaterial({color:c.clone().lerp(M3.white,.35),transparent:true,opacity:.85})));
  M3.ovl.add(new T.Mesh(new T.TubeGeometry(cp,seg,rad*2.2,8,false),new T.MeshBasicMaterial({color:c,transparent:true,opacity:.14,blending:m3B(),depthWrite:false})));
  M3.ovl.add(new T.Mesh(new T.TubeGeometry(cp,seg,rad*1.05,8,false),new T.MeshBasicMaterial({map:tex,color:c,transparent:true,blending:m3B(),depthWrite:false})));M3.texs.push({tex,speed});
  const n=cp.curves.length,last=cp.curves[n-1],dir=new T.Vector3().subVectors(last.v2,last.v1).normalize(),cone=new T.Mesh(G.geo.cone,new T.MeshBasicMaterial({color:c.clone().lerp(M3.white,.3)}));cone.scale.set(rad*5,rad*7,rad*5);cone.position.copy(last.v2).addScaledVector(dir,-rad*3.5);cone.quaternion.setFromUnitVectors(new T.Vector3(0,1,0),dir);M3.ovl.add(cone);
  return len;}
function m3Flow(M,legs,color,n,rad=.07){const T=THREE,P=m3Pts(M,legs);if(P.length<2)return;const cp=m3Curve(P),len=m3Tube(cp,color,rad,1.1),c=new T.Color(color);if(S.mp.floor==='all')rad*=1.7;
  const k=Math.min(3,Math.max(1,n));for(let i=0;i<k;i++){const s=new T.Sprite(new T.SpriteMaterial({map:M3.G.tex.radial,color:c.clone().lerp(M3.white,.45),transparent:true,blending:m3B(),depthWrite:false}));s.scale.setScalar(rad*11);M3.ovl.add(s);M3.movers.push({m:s,cp,len,u:i/k,sp:3.4});}}

function m3Ovl0(M){const T=THREE,o=S.mp,st=M3.st||{},vis=L=>o.floor==='all'||L.lv===+o.floor,vl=r=>r.legs.filter(vis);if(M3.ovl){M3.scene.remove(M3.ovl);m3Dispose(M3.ovl);}M3.ovl=new T.Group();M3.scene.add(M3.ovl);M3.movers=[];M3.texs=[];M3.route=null;M3.cx=null;M3.hud.hidden=true;M3.elevs.forEach(e=>e.busy=false);

  if(o.reg==='emerg'){const e=st._em;if(e&&e.pick){const r=mpRoute(M,e.from,e.pick.it);m3Route(M,r,'#FF3C55',true);}return;}
  if(o.reg==='hyg'){const src=M.items.filter(i=>i.k==='other'&&/steril/i.test(i.name||''));Object.keys(st).forEach(id=>{const s=st[id];if(id[0]==='_'||!(s.k==='clean'||s.k==='prep'))return;const it=mpItem(M,id),from=it&&(src.find(x=>x.lv===it.lv)||src[0]);if(!from)return;const r=mpRoute(M,from,it,{speed:D.mapCfg.walk,handover:0}),lg=vl(r);if(lg.length)m3Flow(M,lg,'#27D6EA',1,.055);});return;}
  if(o.mode==='cmp'){const XF=mpXfers(o.site),X=XF.find(x=>x.id===o.cx)||XF[0];if(X)m3Xfer(X);return;}
  if(o.mode==='flow')mpFlows(M).forEach(f=>{const r=mpRoute(M,f.a,f.b),lg=vl(r);if(lg.length)m3Flow(M,lg,f.c,f.pacu?1:f.n,f.pacu?.055:.07);});
  if(o.mode==='staff'){const fi=mpFloorIdx(M),FH=M3.FH*o.ex;mpStaffFlows(M).forEach(x=>{if(x.st){const r=mpRoute(M,x.st,x.or,{speed:D.mapCfg.walk,handover:0}),lg=vl(r);if(lg.length)m3Flow(M,lg,'#A58BFF',Math.ceil(x.sum),.055);}

  if(o.mode==='route'){const a=mpItem(M,o.from),b=mpItem(M,o.to);if(a&&b){const r=mpRoute(M,a,b);m3Route(M,r,'#5EC8E4',false);}}}
