  const mark=(lv,k,lab)=>{const idx=fi[lv];if(idx==null||idx>top)return;const y=idx*FH,c=new T.Color(m3RfColor(k)),add=m=>{m.userData.fl=true;M3.ovl.add(m);return m;};
    const sq=new T.CylinderGeometry(Math.SQRT1_2,Math.SQRT1_2,1,4,1,true);sq.rotateY(Math.PI/4);const band=add(new T.Mesh(sq,new T.MeshBasicMaterial({color:c,transparent:true,opacity:LT?.4:.3,blending:m3B(),depthWrite:false,side:T.DoubleSide})));band.scale.set(W+1.56,.34,H+1.56);band.position.y=y-.17;band.renderOrder=1;
    const edge=add(new T.LineSegments(G.geo.boxE,new T.LineBasicMaterial({color:c,transparent:true,opacity:LT?.9:.8})));edge.scale.copy(band.scale);edge.position.copy(band.position);
    const wash=add(new T.Mesh(G.geo.plane,new T.MeshBasicMaterial({color:c,transparent:true,opacity:(LT?.085:.065)*(o.floor==='all'?.8:1),blending:m3B(),depthWrite:false})));wash.scale.set(W+1,H+1,1);wash.rotation.x=-Math.PI/2;wash.position.y=y+.03;wash.renderOrder=1;
    const L=M3.labels.find(x=>x.kind==='floor'&&x.lv===lv);if(L){L.el.classList.add('rf');L.el.style.setProperty('--rf',m3RfColor(k));const t=document.createElement('em');t.className='rf-t';t.textContent=lab;L.el.firstChild.appendChild(t);}};
  if(a===b)mark(a,'from','Odkud i kam');else{mark(a,'from','Odkud');mark(b,'to','Kam');}
  return {a:mpFloorName(M,a),b:mpFloorName(M,b),same:a===b};}
function m3Route(M,r,color,em){const T=THREE,G=M3.G,P=m3Pts(M,r.legs);if(P.length<2)return;const cp=m3Curve(P),c=new T.Color(color);m3Tube(cp,color,.1,1.7);const fl=m3RouteFloors(M,r);
  const tok=new T.Group(),beam=new T.Mesh(G.geo.cyl,new T.ShaderMaterial({uniforms:{uC:{value:c.clone()},uO:{value:.55}},vertexShader:'varying float vY;varying float vN;void main(){vY=position.y+.5;vN=abs(normal.y);gl_Position=projectionMatrix*modelViewMatrix*vec4(position,1.);}',fragmentShader:'uniform vec3 uC;uniform float uO;varying float vY;varying float vN;void main(){if(vN>.5)discard;gl_FragColor=vec4(uC,pow(1.-vY,2.)*uO);}',transparent:true,blending:m3B(),depthWrite:false,side:T.DoubleSide}));beam.scale.set(1.2,5,1.2);beam.position.y=2.5;tok.add(beam);
  const disc=new T.Mesh(G.geo.plane,new T.MeshBasicMaterial({map:G.tex.radial,color:c,transparent:true,opacity:.9,blending:m3B(),depthWrite:false}));disc.scale.set(2.4,2.4,1);disc.rotation.x=-Math.PI/2;disc.position.y=.06;tok.add(disc);
  const ring=new T.Mesh(new T.RingGeometry(.74,.8,48),new T.MeshBasicMaterial({color:c.clone().lerp(M3.white,.3),side:T.DoubleSide}));ring.rotation.x=-Math.PI/2;ring.position.y=.09;tok.add(ring);
  const bed=new T.Mesh(G.geo.box,G.mat.mattr);bed.scale.set(1.05,.14,.48);bed.position.y=.3;bed.castShadow=true;tok.add(bed);const bf=new T.Mesh(G.geo.box,G.mat.bedF);bf.scale.set(1.1,.08,.52);bf.position.y=.2;tok.add(bf);const bl=new T.Mesh(G.geo.box,G.mat.blanket);bl.scale.set(.66,.04,.5);bl.position.set(.12,.39,0);tok.add(bl);
  M3.ovl.add(tok);const lp=new T.Vector3(),lab=m3Label(`<b>${em?'Akutní pacient':'Pacient'}</b><span>převoz</span>`,lp,'m3t'+(em?' em':''),{kind:'tok',ov:true});
  const ls=[];for(let i=0;i<P.length-1;i++)ls.push(P[i].distanceTo(P[i+1]));const tot=ls.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);
  let lenA=tot,lenV=0,lenB=0;if(r.elev&&r.legs.length===2){const pa=m3Pts(M,[r.legs[0]]),pb=m3Pts(M,[r.legs[1]]);lenA=0;for(let i=0;i<pa.length-1;i++)lenA+=pa[i].distanceTo(pa[i+1]);lenB=0;for(let i=0;i<pb.length-1;i++)lenB+=pb[i].distanceTo(pb[i+1]);lenV=Math.max(0,tot-lenA-lenB);}
  const el=r.elev?M3.elevs.find(e=>e.it.id===r.elev.id):null;if(el)el.busy=true;
  M3.route={cp,tot,lenA,lenV,lenB,bed:tok,lp,lab,p:r.parts,T:r.t,sim:0,hold:0,el,yaw:0,r,em,beam,fl};M3.hud.hidden=false;M3.hud.classList.toggle('em',!!em);}
function m3RouteTick(dt){const R0=M3.route,p=R0.p,T=R0.T,dur=clampN(T/22,7,15);if(R0.hold>0){R0.hold-=dt;if(R0.hold<=0)R0.sim=0;}else{R0.sim+=dt*T/dur;if(R0.sim>=T){R0.sim=T;R0.hold=2.2;}}
  const s=R0.sim,tA=p.walk,tW=p.wait,tR=p.ride,tB=p.walk2;let u,ph;
  if(s<tA){u=R0.lenA*s/Math.max(.001,tA);ph='Převoz ze stanice';}else if(s<tA+tW){u=R0.lenA;ph='Čekání na výtah';}else if(s<tA+tW+tR){u=R0.lenA+R0.lenV*(s-tA-tW)/Math.max(.001,tR);ph='Jízda výtahem';}else if(s<tA+tW+tR+tB){u=R0.lenA+R0.lenV+R0.lenB*(s-tA-tW-tR)/Math.max(.001,tB);ph='Převoz na sál';}else{u=R0.tot;ph='Předání na sále';}
  if(R0.em&&s<tA)ph='Převoz z příjmu';
  const f=clampN(u/Math.max(.001,R0.tot),0,1),pos=R0.cp.getPointAt(f);R0.bed.position.set(pos.x,pos.y-.42,pos.z);R0.lp.set(pos.x,pos.y+.6,pos.z);
  const tg=R0.cp.getTangentAt(Math.min(.999,f+.0001));if(Math.abs(tg.y)<.9)R0.yaw=Math.atan2(-tg.z,tg.x);R0.bed.rotation.y+=((R0.yaw-R0.bed.rotation.y+Math.PI*3)%(Math.PI*2)-Math.PI)*Math.min(1,dt*8);R0.beam.material.uniforms.uO.value=.4+.2*Math.sin(M3.time*4);
  if(R0.el){const e=R0.el,aY=R0.cp.getPointAt(clampN(R0.lenA/R0.tot,0,1)).y-.42+.06;if(s<tA+tW){const d=aY-e.y;e.y+=Math.sign(d)*Math.min(Math.abs(d),dt*4);}else if(s<tA+tW+tR)e.y=pos.y-.42+.06;e.cab.position.y=e.y;}
  if(S.mp.follow){const g=M3.goal,k=Math.min(1,dt*3);g.tx+=(pos.x-g.tx)*k;g.tz+=(pos.z-g.tz)*k;g.ty+=(pos.y-g.ty)*k;}
  const F=R0.fl,html=`<span>${R0.em?'Akutní převoz · ':''}${ph}</span><b>${fmtMin(s)} <small>/ ${fmtMin(T)} min</small></b><u><i style="width:${(s/T*100).toFixed(1)}%"></i></u>${F?`<em class="m3-rf">${F.same?`<i style="--c:${m3RfColor('from')}"></i>${esc(F.a)} · na stejném patře`:`<i style="--c:${m3RfColor('from')}"></i>Odkud ${esc(F.a)}<s></s><i style="--c:${m3RfColor('to')}"></i>Kam ${esc(F.b)}`}</em>`:''}`;if(M3.hud._h!==html){M3.hud._h=html;M3.hud.innerHTML=html;}}
function m3Elev(dt){for(const e of M3.elevs){if(e.busy)continue;if(e.hold>0){e.hold-=dt;}else{const d=e.tgt-e.y;if(Math.abs(d)<.02){e.y=e.tgt;e.hold=1.6+Math.random()*2.6;e.tgt=e.ys[Math.floor(Math.random()*e.ys.length)];}else e.y+=Math.sign(d)*Math.min(Math.abs(d),dt*(1.2+Math.min(2,Math.abs(d))));}e.cab.position.y=e.y;}}
function m3Labels(){const o=S.mp,r=M3.orb.r,fa=o.floor==='all',flv=fa?null:+o.floor,nar=M3.w<620,dn=fa?(r>45?'cp':r>30?'md':''):nar?(r>40?'cp':'md'):(r>60?'md':'');for(const L of M3.labels){let show;
    const cm=o.mode==='cmp'&&o.reg==='std';switch(L.kind){case 'or':show=cm?r<95&&(fa||L.lv===flv):fa?r<240:L.lv===flv;break;case 'floor':show=!cm||r<95;break;case 'room':show=fa?r<60:L.lv===flv&&r<(nar?30:44);break;case 'elev':show=r<(fa?90:44);break;case 'badge':show=fa?r<120:L.lv===flv;break;default:show=true;}
    if(L.id&&L.id===o.sel)show=true;if(o.mode==='route'&&o.reg==='std'&&L.id&&(L.id===o.from||L.id===o.to))show=true;
    if(show){M3.v3.copy(L.p).project(M3.camera);show=M3.v3.z<1&&Math.abs(M3.v3.x)<1.08&&Math.abs(M3.v3.y)<1.08;}
    if(!show){if(L.on){L.el.style.display='none';L.on=false;}continue;}if(!L.on){L.el.style.display='';L.on=true;}L.el.style.transform=`translate(${((M3.v3.x+1)/2*M3.w).toFixed(1)}px,${((1-M3.v3.y)/2*M3.h).toFixed(1)}px)`;
    const sel=!!L.id&&L.id===o.sel;if(L.sel!==sel){L.sel=sel;L.el.classList.toggle('sel',sel);}if(L.kind==='or'){const d=sel?'':dn;if(L.dn!==d){L.dn=d;L.el.classList.toggle('cp',d==='cp');L.el.classList.toggle('md',d==='md');}}}}
function m3Loop(t){M3.raf=requestAnimationFrame(m3Loop);if(!M3.scene||!M3.host||!M3.host.isConnected)return;const dt=M3.lt?Math.min(.06,(t-M3.lt)/1000):.016;M3.lt=t;M3.time=t/1000;const o=M3.orb,g=M3.goal,k=1-Math.pow(.002,dt),mp=S.mp;
  if(mp.live){if(mp.day!==today)mp.live=false;else mp.t=m3Now();}else if(mp.play){const r=M3.tr||[360,1200];mp.t+=dt*6;if(mp.t>r[1])mp.t=r[0];}
  if(Math.floor(mp.t)!==M3.clk){M3.clk=Math.floor(mp.t);m3Sync();}
  ['r','th','ph','tx','ty','tz'].forEach(p=>o[p]+=(g[p]-o[p])*k);const c=M3.camera;c.position.set(o.tx+o.r*Math.sin(o.ph)*Math.sin(o.th),o.ty+o.r*Math.cos(o.ph),o.tz+o.r*Math.sin(o.ph)*Math.cos(o.th));c.lookAt(o.tx,o.ty,o.tz);
  for(const x of M3.texs)x.tex.offset.x-=dt*x.speed;for(const mv of M3.movers){mv.u=(mv.u+dt*mv.sp/Math.max(1,mv.len))%1;mv.m.position.copy(mv.cp.getPointAt(mv.u));}
  m3Anim(M3.time);m3Elev(dt);if(M3.route)m3RouteTick(dt);if(M3.cx)m3XferTick(dt);
  if(M3.comp)M3.comp.render(dt);else M3.renderer.render(M3.scene,c);m3Labels();
  if(dt>.045){if(++M3.slow>90&&M3.pr>1){M3.pr=1;M3.renderer.setPixelRatio(1);if(M3.comp)M3.comp.setPixelRatio(1);m3Size();M3.slow=0;}}else M3.slow=Math.max(0,M3.slow-1);}
function m3Cam(v,inst){const M=mapCur(),n=M.floors.length,FH=M3.FH*S.mp.ex,g=M3.goal,fa=S.mp.floor==='all',cy=(fa?(n-1)/2:mpFloorIdx(M)[+S.mp.floor]||0)*FH,mx=Math.max(M.W,M.H),nar=M3.w&&M3.w/M3.h<1.15?1.45:1;
  if(v==='cmp'){const b=m3CampusBox(),w=b.x1-b.x0,d=b.z1-b.z0;Object.assign(g,{r:clampN(Math.max(w,d*1.25)*1.32*nar,40,330),th:.5,ph:.92,tx:(b.x0+b.x1)/2,ty:b.top*.25,tz:(b.z0+b.z1)/2});if(inst)Object.assign(M3.orb,g);return;}
  if(v==='top')Object.assign(g,{r:mx*1.6*nar,th:0,ph:.05,tx:0,ty:cy,tz:0});else if(v==='side')Object.assign(g,{r:mx*1.6*nar,th:0,ph:1.3,tx:0,ty:cy+1,tz:0});
  else if(v==='route'&&M3.route&&!(fa&&M3.route.r.floors)){const b=new THREE.Box3().setFromObject(M3.ovl),c=b.getCenter(new THREE.Vector3()),sz=b.getSize(new THREE.Vector3());Object.assign(g,{r:Math.max(30,Math.max(sz.x,sz.y*1.4,sz.z)*1.6)*nar,tx:c.x,ty:c.y,tz:c.z,ph:.9});}
  else{const por=M3.w&&M3.w/M3.h<.8;Object.assign(g,fa?{r:(mx*1.9+n*3.2)*(por?1.25:nar),th:por?1.1:.55,ph:1.08,tx:0,ty:cy,tz:1}:por?{r:mx*1.32,th:1.18,ph:.86,tx:2,ty:cy,tz:-2.5}:{r:mx*1.02*nar,th:.4,ph:.9,tx:0,ty:cy,tz:-3.2});}if(inst)Object.assign(M3.orb,g);}
function m3Fly(it){if(!it||!M3.renderer)return;const M=mapCur(),fi=mpFloorIdx(M),FH=M3.FH*S.mp.ex,g=M3.goal;Object.assign(g,{tx:it.x+it.w/2-M.W/2,tz:it.y+it.h/2-M.H/2,ty:(fi[it.lv]||0)*FH,r:clampN(Math.max(it.w,it.h)*3.6,24,60),ph:Math.min(g.ph,1.0)});}
function m3Down(e){const c=M3.renderer.domElement;try{c.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(x){}M3.ptrs.set(e.pointerId,{x:e.clientX,y:e.clientY});M3.drag={b:e.button,sh:e.shiftKey,mv:0,pinch:M3.ptrs.size===2?m3Pinch():0};if(e.button===0||e.pointerType!=='mouse')S.mp.follow=false;}
function m3Hit(e){const c=M3.renderer.domElement,rc=c.getBoundingClientRect(),m=new THREE.Vector2((e.clientX-rc.left)/rc.width*2-1,-(e.clientY-rc.top)/rc.height*2+1);M3.ray.setFromCamera(m,M3.camera);const h=M3.ray.intersectObjects(M3.pick,false)[0];return {id:h&&h.object.userData.id||null,bld:h&&h.object.userData.bld||null,x:e.clientX-rc.left,y:e.clientY-rc.top};}
function m3Hover(e){if(e.pointerType==='touch')return;const now=performance.now();if(now-(M3.hvT||0)<60)return;M3.hvT=now;const h=m3Hit(e);M3.hov=h.id;M3.renderer.domElement.style.cursor=h.id||h.bld?'pointer':'';m3Tip(h);}
function m3Tip(h){let el=M3.tip;if(!el){el=M3.tip=document.createElement('div');el.className='m3-tip';M3.wrap.appendChild(el);}if(h&&!h.id&&h.bld){const b=mpBlds(S.mp.site).find(x=>x.id===h.bld);if(b){const html=`<div class="tp-h"><b>${esc(b.name)}</b><span>${b.M.floors.length} ${plural(b.M.floors.length,'podlaží','podlaží','podlaží')}</span></div><small class="tp-f">Klepnutím zobrazíte budovu v detailu</small>`;if(el._h!==html){el._h=html;el.innerHTML=html;}el.hidden=false;const W=M3.w,x=h.x+16+250>W?h.x-16-250:h.x+16;el.style.transform=`translate(${x}px,${Math.min(Math.max(8,h.y-20),M3.h-120)}px)`;return;}}
  if(!h||!h.id){el.hidden=true;M3.hov=null;return;}
  const o=S.mp,M=mapCur(),it=mpItem(M,h.id);if(!it){el.hidden=true;return;}let html;
  if(it.k==='or'){const s=(M3.st||{})[it.id]||{k:'off'},c=MST[s.k].c,r=it.room&&ROOM[it.room],sc=mpSched(it,o.day),cases=new Set(sc.segs.filter(x=>x.n!=null).map(x=>x.n)).size,done=new Set(sc.segs.filter(x=>x.k==='clean'&&x.b<=o.t).map(x=>x.n)).size,sp=s.s||(s.prev&&s.prev.s);
    html=`<div class="tp-h"><b>SÁL ${esc(r?r.code:mpTitle(it,true))}</b><span>${esc(mpFloorName(M,it.lv))}</span></div>${r?`<p>${esc(r.name)}</p>`:''}<em style="--c:${c}"><i></i>${MST[s.k].n}</em>${sp?`<div class="tp-r"><span>Obor</span><b>${esc(specName(sp))}</b></div>`:''}${s.sub?`<div class="tp-r"><span>Stav</span><b>${esc(s.sub)}</b></div>`:''}${cases?`<div class="tp-r"><span>Výkony dne</span><b>${done} / ${cases}</b></div>`:''}${s.a!=null&&s.b!=null&&s.k!=='free'&&s.k!=='off'?`<u style="--c:${c}"><i style="width:${clampN((o.t-s.a)/Math.max(1,s.b-s.a)*100,0,100).toFixed(0)}%"></i></u><small>${hm(s.a)} – ${hm(s.b)} · zbývá ${Math.max(0,Math.ceil(s.b-o.t))} min</small>`:''}<small class="tp-f">Klepnutím zobrazíte detail</small>`;}
  else html=`<div class="tp-h"><b>${esc(mpTitle(it,false))}</b><span>${esc(it.k==='elev'?`${mpFloorName(M,it.lv)}–${mpFloorName(M,it.lv1??it.lv)}`:mpFloorName(M,it.lv))}</span></div><p>${esc(MK[it.k].n)}${it.beds?` · ${it.beds} lůžek`:''}${it.specs&&it.specs.length?` · ${esc(it.specs.join(', '))}`:''}</p><small>${it.w*MCELL} × ${it.h*MCELL} m</small>`;
  if(el._h!==html){el._h=html;el.innerHTML=html;}el.hidden=false;const W=M3.w,x=h.x+16+250>W?h.x-16-250:h.x+16,y=Math.min(Math.max(8,h.y-20),M3.h-200);el.style.transform=`translate(${x}px,${y}px)`;}
function m3Pinch(){const p=[...M3.ptrs.values()];return p.length<2?0:Math.hypot(p[0].x-p[1].x,p[0].y-p[1].y);}
function m3Move(e){const pr=M3.ptrs.get(e.pointerId);if(!M3.drag||!pr){m3Hover(e);return;}m3Tip(null);const dx=e.clientX-pr.x,dy=e.clientY-pr.y;pr.x=e.clientX;pr.y=e.clientY;M3.drag.mv+=Math.abs(dx)+Math.abs(dy);const g=M3.goal;
  if(M3.ptrs.size>=2){const d=m3Pinch();if(M3.drag.pinch&&d)g.r=clampN(g.r*M3.drag.pinch/d,14,340);M3.drag.pinch=d;return;}
  if(M3.drag.b===2||M3.drag.b===1||M3.drag.sh){const s=g.r*.0017,sn=Math.sin(g.th),cs=Math.cos(g.th);g.tx+=(-cs*dx+sn*dy)*s;g.tz+=(sn*dx+cs*dy)*s;}
  else{g.th-=dx*.0065;g.ph=clampN(g.ph-dy*.0055,.05,1.48);}}
function m3Up(e){const pr=M3.ptrs.get(e.pointerId);M3.ptrs.delete(e.pointerId);if(!M3.drag)return;const d=M3.drag;if(!M3.ptrs.size)M3.drag=null;if(!pr||d.mv>6||d.b!==0)return;
  const c=M3.renderer.domElement,rc=c.getBoundingClientRect(),m=new THREE.Vector2((e.clientX-rc.left)/rc.width*2-1,-(e.clientY-rc.top)/rc.height*2+1);M3.ray.setFromCamera(m,M3.camera);const hit=M3.ray.intersectObjects(M3.pick,false)[0];
  if(hit&&hit.object.userData.bld){S.mp.bld=hit.object.userData.bld;mpBldReset();render();return;}
  if(hit&&hit.object.userData.id)mpPick(hit.object.userData.id);}
function m3Wheel(e){e.preventDefault();const g=M3.goal;g.r=clampN(g.r*Math.exp(e.deltaY*.0011),14,340);}
function mpPick(id){if(M3.tip)m3Tip(null);const o=S.mp,M=mapCur(),it=mpItem(M,id);if(!it)return;o.sel=id;if(o.reg==='emerg'){if(it.k==='or')o.emTo=id;else if(['ward','er','icu','pacu'].includes(it.k)){o.emFrom=id;o.emTo=null;}mpEmView();}else if(o.mode==='route'){if(it.k==='or')o.to=id;else if(['ward','er','icu','pacu'].includes(it.k))o.from=id;mpRouteView();}render();}
function m3PrintImg(on){const h=$('#m3-host');if(!h)return;h.querySelectorAll('.m3-print').forEach(x=>x.remove());if(!on||!M3.renderer||!M3.scene)return;try{if(M3.comp)M3.comp.render(0);else M3.renderer.render(M3.scene,M3.camera);const img=new Image();img.className='m3-print';img.alt='3D model nemocnice';img.src=M3.renderer.domElement.toDataURL('image/png');h.appendChild(img);}catch(e){}}
function m3Dom(M,st){const set=(id,h)=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e&&e._h!==h){e._h=h;e.innerHTML=h;}},o=S.mp;M3.tr=mpTRange(M);
  set('m3-leg',mpLegHTML(M,st));if(document.getElementById('m3-board'))set('m3-board',mpBoardHTML(M,st));if(document.getElementById('m3-gantt'))set('m3-gantt',mpGanttHTML(M,st));if(document.getElementById('m3-em'))set('m3-em',mpEmHTML(M,st));if(document.getElementById('m3-hy'))set('m3-hy',mpHyHTML(M,st));
  const c=document.getElementById('m3-clock');if(c)c.textContent=hm(o.t);const ts=document.getElementById('m3-ts');if(ts&&document.activeElement!==ts){ts.value=Math.round(o.t);ts.style.setProperty('--p',((o.t-M3.tr[0])/(M3.tr[1]-M3.tr[0])*100).toFixed(2)+'%');}
  const dt=document.querySelector('.m3-dot');if(dt)dt.classList.toggle('on',!!o.live);const lv=document.querySelector('.m3-live');if(lv)lv.classList.toggle('on',!!o.live);}
document.addEventListener('input',e=>{if(!e.target||e.target.id!=='m3-ts')return;const o=S.mp;o.t=+e.target.value;o.live=false;if(o.play){o.play=false;const b=document.querySelector('.m3-tb');if(b){b.classList.remove('on');b.innerHTML=m3I('play');}}const r=M3.tr||[0,1440];e.target.style.setProperty('--p',((o.t-r[0])/(r[1]-r[0])*100).toFixed(2)+'%');if(S.view==='map'&&o.tab==='map'){KM.clk=Math.floor(o.t);kmStatus();}else if(M3.renderer&&S.view==='map'){M3.clk=Math.floor(o.t);m3Sync();}});
document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Escape'&&S.view==='map'&&S.mp&&S.mp.full){S.mp.full=false;if(document.fullscreenElement)document.exitFullscreen().catch(()=>{});render();}});
document.addEventListener('fullscreenchange',()=>{if(!document.fullscreenElement&&S.mp&&S.mp.full&&S.view==='map'){S.mp.full=false;render();}});

let MD=null;
function mpEdLv(M){const fl=mpFloors(M);if(!fl.some(f=>f.lv===S.mp.ed))S.mp.ed=fl.length>1&&S.mp.floor!=='all'?+S.mp.floor:fl[0].lv;if(!fl.some(f=>f.lv===S.mp.ed))S.mp.ed=fl[0].lv;return S.mp.ed;}
function m2dSVG(M,lv){const cs=20,W=M.W,H=M.H,o=S.mp,ed=isCoord(),items=M.items.filter(i=>onFloor(i,lv)).sort((a,b)=>(a.k==='hall'?0:1)-(b.k==='hall'?0:1)),below=M.items.filter(i=>i.k!=='elev'&&i.k!=='hall'&&i.lv===lv-1);
  let s=`<svg id="m2d" class="m2d" viewBox="0 0 ${W*cs} ${H*cs}" data-lv="${lv}" role="img" aria-label="Plán podlaží ${esc(mpFloorName(M,lv))}"><defs><pattern id="m2g" width="${cs}" height="${cs}" patternUnits="userSpaceOnUse"><path d="M ${cs} 0 L 0 0 0 ${cs}" class="m2-gl"/></pattern><pattern id="m2g5" width="${cs*5}" height="${cs*5}" patternUnits="userSpaceOnUse"><rect width="${cs*5}" height="${cs*5}" fill="url(#m2g)"/><path d="M ${cs*5} 0 L 0 0 0 ${cs*5}" class="m2-gl5"/></pattern></defs><rect width="${W*cs}" height="${H*cs}" fill="url(#m2g5)" class="m2-bg"/>`;
  below.forEach(i=>s+=`<rect class="m2-ghost" x="${i.x*cs+2}" y="${i.y*cs+2}" width="${i.w*cs-4}" height="${i.h*cs-4}" rx="4"/>`);
  for(const it of items){const sel=o.sel===it.id,c=mpColor(it,o.day),t=mpTitle(it,true),mx=Math.max(2,Math.floor(it.w*cs/7.5)),tt=t.length>mx?t.slice(0,mx-1)+'…':t,big=it.w*it.h>=6;
    s+=`<g class="m2-it k-${it.k}${sel?' sel':''}" data-mid="${it.id}" style="--c:${c}"><rect class="m2-r" x="${it.x*cs+1}" y="${it.y*cs+1}" width="${it.w*cs-2}" height="${it.h*cs-2}" rx="5"/>${big||it.k==='hall'&&it.w>4?`<text class="m2-t" x="${(it.x+it.w/2)*cs}" y="${(it.y+it.h/2)*cs-(it.h>2?6:0)}">${esc(tt)}</text>${it.h>2?`<text class="m2-s" x="${(it.x+it.w/2)*cs}" y="${(it.y+it.h/2)*cs+10}">${it.k==='elev'?`${esc(mpFloorName(M,it.lv))}–${esc(mpFloorName(M,it.lv1??it.lv))}`:`${it.w*MCELL} × ${it.h*MCELL} m`}</text>`:''}`:''}${sel&&ed?`<rect class="m2-hd" data-h="se" x="${(it.x+it.w)*cs-8}" y="${(it.y+it.h)*cs-8}" width="13" height="13" rx="3"/>`:''}</g>`;}
  return s+'</svg>';}
function m2pt(svg,e){const p=svg.createSVGPoint();p.x=e.clientX;p.y=e.clientY;const q=p.matrixTransform(svg.getScreenCTM().inverse());return {x:q.x/20,y:q.y/20};}
function m2DrawUpd(){if(!MD||!MD.el)return;const a=MD.p,b=MD.c,x0=Math.floor(Math.min(a.x,b.x)),y0=Math.floor(Math.min(a.y,b.y)),x1=Math.ceil(Math.max(a.x,b.x)),y1=Math.ceil(Math.max(a.y,b.y));MD.r={x:x0,y:y0,w:Math.max(1,x1-x0),h:Math.max(1,y1-y0)};const r=MD.el;r.setAttribute('x',MD.r.x*20);r.setAttribute('y',MD.r.y*20);r.setAttribute('width',MD.r.w*20);r.setAttribute('height',MD.r.h*20);}
document.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{const svg=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('#m2d');if(!svg||e.button>0)return;const M=mapCur(),lv=+svg.dataset.lv,p=m2pt(svg,e),h=e.target.closest('[data-h]'),g=e.target.closest('[data-mid]'),ed=isCoord();
  if(h&&ed){const it=mpItem(M,S.mp.sel);if(it){MD={m:'size',id:it.id,p,o:{...it}};e.preventDefault();}return;}
  if(g&&S.mp.tool==='sel'){const id=g.dataset.mid;if(S.mp.sel!==id){S.mp.sel=id;render();}if(ed){const it=mpItem(M,id);if(it)MD={m:'move',id,p,o:{...it}};}e.preventDefault();return;}
  if(S.mp.tool!=='sel'){if(!ed){denied();return;}MD={m:'draw',lv,p,c:p};const r=document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg','rect');r.setAttribute('class','m2-new');r.setAttribute('rx','5');svg.appendChild(r);MD.el=r;m2DrawUpd();e.preventDefault();return;}
  if(S.mp.sel){S.mp.sel=null;render();}});
document.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{if(!MD)return;const svg=$('#m2d');if(!svg)return;const p=m2pt(svg,e);MD.c=p;if(MD.m==='draw'){m2DrawUpd();return;}
  const M=mapCur(),g=svg.querySelector(`[data-mid="${MD.id}"]`);if(!g)return;const o=MD.o,cs=20;
  if(MD.m==='move'){const dx=Math.round(p.x-MD.p.x),dy=Math.round(p.y-MD.p.y);MD.n={x:clampN(o.x+dx,0,M.W-o.w),y:clampN(o.y+dy,0,M.H-o.h),w:o.w,h:o.h};}else{MD.n={x:o.x,y:o.y,w:clampN(Math.round(p.x)-o.x,1,M.W-o.x),h:clampN(Math.round(p.y)-o.y,1,M.H-o.y)};}
  const r=g.querySelector('.m2-r'),n=MD.n;r.setAttribute('x',n.x*cs+1);r.setAttribute('y',n.y*cs+1);r.setAttribute('width',n.w*cs-2);r.setAttribute('height',n.h*cs-2);g.classList.add('drag');g.classList.toggle('bad',mpOverlap(M,{...o,...n},o.id).length>0);});
document.addEventListener('pointerup',()=>{if(!MD)return;const d=MD;MD=null;const M=mapCur();
  if(d.m==='draw'){if(d.el)d.el.remove();const k=S.mp.tool,df=MK[k].def;let r=d.r||{x:Math.floor(d.p.x),y:Math.floor(d.p.y),w:1,h:1};if(r.w*r.h<=2)r={x:clampN(Math.floor(d.p.x),0,M.W-df[0]),y:clampN(Math.floor(d.p.y),0,M.H-df[1]),w:df[0],h:df[1]};
    const it={id:'m'+uid(),k,lv:d.lv,...r,name:MK[k].n};if(k==='elev'){const fl=mpFloors(M);it.lv=fl[0].lv;it.lv1=fl[fl.length-1].lv;it.name=`Výtah ${String.fromCharCode(65+M.items.filter(i=>i.k==='elev').length)}`;}
    if(k==='or'||k==='pacu'){const used=new Set(M.items.map(i=>i.room).filter(Boolean)),rm=D.rooms.find(x=>x.site===S.mp.site&&x.active&&(k==='pacu'?x.kind==='pacu':x.kind!=='pacu')&&!used.has(x.id));if(rm)it.room=rm.id;}
    if(k==='ward'){it.specs=[];it.beds=20;}if(k==='icu'||k==='er')it.beds=6;if(k==='pacu')it.beds=4;
    const ov=mpOverlap(M,it,null);if(ov.length){toast(`Místnost by se překrývala: ${ov.slice(0,3).map(x=>mpTitle(x,true)).join(', ')}`);return;}
    M.items.push(it);S.mp.sel=it.id;S.mp.tool='sel';commit(`Plán nemocnice: přidáno ${mpTitle(it,false)} (${mpFloorName(M,d.lv)})`);return;}
  const it=mpItem(M,d.id);if(!it||!d.n)return;const n=d.n;if(n.x===it.x&&n.y===it.y&&n.w===it.w&&n.h===it.h)return;const ov=mpOverlap(M,{...it,...n},it.id);if(ov.length){toast(`Nelze – překrývá se s: ${ov.slice(0,3).map(x=>mpTitle(x,true)).join(', ')}`);render();return;}
  Object.assign(it,n);commit(`Plán nemocnice: ${mpTitle(it,true)} ${d.m==='move'?'přesunuto':'změněna velikost'}`);});
function mpPropsHTML(M,it){if(!it)return `<section class="card"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Vlastnosti</div></div><p class="hint" style="margin:0">Vyberte místnost v plánu. Novou nakreslíte tak, že zvolíte typ v nástrojích a táhnete myší v mřížce (1 čtverec = ${MCELL} m). Místnosti přesunete tažením, velikost změníte rohovým úchytem.</p></section>`;
  const dis=isCoord()?'':' disabled',fl=mpFloors(M),rooms=D.rooms.filter(r=>r.site===S.mp.site&&(it.k==='pacu'?r.kind==='pacu':r.kind!=='pacu'));
  return `<section class="card"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>${esc(MK[it.k].n)}</div><button class="icon-btn sm" data-act="mpSel" data-v="" aria-label="Zrušit výběr">${ico('x')}</button></div>
    <div class="stack" style="gap:12px">
    <div class="fld"><label for="mp-name">Název</label><input class="in" id="mp-name" data-ch="mpName" value="${esc(it.name||'')}"${dis}></div>
    <div class="fld"><label for="mp-kind">Typ</label><select class="in" id="mp-kind" data-ch="mpKind"${dis}>${opt(Object.entries(MK).map(([k,v])=>[k,v.n]),it.k)}</select></div>
    ${it.k==='or'||it.k==='pacu'?`<div class="fld"><label for="mp-room">Propojení s plánem sálů</label><select class="in" id="mp-room" data-ch="mpRoom"${dis}><option value="">— nepropojeno —</option>${opt(rooms.map(r=>[r.id,`${r.code} · ${r.name}`]),it.room||'')}</select></div>`:''}
    ${['ward','icu','er','pacu'].includes(it.k)?`<div class="fld"><label for="mp-beds">Počet lůžek</label><input class="in" type="number" min="0" max="80" id="mp-beds" data-ch="mpBeds" value="${it.beds||0}"${dis}></div>`:''}
    ${it.k==='ward'?`<div class="fld"><span class="fl">Obory stanice</span><div style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:5px">${D.specs.map(x=>`<button class="chip sm${(it.specs||[]).includes(x.id)?' on':''}" data-act="mpSpecT" data-v="${x.id}" style="--c:${x.c}"><i></i>${esc(x.name)}</button>`).join('')}</div></div>`:''}
    ${it.k==='elev'?`<div class="grid-f" style="grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr"><div class="fld"><label for="mp-l0">Od podlaží</label><select class="in" id="mp-l0" data-ch="mpLv0"${dis}>${opt(fl.map(f=>[String(f.lv),mpFloorName(M,f.lv)]),String(it.lv))}</select></div><div class="fld"><label for="mp-l1">Do podlaží</label><select class="in" id="mp-l1" data-ch="mpLv1"${dis}>${opt(fl.map(f=>[String(f.lv),mpFloorName(M,f.lv)]),String(it.lv1??it.lv))}</select></div></div>`:''}
    <p class="hint" style="margin:0">${it.w*MCELL} × ${it.h*MCELL} m · plocha ${it.w*it.h*MCELL*MCELL} m² · ${it.k==='elev'?`${esc(mpFloorName(M,it.lv))} – ${esc(mpFloorName(M,it.lv1??it.lv))}`:esc(mpFloorName(M,it.lv))}</p>
    ${isCoord()?`<div class="btns"><button class="btn sm" data-act="mpDup">${ico('copy')}Duplikovat</button><button class="btn sm${S.confirm==='mpdel'?' warn':''}" data-act="mpDel">${ico('trash')}${S.confirm==='mpdel'?'Opravdu smazat?':'Smazat'}</button></div>`:''}</div></section>`;}
function mpEditorHTML(M){const o=S.mp,lv=mpEdLv(M),ed=isCoord(),fl=mpFloors(M),sel=mpItem(M,o.sel);
  const tools=[['sel','Výběr a přesun',null],...Object.entries(MK).map(([k,v])=>[k,v.n,v.c])];
  return `<div class="m2-grid"><div class="stack" style="gap:16px">${mpBldCard()}
    <section class="card"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Podlaží</div>${ed?`<button class="btn sm" data-act="mpFloorAdd">${ico('plus')}Přidat podlaží</button>`:''}</div>
      <div class="seg wide" style="flex-wrap:wrap">${[...fl].reverse().map(f=>`<button data-act="mpEdFloor" data-v="${f.lv}" class="${lv===f.lv?'on':''}">${esc(mpFloorName(M,f.lv))}</button>`).join('')}</div>
      ${ed?`<div class="frow" style="margin-top:12px"><label class="flab" for="mp-fn">Název podlaží</label><input class="in" id="mp-fn" data-ch="mpFloorName" data-lv="${lv}" value="${esc((M.floors.find(f=>f.lv===lv)||{}).name||'')}" style="flex:1;min-width:200px">${fl.length>1&&lv===fl[fl.length-1].lv?`<button class="btn sm${S.confirm==='mpfdel'?' warn':''}" data-act="mpFloorDel">${S.confirm==='mpfdel'?'Opravdu odebrat?':'Odebrat podlaží'}</button>`:''}</div>`:''}</section>
    <section class="card m2-card">${ed?`<div class="m2-tools">${tools.map(([k,t,c])=>`<button class="chip sm${o.tool===k?' on':''}" data-act="mpTool" data-v="${k}"${c?` style="--c:${c}"`:''}>${c?'<i></i>':ico('cr')}${t}</button>`).join('')}</div>`:'<p class="hint" style="margin:0 0 10px">Plán upravuje koordinátor operativy.</p>'}
      <div class="ovx"><div class="m2-wrap${o.tool!=='sel'?' draw':''}">${m2dSVG(M,lv)}</div></div>
      <p class="hint" style="margin:10px 0 0">1 čtverec = ${MCELL} × ${MCELL} m · přerušovaně podlaží pod ním · výtahy se zobrazují na všech podlažích, která obsluhují · klávesa Delete smaže vybranou místnost.</p></section></div>
    <div class="stack" style="gap:16px">${mpPropsHTML(M,sel)}
      <section class="card"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Rychlá kontrola</div></div>${mpCheckHTML(M)}</section></div></div>`;}
function mpCheckHTML(M){const st=mpStats(S.mp.site),noLink=M.items.filter(i=>(i.k==='or'||i.k==='pacu')&&(!i.room||!ROOM[i.room])),noSpec=M.items.filter(i=>i.k==='ward'&&!(i.specs||[]).length),fi=mpFloorIdx(M);
  const iso=M.items.filter(i=>!['hall','elev','stair'].includes(i.k)).filter(i=>{const g=mpWalk(M,i.lv);for(let x=i.x;x<i.x+i.w;x++){if(g[(i.y-1)*M.W+x]&&i.y>0)return false;if(i.y+i.h<M.H&&g[(i.y+i.h)*M.W+x])return false;}for(let y=i.y;y<i.y+i.h;y++){if(i.x>0&&g[y*M.W+i.x-1])return false;if(i.x+i.w<M.W&&g[y*M.W+i.x+i.w])return false;}return true;});
  const row=(ok,t)=>`<div class="mp-chk ${ok?'ok':'bad'}">${ico(ok?'check':'warn')}<span>${t}</span></div>`;
  return `<div class="stack" style="gap:6px">${row(!st.missing.length,st.missing.length?`Na mapě chybí sály: ${esc(st.missing.join(', '))}`:'Všechny sály pracoviště jsou na mapě')}${row(!noLink.length,noLink.length?`Nepropojené místnosti: ${esc(noLink.map(i=>i.name).join(', '))}`:'Sály a dospávací pokoje jsou propojené s plánem')}${row(!noSpec.length,noSpec.length?`Stanice bez oboru: ${esc(noSpec.map(i=>i.name).join(', '))}`:'Každá stanice má přiřazený obor')}${row(!iso.length,iso.length?`Bez napojení na chodbu: ${esc(iso.slice(0,4).map(i=>mpTitle(i,true)).join(', '))}`:'Všechny místnosti navazují na chodbu')}${row(M.items.some(i=>i.k==='elev')||M.floors.length<2,M.floors.length>1&&!M.items.some(i=>i.k==='elev')?'Budova má více podlaží, ale žádný výtah':'Výtahy propojují podlaží')}</div>`;}

/* ---------- vzdálenosti a časy ---------- */
function mpDistHTML(M){const C=D.mapCfg,dis=isCoord()?'':' disabled',fi=mpFloorIdx(M),fr=M.items.filter(i=>['ward','er','icu'].includes(i.k)).sort((a,b)=>a.lv-b.lv||a.x-b.x),to=M.items.filter(i=>i.k==='or'&&i.room&&ROOM[i.room]).sort((a,b)=>a.lv-b.lv||a.x-b.x);
  const orSp=it=>{const set=new Set();Object.values(D.template[it.room]||{}).forEach(sl=>tplVars(sl).forEach(([x])=>{if(x){if(x.a)set.add(x.a.s);if(x.split&&x.b)set.add(x.b.s);}}));return set;},OS={};to.forEach(t=>OS[t.id]=orSp(t));
  const cls=t=>t<180?'g1':t<300?'g2':t<480?'g3':'g4';
  const cfg=[['amb','Rychlost sanitky v areálu','km/h',5,60,1],['ambLoad','Naložení a vyložení sanitky','s',0,1800,10],['heliLand','Přistání a vyložení vrtulníku','s',0,1800,10],['speed','Rychlost převozu lůžka','m/s',.2,2.5,.1],['walk','Chůze personálu','m/s',.5,2.5,.1],['wait','Čekání na výtah','s',0,600,5],['floor','Jízda výtahem o 1 patro','s',1,60,1],['handover','Převzetí a příprava na sále','s',0,900,10]];
  return `<section class="card" style="margin-bottom:16px"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Parametry výpočtu</div><span class="hint">Trasa se hledá po chodbách a výtazích nakreslených v editoru (1 čtverec = ${MCELL} m).</span></div>
    <div class="mp-cfg">${cfg.map(([k,t,u,mn,mx,st])=>`<div class="fld"><label for="mc-${k}">${t}</label><div class="mp-in"><input class="in" type="number" id="mc-${k}" min="${mn}" max="${mx}" step="${st}" data-ch="mpCfg" data-k="${k}" value="${C[k]}"${dis}><span>${u}</span></div></div>`).join('')}</div></section>
  <section class="card"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Transport ze stanic na sály · minuty</div><div class="q-leg"><span><i style="background:color-mix(in srgb,var(--ok) 30%,var(--raised))"></i>do 3 min</span><span><i style="background:color-mix(in srgb,var(--ok) 12%,var(--raised))"></i>3–5</span><span><i style="background:color-mix(in srgb,var(--warn) 30%,var(--raised))"></i>5–8</span><span><i style="background:color-mix(in srgb,var(--bad) 28%,var(--raised))"></i>nad 8</span><span><i style="box-shadow:inset 0 0 0 2px var(--ink)"></i>obor stanice operuje na sále</span></div></div>
    ${fr.length&&to.length?`<div class="ovx"><table class="tb dm"><thead><tr><th>Odkud \\ kam</th>${to.map(t=>`<th class="c" title="${esc(mpTitle(t,false))}">${esc(mpTitle(t,true))}<small>${esc(mpFloorName(M,t.lv))}</small></th>`).join('')}<th class="r">Sály oboru</th></tr></thead><tbody>
    ${fr.map(f=>{let s=0,n=0;const cells=to.map(t=>{const r=mpRoute(M,f,t),m=(f.specs||[]).some(x=>OS[t.id].has(x));if(m){s+=r.t;n++;}return `<td class="t ${cls(r.t)}${m?' m':''}"><button data-act="mpOpen" data-a="${f.id}" data-b="${t.id}" title="${esc(mpTitle(f,false))} → ${esc(mpTitle(t,false))}: ${fmtN(r.dist)} m${r.floors?`, výtah ${r.floors} ${plural(r.floors,'patro','patra','pater')}`:''}${r.nc?' · bez napojení na chodbu':''}">${fmtN(r.t/60,1)}</button></td>`;}).join('');
      return `<tr><th><span class="mp-k" style="--c:${mpColor(f)}"></span>${esc(mpTitle(f,true))}<small>${esc(mpFloorName(M,f.lv))}${(f.specs||[]).length?' · '+esc(f.specs.join(', ')):''}</small></th>${cells}<td class="r">${n?`<b>${fmtN(s/n/60,1)} min</b>`:'—'}</td></tr>`;}).join('')}</tbody></table></div>`:empty('Na mapě chybí lůžkové stanice nebo propojené sály.')}
    <p class="hint" style="margin-top:10px">Čas = chůze po chodbách + čekání a jízda výtahem + převzetí na sále. Klepnutím na číslo trasu přehrajete ve 3D.</p></section>
  ${(()=>{const XF=mpXfers(S.mp.site);return XF.length?`<section class="card" style="margin-top:16px"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Převozy v areálu<span class="cnt">${XF.length}</span></div><span class="hint">sanitka mezi budovami · heliport a spojovací chodby</span></div><div class="ovx"><table class="tb"><thead><tr><th>Převoz</th><th>Odkud</th><th>Kam</th><th class="r">Délka</th>${['Příprava','Přesun','Předání'].map(t=>`<th class="r">${t}</th>`).join('')}<th class="r">Celkem</th><th></th></tr></thead><tbody>${XF.map(x=>`<tr><th><span class="mp-k" style="--c:${mpXC(x.k)}"></span>${esc(x.k==='amb'?'Sanitka':'Heliport')} · ${esc(x.name)}</th><td>${esc(x.A.name)}</td><td>${esc(x.B.name)}</td><td class="r">${fmtN(x.len)} m</td>${x.parts.map(p=>`<td class="r">${fmtMin(p[1])}</td>`).join('')}<td class="r"><b>${fmtN(x.t/60,1)} min</b></td><td class="r"><button class="btn sm" data-act="mpCxPlay" data-v="${x.id}">${ico('play')}3D</button></td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table></div></section>`:'';})()}
  ${to.length>1?`<section class="card" style="margin-top:16px"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Vzdálenosti mezi sály · chůze personálu</div><span class="hint">minuty bez převzetí · ${fmtN(C.walk,1)} m/s</span></div><div class="ovx"><table class="tb dm"><thead><tr><th></th>${to.map(t=>`<th class="c">${esc(mpTitle(t,true))}</th>`).join('')}</tr></thead><tbody>${to.map(a=>`<tr><th>${esc(mpTitle(a,true))}<small>${esc(mpFloorName(M,a.lv))}</small></th>${to.map(b=>{if(a===b)return '<td class="t z">—</td>';const r=mpRoute(M,a,b,{speed:C.walk,handover:0});return `<td class="t ${cls(r.t*2.2)}" title="${fmtN(r.dist)} m">${fmtN(r.t/60,1)}</td>`;}).join('')}</tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table></div></section>`:''}`;}

/* ---------- 3D panel a pohled ---------- */
function mpLegHTML(M,st){const o=S.mp,c={};M.items.forEach(it=>{if(it.k!=='or'||!st[it.id]||(o.floor!=='all'&&it.lv!==+o.floor))return;const k=st[it.id].k;c[k]=(c[k]||0)+1;});
  return [...MST_ORD.filter(k=>k!=='emerg'||c.emerg||o.reg==='emerg'),...(c.off?['off']:[])].map(k=>`<span class="${c[k]?'':'z'}" style="--c:${MST[k].c}"><i></i>${MST[k].n}<b>${c[k]||0}</b></span>`).join('');}
function mpBoardHTML(M,st){const o=S.mp,ors=M.items.filter(i=>i.k==='or').sort((a,b)=>b.lv-a.lv||a.x-b.x);if(!ors.length)return '<p class="hint">Na mapě nejsou žádné sály.</p>';let h='',lv=null;
  for(const it of ors){if(it.lv!==lv){lv=it.lv;h+=`<div class="bd-f">${esc((M.floors.find(f=>f.lv===lv)||{}).name||mpFloorName(M,lv))}</div>`;}const s=st[it.id]||{k:'off'},c=MST[s.k].c,code=it.room&&ROOM[it.room]?ROOM[it.room].code:mpTitle(it,true),sp=s.s||(s.prev&&s.prev.s);
    h+=`<button class="bd-r${o.sel===it.id?' on':''}" data-act="mpFocus" data-v="${it.id}" style="--c:${c}"><i></i><b>${esc(code)}</b><span>${sp?esc(specName(sp)):s.k==='off'?'—':'bez programu'}<small>${esc(s.sub||'')}</small></span><em>${MST[s.k].n}</em></button>`;}
  return `<div class="bd">${h}</div>`;}
function mpEmHTML(M,st){const e=st._em;if(!e||!e.pick)return '<p class="hint">Na mapě chybí urgentní příjem nebo propojené sály.</p>';const p=e.pick,fl=it=>mpFloorName(M,it.lv),code=it=>ROOM[it.room]?ROOM[it.room].code:mpTitle(it,true),why=x=>x.st.k==='free'?'volný':x.st.k==='clean'?`úklid ${Math.ceil(x.w)} min`:x.st.k==='off'?'svolat tým':`výkon · volno za ${Math.ceil(x.w)} min`;
  return `<div class="em-top"><span class="m3-cap">Doporučený sál</span><div class="em-h"><b>SÁL ${esc(code(p.it))}</b><span>${esc(fl(p.it))} · ${esc(why(p))}</span></div>
    <div class="em-k"><div><em>${p.w>.5?Math.ceil(p.w):0}</em><small>min do připravenosti</small></div><div><em>${fmtN(p.tr,1)}</em><small>min převoz z ${esc(mpTitle(e.from,true))}</small></div><div><em>${fmtN(p.r.dist)}</em><small>m trasy${p.r.floors?` · ${p.r.floors} ${plural(p.r.floors,'patro','patra','pater')}`:''}</small></div></div></div>
    <div class="em-l">${e.list.slice(0,6).map(x=>`<button class="em-r${x===p?' on':''}" data-act="mpEmTo" data-v="${x.it.id}" style="--c:${MST[x.st.k].c}"><i></i><b>${esc(code(x.it))}</b><span>${esc(fl(x.it))} · ${esc(why(x))}</span><em>${Math.ceil(x.ready)} min</em></button>`).join('')}</div>
    ${S.mp.emTo?'<button class="btn sm" data-act="mpEmTo" data-v="" style="margin-top:8px">Vybírat automaticky</button>':''}`;}
function mpGanttHTML(M,st){const o=S.mp,tr=M3.tr||mpTRange(M),span=tr[1]-tr[0],P=m=>clampN((m-tr[0])/span*100,0,100),ors=M.items.filter(i=>i.k==='or'&&(o.floor==='all'||i.lv===+o.floor)).sort((a,b)=>b.lv-a.lv||a.x-b.x);if(!ors.length)return '<p class="hint">Na tomto podlaží nejsou sály.</p>';
  const tk=[];for(let m=Math.ceil(tr[0]/120)*120;m<=tr[1];m+=120)tk.push(`<span style="left:${P(m).toFixed(2)}%">${m/60}</span>`);
  return `<div class="gt"><div class="gt-ax"><span></span><div>${tk.join('')}</div></div>${ors.map(it=>{const sc=mpSched(it,o.day),s=st[it.id]||{k:'off'},code=it.room&&ROOM[it.room]?ROOM[it.room].code:mpTitle(it,true);
    const bars=sc.block?`<i class="off" style="left:0;width:100%"></i>`:sc.H&&!sc.closed?sc.segs.map(g=>`<i class="${g.k}" style="left:${P(g.a).toFixed(2)}%;width:${(P(g.b)-P(g.a)).toFixed(2)}%;--c:${MST[g.k].c}" title="${MST[g.k].n} ${hm(g.a)}–${hm(g.b)}${g.s?' · '+esc(g.s):''}"></i>`).join(''):'';
    return `<button class="gt-r${o.sel===it.id?' on':''}" data-act="mpFocus" data-v="${it.id}"><b style="--c:${MST[s.k].c}">${esc(code)}</b><div class="gt-b">${bars}${sc.block?`<em>${esc(sc.block.type)}</em>`:sc.closed?`<em>${esc(sc.why)}</em>`:''}</div></button>`;}).join('')}<div class="gt-now" style="--p:${P(o.t).toFixed(2)}"></div></div>`;}
function mpHyHTML(M,st){const o=S.mp,d=o.day,t=o.t,ors=M.items.filter(i=>i.k==='or'&&i.room&&ROOM[i.room]),now=[],next=[];let cnt=0,mins=0;const code=it=>ROOM[it.room].code;
  ors.forEach(it=>{mpSched(it,d).segs.forEach(g=>{if(g.k!=='clean')return;cnt++;mins+=g.b-g.a;if(t>=g.a&&t<g.b)now.push({it,g});else if(g.a>=t)next.push({it,g});});});next.sort((a,b)=>a.g.a-b.g.a);
  const asn=D.blocks.filter(b=>/asan/i.test(b.type)&&b.to>=d&&b.roomIds.some(r=>ROOM[r]&&ROOM[r].site===o.site)).sort((a,b)=>a.from<b.from?-1:1).slice(0,4);
  return `<div class="hy-k"><div><em>${now.length}</em><small>právě úklid</small></div><div><em>${cnt}</em><small>úklidů za den</small></div><div><em>${fmtN(mins/60,1)}</em><small>hodin úklidu</small></div></div>
    <div class="m3-cap" style="margin:14px 0 6px">Právě probíhá</div>${now.length?now.map(({it,g})=>`<div class="hy-r"><b>${esc(code(it))}</b><span>${hm(g.a)}–${hm(g.b)}</span><u><i style="width:${((t-g.a)/(g.b-g.a)*100).toFixed(0)}%"></i></u></div>`).join(''):'<p class="hint">Žádný sál se právě neuklízí.</p>'}
    <div class="m3-cap" style="margin:14px 0 6px">Další úklidy</div>${next.length?next.slice(0,5).map(({it,g})=>`<div class="hy-r n"><b>${esc(code(it))}</b><span>${hm(g.a)}–${hm(g.b)}</span><em>za ${fmtN(Math.round(g.a-t))} min</em></div>`).join(''):'<p class="hint">Dnes už žádný úklid není naplánovaný.</p>'}
    <div class="m3-cap" style="margin:14px 0 6px">Plánované asanace</div>${asn.length?asn.map(b=>`<div class="hy-r n"><b>${esc(b.roomIds.filter(r=>ROOM[r]&&ROOM[r].site===o.site).map(r=>ROOM[r].code).join(', '))}</b><span>${fmt(b.from)}${b.to!==b.from?` – ${fmt(b.to)}`:''} ${b.from.slice(0,4)}</span></div>`).join(''):'<p class="hint">Žádná asanace není naplánovaná.</p>'}`;}
function mp3dHTML(M){const o=S.mp,fl=mpFloors(M),sel=mpItem(M,o.sel),st=mpStatAll(M),fa=o.floor==='all',fN=fa?null:M.floors.find(f=>f.lv===+o.floor),tr=mpTRange(M);M3.tr=tr;
  const cmpV=o.mode==='cmp'&&o.reg==='std',many=mpBlds(o.site).length>1,ttl=cmpV?`${SITE[o.site].name} · areál`:fN?(fN.name.split(' · ')[1]||fN.name):many?mpCurName():`${SITE[o.site].name} · celá budova`,fNo=fN?mpFloorName(M,fN.lv):'',pct=((o.t-tr[0])/(tr[1]-tr[0])*100).toFixed(2);
  const ticks=[];for(let m=Math.ceil(tr[0]/120)*120;m<=tr[1];m+=120)ticks.push(`<span style="left:${((m-tr[0])/(tr[1]-tr[0])*100).toFixed(2)}%">${m/60}</span>`);
  const rt=o.mode==='route'?(()=>{const a=mpItem(M,o.from),b=mpItem(M,o.to);if(!a||!b)return '<p class="hint">Na mapě chybí stanice nebo sál.</p>';const r=mpRoute(M,a,b),p=r.parts;
      const sx=[[ico('user'),`${esc(mpTitle(a,false))}`,esc(mpFloorName(M,a.lv)),''],[ico('cr'),`Převoz chodbou ${fmtN(r.legs[0].d)} m`,'',fmtMin(p.walk)]];
      if(r.elev){sx.push([ico('layers'),`${esc(r.elev.name)} · ${r.floors} ${plural(r.floors,'patro','patra','pater')}`,`čekání ${fmtMin(p.wait)} + jízda ${fmtMin(p.ride)}`,fmtMin(p.wait+p.ride)]);sx.push([ico('cr'),`Převoz chodbou ${fmtN(r.legs[1].d)} m`,'',fmtMin(p.walk2)]);}
      sx.push([ico('check'),'Převzetí a příprava na sále','',fmtMin(p.handover)],[ico('target'),esc(mpTitle(b,false)),esc(mpFloorName(M,b.lv)),'']);
      return `<div class="rt-total"><span>Celkem</span><b>${fmtN(r.t/60,1)}<small> min</small></b><em>${fmtN(r.dist)} m${r.floors?` · ${r.floors} ${plural(r.floors,'patro','patra','pater')}`:' · stejné podlaží'}</em></div>${r.nc?`<p class="warn-t">${ico('warn')}Část trasy nenavazuje na chodbu – doplňte chodby v editoru.</p>`:''}<ol class="rt-steps">${sx.map(([i,t,s,m])=>`<li>${i}<div><b>${t}</b>${s?`<small>${s}</small>`:''}</div>${m?`<em>${m}</em>`:''}</li>`).join('')}</ol>`;})():'';
  const flowList=o.mode==='flow'?mpFlows(M).filter(f=>!f.pacu).map(f=>({f,t:mpRoute(M,f.a,f.b).t})).sort((a,b)=>b.t-a.t):[];
  const staff=o.mode==='staff'?mpStaffFlows(M):[];
  const ctx=o.reg==='emerg'?`<section class="card m3-em"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Akutní pacient</div><button class="btn sm pri" data-act="mpPlay">${ico('play')}Přehrát převoz</button></div>
        <div class="fld"><label for="mp-emf">Pacient přijíždí z</label><select class="in" id="mp-emf" data-ch="mpEmFrom">${opt(M.items.filter(i=>['er','ward','icu','pacu'].includes(i.k)).map(i=>[i.id,`${mpTitle(i,false)} · ${mpFloorName(M,i.lv)}`]),o.emFrom)}</select></div>
        <div id="m3-em" style="margin-top:12px">${mpEmHTML(M,st)}</div><p class="hint" style="margin-top:10px">Doporučí sál, který bude pro akutní výkon připravený nejdříve – započítá převoz, probíhající výkony i úklid. Sál vyberete i klepnutím v modelu.</p></section>`
    :o.reg==='hyg'?`<section class="card m3-hy"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Hygiena a úklid sálů</div></div><div id="m3-hy">${mpHyHTML(M,st)}</div><p class="hint" style="margin-top:10px">Tyrkysové trasy vedou z centrální sterilizace na sály, které se právě uklízejí nebo připravují.</p></section>`
    :o.mode==='cmp'?mpCmpHTML()
    :o.mode==='overview'?`<section class="card"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Tabule sálů</div></div><div id="m3-board">${mpBoardHTML(M,st)}</div></section><section class="card"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Průběh dne</div><span class="hint">příprava · výkon · úklid</span></div><div id="m3-gantt">${mpGanttHTML(M,st)}</div></section>`
    :o.mode==='route'?`<section class="card"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Transport pacienta</div><button class="btn sm pri" data-act="mpPlay">${ico('play')}Přehrát</button></div>
        <div class="fld"><label for="mp-from">Odkud</label><select class="in" id="mp-from" data-ch="mpFrom">${opt(M.items.filter(i=>['ward','er','icu','pacu'].includes(i.k)).map(i=>[i.id,`${mpTitle(i,false)} · ${mpFloorName(M,i.lv)}`]),o.from)}</select></div>
        <div class="fld" style="margin-top:8px"><label for="mp-to">Kam</label><select class="in" id="mp-to" data-ch="mpTo">${opt(M.items.filter(i=>i.k==='or'||i.k==='pacu'||i.k==='icu').map(i=>[i.id,`${mpTitle(i,false)} · ${mpFloorName(M,i.lv)}`]),o.to)}</select></div>${rt}</section>`
    :o.mode==='flow'?`<section class="card"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Toky pacientů · ${DOW[isoDow(o.day)].toLowerCase()} ${fmt(o.day)}<span class="cnt">${flowList.length}</span></div></div>${flowList.length?`<div class="mp-fl">${flowList.slice(0,9).map(({f,t})=>`<button class="mp-fr" data-act="mpOpen" data-a="${f.a.id}" data-b="${f.b.id}"><i style="--c:${f.c}"></i><span>${esc(mpTitle(f.a,true))} → ${esc(mpTitle(f.b,true))}</span><em>${fmtN(t/60,1)} min</em></button>`).join('')}</div>`:'<p class="hint">V tento den není na pracovišti plánovaná operativa.</p>'}</section>`
    :`<section class="card"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Personál na sálech · ${o.half==='pm'?'odpoledne':'dopoledne'}</div></div>${staff.length?`<div class="mp-fl">${staff.map(x=>`<div class="mp-fr"><i style="--c:${(SPEC[x.h.s]||{}).c}"></i><span>${esc(mpTitle(x.or,true))} · ${esc(x.h.s)} ${x.h.an}</span><em>AL ${fmtN(x.need.doc,1)} · AS ${fmtN(x.need.nur,1)} · SS ${fmtN(x.need.scr,1)}</em></div>`).join('')}</div><p class="hint" style="margin:10px 0 0">Fialové trasy: cesta personálu ze zázemí na sál.</p>`:'<p class="hint">V této části dne není naplánovaná operativa.</p>'}</section>`;
  return `<div class="m3-grid"><section class="card m3-card${o.full?' full':''}">
      <div id="m3-host" class="m3-host"><div class="m3-load"><i></i>${window.THREE?'Připravuji 3D model…':'Načítám 3D model…'}</div></div>
      <div class="m3-ov">
        <div class="m3-top"><div class="m3-ttl"><h3>${esc(ttl)}<span> – 3D přehled</span></h3><p><i class="m3-dot${o.live?' on':''}"></i><span>${o.live?'Aktuální stav operačních sálů v reálném čase':'Stav operačních sálů podle plánu'}</span><span class="m3-sep" aria-hidden="true">·</span><span>${fNo?esc(fNo)+' · ':''}${DOW[isoDow(o.day)]} ${fmt(o.day)}</span></p></div>
          <div class="m3-reg" role="group" aria-label="Režim zobrazení">${MP_REG.map(([v,t])=>`<button data-act="mpReg" data-v="${v}" class="r-${v}${o.reg===v?' on':''}" aria-pressed="${o.reg===v}">${m3I(v)}<span>${t}</span></button>`).join('')}</div></div>
        <div class="m3-view"><span class="m3-cap">3D pohled</span><div class="m3-vb">${[['iso','Prostorový pohled','cube'],['top','Půdorys','plan'],['side','Řez budovou','side']].map(([v,t,i])=>`<button data-act="mpCam" data-v="${v}" title="${t}" aria-label="${t}">${m3I(i)}</button>`).join('')}<button data-act="mpFull" class="${o.full?'on':''}" title="${o.full?'Zmenšit':'Celá obrazovka'}" aria-label="${o.full?'Zmenšit':'Celá obrazovka'}">${m3I(o.full?'shrink':'full')}</button>${o.mode==='route'||o.mode==='cmp'||o.reg==='emerg'?`<button data-act="mpFollow" class="${o.follow?'on':''}" title="Sledovat převoz" aria-label="Sledovat převoz">${ico('target')}</button>`:''}</div>
          <div class="m3-fls"><button data-act="mpFloor" data-v="all" class="${fa?'on':''}" title="Celá budova">Vše</button>${[...fl].reverse().map(f=>`<button data-act="mpFloor" data-v="${f.lv}" class="${o.floor===String(f.lv)?'on':''}" title="${esc(f.name)}">${esc(mpFloorName(M,f.lv).replace(/\.? NP$/,''))}</button>`).join('')}</div></div>
        <div class="m3-bot"><div class="m3-leg" id="m3-leg">${mpLegHTML(M,st)}</div>
          <div class="m3-time"><button class="m3-tb${o.play?' on':''}" data-act="mpTime" data-v="play" aria-label="${o.play?'Pozastavit průběh dne':'Přehrát průběh dne'}" title="${o.play?'Pozastavit':'Přehrát průběh dne'}">${m3I(o.play?'pause':'play')}</button><button class="m3-live${o.live?' on':''}" data-act="mpTime" data-v="live" title="Aktuální čas"><i></i>Živě</button>
            <div class="m3-tr"><input type="range" id="m3-ts" min="${tr[0]}" max="${tr[1]}" step="1" value="${Math.round(o.t)}" style="--p:${pct}%" aria-label="Čas dne"><div class="m3-tk">${ticks.join('')}</div></div><b id="m3-clock">${hm(o.t)}</b></div></div>
        <div class="m3-help">Tažením otáčíte · pravým tlačítkem posouváte · kolečkem přibližujete</div>
      </div></section>
    <aside class="m3-side">
      <section class="card"><div class="card-h" style="margin-bottom:10px"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Analýza</div></div><div class="seg wide">${MP_MODES.map(([v,t])=>`<button data-act="mpMode" data-v="${v}" class="${o.mode===v?'on':''}">${t}</button>`).join('')}</div><p class="hint" style="margin:10px 0 12px">${o.reg!=='std'?(o.reg==='emerg'?'Režim Emergency: model ukazuje nejrychlejší cestu akutního pacienta na sál. Ostatní sály jsou ztlumené.':'Režim Hygiena: zvýrazněné jsou sály v úklidu a asanace, ostatní jsou ztlumené.'):MP_DESC[o.mode]}</p>
        <div class="frow"><label class="flab" for="mp-day">Den</label>${dateField('mp-day',o.day,'data-mp="day"')}${o.mode==='staff'?`<div class="seg">${[['am','Dop'],['pm','Odp']].map(([v,t])=>`<button data-act="mpHalf" data-v="${v}" class="${o.half===v?'on':''}">${t}</button>`).join('')}</div>`:''}</div>
        ${fa?`<div class="fld" style="margin-top:12px"><label for="mp-ex">Rozložení pater</label><input type="range" id="mp-ex" min="1" max="2.6" step=".05" value="${o.ex}" data-ch="mpEx"></div>`:''}</section>
      ${ctx}
      ${sel?mpSelHTML(M,sel,st):''}
      <section class="card"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Legenda</div></div><div class="mp-lg">${Object.keys(MST).map(k=>`<span><i class="st" style="--c:${MST[k].c}"></i>${MST[k].n}</span>`).join('')}<span><i class="ar" style="--c:#5EC8E4"></i>trasa transportu</span><span><i class="ar" style="--c:#FF3C55"></i>akutní převoz</span><span><i class="ar" style="--c:#27D6EA"></i>sterilizace a úklid</span><span><i class="ar" style="--c:#A58BFF"></i>pohyb personálu</span><span><i class="ar" style="--c:${mpXC('amb')}"></i>trasa sanitky</span><span><i class="ar" style="--c:${mpXC('heli')}"></i>heliport a lávka</span></div><p class="hint" style="margin:10px 0 0">Stav sálu vychází z plánu operativy a pracovní doby: příprava, výkon a úklid se střídají podle typu anestezie. Barevné šipky v režimu Tok pacientů mají barvu oboru.</p></section>
    </aside></div>`;}
function mpSelHTML(M,it,st){const d=S.mp.day;let body='';
  if(it.k==='or'&&it.room&&ROOM[it.room]){const r=R(ROOM[it.room],d),s=(st||{})[it.id];body=(s?`<div class="sel-st" style="--c:${MST[s.k].c}"><i></i><b>${MST[s.k].n}</b><span>${esc(s.sub||'')}</span></div>`:'')+`<div class="mp-hs">${[0,1].map(i=>{const h=r.h[i];return h?`<span class="hp ${h.an}" style="--c:${(SPEC[h.s]||{}).c}">${i?'odp':'dop'} · ${esc(h.s)}${h.released?' (uvolněno)':''}</span>`:`<span class="hp free">${i?'odp':'dop'} · volno</span>`;}).join('')}</div>`;
    const sc=mpSched(it,d),cl=[];let cur=null;sc.segs.forEach(g=>{if(g.n==null){cur=null;return;}if(g.k==='prep'||!cur){cur={s:g.s,an:g.an,a:g.a,b:g.b,segs:[]};cl.push(cur);}cur.segs.push(g);cur.b=g.b;});const t=S.mp.t;
    if(cl.length)body+=`<div class="m3-cap" style="margin:4px 0 6px">Výkony dne</div><ol class="cs">${cl.map((c,i)=>{const op=c.segs.find(g=>g.k==='op'),now=t>=c.a&&t<c.b,done=t>=c.b;return `<li class="${now?'now':done?'done':''}" style="--c:${(SPEC[c.s]||{}).c||'#8aa'}"><i></i><b>${i+1}. výkon · ${esc(c.s)} · ${esc(c.an)}</b><span>${hm(op?op.a:c.a)}–${hm(op?op.b:c.b)}</span><em>${now?'probíhá':done?'hotovo':'plán'}</em></li>`;}).join('')}</ol>`;
    const sp=[...new Set(r.h.filter(h=>h&&!h.released).map(h=>h.s))];body+=sp.map(s=>{const w=mpNearWard(M,s,it);if(!w)return '';const rt=mpRoute(M,w,it);return `<button class="mp-fr" data-act="mpOpen" data-a="${w.id}" data-b="${it.id}"><i style="--c:${(SPEC[s]||{}).c}"></i><span>${esc(mpTitle(w,true))}</span><em>${fmtN(rt.t/60,1)} min</em></button>`;}).join('');}
  else if(it.k==='ward'){const ors=M.items.filter(x=>x.k==='or'&&x.room).map(x=>({x,t:mpRoute(M,it,x).t})).sort((a,b)=>a.t-b.t).slice(0,5);body=`<p class="hint" style="margin:0 0 8px">Obory: ${esc((it.specs||[]).map(specName).join(', ')||'—')} · ${it.beds||0} lůžek</p>${ors.map(({x,t})=>`<button class="mp-fr" data-act="mpOpen" data-a="${it.id}" data-b="${x.id}"><i style="--c:${mpColor(x,d)}"></i><span>${esc(mpTitle(x,true))} · ${esc(mpFloorName(M,x.lv))}</span><em>${fmtN(t/60,1)} min</em></button>`).join('')}`;}
  else body=`<p class="hint" style="margin:0">${esc(MK[it.k].n)} · ${it.w*MCELL} × ${it.h*MCELL} m</p>`;
  return `<section class="card"><div class="card-h"><div><div class="eyebrow"><i></i>${esc(MK[it.k].n)} · ${esc(it.k==='elev'?`${mpFloorName(M,it.lv)}–${mpFloorName(M,it.lv1??it.lv)}`:mpFloorName(M,it.lv))}</div><h3 class="bc-st-h">${esc(mpTitle(it,false))}</h3></div><button class="icon-btn sm" data-act="mpSel" data-v="" aria-label="Zavřít detail">${ico('x')}</button></div>${body}${isCoord()?`<div class="btns" style="margin-top:10px"><button class="btn sm" data-act="mpEditIt" data-v="${it.id}">${ico('edit')}Upravit v editoru</button></div>`:''}</section>`;}
function vMap(){const o=mpInit(),M=mapCur(),st=mpStats(o.site),MM=mapOf(o.site),nB=mpBlds(o.site).length;
  const seg=`<div class="seg">${SITES.map(x=>`<button data-act="mpSite" data-v="${x.id}" class="${o.site===x.id?'on':''}">${x.name}</button>`).join('')}</div>`;
  return ph('Provoz','Operační sály 3D',seg)+`
  <div class="kpis k4">
    ${kpi('Sály na mapě',st.placed,`z ${st.rooms}`,st.missing.length?`chybí ${esc(st.missing.join(', '))}`:'všechny sály pracoviště jsou umístěné','var(--dot)')}
    ${kpi('Průměrný transport',st.avg?fmtN(st.avg/60,1):'—','min','ze stanice oboru na sál podle týdenní šablony','#86A0C2')}
    ${kpi('Nejdelší transport',st.max?fmtN(st.max.t/60,1):'—','min',st.max?esc(`${st.max.from} → ${st.max.to}`):'—',st.max&&st.max.t>480?'var(--bad)':st.max&&st.max.t>300?'var(--warn)':'#CF95A6')}
    ${kpi('Podlaží a výtahy',M.floors.length,`· ${st.elev} ${plural(st.elev,'výtah','výtahy','výtahů')}`,`${st.wards} ${plural(st.wards,'lůžková stanice','lůžkové stanice','lůžkových stanic')}${nB>1?` · areál ${nB} ${plural(nB,'budova','budovy','budov')}`:''}${MM.heli.length?` · ${MM.heli.length} ${plural(MM.heli.length,'heliport','heliporty','heliportů')}`:''}`,'#D6A06E')}
  </div>
  <div class="st-tabs"><div class="seg">${[...(o.site==='LB'?[['map','Mapa areálu']]:[]),['3d','Sály 3D'],['ed','Editor plánu'],['dist','Vzdálenosti a časy']].map(([v,t])=>`<button data-act="mpTab" data-v="${v}" class="${o.tab===v?'on':''}">${t}</button>`).join('')}</div><span class="hint">${o.tab==='map'?'Rozmístění pavilonů a oddělení podle mapy areálu nemocnice.':'Ukázkové rozvržení – upravte ho v editoru podle skutečné budovy.'}</span>${isCoord()&&o.tab==='ed'?`<span class="sp"></span><button class="btn sm${S.confirm==='mpreset'?' warn':''}" data-act="mpReset">${ico('reset')}${S.confirm==='mpreset'?'Opravdu obnovit ukázkový plán?':'Obnovit ukázkový plán'}</button>`:''}</div>
  ${mpBldBar()}
  ${o.tab==='map'?kmHTML():o.tab==='ed'?(o.cmp?mpCampusHTML():mpEditorHTML(M)):o.tab==='dist'?mpDistHTML(M):mp3dHTML(M)}`;}


/* ---------- areál: budovy, heliporty, spojovací chodby a trasy sanitky ---------- */
const MP_MAIN='main',MFLOOR=4,MCS=6,MP_XC={amb:['#F5B83D','#B8770B'],heli:['#7FD3C6','#25867A']};
const mpLT=()=>(S.theme||'light')!=='dark';
const mpXC=k=>MP_XC[k][mpLT()?1:0];
function mpCampusSeed(site){if(site!=='LB')return {blds:[],amb:[],heli:[],links:[]};
  const id='bLBG',it=[];let n=0;const add=(k,lv,x,y,w,h,o={})=>it.push({id:`${id}i${++n}`,k,lv,x,y,w,h,name:o.name||MK[k].n,...o});
  const F=[{lv:0,name:'1. NP · Příjem a interní JIP'},{lv:1,name:'2. NP · Interní lůžka'}];
  F.forEach(f=>{add('hall',f.lv,1,8,28,2,{name:'Chodba'});add('stair',f.lv,13,3,3,5,{name:'Schodiště'});});add('elev',0,17,3,3,5,{name:'Výtah B',lv1:1});
  add('er',0,1,1,11,7,{name:'Vstup a příjem pavilonu B',beds:4});add('staff',0,21,1,8,7,{name:'Zázemí personálu'});add('icu',0,1,10,14,7,{name:'Interní JIP',beds:10});add('other',0,16,10,13,7,{name:'Laboratoř a vyšetřovny'});
  add('other',1,1,1,11,7,{name:'Interní lůžka A'});add('other',1,21,1,8,7,{name:'Zázemí personálu'});add('other',1,1,10,28,7,{name:'Interní lůžka B'});
  return {blds:[{id,name:'Pavilon B – interní obory',pos:{x:98,y:8},W:30,H:18,floors:F,items:it}],
    amb:[{id:'aLB1',name:'Pavilon B → urgentní příjem',a:id,b:MP_MAIN,pts:[[106,26],[106,33],[38,33],[38,40]],note:'Areálová komunikace s vjezdem k urgentnímu příjmu'}],
    heli:[{id:'hLB1',name:'Heliport',pos:{x:84,y:44},sz:12,alt:16}],
    links:[{id:'lLB1',name:'Lávka z heliportu',a:{p:'hLB1'},b:{p:MP_MAIN,lv:4}}]};}
function mpBlds(site){const M=mapOf(site);return [{id:MP_MAIN,name:M.bname,M},...M.blds.map(b=>({id:b.id,name:b.name,M:b}))];}
function mpCurId(){const L=mpBlds(S.mp.site);return (L.find(b=>b.id===S.mp.bld)||L[0]).id;}
function mapCur(){const L=mpBlds(S.mp.site);return (L.find(b=>b.id===S.mp.bld)||L[0]).M;}
function mpCurName(){const L=mpBlds(S.mp.site);return (L.find(b=>b.id===S.mp.bld)||L[0]).name;}
function mpPlaces(site){const M=mapOf(site);return [...mpBlds(site).map(b=>({t:'bld',id:b.id,name:b.name,x:b.M.pos.x,y:b.M.pos.y,w:b.M.W,h:b.M.H,M:b.M,top:b.M.floors.length*MFLOOR})),...M.heli.map(h=>({t:'heli',id:h.id,name:h.name,x:h.pos.x,y:h.pos.y,w:h.sz,h:h.sz,H:h,top:h.alt}))];}
const mpPlace=(site,id)=>mpPlaces(site).find(p=>p.id===id)||null;
function mpEdge(p,x,y){const x0=p.x,y0=p.y,x1=p.x+p.w,y1=p.y+p.h,cx=clampN(x,x0,x1),cy=clampN(y,y0,y1);if(x<x0||x>x1||y<y0||y>y1)return [cx,cy];
  const d=[[cx-x0,'l'],[x1-cx,'r'],[cy-y0,'t'],[y1-cy,'b']].sort((a,b)=>a[0]-b[0])[0][1];return d==='l'?[x0,cy]:d==='r'?[x1,cy]:d==='t'?[cx,y0]:[cx,y1];}
const mpIn=(p,x,y)=>x>=p.x&&x<=p.x+p.w&&y>=p.y&&y<=p.y+p.h;
function mpEndAlt(P,e){if(P.t==='heli')return P.H.alt;const fi=mpFloorIdx(P.M);return (fi[e&&e.lv]??0)*MFLOOR;}
function mpLinkEnds(site,L){const A=mpPlace(site,L.a.p),B=mpPlace(site,L.b.p);if(!A||!B||A===B)return null;let pa,pb;
  const ox0=Math.max(A.x,B.x),ox1=Math.min(A.x+A.w,B.x+B.w),oy0=Math.max(A.y,B.y),oy1=Math.min(A.y+A.h,B.y+B.h);
  if(ox1-ox0>=2&&(A.y+A.h<=B.y||B.y+B.h<=A.y)){const x=(ox0+ox1)/2;if(A.y+A.h<=B.y){pa=[x,A.y+A.h];pb=[x,B.y];}else{pa=[x,A.y];pb=[x,B.y+B.h];}}
  else if(oy1-oy0>=2&&(A.x+A.w<=B.x||B.x+B.w<=A.x)){const y=(oy0+oy1)/2;if(A.x+A.w<=B.x){pa=[A.x+A.w,y];pb=[B.x,y];}else{pa=[A.x,y];pb=[B.x+B.w,y];}}
  else{pa=mpEdge(A,B.x+B.w/2,B.y+B.h/2);pb=mpEdge(B,pa[0],pa[1]);}
  const za=mpEndAlt(A,L.a),zb=mpEndAlt(B,L.b);return {A,B,pa,pb,za,zb,len:Math.hypot(pb[0]-pa[0],pb[1]-pa[1])*MCELL};}
function mpAmbLen(r){let d=0;for(let i=1;i<r.pts.length;i++)d+=Math.hypot(r.pts[i][0]-r.pts[i-1][0],r.pts[i][1]-r.pts[i-1][1]);return d*MCELL;}
function mpXfers(site){const M=mapOf(site),C=D.mapCfg,out=[];
  M.amb.forEach(r=>{const A=mpPlace(site,r.a),B=mpPlace(site,r.b);if(!A||!B||r.pts.length<2)return;const len=mpAmbLen(r);out.push({id:r.id,k:'amb',name:r.name,A,B,len,r,parts:[['Naložení pacienta',C.ambLoad/2],['Jízda areálem',len/(C.amb/3.6)],['Vyložení a předání',C.ambLoad/2]]});});
  M.links.forEach(L=>{const e=mpLinkEnds(site,L);if(!e)return;const H=e.A.t==='heli'?e.A:e.B.t==='heli'?e.B:null;if(!H)return;const to=H===e.A?e.B:e.A;
    out.push({id:L.id,k:'heli',name:`${H.name} → ${to.name}`,A:H,B:to,len:e.len,L,e,parts:[['Přistání a vyložení',C.heliLand],['Převoz spojovací chodbou',e.len/C.speed],['Předání v budově',60]]});});
  out.forEach(x=>x.t=x.parts.reduce((s,p)=>s+p[1],0));return out;}
function mpBldReset(){const o=S.mp;o.floor=null;o.sel=null;o.from=null;o.to=null;o.ed=null;o.emFrom=null;o.emTo=null;}
function mpCampusDims(site){const M=mapOf(site);let x0=1e9,y0=1e9,x1=-1e9,y1=-1e9;const add=(x,y)=>{x0=Math.min(x0,x);y0=Math.min(y0,y);x1=Math.max(x1,x);y1=Math.max(y1,y);};mpPlaces(site).forEach(p=>{add(p.x,p.y);add(p.x+p.w,p.y+p.h);});M.amb.forEach(r=>r.pts.forEach(([x,y])=>add(x,y)));
  const m=16;x0=Math.max(0,Math.floor(x0-m));y0=Math.max(0,Math.floor(y0-m));x1=Math.ceil(x1+m);y1=Math.ceil(y1+m);if(x1-x0<80)x1=x0+80;if(y1-y0<50)y1=y0+50;return {X:x0,Y:y0,W:x1,H:y1};}
function mpFreeSpot(site,w,h){const P=mpPlaces(site),{W,H}=mpCampusDims(site);for(let y=4;y<H+60;y+=4)for(let x=4;x<W+60;x+=4){if(P.every(p=>x+w+4<=p.x||x>=p.x+p.w+4||y+h+4<=p.y||y>=p.y+p.h+4))return {x,y};}return {x:W+4,y:4};}

/* ---------- přepínač budov ---------- */
function mpBldBar(){const o=S.mp,L=mpBlds(o.site),ed=o.tab==='ed',cur=mpCurId();if(o.tab==='map'||(L.length<2&&!ed))return '';
  return `<div class="mp-bb"><span class="flab">Budova</span><div class="seg">${ed?`<button data-act="mpCampus" class="${o.cmp?'on':''}">Areál</button>`:''}${L.map(b=>`<button data-act="mpBld" data-v="${b.id}" class="${!(ed&&o.cmp)&&cur===b.id?'on':''}">${esc(b.name)}</button>`).join('')}</div>${ed&&isCoord()?`<button class="btn sm" data-act="mpBldAdd">${ico('plus')}Přidat budovu</button>`:''}</div>`;}
function mpBldCard(){const o=S.mp,id=mpCurId(),M=mapCur(),ed=isCoord(),n=mpBlds(o.site).length;
  return `<section class="card"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Budova</div>${ed&&id!==MP_MAIN?`<button class="btn sm${S.confirm==='bdel:'+id?' warn':''}" data-act="mpBldDel" data-v="${id}">${S.confirm==='bdel:'+id?'Opravdu odebrat i s plánem?':'Odebrat budovu'}</button>`:''}</div>
    <div class="frow"><label class="flab" for="mp-bn">Název</label><input class="in" id="mp-bn" data-ch="mpBldName" data-id="${id}" value="${esc(mpCurName())}" style="flex:1;min-width:200px"${ed?'':' disabled'}></div>
    <p class="hint" style="margin:8px 0 0">${M.W*MCELL} × ${M.H*MCELL} m · ${M.floors.length} ${plural(M.floors.length,'podlaží','podlaží','podlaží')}${n>1?' · umístění budovy v areálu změníte v části Areál':''}</p></section>`;}

/* ---------- editor areálu ---------- */
const MC_TOOLS=[['sel','Výběr a přesun'],['amb','Trasa sanitky'],['heli','Heliport'],['link','Spojovací chodba']];
const MC_HINT={sel:'Budovy a heliporty přesunete tažením. U vybrané trasy sanitky táhněte body trasy.',amb:'Klepněte na budovu nebo heliport, odkud sanitka vyjíždí, pak klepáním přidávejte body cesty a skončete klepnutím na cílovou budovu. Esc kreslení zruší, Backspace vrátí poslední bod.',heli:'Klepnutím do plánu umístíte heliport. Výšku přistávací plochy nastavíte vpravo – může stát na zemi, na pilířích nebo na střeše budovy.',link:'Klepněte na první a pak na druhou budovu nebo heliport. Podlaží, kam chodba ústí, nastavíte vpravo.'};
let CD=null;
function mpCampusSVG(site){const o=S.mp,M=mapOf(site),{X:VX,Y:VY,W,H}=mpCampusDims(site),P=mpPlaces(site),cs=MCS,cur=mpCurId(),sel=o.csel||{};
  let s=`<svg id="mpc" class="mpc m2d" viewBox="${VX*cs} ${VY*cs} ${(W-VX)*cs} ${(H-VY)*cs}" role="img" aria-label="Plán areálu"><defs><pattern id="mcg" width="${cs*5}" height="${cs*5}" patternUnits="userSpaceOnUse"><path d="M ${cs*5} 0 L 0 0 0 ${cs*5}" class="m2-gl"/></pattern><pattern id="mcg25" width="${cs*25}" height="${cs*25}" patternUnits="userSpaceOnUse"><rect width="${cs*25}" height="${cs*25}" fill="url(#mcg)"/><path d="M ${cs*25} 0 L 0 0 0 ${cs*25}" class="m2-gl5"/></pattern></defs><rect x="${VX*cs}" y="${VY*cs}" width="${(W-VX)*cs}" height="${(H-VY)*cs}" class="mc-bg"/><rect x="${VX*cs}" y="${VY*cs}" width="${(W-VX)*cs}" height="${(H-VY)*cs}" fill="url(#mcg25)"/>`;
  const X=mpXfers(site);
  M.amb.forEach(r=>{if(r.pts.length<2)return;const pts=r.pts.map(([x,y])=>`${x*cs},${y*cs}`).join(' '),on=sel.t==='amb'&&sel.id===r.id;s+=`<g class="mc-rd${on?' on':''}" data-rid="${r.id}"><polyline class="mc-road" points="${pts}"/><polyline class="mc-rc" points="${pts}"/></g>`;});
  P.filter(p=>p.t==='bld').forEach(p=>{const on=sel.t==='bld'&&sel.id===p.id,f0=mpFloors(p.M)[0].lv,g0=p.M.items.filter(i=>i.lv===f0&&i.k!=='hall'&&i.k!=='elev'&&i.k!=='stair');
    s+=`<g class="mc-b${on?' on':''}${p.id===cur?' cur':''}" data-pid="${p.id}" transform="translate(${p.x*cs},${p.y*cs})"><rect class="mc-br" width="${p.w*cs}" height="${p.h*cs}" rx="6"/>${g0.map(i=>`<rect class="mc-bi${i.k==='er'?' er':''}" x="${i.x*cs+1.5}" y="${i.y*cs+1.5}" width="${Math.max(1,i.w*cs-3)}" height="${Math.max(1,i.h*cs-3)}" rx="2"/>`).join('')}<text class="mc-bt" x="${p.w*cs/2}" y="${p.h*cs/2-3}">${esc(p.name)}</text><text class="mc-bs" x="${p.w*cs/2}" y="${p.h*cs/2+13}">${p.M.floors.length} ${plural(p.M.floors.length,'podlaží','podlaží','podlaží')} · ${p.w*MCELL} × ${p.h*MCELL} m</text></g>`;});
  M.links.forEach(L=>{const e=mpLinkEnds(site,L);if(!e)return;const on=sel.t==='link'&&sel.id===L.id,[ax,ay]=e.pa,[bx,by]=e.pb;s+=`<g class="mc-l${on?' on':''}" data-lid="${L.id}"><line class="mc-lw" x1="${ax*cs}" y1="${ay*cs}" x2="${bx*cs}" y2="${by*cs}"/><line class="mc-lc" x1="${ax*cs}" y1="${ay*cs}" x2="${bx*cs}" y2="${by*cs}"/></g>`;});
  P.filter(p=>p.t==='heli').forEach(p=>{const on=sel.t==='heli'&&sel.id===p.id,r=p.w*cs/2,roof=P.find(b=>b.t==='bld'&&mpIn(b,p.x+p.w/2,p.y+p.h/2));
    s+=`<g class="mc-h${on?' on':''}" data-pid="${p.id}" transform="translate(${p.x*cs},${p.y*cs})"><circle class="mc-hc" cx="${r}" cy="${r}" r="${r-1}"/><circle class="mc-hr" cx="${r}" cy="${r}" r="${r*.68}"/><text class="mc-hh" x="${r}" y="${r+1}">H</text><text class="mc-hs" x="${r}" y="${p.w*cs+13}">${esc(p.name)} · ${fmtN(p.H.alt)} m${roof?' · střecha':''}</text></g>`;});
  X.filter(x=>x.k==='amb').forEach(x=>{const r=x.r,on=sel.t==='amb'&&sel.id===r.id;let bi=1,bl=0;for(let i=1;i<r.pts.length;i++){const l=Math.hypot(r.pts[i][0]-r.pts[i-1][0],r.pts[i][1]-r.pts[i-1][1]);if(l>bl){bl=l;bi=i;}}const mx=(r.pts[bi][0]+r.pts[bi-1][0])/2*cs,my=(r.pts[bi][1]+r.pts[bi-1][1])/2*cs;
    s+=`<g class="mc-tag${on?' on':''}" data-rid="${r.id}"><rect x="${mx-58}" y="${my-11}" width="116" height="22" rx="11"/><text x="${mx}" y="${my+1}">${fmtN(x.len)} m · ${fmtN(x.t/60,1)} min</text></g>`;
    [r.pts[0],r.pts[r.pts.length-1]].forEach(([x0,y0],i)=>s+=`<circle class="mc-end${i?' b':''}" cx="${x0*cs}" cy="${y0*cs}" r="6"/>`);
    if(on&&isCoord())r.pts.forEach(([x0,y0],i)=>s+=`<circle class="mc-vx" data-vi="${i}" cx="${x0*cs}" cy="${y0*cs}" r="7"/>`);});
  return s+'</svg>';}
function mpCampusHTML(){const o=S.mp,site=o.site,ed=isCoord();if(!MC_TOOLS.some(t=>t[0]===o.ctool))o.ctool='sel';
  return `<div class="m2-grid"><div class="stack" style="gap:16px"><section class="card m2-card">${ed?`<div class="m2-tools">${MC_TOOLS.map(([k,t])=>`<button class="chip sm${o.ctool===k?' on':''}" data-act="mpCTool" data-v="${k}">${t}</button>`).join('')}</div><p class="hint" id="mc-hint" style="margin:0 0 10px">${MC_HINT[o.ctool]}</p>`:'<p class="hint" style="margin:0 0 10px">Areál upravuje koordinátor operativy.</p>'}
      <div class="ovx"><div class="m2-wrap mc-wrap${o.ctool!=='sel'?' draw':''}">${mpCampusSVG(site)}</div></div>
      <p class="hint" style="margin:10px 0 0">1 čtverec = ${MCELL*5} × ${MCELL*5} m · silná mřížka po ${MCELL*25} m · výška podlaží v areálu ${MFLOOR} m · plán budovy upravíte po výběru budovy nahoře.</p></section></div>
    <div class="stack" style="gap:16px">${mpCampusSide(site)}</div></div>`;}
function mpCampusSide(site){const o=S.mp,M=mapOf(site),sel=o.csel,ed=isCoord(),dis=ed?'':' disabled',P=mpPlaces(site),X=mpXfers(site),C=D.mapCfg;let det='';
  const plOpt=(cur)=>P.map(p=>[p.id,p.name]);
  if(sel&&sel.t==='bld'){const p=P.find(x=>x.id===sel.id);if(p)det=`<section class="card"><div class="card-h"><div><div class="eyebrow"><i></i>Budova</div><h3 class="bc-st-h">${esc(p.name)}</h3></div><button class="icon-btn sm" data-act="mpCSel" data-v="" aria-label="Zavřít">${ico('x')}</button></div>
      <div class="fld"><label for="mc-bn">Název budovy</label><input class="in" id="mc-bn" data-ch="mpBldName" data-id="${p.id}" value="${esc(p.name)}"${dis}></div>

      <div class="btns" style="margin-top:10px"><button class="btn sm pri" data-act="mpBld" data-v="${p.id}">${ico('edit')}Upravit plán budovy</button>${ed&&p.id!==MP_MAIN?`<button class="btn sm${S.confirm==='bdel:'+p.id?' warn':''}" data-act="mpBldDel" data-v="${p.id}">${S.confirm==='bdel:'+p.id?'Opravdu odebrat?':'Odebrat'}</button>`:''}</div></section>`;}
  if(sel&&sel.t==='heli'){const p=P.find(x=>x.id===sel.id);if(p){const h=p.H,roof=P.find(b=>b.t==='bld'&&mpIn(b,p.x+p.w/2,p.y+p.h/2)),ls=M.links.filter(L=>L.a.p===h.id||L.b.p===h.id);
    det=`<section class="card"><div class="card-h"><div><div class="eyebrow"><i></i>Heliport</div><h3 class="bc-st-h">${esc(h.name)}</h3></div><button class="icon-btn sm" data-act="mpCSel" data-v="" aria-label="Zavřít">${ico('x')}</button></div>

      <div class="grid-f" style="margin:10px 0 0"><div class="fld"><label for="mc-ha">Výška plochy nad terénem</label><div class="mp-in"><input class="in" type="number" id="mc-ha" min="0" max="120" step="1" data-ch="mpHeliF" data-k="alt" data-id="${h.id}" value="${h.alt}"${dis}><span>m</span></div></div>
        <div class="fld"><label for="mc-hs">Průměr plochy</label><div class="mp-in"><input class="in" type="number" id="mc-hs" min="16" max="40" step="2" data-ch="mpHeliF" data-k="sz" data-id="${h.id}" value="${h.sz*MCELL}"${dis}><span>m</span></div></div></div>
      <div class="seg" style="margin-top:8px">${[0,8,16,24,32].map(v=>`<button data-act="mpHeliAlt" data-id="${h.id}" data-v="${v}" class="${h.alt===v?'on':''}"${ed?'':' disabled'}>${v?v+' m':'terén'}</button>`).join('')}${roof?`<button data-act="mpHeliAlt" data-id="${h.id}" data-v="${roof.top}" class="${h.alt===roof.top?'on':''}"${ed?'':' disabled'}>střecha ${roof.top} m</button>`:''}</div>
      <p class="hint" style="margin:10px 0 0">${roof?(h.alt>=roof.top?`Stojí na střeše budovy ${esc(roof.name)}.`:`Plocha je níž než střecha budovy ${esc(roof.name)} (${roof.top} m) – zvyšte ji, nebo heliport přesuňte.`):h.alt>0?'Samostatná plošina na pilířích.':'Plocha na úrovni terénu.'} ${ls.length?`Spojovací chodby: ${ls.map(L=>esc(L.name)).join(', ')}.`:'Zatím bez spojovací chodby – přidáte ji nástrojem Spojovací chodba.'}</p>
      ${ed?`<div class="btns" style="margin-top:10px"><button class="btn sm${S.confirm==='hdel:'+h.id?' warn':''}" data-act="mpHeliDel" data-v="${h.id}">${S.confirm==='hdel:'+h.id?'Opravdu odebrat?':'Odebrat heliport'}</button></div>`:''}</section>`;}}
  if(sel&&sel.t==='amb'){const r=M.amb.find(x=>x.id===sel.id),x=X.find(y=>y.id===sel.id);if(r)det=`<section class="card"><div class="card-h"><div><div class="eyebrow"><i></i>Trasa sanitky</div><h3 class="bc-st-h">${esc(r.name)}</h3></div><button class="icon-btn sm" data-act="mpCSel" data-v="" aria-label="Zavřít">${ico('x')}</button></div>
      <div class="fld"><label for="mc-an">Název trasy</label><input class="in" id="mc-an" data-ch="mpAmbF" data-k="name" data-id="${r.id}" value="${esc(r.name)}"${dis}></div>
      <div class="fld" style="margin-top:8px"><label for="mc-ao">Poznámka</label><input class="in" id="mc-ao" data-ch="mpAmbF" data-k="note" data-id="${r.id}" value="${esc(r.note||'')}" placeholder="Např. vjezd přes závoru, couvání k rampě"${dis}></div>
      ${x?mpXferSteps(x):'<p class="warn-t">Trasa nemá platný začátek nebo cíl.</p>'}
      <div class="btns" style="margin-top:10px">${x?`<button class="btn sm pri" data-act="mpCxPlay" data-v="${r.id}">${ico('play')}Přehrát ve 3D</button>`:''}${ed?`<button class="btn sm${S.confirm==='adel:'+r.id?' warn':''}" data-act="mpAmbDel" data-v="${r.id}">${S.confirm==='adel:'+r.id?'Opravdu smazat?':'Smazat trasu'}</button>`:''}</div></section>`;}
  if(sel&&sel.t==='link'){const L=M.links.find(x=>x.id===sel.id),e=L&&mpLinkEnds(site,L);if(L){const end=(k)=>{const p=mpPlace(site,L[k].p);if(!p)return '—';return p.t==='bld'?`<div class="frow"><span class="flab">${k==='a'?'Z':'Do'}</span><b style="font-weight:600">${esc(p.name)}</b><select class="in" data-ch="mpLinkLv" data-id="${L.id}" data-k="${k}" aria-label="Podlaží" style="width:auto"${dis}>${opt(mpFloors(p.M).map(f=>[String(f.lv),`${mpFloorName(p.M,f.lv)} · ${(mpFloorIdx(p.M)[f.lv])*MFLOOR} m`]),String(L[k].lv??mpFloors(p.M)[0].lv))}</select></div>`:`<div class="frow"><span class="flab">${k==='a'?'Z':'Do'}</span><b style="font-weight:600">${esc(p.name)}</b><span class="hint">plocha ve výšce ${fmtN(p.H.alt)} m</span></div>`;};
    det=`<section class="card"><div class="card-h"><div><div class="eyebrow"><i></i>Spojovací chodba</div><h3 class="bc-st-h">${esc(L.name)}</h3></div><button class="icon-btn sm" data-act="mpCSel" data-v="" aria-label="Zavřít">${ico('x')}</button></div>
      <div class="fld"><label for="mc-ln">Název</label><input class="in" id="mc-ln" data-ch="mpLinkF" data-id="${L.id}" value="${esc(L.name)}"${dis}></div>
      <div class="stack" style="gap:8px;margin-top:10px">${end('a')}${end('b')}</div>
      ${e?`<p class="hint" style="margin:10px 0 0">Délka ${fmtN(e.len)} m · převoz lůžka asi ${fmtN(e.len/C.speed/60,1)} min${Math.abs(e.za-e.zb)>.1?` · výškový rozdíl ${fmtN(Math.abs(e.za-e.zb))} m (rampa, sklon ${fmtN(Math.abs(e.za-e.zb)/Math.max(1,e.len)*100)} %)`:' · bez výškového rozdílu'}</p>${Math.abs(e.za-e.zb)/Math.max(1,e.len)>.06?`<p class="warn-t">${ico('warn')}Sklon nad 6 % je pro převoz lůžka příliš strmý – zvolte jiné podlaží nebo výšku heliportu.</p>`:''}`:''}
      ${ed?`<div class="btns" style="margin-top:10px"><button class="btn sm${S.confirm==='ldel:'+L.id?' warn':''}" data-act="mpLinkDel" data-v="${L.id}">${S.confirm==='ldel:'+L.id?'Opravdu odebrat?':'Odebrat chodbu'}</button></div>`:''}</section>`;}}
  const row=(t,id,c,name,meta)=>`<button class="mp-fr${sel&&sel.t===t&&sel.id===id?' on':''}" data-act="mpCSel" data-v="${t}:${id}"><i style="--c:${c}"></i><span>${esc(name)}</span><em>${meta}</em></button>`;
  const list=`<section class="card"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Prvky areálu</div></div>
    <div class="m3-cap" style="margin:0 0 4px">Budovy</div><div class="mp-fl">${P.filter(p=>p.t==='bld').map(p=>row('bld',p.id,'var(--ink-3)',p.name,`${p.M.floors.length} ${plural(p.M.floors.length,'podlaží','podlaží','podlaží')}`)).join('')}</div>
    <div class="m3-cap" style="margin:12px 0 4px">Heliporty</div>${M.heli.length?`<div class="mp-fl">${M.heli.map(h=>row('heli',h.id,mpXC('heli'),h.name,`${fmtN(h.alt)} m`)).join('')}</div>`:'<p class="hint" style="margin:0">Zatím žádný.</p>'}
    <div class="m3-cap" style="margin:12px 0 4px">Trasy sanitky</div>${M.amb.length?`<div class="mp-fl">${M.amb.map(r=>{const x=X.find(y=>y.id===r.id);return row('amb',r.id,mpXC('amb'),r.name,x?`${fmtN(x.t/60,1)} min`:'—');}).join('')}</div>`:'<p class="hint" style="margin:0">Zatím žádná.</p>'}
    <div class="m3-cap" style="margin:12px 0 4px">Spojovací chodby</div>${M.links.length?`<div class="mp-fl">${M.links.map(L=>{const e=mpLinkEnds(site,L);return row('link',L.id,'var(--dot)',L.name,e?`${fmtN(e.len)} m`:'—');}).join('')}</div>`:'<p class="hint" style="margin:0">Zatím žádná.</p>'}</section>`;
  return det+list;}
function mpXferSteps(x){const ic={'Naložení pacienta':'user','Jízda areálem':'cr','Vyložení a předání':'check','Přistání a vyložení':'target','Převoz spojovací chodbou':'cr','Předání v budově':'check'};
  return `<p class="mp-ft"><i style="--c:${m3RfColor('from')}"></i>${esc(x.A.name)}<s></s><i style="--c:${m3RfColor('to')}"></i>${esc(x.B.name)}</p><div class="rt-total"><span>Celkem</span><b>${fmtN(x.t/60,1)}<small> min</small></b><em>${fmtN(x.len)} m</em></div><ol class="rt-steps">${x.parts.map(([t,s])=>`<li>${ico(ic[t]||'cr')}<div><b>${t}</b></div><em>${fmtMin(s)}</em></li>`).join('')}</ol>`;}
function mcpt(svg,e){const p=svg.createSVGPoint();p.x=e.clientX;p.y=e.clientY;const q=p.matrixTransform(svg.getScreenCTM().inverse());return {x:q.x/MCS,y:q.y/MCS};}
const mcR=v=>Math.round(v*2)/2;
function mcDraft(svg){if(!CD||CD.m!=='amb')return;let el=svg.querySelector('#mc-draft');if(!el){el=document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg','polyline');el.id='mc-draft';el.setAttribute('class','mc-draft');svg.appendChild(el);}const pts=[...CD.pts,CD.c?[CD.c.x,CD.c.y]:null].filter(Boolean);el.setAttribute('points',pts.map(([x,y])=>`${x*MCS},${y*MCS}`).join(' '));}
function mcHit(site,p){const P=mpPlaces(site);return P.filter(x=>x.t==='heli').find(x=>Math.hypot(p.x-(x.x+x.w/2),p.y-(x.y+x.h/2))<=x.w/2)||P.filter(x=>x.t==='bld').find(x=>mpIn(x,p.x,p.y))||null;}

  if(tool==='sel'){const vx=e.target.closest('[data-vi]');if(vx&&ed&&o.csel&&o.csel.t==='amb'){CD={m:'vx',rid:o.csel.id,i:+vx.dataset.vi};return;}
    const pg=e.target.closest('[data-pid]');if(pg){const id=pg.dataset.pid,pl=mpPlace(site,id);if(!pl)return;o.csel={t:pl.t,id};if(pl.t==='bld')o.bld=id;if(ed)CD={m:'mv',id,t:pl.t,p,o:{x:pl.x,y:pl.y},el:pg};else render();if(ed){svg.querySelectorAll('.mc-b.on,.mc-h.on').forEach(x=>x.classList.remove('on'));pg.classList.add('on');}return;}
    const rg=e.target.closest('[data-rid]');if(rg){o.csel={t:'amb',id:rg.dataset.rid};render();return;}
    const lg=e.target.closest('[data-lid]');if(lg){o.csel={t:'link',id:lg.dataset.lid};render();return;}
    if(o.csel){o.csel=null;render();}return;}
  if(!ed){denied();return;}
  if(tool==='heli'){const sz=12,x=Math.max(0,Math.round(p.x-sz/2)),y=Math.max(0,Math.round(p.y-sz/2)),roof=mpPlaces(site).find(b=>b.t==='bld'&&mpIn(b,p.x,p.y)),h={id:'h'+uid(),name:`Heliport${M.heli.length?' '+(M.heli.length+1):''}`,pos:{x,y},sz,alt:roof?roof.top:20};M.heli.push(h);o.csel={t:'heli',id:h.id};o.ctool='sel';commit(`Areál: přidán ${h.name} ve výšce ${h.alt} m`);return;}
  const hit=mcHit(site,p);
  if(tool==='link'){if(!hit){toast('Klepněte na budovu nebo heliport');return;}if(!CD||CD.m!=='lk'){CD={m:'lk',a:hit.id};const g=svg.querySelector(`[data-pid="${hit.id}"]`);if(g)g.classList.add('pick');const h=$('#mc-hint');if(h)h.textContent=`Začátek: ${hit.name}. Teď klepněte na druhou budovu nebo heliport.`;return;}
    if(hit.id===CD.a){return;}const A=mpPlace(site,CD.a),B=hit;CD=null;const lvFor=(P,other)=>{if(P.t!=='bld')return undefined;const fl=mpFloors(P.M),want=other.t==='heli'?other.H.alt:(fl.length>1&&other.M&&other.M.floors.length>1?MFLOOR:0);let best=fl[0].lv,bd=1e9;fl.forEach((f,i)=>{const d=Math.abs(i*MFLOOR-want);if(d<bd){bd=d;best=f.lv;}});return best;};
    const L={id:'l'+uid(),name:A.t==='heli'||B.t==='heli'?`Lávka ${A.t==='heli'?A.name:B.name} – ${A.t==='heli'?B.name:A.name}`:`Spojovací chodba ${A.name} – ${B.name}`,a:{p:A.id,lv:lvFor(A,B)},b:{p:B.id,lv:lvFor(B,A)}};M.links.push(L);o.csel={t:'link',id:L.id};o.ctool='sel';commit(`Areál: přidána spojovací chodba ${L.name}`);return;}
  if(tool==='amb'){if(!CD||CD.m!=='amb'){if(!hit){toast('Začněte klepnutím na budovu nebo heliport, odkud sanitka vyjíždí');return;}CD={m:'amb',a:hit.id,pts:[mpEdge(hit,p.x,p.y).map(mcR)],c:p};mcDraft(svg);const h=$('#mc-hint');if(h)h.textContent=`Start: ${hit.name}.     if(hit&&hit.id===CD.a)return;CD.pts.push([mcR(p.x),mcR(p.y)]);mcDraft(svg);}});

  if(CD.m==='amb'){CD.c=p;mcDraft(svg);return;}
  if(CD.m==='mv'){const dx=Math.round(p.x-CD.p.x),dy=Math.round(p.y-CD.p.y);CD.n={x:Math.max(0,CD.o.x+dx),y:Math.max(0,CD.o.y+dy)};CD.el.setAttribute('transform',`translate(${CD.n.x*MCS},${CD.n.y*MCS})`);CD.el.classList.add('drag');return;}
