  if(!d.n||(d.n.x===d.o.x&&d.n.y===d.o.y)){render();return;}const pl=mpPlace(site,d.id);if(!pl)return;const dx=d.n.x-d.o.x,dy=d.n.y-d.o.y;
  if(d.t==='bld'){const ov=mpPlaces(site).filter(q=>q.t==='bld'&&q.id!==d.id&&!(d.n.x+pl.w<=q.x||d.n.x>=q.x+q.w||d.n.y+pl.h<=q.y||d.n.y>=q.y+q.h));if(ov.length){toast(`Budova by se překrývala s: ${ov.map(q=>q.name).join(', ')}`);render();return;}
    pl.M.pos={x:d.n.x,y:d.n.y};}else pl.H.pos={x:d.n.x,y:d.n.y};
  M.amb.forEach(r=>{if(r.a===d.id){r.pts[0]=[r.pts[0][0]+dx,r.pts[0][1]+dy];}if(r.b===d.id){const i=r.pts.length-1;r.pts[i]=[r.pts[i][0]+dx,r.pts[i][1]+dy];}});
  commit(`Areál: přesunuto ${pl.name}`);});
document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(!CD||!$('#mpc'))return;if(e.key==='Escape'){CD=null;render();e.preventDefault();}else if(e.key==='Backspace'&&CD.m==='amb'&&!e.target.closest('input,textarea')){if(CD.pts.length>1){CD.pts.pop();mcDraft($('#mpc'));}else{CD=null;render();}e.preventDefault();}});
Object.assign(ACT,{
  mpBld(t){const o=S.mp,v=t.dataset.v;if(!mpBlds(o.site).some(b=>b.id===v))return;if(o.bld!==v){o.bld=v;mpBldReset();}o.cmp=false;CD=null;render();if(M3.renderer&&o.tab==='3d')m3Cam(o.mode==='cmp'?'cmp':'iso');},
  mpCampus(){S.mp.cmp=true;S.mp.tab='ed';CD=null;render();},
  mpCampusGo(){S.mp.cmp=true;S.mp.tab='ed';CD=null;render();const c=$('.st-tabs');if(c)c.scrollIntoView({block:'start',behavior:RM()?'auto':'smooth'});},
  mpCTool(t){S.mp.ctool=t.dataset.v;CD=null;render();},
  mpCSel(t){const v=t.dataset.v;if(!v){S.mp.csel=null;render();return;}const [k,id]=v.split(':');S.mp.csel={t:k,id};if(k==='bld')S.mp.bld=id;if(k==='amb'||k==='link')S.mp.cx=id;render();},
  mpBldAdd(){const o=S.mp,site=o.site,M=mapOf(site),n=M.blds.length,W=30,H=18,pos=mpFreeSpot(site,W,H),id='b'+uid(),nm=`Budova ${String.fromCharCode(66+n)}`,it=[{id:'m'+uid(),k:'hall',lv:0,x:1,y:8,w:28,h:2,name:'Chodba'},{id:'m'+uid(),k:'other',lv:0,x:1,y:1,w:10,h:7,name:'Vstup do budovy'}];
    M.blds.push({id,name:nm,pos,W,H,floors:[{lv:0,name:'1. NP'}],items:it});o.bld=id;mpBldReset();o.cmp=false;o.tab='ed';commit(`Areál: přidána ${nm} – nakreslete její plán`);},
  mpBldDel(t){const o=S.mp,site=o.site,M=mapOf(site),id=t.dataset.v;if(id===MP_MAIN)return;if(S.confirm!=='bdel:'+id){S.confirm='bdel:'+id;render();return;}S.confirm=null;const b=M.blds.find(x=>x.id===id);M.blds=M.blds.filter(x=>x.id!==id);M.amb=M.amb.filter(r=>r.a!==id&&r.b!==id);M.links=M.links.filter(L=>L.a.p!==id&&L.b.p!==id);if(o.bld===id){o.bld=MP_MAIN;mpBldReset();}o.csel=null;commit(`Areál: odebrána ${b?b.name:'budova'}`);},
  mpHeliAlt(t){const M=mapOf(S.mp.site),h=M.heli.find(x=>x.id===t.dataset.id);if(!h)return;h.alt=+t.dataset.v;commit(`Areál: ${h.name} ve výšce ${h.alt} m`);},
  mpHeliDel(t){const o=S.mp,M=mapOf(o.site),id=t.dataset.v;if(S.confirm!=='hdel:'+id){S.confirm='hdel:'+id;render();return;}S.confirm=null;const h=M.heli.find(x=>x.id===id);M.heli=M.heli.filter(x=>x.id!==id);M.links=M.links.filter(L=>L.a.p!==id&&L.b.p!==id);M.amb=M.amb.filter(r=>r.a!==id&&r.b!==id);o.csel=null;commit(`Areál: odebrán ${h?h.name:'heliport'}`);},
  mpAmbDel(t){const o=S.mp,M=mapOf(o.site),id=t.dataset.v;if(S.confirm!=='adel:'+id){S.confirm='adel:'+id;render();return;}S.confirm=null;M.amb=M.amb.filter(r=>r.id!==id);o.csel=null;commit('Areál: trasa sanitky smazána');},
  mpLinkDel(t){const o=S.mp,M=mapOf(o.site),id=t.dataset.v;if(S.confirm!=='ldel:'+id){S.confirm='ldel:'+id;render();return;}S.confirm=null;M.links=M.links.filter(L=>L.id!==id);o.csel=null;commit('Areál: spojovací chodba odebrána');},
  mpCx(t){S.mp.cx=t.dataset.v;render();setTimeout(()=>{if(M3.renderer)m3Cam('cmp');},60);},
  mpCxPlay(t){const o=S.mp;o.cx=t.dataset.v;o.tab='3d';o.mode='cmp';o.reg='std';o.floor='all';render();const c=$('.st-tabs');if(c)c.scrollIntoView({block:'start',behavior:RM()?'auto':'smooth'});setTimeout(()=>{if(M3.renderer)m3Cam('cmp');},90);}});
Object.assign(CH,{
  mpBldName(t){const M=mapOf(S.mp.site),v=t.value.trim(),id=t.dataset.id;if(!v)return;if(id===MP_MAIN){if(M.bname===v)return;M.bname=v;}else{const b=M.blds.find(x=>x.id===id);if(!b||b.name===v)return;b.name=v;}commit(`Areál: budova přejmenována na ${v}`);},
  mpHeliF(t){const M=mapOf(S.mp.site),h=M.heli.find(x=>x.id===t.dataset.id),k=t.dataset.k;if(!h)return;if(k==='name'){const v=t.value.trim();if(!v||v===h.name)return;h.name=v;}else if(k==='alt'){h.alt=clampN(Math.round(+String(t.value).replace(',','.')||0),0,120);}else if(k==='sz'){const c=h.pos.x+h.sz/2,d=h.pos.y+h.sz/2;h.sz=clampN(Math.round((+t.value||24)/MCELL/2)*2/1,8,20);h.pos={x:Math.max(0,Math.round(c-h.sz/2)),y:Math.max(0,Math.round(d-h.sz/2))};}commit(`Areál: upraven ${h.name}`);},
  mpAmbF(t){const M=mapOf(S.mp.site),r=M.amb.find(x=>x.id===t.dataset.id),k=t.dataset.k;if(!r)return;const v=t.value.trim();if(k==='name'&&!v)return;if((r[k]||'')===v)return;r[k]=v;commit(`Areál: upravena trasa ${r.name}`);},
  mpLinkF(t){const M=mapOf(S.mp.site),L=M.links.find(x=>x.id===t.dataset.id),v=t.value.trim();if(!L||!v||L.name===v)return;L.name=v;commit(`Areál: chodba přejmenována na ${v}`);},
  mpLinkLv(t){const M=mapOf(S.mp.site),L=M.links.find(x=>x.id===t.dataset.id);if(!L)return;L[t.dataset.k].lv=+t.value;commit(`Areál: ${L.name} ústí do jiného podlaží`);}});

/* ---------- 3D: areál kolem budovy ---------- */
function m3XY(M){return [M.pos.x+M.W/2,M.pos.y+M.H/2];}
function m3Campus(M){const T=THREE,o=S.mp,site=o.site,G=M3.G,m=G.mat,gm=G.geo,FH=M3.FH*o.ex,cc=m3XY(M),to3=(x,y)=>[x-cc[0],y-cc[1]],yA=a=>a/MFLOOR*FH,g=new T.Group(),P=mpPlaces(site),MM=mapOf(site);M3.base.add(g);M3.cmpG=g;
  const many=P.length>1;if(!many&&!MM.amb.length)return;
  MM.amb.forEach(r=>{if(r.pts.length<2)return;const pts=r.pts.map(([x,y])=>{const [a,b]=to3(x,y);return new T.Vector3(a,-.36,b);});
    for(let i=1;i<pts.length;i++){const a=pts[i-1],b=pts[i],d=b.clone().sub(a),len=d.length();if(len<.01)continue;const s=new T.Mesh(gm.box,m.road);s.scale.set(len,.04,3.2);s.position.copy(a).add(b).multiplyScalar(.5);s.rotation.y=-Math.atan2(d.z,d.x);s.receiveShadow=true;g.add(s);}
    pts.forEach(p=>{const c=new T.Mesh(gm.cyl,m.road);c.scale.set(3.2,.04,3.2);c.position.copy(p);g.add(c);});
    const ln=new T.Line(new T.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(pts.map(p=>p.clone().setY(-.32))),m.roadL);ln.computeLineDistances();g.add(ln);});
  P.forEach(p=>{if(p.t!=='bld'||p.M===M)return;const B=p.M,[cx,cz]=to3(B.pos.x+B.W/2,B.pos.y+B.H/2),n=B.floors.length;
    for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const y=i*FH,sl=new T.Mesh(gm.box,m.slab);sl.scale.set(B.W+.6,.3,B.H+.6);sl.position.set(cx,y-.15,cz);sl.receiveShadow=true;sl.castShadow=true;g.add(sl);
      const sh=new T.Mesh(gm.box,m.bldGlass);sh.scale.set(B.W,FH*.82,B.H);sh.position.set(cx,y+FH*.41,cz);sh.userData.bld=p.id;sh.renderOrder=2;g.add(sh);M3.pick.push(sh);const se=new T.LineSegments(gm.boxE,m.slabE);se.scale.copy(sh.scale);se.position.copy(sh.position);g.add(se);}
    const rf=new T.Mesh(gm.box,m.slab);rf.scale.set(B.W+.6,.3,B.H+.6);rf.position.set(cx,n*FH-.15,cz);rf.castShadow=true;g.add(rf);
    m3Label(`<b>${esc(p.name)}</b><span>${n} ${plural(n,'podlaží','podlaží','podlaží')} · klepnutím zobrazíte</span>`,new T.Vector3(cx,n*FH+.5,cz),'m3k',{kind:'bld',bid:p.id});});
  if(many){const n=M.floors.length;m3Label(`<b>${esc(mpCurName())}</b>`,new T.Vector3(0,n*FH+.9,-M.H/2-.5),'m3k cur',{kind:'bld'});}
  P.forEach(p=>{if(p.t!=='heli')return;const h=p.H,[cx,cz]=to3(h.pos.x+h.sz/2,h.pos.y+h.sz/2),y=yA(h.alt),r=h.sz/2,roof=P.find(b=>b.t==='bld'&&mpIn(b,p.x+p.w/2,p.y+p.h/2)),base=roof&&h.alt>=roof.top?yA(roof.top):0;
    const dk=new T.Mesh(gm.cyl,m.heliDeck);dk.scale.set(r*2,.28,r*2);dk.position.set(cx,y-.14,cz);dk.castShadow=true;dk.receiveShadow=true;g.add(dk);
    const mk=new T.Mesh(gm.plane,m.heliMark);mk.scale.set(r*1.84,r*1.84,1);mk.rotation.x=-Math.PI/2;mk.position.set(cx,y+.012,cz);g.add(mk);
    const ring=new T.Mesh(gm.torus,m.heliRing);ring.scale.set(r/.99,r/.99,.12);ring.rotation.x=Math.PI/2;ring.position.set(cx,y+.04,cz);g.add(ring);
    if(y-base>.4){const hh=y-base,core=new T.Mesh(gm.box,m.slab);core.scale.set(2.2,hh,2.2);core.position.set(cx,base+hh/2,cz);core.castShadow=true;g.add(core);[0,1,2,3].forEach(i=>{const a=i*Math.PI/2+Math.PI/4,pl=new T.Mesh(gm.cyl8,m.post);pl.scale.set(.5,hh,.5);pl.position.set(cx+Math.cos(a)*r*.7,base+hh/2,cz+Math.sin(a)*r*.7);pl.castShadow=true;g.add(pl);});}
    const net=new T.Mesh(new T.CylinderGeometry(r+.5,r+.9,.5,40,1,true),m.heliNet);net.position.set(cx,y-.1,cz);g.add(net);
    m3Label(`<b>${esc(h.name)}</b><span>plocha ve výšce ${fmtN(h.alt)} m</span>`,new T.Vector3(cx,y+.6,cz),'m3k heli',{kind:'bld'});});
  MM.links.forEach(L=>{const e=mpLinkEnds(site,L);if(!e)return;const [ax,az]=to3(...e.pa),[bx,bz]=to3(...e.pb),a=new T.Vector3(ax,yA(e.za),az),b=new T.Vector3(bx,yA(e.zb),bz),d=b.clone().sub(a),len=d.length();if(len<.05)return;
    const dir=d.clone().normalize(),q=new T.Quaternion().setFromUnitVectors(new T.Vector3(1,0,0),dir),mid=a.clone().add(b).multiplyScalar(.5),wh=Math.min(1.5,FH*.6);
    const fl=new T.Mesh(gm.box,m.slab);fl.scale.set(len,.22,2.4);fl.quaternion.copy(q);fl.position.copy(mid).add(new T.Vector3(0,-.11,0));fl.castShadow=true;fl.receiveShadow=true;g.add(fl);
    const tb=new T.Mesh(gm.box,m.linkGlass);tb.scale.set(len,wh,2.4);tb.quaternion.copy(q);tb.position.copy(mid).add(new T.Vector3(0,wh/2,0));tb.renderOrder=2;g.add(tb);const te=new T.LineSegments(gm.boxE,m.linkE);te.scale.copy(tb.scale);te.quaternion.copy(q);te.position.copy(tb.position);g.add(te);
    const nP=Math.floor(len/7);for(let i=1;i<=nP;i++){const p=a.clone().lerp(b,i/(nP+1));if(p.y<.6)continue;const pl=new T.Mesh(gm.cyl8,m.post);pl.scale.set(.42,p.y,.42);pl.position.set(p.x,p.y/2-.2,p.z);pl.castShadow=true;g.add(pl);}
    m3Label(`<b>${esc(L.name)}</b><span>${fmtN(e.len)} m</span>`,mid.clone().add(new T.Vector3(0,wh+.3,0)),'m3k lk',{kind:'elev'});});}
function m3CampusBox(){const o=S.mp,M=mapCur(),cc=m3XY(M),FH=M3.FH*o.ex,P=mpPlaces(o.site),MM=mapOf(o.site);let x0=1e9,x1=-1e9,z0=1e9,z1=-1e9,top=0;
  const add=(x,z)=>{x0=Math.min(x0,x-cc[0]);x1=Math.max(x1,x-cc[0]);z0=Math.min(z0,z-cc[1]);z1=Math.max(z1,z-cc[1]);};P.forEach(p=>{add(p.x,p.y);add(p.x+p.w,p.y+p.h);top=Math.max(top,p.top/MFLOOR*FH);});MM.amb.forEach(r=>r.pts.forEach(([x,y])=>add(x,y)));return {x0,x1,z0,z1,top};}
function m3XferPts(X){const T=THREE,o=S.mp,M=mapCur(),cc=m3XY(M),FH=M3.FH*o.ex,to=(x,y,a)=>new T.Vector3(x-cc[0],a/MFLOOR*FH,y-cc[1]);
  if(X.k==='amb')return X.r.pts.map(([x,y])=>to(x,y,0).setY(-.05));const e=X.e,H=X.A,hc=[H.x+H.w/2,H.y+H.h/2],hs=H===e.A?e.pa:e.pb,bs=H===e.A?e.pb:e.pa,za=H.H.alt,zb=H===e.A?e.zb:e.za,B=X.B,inn=[bs[0]+Math.sign(B.x+B.w/2-bs[0])*(Math.abs(B.x+B.w/2-bs[0])>1?1.6:0),bs[1]+Math.sign(B.y+B.h/2-bs[1])*(Math.abs(B.y+B.h/2-bs[1])>1?1.6:0)];
  return [to(hc[0],hc[1],za),to(hs[0],hs[1],za),to(bs[0],bs[1],zb),to(inn[0],inn[1],zb)].map(v=>v.setY(v.y+.42));}
function m3Amb(){const T=THREE,G=M3.G,g=new T.Group(),B=(mat,w,h,d,x,y,z)=>{const q=new T.Mesh(G.geo.box,mat);q.scale.set(w,h,d);q.position.set(x,y,z);q.castShadow=true;g.add(q);return q;};
  B(G.mat.ambBody,2.2,1.05,1.15,-.2,.78,0);B(G.mat.ambBody,.85,.78,1.1,1.28,.64,0);B(G.mat.ambGlass,.06,.42,.98,1.71,.82,0);B(G.mat.ambStripe,2.22,.14,1.17,-.2,.6,0);B(G.mat.ambStripe,.86,.12,1.12,1.28,.52,0);
  const lb=B(G.mat.ambLight,.5,.1,.9,.8,1.35,0);[[-.9,.62],[1.2,.62],[-.9,-.62],[1.2,-.62]].forEach(([x,z])=>{const w=new T.Mesh(G.geo.cyl,G.mat.dark);w.scale.set(.5,.22,.5);w.rotation.x=Math.PI/2;w.position.set(x,.25,z*.95);g.add(w);});g.userData.lb=lb;return g;}
function m3Heli(){const T=THREE,G=M3.G,g=new T.Group(),body=new T.Mesh(G.geo.sph,G.mat.heliBody);body.scale.set(3,1.5,1.6);body.position.y=1.2;body.castShadow=true;g.add(body);
  const gl=new T.Mesh(G.geo.sph,G.mat.ambGlass);gl.scale.set(1.3,.9,1.3);gl.position.set(1.05,1.35,0);g.add(gl);const tail=new T.Mesh(G.geo.box,G.mat.heliBody);tail.scale.set(3.4,.32,.32);tail.position.set(-2.8,1.45,0);tail.castShadow=true;g.add(tail);
  const fin=new T.Mesh(G.geo.box,G.mat.ambStripe);fin.scale.set(.6,.9,.12);fin.position.set(-4.4,1.85,0);g.add(fin);[-1,1].forEach(s=>{const sk=new T.Mesh(G.geo.box,G.mat.metalD);sk.scale.set(2.8,.08,.1);sk.position.set(0,.12,s*.7);g.add(sk);});
  const rot=new T.Group();rot.position.y=2.15;[0,1].forEach(i=>{const bl=new T.Mesh(G.geo.box,G.mat.metalD);bl.scale.set(8.4,.04,.26);bl.rotation.y=i*Math.PI/2;rot.add(bl);});g.add(rot);g.userData.rot=rot;return g;}
function m3Xfer(X){const T=THREE,G=M3.G,P=m3XferPts(X);if(P.length<2)return;const col=mpXC(X.k),cp=m3Curve(P),add=x=>{x.traverse(y=>y.userData.fl=true);return x;};
  const n0=M3.ovl.children.length;m3Tube(cp,col,X.k==='amb'?.16:.12,1.4);M3.ovl.children.slice(n0).forEach(add);
  let veh,heli=null;if(X.k==='amb'){veh=m3Amb();}else{veh=new T.Group();const bed=new T.Mesh(G.geo.box,G.mat.mattr);bed.scale.set(1.05,.14,.48);bed.position.y=.3;veh.add(bed);const bf=new T.Mesh(G.geo.box,G.mat.bedF);bf.scale.set(1.1,.08,.52);bf.position.y=.2;veh.add(bf);const bl=new T.Mesh(G.geo.box,G.mat.blanket);bl.scale.set(.66,.04,.5);bl.position.set(.12,.39,0);veh.add(bl);
    heli=m3Heli();heli.position.copy(P[0]).add(new T.Vector3(0,-.42,0));heli.rotation.y=-Math.atan2(P[1].z-P[0].z,P[1].x-P[0].x)+Math.PI/2;M3.ovl.add(add(heli));}
  M3.ovl.add(add(veh));const lp=new T.Vector3(),lab=m3Label(`<b>${X.k==='amb'?'Sanitka':'Pacient'}</b><span>${X.k==='amb'?'převoz areálem':'z heliportu'}</span>`,lp,'m3t amb',{kind:'tok',ov:true});lab.el.style.setProperty('--xc',col);
  let tot=0;for(let i=1;i<P.length;i++)tot+=P[i].distanceTo(P[i-1]);
  M3.cx={X,cp,tot,veh,heli,lab,lp,sim:0,hold:0,yaw:0,y0:heli?heli.position.y:0};M3.hud.hidden=false;M3.hud.classList.remove('em');}
function m3XferTick(dt){const R0=M3.cx,X=R0.X,T=X.t,p=X.parts,dur=clampN(T/22,7,15);if(R0.hold>0){R0.hold-=dt;if(R0.hold<=0)R0.sim=0;}else{R0.sim+=dt*T/dur;if(R0.sim>=T){R0.sim=T;R0.hold=2.4;}}
  const s=R0.sim,t1=p[0][1],t2=t1+p[1][1];let u,ph,f;if(s<t1){u=0;ph=p[0][0];f=s/Math.max(1,t1);}else if(s<t2){u=(s-t1)/Math.max(1,p[1][1]);ph=p[1][0];f=1;}else{u=1;ph=p[2][0];f=1;}
  if(X.k==='heli'){const h=R0.heli,land=clampN(f/.7,0,1);h.position.y=R0.y0+(1-land)*(1-land)*14;h.userData.rot.rotation.y+=dt*(land<1?26:Math.max(2,26*(1-(s-t1*.7)/Math.max(1,t1*.3))));R0.veh.visible=s>=t1*.85;}
  else if(R0.veh.userData.lb)R0.veh.userData.lb.material=Math.floor(M3.time*4)%2?M3.G.mat.ambLight:M3.G.mat.ambLight2;
  const pos=R0.cp.getPointAt(clampN(u,0,1));R0.veh.position.set(pos.x,pos.y-.42,pos.z);R0.lp.set(pos.x,pos.y+(X.k==='amb'?1.4:.6),pos.z);
  const tg=R0.cp.getTangentAt(Math.min(.999,clampN(u,0,1)+.0001));if(Math.abs(tg.y)<.9)R0.yaw=Math.atan2(-tg.z,tg.x);R0.veh.rotation.y+=((R0.yaw-R0.veh.rotation.y+Math.PI*3)%(Math.PI*2)-Math.PI)*Math.min(1,dt*8);
  if(S.mp.follow){const g=M3.goal,k=Math.min(1,dt*3);g.tx+=(pos.x-g.tx)*k;g.tz+=(pos.z-g.tz)*k;g.ty+=(pos.y-g.ty)*k;}
  const html=`<span>${X.k==='amb'?'Převoz sanitkou':'Převoz z heliportu'} · ${ph}</span><b>${fmtMin(s)} <small>/ ${fmtMin(T)} min</small></b><u><i style="width:${(s/T*100).toFixed(1)}%"></i></u><em class="m3-rf"><i style="--c:${m3RfColor('from')}"></i>Odkud ${esc(X.A.name)}<s></s><i style="--c:${m3RfColor('to')}"></i>Kam ${esc(X.B.name)}</em>`;if(M3.hud._h!==html){M3.hud._h=html;M3.hud.innerHTML=html;}}
function mpCmpHTML(){const o=S.mp,X=mpXfers(o.site),x=X.find(y=>y.id===o.cx)||X[0];
  return `<section class="card"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Převozy v areálu<span class="cnt">${X.length}</span></div>${x?`<button class="btn sm pri" data-act="mpPlay">${ico('play')}Přehrát</button>`:''}</div>
    ${X.length?`<div class="mp-fl">${X.map(y=>`<button class="mp-fr${x&&y.id===x.id?' on':''}" data-act="mpCx" data-v="${y.id}"><i style="--c:${mpXC(y.k)}"></i><span>${y.k==='amb'?'Sanitka':'Heliport'} · ${esc(y.name)}</span><em>${fmtN(y.t/60,1)} min</em></button>`).join('')}</div>${x?mpXferSteps(x):''}`:'<p class="hint" style="margin:0">V areálu zatím nejsou trasy sanitky ani heliport se spojovací chodbou.</p>'}
    <div class="btns" style="margin-top:10px"><button class="btn sm" data-act="mpCampusGo">${ico('edit')}Upravit areál v editoru</button></div></section>`;}


/* =========================================================
   Mapa areálu KNL Liberec – 3D podle mapy areálu nemocnice
   ========================================================= */
const KM_FH=3.8,KM_S=.7,KM_C=[690,410];
const kmZ=(x,y)=>[300+x*.4429,260+y*.4434];
function kmP(sp,x,y){const [a,b]=sp==='z'?kmZ(x,y):[x,y];return [(a-KM_C[0])*KM_S,(b-KM_C[1])*KM_S];}

const KM={renderer:null,scene:null,camera:null,host:null,wrap:null,lbl:null,raf:0,lt:0,B:{},labels:[],pick:[],orb:null,goal:null,theme:null,ptrs:new Map(),drag:null};
const kmB=id=>KNL.find(b=>b.id===id)||null;
const kmIsB=n=>/^(Podlaží S|Suterén)/.test(n);
function kmFloors(b){const bs=b.f.filter(x=>kmIsB(x[0])),ab=b.f.filter(x=>!kmIsB(x[0]));return {bs,ab};}
function kmFloorY(b,i){const {bs}=kmFloors(b),k=i-bs.length;return k*KM_FH;}
function kmNorm(s){return String(s).toLowerCase().normalize('NFD').replace(/[̀-ͯ]/g,'');}
function kmIdx(){if(KM.idx)return KM.idx;const L=[];KNL.forEach(b=>b.f.forEach(([fn,it],i)=>it.forEach(t=>L.push({t,b:b.id,f:i,fn,k:kmNorm(t)}))));KNL.forEach(b=>L.push({t:b.n,b:b.id,f:null,fn:'',k:kmNorm(b.n+' '+b.id),bld:true}));return KM.idx=L;}
function kmOrs(bid){const res=[];KM_OR.filter(g=>g.b===bid).forEach(g=>{const rooms=kmGrpRooms(g);if(!rooms.length)return;res.push({...g,rooms:rooms.map(r=>({r,st:kmStat(r)}))});});return res;}
function kmTR(){const d=S.mp.day,rs=[];KM_OR.forEach(g=>kmGrpRooms(g).forEach(r=>rs.push(hoursFor(r,d))));const a=rs.length?Math.min(...rs.map(h=>tmin(h.from))):tmin(D.hours.from),b=rs.length?Math.max(...rs.map(h=>tmin(h.to))):tmin(D.hours.to);return [Math.max(0,Math.floor((a-60)/30)*30),Math.min(1439,Math.ceil((b+60)/30)*30)];}
function kmTimeHTML(tr){const o=S.mp,pct=clampN((o.t-tr[0])/(tr[1]-tr[0])*100,0,100).toFixed(2),ticks=[];for(let m=Math.ceil(tr[0]/120)*120;m<=tr[1];m+=120)ticks.push(`<span style="left:${((m-tr[0])/(tr[1]-tr[0])*100).toFixed(2)}%">${m/60}</span>`);
  return `<div class="m3-time"><button class="m3-tb${o.play?' on':''}" data-act="mpTime" data-v="play" aria-label="${o.play?'Pozastavit průběh dne':'Přehrát průběh dne'}" title="${o.play?'Pozastavit':'Přehrát průběh dne'}">${m3I(o.play?'pause':'play')}</button><button class="m3-live${o.live?' on':''}" data-act="mpTime" data-v="live" title="Aktuální čas"><i></i>Živě</button><div class="m3-tr"><input type="range" id="m3-ts" min="${tr[0]}" max="${tr[1]}" step="1" value="${Math.round(o.t)}" style="--p:${pct}%" aria-label="Čas dne"><div class="m3-tk">${ticks.join('')}</div></div><b id="m3-clock">${hm(o.t)}</b></div>`;}
function kmOrSum(g){const c={};g.rooms.forEach(x=>c[x.st.k]=(c[x.st.k]||0)+1);return MST_ORD.concat(['off']).filter(k=>c[k]).map(k=>`${c[k]} ${k==='op'?plural(c[k],'operace','operace','operací'):k==='prep'?'příprava':k==='clean'?'úklid':k==='free'?plural(c[k],'volný','volné','volných'):'mimo provoz'}`).join(' · ');}

/* ---------- scéna (stejný vizuální jazyk jako Sály 3D) ---------- */
function kmKey(){return S.theme+'|'+D.rooms.filter(r=>r.site==='LB'&&r.active&&r.kind!=='pacu').map(r=>r.code).join(',');}
function kmGrpRooms(g){return D.rooms.filter(r=>r.site==='LB'&&r.active&&r.kind!=='pacu'&&g.re.test(r.code));}
function kmAssets(){const T=THREE,LT=mpLT(),P=(d,l)=>LT?l:d,AB=LT?T.NormalBlending:T.AdditiveBlending,FONT=getComputedStyle(document.body).fontFamily||'sans-serif';
  const S_=(c,r,mt,o={})=>new T.MeshStandardMaterial({color:c,roughness:r,metalness:mt,...o}),G_=(c,op,o={})=>S_(c,.14,.12,{transparent:true,opacity:op,depthWrite:false,...o}),L_=(c,op)=>new T.LineBasicMaterial({color:c,transparent:true,opacity:op}),acc=P(0x5ec8e4,0x2a8576);
  const tex={
    bg:kmTex(512,512,(x,w,h)=>{const g=x.createRadialGradient(w*.5,h*.42,0,w*.5,h*.5,w*.72);g.addColorStop(0,P('#13203f','#f8fbfc'));g.addColorStop(.45,P('#0a1229','#e7eef1'));g.addColorStop(1,P('#03061a','#c9d5db'));x.fillStyle=g;x.fillRect(0,0,w,h);}),
    radial:kmTex(128,128,(x,w,h)=>{const g=x.createRadialGradient(64,64,0,64,64,64);g.addColorStop(0,'rgba(255,255,255,1)');g.addColorStop(.4,'rgba(255,255,255,.5)');g.addColorStop(1,'rgba(255,255,255,0)');x.fillStyle=g;x.fillRect(0,0,w,h);}),
    dots:kmTex(64,64,x=>{x.fillStyle=P('rgba(150,190,245,1)','rgba(96,120,134,1)');x.beginPath();x.arc(32,32,2.6,0,7);x.fill();}),
    heli:kmTex(256,256,(x,w,h)=>{x.clearRect(0,0,w,h);x.fillStyle=P('#23304a','#5b6870');x.beginPath();x.arc(128,128,126,0,7);x.fill();x.strokeStyle='#f5c542';x.lineWidth=7;x.beginPath();x.arc(128,128,112,0,7);x.stroke();x.strokeStyle='#fff';x.lineWidth=6;x.beginPath();x.arc(128,128,82,0,7);x.stroke();x.fillStyle='#fff';x.font=`800 104px ${FONT}`;x.textAlign='center';x.textBaseline='middle';x.fillText('H',128,134);}),
    hatch:kmTex(64,64,(x,w,h)=>{x.fillStyle=P('#15130d','#efe5c9');x.fillRect(0,0,w,h);x.strokeStyle=P('rgba(224,180,90,.34)','rgba(200,130,50,.42)');x.lineWidth=6;x.beginPath();for(let i=-64;i<128;i+=16){x.moveTo(i,0);x.lineTo(i+64,64);}x.stroke();})};
  const mat={
    slab:S_(P(0x08101d,0xc3ced5),.75,.2),slabG:S_(P(0x0b1628,0xcfd9de),.8,0,{transparent:true,opacity:P(.26,.32),depthWrite:false}),
    glass:G_(P(0x5f8fd6,0xb9ccd5),P(.1,.3)),glassG:G_(P(0x5f8fd6,0xc9d6dd),P(.025,.08)),glassS:G_(P(0x6fa8f0,0xa7c5d2),P(.16,.4)),
    glassH:G_(P(acc,0xa9ddd4),P(.2,.2),{emissive:new T.Color(acc),emissiveIntensity:P(.4,.03)}),glassOr:G_(P(0x3d8dff,0x8fb6dc),P(.11,.32)),glassK:G_(P(0xe0b45a,0xe8d39a),P(.05,.16)),glassP:G_(P(0x58709a,0xb4c0c8),P(.08,.3)),
    edge:L_(P(0x2f6cc0,0x8394a0),P(.55,.65)),edgeG:L_(P(0x2a5a98,0xa9b8c1),P(.12,.22)),edgeS:L_(P(0x7cc0ff,0x4d6472),.9),edgeH:L_(P(0xb4f1ff,0x1f7a6c),1),
    edgeOr:L_(P(0x4f9bff,0x3a78b5),.85),edgeK:L_(P(0xe0b45a,0xb07a1e),P(.75,.85)),edgeP:L_(P(0x46668f,0x8a9ba6),P(.5,.55)),
    road:new T.MeshBasicMaterial({color:P(0x111a30,0xc3cdd3)}),roadL:new T.LineDashedMaterial({color:P(0x6a7ea6,0xffffff),dashSize:4,gapSize:5,transparent:true,opacity:P(.5,.95)}),
    deck:S_(P(0x1b2438,0x8f9ba3),.7,.2),mark:new T.MeshBasicMaterial({map:tex.heli,transparent:true}),ring:new T.MeshBasicMaterial({color:P(0x7fffc0,0x25a86f)})};
  const VS='varying float vY;varying float vN;void main(){vY=position.y+.5;vN=abs(normal.y);gl_Position=projectionMatrix*modelViewMatrix*vec4(position,1.);}';
  const FS='uniform vec3 uC;uniform float uO;varying float vY;varying float vN;void main(){if(vN>.5)discard;float a=pow(1.-clamp(vY,0.,1.),1.7)*uO;gl_FragColor=vec4(uC,a);}';
  const vol=()=>new T.ShaderMaterial({uniforms:{uC:{value:new T.Color(0x25d07c)},uO:{value:.3}},vertexShader:VS,fragmentShader:FS,transparent:true,blending:AB,depthWrite:false,side:T.DoubleSide});
  return {LT,P,AB,tex,mat,vol};}
function kmInit(){if(KM.renderer)return true;const T=THREE;let r;try{r=new T.WebGLRenderer({antialias:true,powerPreference:'high-performance'});}catch(e){return false;}
  KM.pr=Math.min(1.6,window.devicePixelRatio||1);r.setPixelRatio(KM.pr);r.shadowMap.enabled=true;r.shadowMap.type=T.PCFSoftShadowMap;KM.renderer=r;
  const w=document.createElement('div');w.className='m3-wrap';w.appendChild(r.domElement);const lb=document.createElement('div');lb.className='m3-lbl';w.appendChild(lb);KM.wrap=w;KM.lbl=lb;
  KM.camera=new T.PerspectiveCamera(32,1,2,5000);KM.ray=new T.Raycaster();KM.v=new T.Vector3();KM.orb={r:740,th:.22,ph:.86,tx:0,ty:0,tz:28};KM.goal={...KM.orb};
  if(T.EffectComposer&&T.RenderPass&&T.UnrealBloomPass&&T.ShaderPass&&T.CopyShader&&T.LuminosityHighPassShader){try{let rt;if(r.capabilities.isWebGL2&&T.WebGLMultisampleRenderTarget)rt=new T.WebGLMultisampleRenderTarget(8,8,{format:T.RGBAFormat});
      const c=new T.EffectComposer(r,rt);c.setPixelRatio(KM.pr);KM.rp=new T.RenderPass(new T.Scene(),KM.camera);c.addPass(KM.rp);KM.bloom=new T.UnrealBloomPass(new T.Vector2(256,256),.85,.6,.5);c.addPass(KM.bloom);KM.comp=c;}catch(e){KM.comp=null;}}
  const c=r.domElement;c.style.touchAction='none';c.addEventListener('pointerdown',kmDown);c.addEventListener('pointermove',kmMove);c.addEventListener('pointerup',kmUp);c.addEventListener('pointercancel',kmUp);c.addEventListener('pointerleave',()=>kmTip(null));c.addEventListener('wheel',e=>{e.preventDefault();KM.goal.r=clampN(KM.goal.r*Math.exp(e.deltaY*.0011),40,1400);},{passive:false});c.addEventListener('contextmenu',e=>e.preventDefault());
  return true;}
function kmTex(w,h,draw){const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=w;c.height=h;draw(c.getContext('2d'),w,h);const t=new THREE.CanvasTexture(c);t.anisotropy=8;return t;}
function kmDispose(){if(!KM.scene)return;const seen=new Set();KM.scene.traverse(o=>{if(o.geometry&&!seen.has(o.geometry)){seen.add(o.geometry);o.geometry.dispose();}const m=o.material;if(m)(Array.isArray(m)?m:[m]).forEach(x=>{if(seen.has(x))return;seen.add(x);if(x.map&&!seen.has(x.map)){seen.add(x.map);x.map.dispose();}x.dispose();});});
  if(KM.A){Object.values(KM.A.tex).forEach(t=>t.dispose());Object.values(KM.A.mat).forEach(x=>x.dispose());}KM.scene=null;}
function kmBuild(){const T=THREE;kmDispose();const A=KM.A=kmAssets(),LT=A.LT,P=A.P,m=A.mat;
  KM.theme=S.theme;KM.key=kmKey();KM.labels=[];KM.lbl.innerHTML='';KM.pick=[];KM.B={};KM.rooms=[];KM.rows=[];KM.col={};KM.renderer.setClearColor(P(0x050920,0xdce5e8),1);
  const sc=new T.Scene();sc.background=A.tex.bg;sc.fog=new T.FogExp2(P(0x050920,0xdce5e8),P(.00034,.0003));KM.scene=sc;if(KM.rp)KM.rp.scene=sc;if(KM.bloom)KM.bloom.enabled=!LT;
  sc.add(new T.HemisphereLight(LT?0xffffff:0x87a5d8,LT?0xa9b8c0:0x04070d,LT?.78:.62));sc.add(new T.AmbientLight(LT?0xffffff:0x1c2d4c,LT?.34:.42));
  const key=new T.DirectionalLight(LT?0xffffff:0xd6e4ff,LT?.82:.58);key.position.set(-260,420,260);key.castShadow=true;Object.assign(key.shadow.camera,{left:-400,right:400,top:400,bottom:-400,near:50,far:1400});key.shadow.mapSize.set(2048,2048);key.shadow.bias=-.0004;key.shadow.normalBias=.4;sc.add(key);
  const rim=new T.DirectionalLight(LT?0xcfe2ee:0x3f6fff,LT?.3:.4);rim.position.set(380,220,-420);sc.add(rim);
  const gm={box:new T.BoxGeometry(1,1,1),plane:new T.PlaneGeometry(1,1),cyl:new T.CylinderGeometry(.5,.5,1,40)};gm.boxE=new T.EdgesGeometry(gm.box);gm.cylE=new T.EdgesGeometry(gm.cyl,20);KM.gm=gm;
  const flat=(mt,sx,sz,y,ro)=>{const p=new T.Mesh(gm.plane,mt);p.scale.set(sx,sz,1);p.rotation.x=-Math.PI/2;p.position.y=y;if(ro!=null)p.renderOrder=ro;sc.add(p);return p;};
  KM.gnd=flat(new T.MeshBasicMaterial({color:P(0x050a14,0xd2dce1)}),7000,7000,-.5,-3);
  const dt=A.tex.dots;dt.wrapS=dt.wrapT=T.RepeatWrapping;dt.repeat.set(330,330);flat(new T.MeshBasicMaterial({map:dt,transparent:true,opacity:P(.3,.24),depthWrite:false}),3000,3000,-.45,-2);
  flat(new T.MeshBasicMaterial({map:A.tex.radial,color:P(0x1d58b0,0xffffff),transparent:true,opacity:P(.5,.75),blending:A.AB,depthWrite:false}),1500,1100,-.42,-1);
  const pts=KM_BLOB.map(p=>kmP('f',p[0],p[1])),sh=new T.Shape(),mid=(a,b)=>[(a[0]+b[0])/2,(a[1]+b[1])/2],nP=pts.length;let m0=mid(pts[nP-1],pts[0]);sh.moveTo(m0[0],-m0[1]);for(let i=0;i<nP;i++){const p=pts[i],q=mid(p,pts[(i+1)%nP]);sh.quadraticCurveTo(p[0],-p[1],q[0],-q[1]);}
  const blob=new T.Mesh(new T.ShapeGeometry(sh,24),new T.MeshBasicMaterial({color:P(0x0b1630,0xe9eff1),transparent:true,opacity:P(.62,.72),depthWrite:false}));blob.rotation.x=-Math.PI/2;blob.position.y=-.3;blob.renderOrder=-1;sc.add(blob);
  const bp=sh.getPoints(24).map(p=>new T.Vector3(p.x,-.2,-p.y));bp.push(bp[0].clone());sc.add(new T.Line(new T.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(bp),new T.LineBasicMaterial({color:P(0x2f6cc0,0x7f909c),transparent:true,opacity:P(.6,.6)})));
  KM_ROADS.forEach(([w,L])=>{const V=L.map(p=>kmP('f',p[0],p[1]));for(let i=1;i<V.length;i++){const a=V[i-1],b=V[i],dx=b[0]-a[0],dz=b[1]-a[1],len=Math.hypot(dx,dz);const r=new T.Mesh(gm.box,m.road);r.scale.set(len,.2,w);r.position.set((a[0]+b[0])/2,.1,(a[1]+b[1])/2);r.rotation.y=-Math.atan2(dz,dx);sc.add(r);}
    V.forEach(p=>{const d=new T.Mesh(gm.cyl,m.road);d.scale.set(w,.2,w);d.position.set(p[0],.1,p[1]);sc.add(d);});const ln=new T.Line(new T.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(V.map(p=>new T.Vector3(p[0],.24,p[1]))),m.roadL);ln.computeLineDistances();sc.add(ln);});
  const zt=A.tex.hatch;zt.wrapS=zt.wrapT=T.RepeatWrapping;zt.repeat.set(.06,.06);const zs=new T.Shape();KM_ZONE.forEach((p,i)=>{const q=kmP('z',p[0],p[1]);i?zs.lineTo(q[0],-q[1]):zs.moveTo(q[0],-q[1]);});
  const zone=new T.Mesh(new T.ShapeGeometry(zs),new T.MeshBasicMaterial({map:zt,transparent:true,opacity:P(.75,.85),depthWrite:false}));zone.rotation.x=-Math.PI/2;zone.position.y=.22;sc.add(zone);
  const shd=new T.Mesh(gm.plane,new T.ShadowMaterial({opacity:LT?.14:.45}));shd.scale.set(1600,1200,1);shd.rotation.x=-Math.PI/2;shd.position.y=.3;shd.receiveShadow=true;sc.add(shd);
  KNL.forEach(b=>{if(b.nopos)return;const k=b.k||'bld',cyl=k==='heli',{bs,ab}=kmFloors(b),nA=Math.max(1,ab.length),g=new T.Group();sc.add(g);
    const R=b.r.map(([sp,x,y,l,w,a])=>{const [px,pz]=kmP(sp,x,y);return {x:px,z:pz,l:kmL(sp,l),w:kmL(sp,w),a:-a*Math.PI/180};});let cx=0,cz=0,ext=0;R.forEach(q=>{cx+=q.x*q.l*q.w;cz+=q.z*q.l*q.w;ext=Math.max(ext,q.l,q.w);});const area=R.reduce((s,q)=>s+q.l*q.w,0);cx/=area;cz/=area;
    const B={b,g,R,k,cx,cz,ext,fl:[],bsF:[],roof:[],deck:[],gB:k==='cum'?m.glassK:k==='park'?m.glassP:m.glass,eB:k==='cum'?m.edgeK:k==='park'?m.edgeP:m.edge,sB:k==='cum'?null:m.slab};
    const geo=cyl?gm.cyl:gm.box,geoE=cyl?gm.cylE:gm.boxE,gh=KM_FH-.32;
    const floor=(y,under)=>{const F={y,slabs:[],glass:[],edges:[],or:false,grp:new T.Group()};g.add(F.grp);R.forEach(q=>{const lw=cyl?q.l:q.w;
        if(B.sB){const sl=new T.Mesh(geo,B.sB);sl.scale.set(q.l+.6,.32,lw+.6);sl.position.set(q.x,y+.16,q.z);sl.rotation.y=q.a;sl.castShadow=true;sl.receiveShadow=true;F.grp.add(sl);F.slabs.push(sl);}
        const gl=new T.Mesh(geo,B.gB);gl.scale.set(q.l,gh,lw);gl.position.set(q.x,y+.32+gh/2,q.z);gl.rotation.y=q.a;gl.renderOrder=2;gl.userData.b=b.id;F.grp.add(gl);F.glass.push(gl);if(!under)KM.pick.push(gl);
        const e=new T.LineSegments(geoE,B.eB);e.scale.copy(gl.scale);e.position.copy(gl.position);e.rotation.y=q.a;F.grp.add(e);F.edges.push(e);});return F;};
    for(let i=0;i<nA;i++)B.fl.push(floor(i*KM_FH,false));
    bs.forEach((f,j)=>{const F=floor(-(bs.length-j)*KM_FH,true);F.grp.visible=false;B.bsF.push(F);});
    let H=nA*KM_FH;if(B.sB){R.forEach(q=>{const lw=cyl?q.l:q.w,rf=new T.Mesh(geo,B.sB);rf.scale.set(q.l+.6,.36,lw+.6);rf.position.set(q.x,H+.18,q.z);rf.rotation.y=q.a;rf.castShadow=true;rf.receiveShadow=true;g.add(rf);B.roof.push(rf);});H+=.36;}
    if(cyl){const q=R[0],r=q.l/2,dk=new T.Mesh(gm.cyl,m.deck);dk.scale.set(r*1.9,.3,r*1.9);dk.position.set(q.x,H+.15,q.z);dk.castShadow=true;g.add(dk);
      const mk=new T.Mesh(gm.plane,m.mark);mk.scale.set(r*1.78,r*1.78,1);mk.rotation.x=-Math.PI/2;mk.position.set(q.x,H+.32,q.z);g.add(mk);
      const ring=new T.Mesh(new T.TorusGeometry(r*.97,.32,12,64),m.ring);ring.rotation.x=Math.PI/2;ring.position.set(q.x,H+.34,q.z);g.add(ring);B.deck.push(dk,mk,ring);H+=.4;}
    B.H=H;KM.B[b.id]=B;
    const lab=kmLabel(`<b>${esc(b.id==='PD'?'Parkovací dům':b.id==='CUM'?'Stavba CUM':b.id)}</b>`,new T.Vector3(cx,H+2,cz),'kml'+(k!=='bld'?' k-'+k:''),{b:b.id,kind:'b'});lab.el.title=b.n;});
  KM_MK.forEach(mk=>{const [x,z]=kmP('f',mk.p[0],mk.p[1]);kmLabel(`${mk.ic==='P'?'<i class="kmP">P</i>':`<i class="kmA">${ico(mk.ic==='car'?'arr':'user')}</i>`}${mk.s||mk.ic!=='P'?`<span><b>${esc(mk.t)}</b>${mk.s?`<small>${esc(mk.s)}</small>`:''}</span>`:''}`,new T.Vector3(x,1.5,z),'kmm'+(mk.ic==='P'?' kp':''),{kind:'mk'});});
  KM_URG.forEach(u=>{const B=KM.B[u.b];if(!B)return;kmLabel(`<i class="kmU">+</i><span>${esc(u.t)}</span>`,new T.Vector3(B.cx,2.5,B.cz),'kmu',{b:u.b,kind:'urg'});});
  kmRoomsBuild();
  Object.values(KM.B).forEach(B=>{if(!B.orTop)return;kmLabel('',B.orTop.clone(),'kmo',{b:B.b.id,kind:'ors'});});
  KM.flL=kmLabel('',new T.Vector3(),'m3f kmf',{kind:'fl'});
  kmApply();}
function kmRoomsBuild(){const T=THREE,A=KM.A,LT=A.LT,gm=KM.gm;
  KM_OR.forEach((g,gi)=>{const B=KM.B[g.b];if(!B)return;const fi=B.b.f.findIndex(x=>x[0]===g.f);if(fi<0)return;const rooms=kmGrpRooms(g);if(!rooms.length)return;
    const nb=kmFloors(B.b).bs.length,ia=fi-nb,F=ia>=0?B.fl[ia]:B.bsF[fi];if(!F)return;if(g.or)F.or=true;
    const q=B.R[g.ri||0],n=rooms.length,slot=Math.min(q.l*.88/n,13),rl=slot*.8,rd=Math.min(q.w*.62,10.5),y=kmFloorY(B.b,fi)+.34,h=KM_FH-1,c=Math.cos(q.a),s=Math.sin(q.a),at=(u,v,yy)=>new T.Vector3(q.x+u*c+v*s,yy,q.z-u*s+v*c);
    rooms.forEach((r,i)=>{const u=(i-(n-1)/2)*slot,p=at(u,0,y),grp=new T.Group();grp.position.copy(p);grp.rotation.y=q.a;F.grp.add(grp);
      const pl=(mt,sx,sz,yy)=>{const x=new T.Mesh(gm.plane,mt);x.scale.set(sx,sz,1);x.rotation.x=-Math.PI/2;x.position.y=yy;grp.add(x);return x;};
      const tint=pl(new T.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0xffffff,transparent:true,opacity:.2,blending:A.AB,depthWrite:false}),rl,rd,.03);
      const glow=pl(new T.MeshBasicMaterial({map:A.tex.radial,color:0xffffff,transparent:true,opacity:.6,blending:A.AB,depthWrite:false,depthTest:false}),rl*1.5,rd*1.5,.05);glow.renderOrder=6;
      const vol=new T.Mesh(gm.box,A.vol());vol.scale.set(rl,h,rd);vol.position.y=h/2+.04;vol.renderOrder=3;grp.add(vol);
      const ln=(yy,ins)=>{const a=rl/2-ins,b=rd/2-ins,l=new T.LineLoop(new T.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints([[-a,-b],[a,-b],[a,b],[-a,b]].map(([x,z])=>new T.Vector3(x,yy,z))),new T.LineBasicMaterial({color:0xffffff,transparent:true,opacity:1}));grp.add(l);return l;};
      const top=ln(h+.04,0),bot=ln(.06,.3),lamp=pl(new T.MeshBasicMaterial({map:A.tex.radial,color:LT?0xfff3d0:0xffffff,transparent:true,opacity:LT?.6:.95,blending:A.AB,depthWrite:false}),rl*.5,rd*.5,h-.3);
      const L=kmLabel('',at(u,0,y+h+.6),'m3o cp kmr'+(i%2?' alt':''),{b:g.b,kind:'room',fi});
      KM.rooms.push({r,g,gi,B,fi,ia,grp,tint,glow,vol,top,bot,lamp,L,ph:i*1.7+gi,k:null,fade:1,ff:-1,glowO:0,volO:0});});
    KM.rows.push({b:g.b,fi,a:q.a,L:n*slot+6,c:at(0,0,y+1.2)});B.ng=(B.ng||0)+1;const lf=B.ng%2===0,end=at((lf?-1:1)*(n*slot/2+2),0,y+KM_FH*.5);kmLabel('',end,'m3f kmg'+(lf?' lf':''),{b:g.b,kind:'grp',fi,gi,n});
    if(g.or){const tp=at(n*slot/2-slot/2,0,y+h+.4);if(!B.orTop||tp.y>B.orTop.y)B.orTop=tp;}});}
function kmLabel(html,p,cls,meta){const el=document.createElement('div');el.className='m3l '+cls;el.innerHTML=`<div>${html}</div>`;el.style.display='none';KM.lbl.appendChild(el);const L={el,p,on:false,...meta};KM.labels.push(L);return L;}
function kmFlMat(B,F,st){const m=KM.A.mat,gl=st==='g'?m.glassG:st==='h'?m.glassH:F.or?m.glassOr:st==='s'?m.glassS:B.gB,ed=st==='g'?m.edgeG:st==='h'?m.edgeH:F.or?m.edgeOr:st==='s'?m.edgeS:B.eB,sl=st==='g'?m.slabG:B.sB;
  F.glass.forEach(x=>x.material=gl);F.edges.forEach(x=>x.material=ed);F.slabs.forEach(x=>{x.material=sl;x.castShadow=st!=='g';});}
function kmApply(){const o=S.km||{},sel=o.b;if(!KM.scene||!KM.A)return;const m=KM.A.mat;let under=false;
  Object.values(KM.B).forEach(B=>{const on=sel===B.b.id,dim=!!sel&&!on,nb=kmFloors(B.b).bs.length,fs=on&&o.f!=null?o.f-nb:null;
    B.fl.forEach((F,i)=>kmFlMat(B,F,dim?'g':fs==null?(on?'s':'n'):fs<0?'n':i===fs?'h':i>fs?'g':'n'));
    B.bsF.forEach((F,j)=>{const v=on&&o.f===j;F.grp.visible=v;if(v){kmFlMat(B,F,'h');under=true;}});
    const rg=dim||(fs!=null&&fs>=0);B.roof.forEach(x=>{x.material=rg?m.slabG:B.sB;x.castShadow=!rg;});B.deck.forEach(x=>x.visible=!(fs!=null&&fs>=0));
    B.fs=fs;B.on=on;B.dim=dim;});
  KM.rooms.forEach(rm=>{const B=rm.B;let f=1;if(B.dim)f=.2;else if(B.fs!=null&&B.fs>=0&&rm.ia>B.fs)f=.1;else if(B.fs!=null&&rm.ia!==B.fs)f=.22;rm.fade=f;});
  const gm=KM.gnd.material;gm.transparent=under;gm.opacity=under?.45:1;gm.depthWrite=!under;
  const L=KM.flL,B=sel&&KM.B[sel];L.vis=false;
  if(B&&o.f!=null&&B.b.f[o.f]&&!KM.rooms.some(r=>r.B===B&&r.fi===o.f)){const q=B.R[0],u=q.l/2+3,fl=B.b.f[o.f];L.p.set(q.x+u*Math.cos(q.a),kmFloorY(B.b,o.f)+KM_FH*.5,q.z-u*Math.sin(q.a));
    const h=`<b>${esc(fl[0])}</b><span>${fl[1].length?esc(fl[1][0])+(fl[1].length>1?` + ${fl[1].length-1} další`:''):'bez veřejných pracovišť'}</span>`;if(L.h!==h){L.h=h;L.el.firstChild.innerHTML=h;}L.vis=true;}
  kmStatus();}
function kmStat(r){const o=S.mp;return mpStat({id:'km'+r.id,k:'or',room:r.id},o.day,o.t);}
function kmCounts(){const c={};KM_OR.forEach(g=>kmGrpRooms(g).forEach(r=>{const k=kmStat(r).k;c[k]=(c[k]||0)+1;}));return c;}
function kmLegHTML(c){return [...MST_ORD.filter(k=>k!=='emerg'||c.emerg),...(c.off?['off']:[])].map(k=>`<span class="${c[k]?'':'z'}" style="--c:${MST[k].c}"><i></i>${MST[k].n}<b>${c[k]||0}</b></span>`).join('')+`<span class="ku" style="--c:#D9273E"><i></i>Urgentní příjem</span>`;}
function kmPaint(rm){const T=THREE,st=rm.st||{k:'off'},k=st.k||'off',f=rm.fade,LT=KM.A.LT,c=KM.col[k]||(KM.col[k]=new T.Color(MST[k].c));
  if(rm.k!==k||rm.ff!==f){rm.k=k;rm.ff=f;const lc=c.clone().lerp(new T.Color(LT?0x1b2a33:0xffffff),.28);
    rm.tint.material.color.copy(c);rm.tint.material.opacity=(k==='off'?.05:LT?.3:.2)*f;rm.glow.material.color.copy(c);rm.glowO=(k==='off'?.1:k==='free'?.4:.58)*f*(LT?1:1);rm.glow.material.opacity=rm.glowO;
    rm.vol.material.uniforms.uC.value.copy(c);rm.volO=(k==='off'?.06:k==='free'?.24:.38)*f*(LT?.6:1);rm.vol.material.uniforms.uO.value=rm.volO;
    [rm.top,rm.bot].forEach((l,i)=>{l.material.color.copy(lc);l.material.opacity=(k==='off'?.35:i?.8:1)*Math.max(.15,f);});rm.lamp.visible=(k==='op')&&f>.4;}
  const sub=st.sub||'',html=`<b><span>SÁL </span>${esc(rm.r.code)}</b><em style="--c:${MST[k].c}"><i></i>${MST[k].n}</em>${sub?`<small>${esc(sub)}</small>`:''}`;if(rm.L.h!==html){rm.L.h=html;rm.L.el.firstChild.innerHTML=html;}}
function kmStatus(){if(!KM.scene)return;const c={};KM.rooms.forEach(rm=>{rm.st=kmStat(rm.r);c[rm.st.k]=(c[rm.st.k]||0)+1;kmPaint(rm);});kmOrLabels();kmTimeDom();}
function kmOrLabels(){KM.labels.forEach(L=>{let html=null;
    if(L.kind==='ors'){const R=KM.rooms.filter(x=>x.B.b.id===L.b&&x.g.or),n=R.length,op=R.filter(x=>x.k==='op').length;html=`<i class="kmO"></i><b>${n} ${plural(n,'operační sál','operační sály','operačních sálů')}</b>${op?`<span>${op} ${plural(op,'operace','operace','operací')}</span>`:''}`;}
    else if(L.kind==='grp'){const g=KM_OR[L.gi],R=KM.rooms.filter(x=>x.gi===L.gi),op=R.filter(x=>x.k==='op').length;html=`<b>${esc(g.f)}</b><span>${esc(g.t)} · ${R.length} ${plural(R.length,'sál','sály','sálů')}${op?` · ${op} ${plural(op,'operace','operace','operací')}`:''}</span>`;}
    if(html!=null&&L.h!==html){L.h=html;L.el.firstChild.innerHTML=html;}});}
function kmTimeDom(){const o=S.mp,tr=M3.tr||[360,1200],c=document.getElementById('m3-clock');if(c)c.textContent=hm(o.t);const ts=document.getElementById('m3-ts');if(ts&&document.activeElement!==ts){ts.value=Math.round(o.t);ts.style.setProperty('--p',((o.t-tr[0])/(tr[1]-tr[0])*100).toFixed(2)+'%');}
  const lg=document.getElementById('km-leg');if(lg){const h=kmLegHTML(kmCounts());if(lg._h!==h){lg._h=h;lg.innerHTML=h;}}
  const sd=document.getElementById('km-ors'),b=kmB((S.km||{}).b);if(sd&&b){const h=kmOrsHTML(b);if(sd._h!==h){sd._h=h;sd.innerHTML=h;}}}
function kmLabels(){const r=KM.orb.r,o=S.km||{},sel=o.b,cam=KM.camera,W=KM.w,H=KM.h;for(const L of KM.labels){let show=true;
    switch(L.kind){case 'mk':show=r<1500&&!sel;break;
      case 'urg':{show=!sel||sel===L.b;const cp=!sel&&r>420;if(L.cp!==cp){L.cp=cp;L.el.classList.toggle('cp',cp);}break;}
      case 'ors':show=!sel&&r<620;break;
      case 'grp':show=sel===L.b&&o.f==null;break;
      case 'room':show=sel===L.b&&o.f===L.fi;if(show&&!L.md){L.md=1;L.el.classList.remove('cp');L.el.classList.add('md');}break;
      case 'fl':show=!!L.vis;break;}
    if(show){KM.v.copy(L.p).project(cam);show=KM.v.z<1&&Math.abs(KM.v.x)<1.05&&Math.abs(KM.v.y)<1.05;}
    if(!show){if(L.on){L.el.style.display='none';L.on=false;}continue;}if(!L.on){L.el.style.display='';L.on=true;}L.el.style.transform=`translate(${((KM.v.x+1)/2*W).toFixed(1)}px,${((1-KM.v.y)/2*H).toFixed(1)}px)`;
    if(L.kind==='b'){const on=sel===L.b,dm=!!sel&&!on;if(L.on2!==on||L.dm!==dm){L.on2=on;L.dm=dm;L.el.classList.toggle('sel',on);L.el.classList.toggle('dm',dm);}}}}
function kmMount(){const host=$('#km-host');if(!host){kmStop();return;}
  if(!window.THREE||!M3.libs){m3Load().then(()=>{if(S.view==='map'&&S.mp.tab==='map')kmMount();}).catch(()=>{const h=$('#km-host');if(h)h.innerHTML='<div class="m3-load">3D knihovnu se nepodařilo načíst. Zkontrolujte připojení k internetu a obnovte stránku.</div>';});return;}
  if(!kmInit()){host.innerHTML='<div class="m3-load">3D zobrazení není v tomto prohlížeči dostupné (vyžaduje WebGL).</div>';return;}
  if(!KM.scene||KM.key!==kmKey())kmBuild();else kmApply();
  host.innerHTML='';host.appendChild(KM.wrap);KM.host=host;if(KM.ro)KM.ro.disconnect();KM.ro=new ResizeObserver(()=>kmSize());KM.ro.observe(host);kmSize();if(!KM.fit){KM.fit=1;const ks=S.km||{};if(ks.b&&KM.B[ks.b]){kmFly(ks.b,ks.f);Object.assign(KM.orb,KM.goal);}else kmHome(true);}
  KM.clk=Math.floor(S.mp.t);if(!KM.raf){KM.lt=0;KM.raf=requestAnimationFrame(kmLoop);}}
function kmStop(){if(KM.raf){cancelAnimationFrame(KM.raf);KM.raf=0;}if(KM.ro){KM.ro.disconnect();KM.ro=null;}}
function kmSize(){if(!KM.host||!KM.renderer)return;const w=KM.host.clientWidth,h=KM.host.clientHeight;if(!w||!h)return;KM.w=w;KM.h=h;KM.renderer.setSize(w,h);if(KM.comp)KM.comp.setSize(w,h);KM.camera.aspect=w/h;KM.camera.updateProjectionMatrix();}
function kmAnim(t){for(const r of KM.rooms){const k=r.k;let f=1;if(k==='emerg')f=.5+.8*Math.abs(Math.sin(t*3.2));else if(k==='prep')f=.84+.2*Math.sin(t*2.2+r.ph);else if(k==='op')f=.94+.08*Math.sin(t*1.2+r.ph);else if(k==='clean')f=.88+.14*Math.sin(t*3+r.ph);
  r.vol.material.uniforms.uO.value=r.volO*f;r.glow.material.opacity=r.glowO*(.82+.18*f);}}
function kmLoop(t){KM.raf=requestAnimationFrame(kmLoop);if(!KM.host||!KM.host.isConnected||!KM.scene)return;const dt=KM.lt?Math.min(.06,(t-KM.lt)/1000):.016;KM.lt=t;const o=KM.orb,g=KM.goal,k=1-Math.pow(.002,dt),mp=S.mp;
  if(mp.live){if(mp.day!==today)mp.live=false;else mp.t=m3Now();}else if(mp.play){const r=M3.tr||[360,1200];mp.t+=dt*6;if(mp.t>r[1])mp.t=r[0];}
  if(Math.floor(mp.t)!==KM.clk){KM.clk=Math.floor(mp.t);kmStatus();}
  ['r','th','ph','tx','ty','tz'].forEach(p=>o[p]+=(g[p]-o[p])*k);const c=KM.camera;c.position.set(o.tx+o.r*Math.sin(o.ph)*Math.sin(o.th),o.ty+o.r*Math.cos(o.ph),o.tz+o.r*Math.sin(o.ph)*Math.cos(o.th));c.lookAt(o.tx,o.ty,o.tz);
  kmAnim(t/1000);if(KM.comp&&!KM.A.LT)KM.comp.render(dt);else KM.renderer.render(KM.scene,c);kmLabels();
  if(dt>.045){if(++KM.slow>90&&KM.pr>1){KM.pr=1;KM.renderer.setPixelRatio(1);if(KM.comp)KM.comp.setPixelRatio(1);kmSize();KM.slow=0;}}else KM.slow=Math.max(0,(KM.slow||0)-1);}
function kmHome(inst){const asp=KM.w&&KM.h?KM.w/KM.h:1.3;Object.assign(KM.goal,{r:clampN(1000/Math.min(1.5,Math.max(.55,asp)),640,1500),th:.22,ph:.8,tx:-10,ty:0,tz:-8});if(inst)Object.assign(KM.orb,KM.goal);}
function kmFly(id,fi){const B=KM.B[id];if(!B)return;const row=fi!=null&&(KM.rows||[]).find(x=>x.b===id&&x.fi===fi);
  if(row){const asp=KM.w&&KM.h?KM.w/KM.h:1.3,g=KM.goal,TAU=Math.PI*2,d=th=>{const x=((th-g.th)%TAU+TAU)%TAU;return Math.min(x,TAU-x);},th=d(row.a)<=d(row.a+Math.PI)?row.a:row.a+Math.PI,dt=((th-g.th)%TAU+TAU+Math.PI)%TAU-Math.PI;
    Object.assign(g,{tx:row.c.x,ty:row.c.y,tz:row.c.z,th:g.th+dt,ph:.72,r:clampN(row.L/(.34*Math.min(asp,1.6)),150,320)});return;}
const nar=KM.w&&KM.w/KM.h<.9?1.35:1,y=fi!=null?kmFloorY(B.b,fi):B.H*.35;Object.assign(KM.goal,{tx:B.cx,tz:B.cz,ty:Math.max(-8,y),r:clampN(Math.max(B.ext*3.4,B.H*4,210),210,520)*nar,ph:Math.min(KM.goal.ph,.95)});}
function kmHit(e){const c=KM.renderer.domElement,rc=c.getBoundingClientRect(),m=new THREE.Vector2((e.clientX-rc.left)/rc.width*2-1,-(e.clientY-rc.top)/rc.height*2+1);KM.ray.setFromCamera(m,KM.camera);const h=KM.ray.intersectObjects(KM.pick,false)[0];return {b:h&&h.object.userData.b||null,x:e.clientX-rc.left,y:e.clientY-rc.top};}
function kmTip(h){let el=KM.tip;if(!el){el=KM.tip=document.createElement('div');el.className='m3-tip';KM.wrap.appendChild(el);}if(!h||!h.b){el.hidden=true;if(KM.renderer)KM.renderer.domElement.style.cursor='';return;}const b=kmB(h.b);if(!b){el.hidden=true;return;}const {ab,bs}=kmFloors(b);
  const html=`<div class="tp-h"><b>${esc(b.id.length<3?b.id+' · ':'')}${esc(b.n)}</b></div><p>${ab.length} ${plural(ab.length,'nadzemní podlaží','nadzemní podlaží','nadzemních podlaží')}${bs.length?` · ${bs.length} ${plural(bs.length,'podzemní','podzemní','podzemních')}`:''}</p><small class="tp-f">Klepnutím zobrazíte oddělení</small>`;if(el._h!==html){el._h=html;el.innerHTML=html;}el.hidden=false;KM.renderer.domElement.style.cursor='pointer';const x=h.x+16+250>KM.w?h.x-16-250:h.x+16;el.style.transform=`translate(${x}px,${Math.min(Math.max(8,h.y-20),KM.h-120)}px)`;}
function kmDown(e){const c=KM.renderer.domElement;try{c.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(x){}KM.ptrs.set(e.pointerId,{x:e.clientX,y:e.clientY});KM.drag={b:e.button,sh:e.shiftKey,mv:0,pinch:KM.ptrs.size===2?kmPinch():0};}
function kmPinch(){const p=[...KM.ptrs.values()];return p.length<2?0:Math.hypot(p[0].x-p[1].x,p[0].y-p[1].y);}
function kmMove(e){const pr=KM.ptrs.get(e.pointerId);if(!KM.drag||!pr){if(e.pointerType!=='touch'){const now=performance.now();if(now-(KM.hvT||0)>60){KM.hvT=now;kmTip(kmHit(e));}}return;}kmTip(null);const dx=e.clientX-pr.x,dy=e.clientY-pr.y;pr.x=e.clientX;pr.y=e.clientY;KM.drag.mv+=Math.abs(dx)+Math.abs(dy);const g=KM.goal;
  if(KM.ptrs.size>=2){const d=kmPinch();if(KM.drag.pinch&&d)g.r=clampN(g.r*KM.drag.pinch/d,40,1400);KM.drag.pinch=d;return;}
  if(KM.drag.b===2||KM.drag.b===1||KM.drag.sh){const s=g.r*.0017,sn=Math.sin(g.th),cs=Math.cos(g.th);g.tx+=(-cs*dx+sn*dy)*s;g.tz+=(sn*dx+cs*dy)*s;}else{g.th-=dx*.0065;g.ph=clampN(g.ph-dy*.0055,.06,1.42);}}
function kmUp(e){const pr=KM.ptrs.get(e.pointerId);KM.ptrs.delete(e.pointerId);if(!KM.drag)return;const d=KM.drag;if(!KM.ptrs.size)KM.drag=null;if(!pr||d.mv>6||d.b!==0)return;const h=kmHit(e);kmSel(h.b&&h.b===(S.km||{}).b?h.b:h.b||null,null);}
function kmSel(b,f){const o=S.km||(S.km={b:null,f:null,q:''});o.b=b&&kmB(b)?b:null;o.f=o.b&&f!=null?f:null;if(KM.tip)kmTip(null);render();if(KM.scene){if(o.b){const B=kmB(o.b);if(B&&!B.nopos)kmFly(o.b,o.f);}else kmHome();}}

/* ---------- rozhraní ---------- */
function kmFloorOpts(b){const L=b.f.map((x,i)=>[i,x]).filter(([,x])=>x[0]);return [...L].reverse();}
function kmHTML(){const o=S.km||(S.km={b:null,f:null,q:''}),b=o.b?kmB(o.b):null,mp=S.mp,tr=kmTR();M3.tr=tr;
  const bopt=`<option value="">Vyberte budovu…</option>${KNL.map(x=>`<option value="${x.id}"${o.b===x.id?' selected':''}>${esc(x.id.length<3?x.id+' – ':'')}${esc(x.n)}</option>`).join('')}`;
  const fopt=b?`<option value="">Celá budova</option>${kmFloorOpts(b).map(([i,x])=>`<option value="${i}"${o.f===i?' selected':''}>${esc(x[0])}${x[1].length?` · ${x[1].length}`:''}</option>`).join('')}`:'<option value="">Podlaží</option>';
  return `<div class="m3-grid km-grid"><section class="card m3-card km-card">
      <div id="km-host" class="m3-host km-host"><div class="m3-load"><i></i>${window.THREE?'Připravuji 3D mapu areálu…':'Načítám 3D mapu areálu…'}</div></div>

          </div>
        <div class="km-sel"><div class="km-find"><div class="km-q">${ico('search')}<input id="km-q" type="search" autocomplete="off" placeholder="Najděte oddělení, ambulanci nebo poradnu" value="${esc(o.q||'')}" aria-label="Hledat oddělení"></div><div class="km-res" id="km-res" hidden></div></div><select class="in" id="km-b" data-ch="kmBSel" aria-label="Budova">${bopt}</select><select class="in" id="km-f" data-ch="kmFSel" aria-label="Podlaží"${b?'':' disabled'}>${fopt}</select></div>
        <div class="m3-view"><span class="m3-cap">Pohled</span><div class="m3-vb"><button data-act="kmCam" data-v="home" title="Celý areál" aria-label="Celý areál">${m3I('cube')}</button><button data-act="kmCam" data-v="top" title="Půdorys" aria-label="Půdorys">${m3I('plan')}</button><button data-act="kmCam" data-v="in" title="Přiblížit" aria-label="Přiblížit">${ico('plus')}</button><button data-act="kmCam" data-v="out" title="Oddálit" aria-label="Oddálit"><b style="font-size:16px;line-height:1">−</b></button></div></div>
        <div class="m3-bot"><div class="m3-leg" id="km-leg">${kmLegHTML(kmCounts())}</div>${kmTimeHTML(tr)}</div>
        <div class="m3-help">Tažením otáčíte · pravým tlačítkem posouváte · kolečkem přibližujete · klepnutím vyberete budovu</div>
      </div></section>
    <aside class="m3-side">${b?kmBldHTML(b):kmListHTML()}</aside></div>`;}
function kmListHTML(){const G=[['Pavilony a centra',KNL.filter(x=>!x.k&&x.id.length<3&&!x.nopos)],['Další objekty',KNL.filter(x=>x.k||x.nopos||x.id.length>2)]];
  return `<section class="card"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Budovy areálu</div><span class="cnt">${KNL.length}</span></div>${G.map(([t,L])=>`<div class="m3-cap" style="margin:6px 0 6px">${t}</div><div class="km-bl">${L.map(x=>`<button class="km-bb" data-act="kmB" data-v="${x.id}"><b>${esc(x.id==='PD'?'P':x.id==='CUM'?'✕':x.id)}</b><span>${esc(x.n)}</span>${kmOrs(x.id).length?'<em title="Operační sály"><s>operační sály</s></em>':''}</button>`).join('')}</div>`).join('')}</section>
    <section class="card"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Vstupy a urgentní příjmy</div></div><div class="mp-fl">${KM_URG.map(u=>`<button class="mp-fr" data-act="kmB" data-v="${u.b}"><i style="--c:#D9273E"></i><span>${esc(u.t)}</span><em>${u.b} · přízemí</em></button>`).join('')}${KM_MK.filter(m=>m.ic!=='P').map(m=>`<div class="mp-fr"><i style="--c:var(--ink-3)"></i><span>${esc(m.t)}</span><em>${esc(m.s)}</em></div>`).join('')}</div>
    <p class="hint" style="margin:10px 0 0">Do areálu se vjíždí hlavním vjezdem, parkovací dům má vjezd ze Šaldova náměstí. ${esc(KM_SRC)}</p></section>`;}
function kmBldHTML(b){const o=S.km,ors=kmOrs(b.id),{bs,ab}=kmFloors(b),urg=KM_URG.filter(u=>u.b===b.id);
  return `<section class="card"><div class="card-h km-h"><div style="display:flex;gap:12px;align-items:center;min-width:0"><span class="km-badge${b.k?' k-'+b.k:''}">${esc(b.id==='PD'?'P':b.id==='CUM'?'✕':b.id)}</span><div style="min-width:0"><div class="eyebrow"><i></i>${b.k==='heli'?'Heliport':b.k==='park'?'Parkování':b.k==='cum'?'Stavba':'Budova'}${b.nopos?' · poloha na mapě neuvedena':''}</div><h3 class="bc-st-h">${esc(b.n)}</h3></div></div><button class="icon-btn sm" data-act="kmB" data-v="" aria-label="Zavřít">${ico('x')}</button></div>
    <p class="hint" style="margin:0">${ab.length} ${plural(ab.length,'nadzemní podlaží','nadzemní podlaží','nadzemních podlaží')}${bs.length?` · ${bs.length} ${plural(bs.length,'podzemní','podzemní','podzemních')}`:''}${b.info?` · ${esc(b.info)}`:''}${b.pad?` · ${esc(b.pad)}`:''}</p>
    ${urg.map(u=>`<div class="km-urg"><i>+</i>${esc(u.t)} · přízemí</div>`).join('')}</section>
    ${ors.length?`<section class="card"><div id="km-ors">${kmOrsHTML(b)}</div><button class="btn sm pri" data-act="kmGo3d">${m3I('cube')}Otevřít Sály 3D</button></section>`:''}
    <section class="card"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Podlaží a oddělení</div></div><div class="km-fl">${kmFloorOpts(b).map(([i,x])=>{const on=o.f===i,hasOr=ors.some(g=>g.f===x[0]);return `<div class="km-f${on?' on':''}"><button class="km-fh" data-act="kmF" data-v="${i}" aria-expanded="${on}"><b>${esc(x[0])}</b>${hasOr?'<em class="or">sály</em>':''}<span>${x[1].length?`${x[1].length} ${plural(x[1].length,'pracoviště','pracoviště','pracovišť')}`:'bez veřejných pracovišť'}</span></button>${on&&x[1].length?`<ul>${x[1].map(t=>`<li${/24\/7|pohotovost/i.test(t)?' class="u"':''}>${esc(t)}</li>`).join('')}</ul>`:''}</div>`;}).join('')}</div>
    <p class="hint" style="margin:10px 0 0">${esc(KM_SRC)}</p></section>`;}
function kmOrsHTML(b){const ors=kmOrs(b.id);return `<div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Operační sály · ${DOW[isoDow(S.mp.day)].toLowerCase()} ${fmt(S.mp.day)} ${hm(S.mp.t)}</div></div>${ors.map(g=>`<div class="m3-cap" style="margin:4px 0 6px">${esc(g.t)} · ${esc(g.f)}</div><div class="mp-fl">${g.rooms.map(x=>`<div class="mp-fr"><i style="--c:${MST[x.st.k].c}"></i><span><b>${esc(x.r.code)}</b> ${esc(x.r.name)}</span><em>${MST[x.st.k].n}</em></div>`).join('')}</div><p class="hint" style="margin:6px 0 10px">${kmOrSum(g)}</p>`).join('')}`;}
function kmSearch(q){const box=$('#km-res');if(!box)return;const k=kmNorm(q.trim());if(k.length<2){box.hidden=true;box.innerHTML='';return;}const L=kmIdx().filter(x=>x.k.includes(k)).slice(0,9);

const SR=[['doc','capDoc','Anesteziologové','#76AFAA','AL'],['nur','capNur','Anest. sestry','#A496CB','AS'],['scr','capScr','Sálové sestry','#CF95A6','SS']];
function firstWork(y){let d=`${y}-01-01`;while(DI(d).weekend||DI(d).holiday)d=addD(d,1);return d;}
function stInit(){const o=S.st,y=S.year;let fd=focusDay();if(+fd.slice(0,4)!==y)fd=firstWork(y);
  if(!o.wk||+addD(o.wk,3).slice(0,4)!==y)o.wk=addD(fd,-(isoDow(fd)-1));
  if(!o.m||+o.m.slice(0,4)!==y)o.m=fd.slice(0,7)+'-01';
  if(!o.day||+o.day.slice(0,4)!==y)o.day=fd;return o;}
function gauge(need,cap,col,lab,sub){const r=26,C=2*Math.PI*r,p=cap?Math.min(1,need/cap):need?1:0,st=need>cap?'bad':cap&&need/cap>=.9?'tight':'ok';
  return `<div class="gauge ${st}"><svg viewBox="0 0 64 64" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="32" cy="32" r="${r}" class="bg"/><circle cx="32" cy="32" r="${r}" class="fg" style="stroke:${st==='bad'?'var(--bad)':col};stroke-dasharray:${(p*C).toFixed(1)} ${C.toFixed(1)}"/></svg><div><span>${lab}</span><b>${need}<small> / ${cap}</small></b><em>${need>cap?`chybí ${need-cap}`:`rezerva ${cap-need}`}</em>${sub?`<small class="gs">${sub}</small>`:''}</div></div>`;}
function stMeter(need,cap,hh,c,d){const mx=Math.max(need,cap),st=need>cap?'bad':cap&&need/cap>=.9?'tight':'ok';let u='';
  if(mx<=36){for(let i=0;i<mx;i++)u+=`<i class="${i<Math.min(need,cap)?'f':i>=cap?'x':''}"></i>`;u=`<div class="sm-u" style="--c:${c}">${u}</div>`;}
  else u=`<div class="sm-u bar" style="--c:${c}"><u><em style="width:${Math.min(100,cap?need/cap*100:100)}%"></em></u></div>`;
  return `<div class="sm-c ${st}" role="button" tabindex="0" data-act="stDay" data-d="${d}" title="Potřeba ${need}, kapacita ${cap}${hh?` · dopoledne ${hh[0]}, odpoledne ${hh[1]}`:''}"><div class="sm-n"><b>${need}</b><small>/ ${cap}</small><em>${need>cap?`chybí ${need-cap}`:`rezerva ${cap-need}`}</em></div>${u}${hh?`<div class="sm-h">dop ${hh[0]} · odp ${hh[1]}</div>`:''}</div>`;}
function stOps(site,d){let rooms=0,ca=0,la=0;for(const room of D.rooms){if(!room.active||room.site!==site||room.kind==='pacu')continue;const r=R(room,d);let on=false;r.h.forEach(h=>{if(h&&!h.released){on=true;if(h.an==='CA')ca++;else la++;}});if(on)rooms++;}return {rooms,ca,la};}
function stSumH(sites,d){const v=SDsum(sites,d);v.h=[0,1].map(i=>{const o={doc:0,nur:0,scr:0};sites.forEach(x=>{const hh=SD(x,d).h[i];o.doc+=hh.doc;o.nur+=hh.nur;o.scr+=hh.scr;});return o;});return v;}
function stWeekHTML(){const o=stInit(),sites=scopeSites(S.staffSite),mon=o.wk,days=[0,1,2,3,4].map(i=>addD(mon,i));
  const gauges=SR.map(([k,ck,lab,c])=>{let best=null;days.forEach(d=>{const di=DI(d);if(di.weekend||di.holiday)return;const v=SDsum(sites,d),r=v[ck]?v[k]/v[ck]:v[k]?9:0;if(!best||r>best.r)best={d,r,n:v[k],c:v[ck]};});return best?gauge(best.n,best.c,c,lab,`nejnáročnější den týdne: ${DOW[isoDow(best.d)].toLowerCase()} ${fmt(best.d)}`):gauge(0,0,c,lab,'v týdnu bez operativy');}).join('');
  let h=`<div class="wt" style="--nd:5"><div class="wt-hd corner"><b>Profese</b></div>`;

    h+=`<div class="wt-hd${d===today?' td':''}${di.holiday?' hol':''}"><b>${DOW_L[di.iso]}<span>${+d.slice(8)}. ${+d.slice(5,7)}.</span></b><div class="wt-tags">${d===today?'<span class="tag ok">Dnes</span>':''}${di.holiday?`<span class="tag hol">${esc(di.holiday)}</span>`:''}${di.school?`<span class="tag sch">${esc(di.school.short)} · prázdninová kapacita</span>`:''}</div><div class="wt-hm">${off?'<span>bez operativy</span>':miss?`<span class="bad">${ico('warn')}chybí ${miss} ${plural(miss,'osoba','osoby','osob')}</span>`:'<span class="ok">personál pokryt ✓</span>'}</div></div>`;});

    SR.forEach(([k,ck,lab,c,ab])=>{x+=`<div class="wt-lab"><i style="--c:${c}"></i><div><b>${lab}</b><code>${ab} · potřeba / kapacita</code></div></div>`+days.map(d=>{const di=DI(d);if(di.weekend||di.holiday)return `<div class="sm-off">${di.holiday?'svátek':'víkend'}</div>`;const v=getV(d);return stMeter(v[k],v[ck],v.h?[v.h[0][k],v.h[1][k]]:null,c,d);}).join('');});
    return x;};
  sites.forEach(st=>{const cp=D.staff.cap[st]||{};h+=block(SITE[st].name,`kapacita AL ${cp.doc||0} · AS ${cp.nurse||0} · SS ${cp.scrub||0} · o prázdninách ${cp.docH||0} / ${cp.nurseH||0} / ${cp.scrubH||0}`,d=>SD(st,d),st);});
  if(sites.length>1)h+=block('Celkem KNL','součet pracovišť – personál se mezi nimi nepřesouvá',d=>stSumH(sites,d),null);

  return `<div class="gg" style="margin-bottom:16px">${gauges}</div><section class="card wt-card" style="margin:0 0 16px"><div class="wt-top"><div class="wt-nav"><button class="icon-btn" data-act="stWk" data-v="-7" aria-label="Předchozí týden">${ico('cl')}</button><div class="wt-ttl">Týdenní rozpis personálu<small>Týden ${isoWeek(mon)} · ${PAR_N[parity(mon)]} · ${fmtR(mon,days[4])}</small></div><button class="icon-btn" data-act="stWk" data-v="7" aria-label="Další týden">${ico('cr')}</button></div><button class="btn sm" data-act="stWkToday">Tento týden</button><span class="sp"></span><span class="hint">Klepnutím na buňku otevřete rozpis dne po sálech</span></div><div class="ovx">${h}</div>
    <div class="wt-lg"><span><i style="background:var(--ink-3)"></i>potřeba – 1 dílek = 1 osoba</span><span><i style="background:var(--line-soft)"></i>volná kapacita</span><span><i style="background:var(--bad)"></i>chybí</span><span class="wt-lgs">Denní potřeba je vyšší z dopoledne a odpoledne podle normativu.</span></div></section>`;}

function stDayHTML(){const o=stInit(),d=o.day,di=DI(d),sites=scopeSites(S.staffSite),N=D.staff.norm,f1=v=>fmtN(v,v%1?1:0),up=x=>Math.ceil(x-1e-9);
  let h=`<section class="card"><div class="wt-top" style="padding:0 0 4px"><div class="wt-nav"><button class="icon-btn" data-act="stDayNav" data-v="-1" aria-label="Předchozí pracovní den">${ico('cl')}</button><div class="wt-ttl">${cap(DOW_L[di.iso])} ${+d.slice(8)}. ${MONTHS_G[+d.slice(5,7)-1]} ${d.slice(0,4)}<small>Týden ${isoWeek(d)} · ${PAR_N[parity(d)]}${di.school?' · '+esc(di.school.name)+' – prázdninová kapacita':''}${di.holiday?' · '+esc(di.holiday):''}</small></div><button class="icon-btn" data-act="stDayNav" data-v="1" aria-label="Další pracovní den">${ico('cr')}</button></div><span class="sp"></span><button class="btn sm" data-act="jump" data-d="${d}" data-z="week">${ico('tl')}Na časové ose</button></div>`;
  for(const st of sites){const v=SD(st,d),T=[{d:0,n:0,s:0},{d:0,n:0,s:0}];

      if(room.kind==='pacu'){const sf=room.staff||{doc:0,nurse:0};return `<tr class="pacu"><th><code>${esc(room.code)}</code>${esc(room.name)}</th>${[0,1].map(i=>{const op=r.open&&r.open[i];if(op){T[i].d+=+sf.doc||0;T[i].n+=+sf.nurse||0;}return op?`<td><span class="hp" style="--c:#7FBFB1">DP otevřeno</span></td><td class="r">${f1(+sf.doc||0)}</td><td class="r">${f1(+sf.nurse||0)}</td><td class="r">–</td>`:'<td class="mut">zavřeno</td><td></td><td></td><td></td>';}).join('')}</tr>`;}
      const blk=r.block&&!(r.src==='override'&&(r.h[0]||r.h[1]));
      return `<tr><th><code>${esc(room.code)}</code>${esc(room.name)}</th>${[0,1].map(i=>{const hh=r.h[i];if(!hh)return `<td class="mut">${blk?esc(r.block.type):'volno'}</td><td></td><td></td><td></td>`;if(hh.released)return `<td class="mut">${esc(hh.s)} · uvolněno</td><td></td><td></td><td></td>`;const sp=SPEC[hh.s]||{c:'#999'},q=N[hh.an]||{};T[i].d+=+q.doc||0;T[i].n+=+q.nurse||0;T[i].s+=+q.scrub||0;return `<td><span class="hp ${hh.an}" style="--c:${sp.c}">${esc(hh.s)}</span> <small>${hh.an}</small></td><td class="r">${f1(+q.doc||0)}</td><td class="r">${f1(+q.nurse||0)}</td><td class="r">${f1(+q.scrub||0)}</td>`;}).join('')}</tr>`;}).join('');
    h+=`<div class="st-site"><h3>${SITE[st].name}</h3><div class="gg">${SR.map(([k,ck,lab,c])=>gauge(v[k],v[ck],c,lab,`dopoledne ${v.h[0][k]} · odpoledne ${v.h[1][k]}`)).join('')}</div>

      <tr class="tot"><th>Potřeba (zaokrouhleno)</th>${T.map(t=>`<td></td><td class="r">${up(t.d)}</td><td class="r">${up(t.n)}</td><td class="r">${up(t.s)}</td>`).join('')}</tr>
      <tr class="tot cap"><th>Kapacita${di.school?' (prázdninová)':''}</th>${[0,1].map(()=>`<td></td><td class="r">${v.capDoc}</td><td class="r">${v.capNur}</td><td class="r">${v.capScr}</td>`).join('')}</tr></tbody></table></div></div>`;}
  return h+`<p class="hint" style="margin-top:12px">Normativ na sál a půlden: CA – AL ${f1(N.CA.doc)}, AS ${f1(N.CA.nurse)}, SS ${f1(N.CA.scrub)} · LA – AL ${f1(N.LA.doc)}, AS ${f1(N.LA.nurse)}, SS ${f1(N.LA.scrub)}. Dospávací pokoje podle nastavení pokoje. Upravíte na záložce Normativ a kapacita.</p></section>`;}

/* =========================================================
   Svátky a prázdniny: roční pás, mosty, fond pracovní doby
   ========================================================= */
const holOff=d=>{const di=DI(d);return di.weekend||!!di.holiday;};
const LWC={};
function longWeekends(y){if(LWC[y])return LWC[y];const out=[];
  for(let d=`${y}-01-01`;d<=`${y}-12-31`;d=addD(d,1)){
    if(holOff(d)&&!holOff(addD(d,-1))){let e=d;while(holOff(addD(e,1)))e=addD(e,1);const len=diffD(d,e)+1,hn=[];for(let x=d;x<=e;x=addD(x,1))if(DI(x).holiday)hn.push(DI(x).holiday);if(hn.length&&len>=3)out.push({k:'long',from:d,to:e,len,names:[...new Set(hn)]});}
    if(!holOff(d)&&holOff(addD(d,-1))&&holOff(addD(d,1))){const a=addD(d,-1),b=addD(d,1);if(DI(a).holiday||DI(b).holiday){let st=a;while(holOff(addD(st,-1)))st=addD(st,-1);let e=b;while(holOff(addD(e,1)))e=addD(e,1);out.push({k:'most',from:st,to:e,day:d,len:diffD(st,e)+1,names:[DI(a).holiday,DI(b).holiday].filter(Boolean)});}}}
  return LWC[y]=out.sort((a,b)=>a.from.localeCompare(b.from)||(a.k==='most'?1:-1));}
function holTag(d){const L=longWeekends(+d.slice(0,4)),b=L.find(x=>x.k==='most'&&d>=x.from&&d<=x.to),l=L.find(x=>x.k==='long'&&d>=x.from&&d<=x.to);
  return b?` <span class="tag" style="background:color-mix(in srgb,var(--warn) 22%,var(--raised))">most ${DOW[isoDow(b.day)].toLowerCase()} ${fmt(b.day)}</span>`:l?` <span class="tag hol">volno ${l.len} ${plural(l.len,'den','dny','dnů')}</span>`:'';}
function dayHalves(d){let n=0;for(const room of D.rooms){if(!room.active||room.kind==='pacu')continue;for(const h of R(room,d).h)if(h&&!h.released)n++;}return n;}
function holStripHTML(y,rg){const y0=`${y}-01-01`,y1=`${y}-12-31`,N=yearDays(y).length,cl=d=>d<y0?y0:d>y1?y1:d,X=d=>diffD(y0,cl(d))/N*100,W=(a,b)=>(diffD(cl(a),cl(b))+1)/N*100,hd=50/N;
  const td=today>=y0&&today<=y1?`<i class="hs-td" style="left:${(X(today)+hd).toFixed(2)}%"></i>`:'';
  const months=MONTHS.map((mn,i)=>{const a=`${y}-${pad(i+1)}-01`;return `<span style="left:${X(a).toFixed(2)}%;width:${W(a,mEnd(a)).toFixed(2)}%">${mn}</span>`;}).join('');
  const band=(a,b,cls,style,lab,tip)=>{const w=W(a,b);return `<div class="hs-b ${cls}" style="left:${X(a).toFixed(2)}%;width:${w.toFixed(2)}%;${style}" title="${esc(tip)}">${w>3?esc(lab):''}</div>`;};
  const hol=Object.entries(HOLI(y)).map(([d,n])=>`<i class="hs-h${DI(d).weekend?' we':''}" style="left:${(X(d)+hd).toFixed(2)}%" title="${fmtY(d)} · ${esc(n)}${DI(d).weekend?' (připadá na víkend)':''}"></i>`).join('');
  const lw=longWeekends(y).map(x=>x.k==='most'?`<i class="hs-br" style="left:${(X(x.day)+hd).toFixed(2)}%" title="Most ${cap(DOW_L[isoDow(x.day)])} ${fmtY(x.day)} · s mostem volno ${fmtR(x.from,x.to)} (${x.len} dny)"></i>`:band(x.from,x.to,'lw','','',`Prodloužený víkend ${fmtR(x.from,x.to)} · ${x.names.join(', ')}`)).join('');
  const sch=SCHOOL.filter(x=>x.to>=y0&&x.from<=y1).map(x=>band(x.from,x.to,'sch','',x.short||x.name,`${x.name} · ${fmtR(x.from,x.to)}`)).join('');
  const my=OKRES_G[rg.okres],spr=springWeeks(y).map(w=>band(w.a,w.b,w.g===my?'':'dim',`--c:${SPW_C[w.i]}`,String(w.i+1),`${w.i+1}. termín · ${fmtR(w.a,w.b)} · ${SPRING_G[w.g].join(', ')}`)).join('');
  const blk=D.blocks.filter(b=>b.to>=y0&&b.from<=y1).map(b=>band(b.from,b.to,'',`--c:${BT_C[b.type]||'#888'}`,BT_A[b.type]||'',`${b.type} · ${b.roomIds.map(id=>(ROOM[id]||{}).code||id).join(', ')} · ${fmtR(b.from,b.to)}`)).join('');
  let def='',st=null;const all=SITES.map(x=>x.id);for(const d of yearDays(y)){const z=SDsum(all,d).def;if(z&&!st)st=d;if(!z&&st){def+=band(st,addD(d,-1),'','--c:var(--bad)','',`Nedostatek personálu ${fmtR(st,addD(d,-1))}`);st=null;}}if(st)def+=band(st,y1,'','--c:var(--bad)','',`Nedostatek personálu ${fmtR(st,y1)}`);
  const row=(lab,sub,inner)=>`<div class="hs-l">${lab}${sub?`<small>${sub}</small>`:''}</div><div class="hs-t">${inner}${td}</div>`;
  return `<section class="card" style="margin-bottom:16px"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Roční přehled volna ${y}</div><span class="hint">Najetím myší zobrazíte detail · svislá čára = dnes</span></div>
    <div class="ovx"><div class="hs"><div></div><div class="hs-m">${months}</div>
    ${row('Státní svátky','šedě = připadá na víkend',hol)}${row('Prodloužené víkendy','šrafovaně = most',lw)}${row('Školní prázdniny',`okres ${esc(rg.okres)}`,sch)}${row('Jarní prázdniny v ČR','6 termínů podle okresů',spr)}${row('Omezení sálů','asanace, servis, útlum…',blk)}${row('Nedostatek personálu','celá KNL',def)}</div></div></section>`;}
function bridgesHTML(y){const L=longWeekends(y);if(!L.length)return '';
  return `<section class="card" style="margin-top:16px"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Prodloužené víkendy a mosty ${y}<span class="cnt">${L.length}</span></div><span class="hint">Most = jeden pracovní den mezi svátkem a víkendem. Počítejte se žádostmi o dovolenou a nižším obsazením.</span></div>
    <div class="ovx"><table class="tb"><thead><tr><th>Volno</th><th>Typ</th><th>Svátek</th><th>Délka volna</th><th>Den mostu</th><th class="r">Plán v den mostu</th><th></th></tr></thead><tbody>
    ${L.map(x=>`<tr${x.to<today?' class="past"':''}><th>${fmtR(x.from,x.to)}</th><td>${x.k==='most'?'<span class="tag" style="background:color-mix(in srgb,var(--warn) 22%,var(--raised))">Most</span>':'<span class="tag hol">Prodloužený víkend</span>'}</td><td class="wrap">${esc(x.names.join(', '))}</td><td><span class="lw">${'<i></i>'.repeat(x.len)}</span>${x.len} ${plural(x.len,'den','dny','dnů')}${x.k==='most'?' s mostem':''}</td><td>${x.k==='most'?`<b>${cap(DOW_L[isoDow(x.day)])} ${fmtY(x.day)}</b>`:'—'}</td><td class="r">${x.k==='most'?`${fmtN(dayHalves(x.day)/2,1)} sálodnů`:'—'}</td><td class="r"><div class="btns" style="justify-content:flex-end">${x.k==='most'&&isCoord()?`<button class="btn sm" data-act="bcBlock" data-d="${x.day}">Útlum</button>`:''}<button class="btn sm" data-act="jump" data-d="${x.k==='most'?x.day:x.from}" data-z="week">Na ose</button></div></td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table></div></section>`;}
function fundHTML(y){const rooms=D.rooms.filter(r=>r.active&&r.kind!=='pacu'),rows=[],T={cd:0,we:0,hw:0,wd:0,sch:0,pl:0,lost:0};
  for(let mo=0;mo<12;mo++){const m=`${y}-${pad(mo+1)}-01`,e=mEnd(m),r={mo,cd:0,we:0,hw:0,wd:0,sch:0,pl:0,lost:0};
    for(let d=m;d<=e;d=addD(d,1)){const di=DI(d);r.cd++;if(di.weekend)r.we++;else if(di.holiday){r.hw++;r.lost+=rooms.reduce((a,x)=>a+R(x,d).holLost,0)/2;}else{r.wd++;if(di.school)r.sch++;r.pl+=calDay(d,'all').pl;}}
    rows.push(r);Object.keys(T).forEach(k=>T[k]+=r[k]);}
  const mx=Math.max(...rows.map(r=>r.wd)),cm=y===todayY?+today.slice(5,7)-1:-1;
  return `<section class="card" style="margin-top:16px"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Fond pracovní doby ${y}</div><span class="hint">Pracovní dny bez víkendů a státních svátků · fond pro úvazek 40 h a 37,5 h týdně</span></div>
    <div class="ovx"><table class="tb fund"><thead><tr><th>Měsíc</th><th class="r">Kal. dny</th><th class="r">Víkendy</th><th class="r">Svátky v PD</th><th>Pracovní dny</th><th class="r">Fond 40 h</th><th class="r">Fond 37,5 h</th><th>PD v prázdninách</th><th class="r">Plán operativy</th><th class="r">Ztráta svátky</th></tr></thead><tbody>
    ${rows.map(r=>`<tr${r.mo===cm?' class="cur"':''}><th>${cap(MONTHS[r.mo])}</th><td class="r">${r.cd}</td><td class="r">${r.we}</td><td class="r">${r.hw?`<span class="tag hol">${r.hw}</span>`:'0'}</td><td><div class="fb"><u><em style="width:${(r.wd/mx*100).toFixed(0)}%"></em></u><b>${r.wd}</b></div></td><td class="r">${fmtN(r.wd*8)} h</td><td class="r">${fmtN(r.wd*7.5,1)} h</td><td><div class="fb sch"><u><em style="width:${r.wd?(r.sch/r.wd*100).toFixed(0):0}%"></em></u><b>${r.sch}</b></div></td><td class="r">${fmtN(r.pl)} h</td><td class="r">${r.lost?`−${fmtN(r.lost,1)} sálodnů`:'—'}</td></tr>`).join('')}
    <tr class="tot"><th>Celkem ${y}</th><td class="r">${T.cd}</td><td class="r">${T.we}</td><td class="r">${T.hw}</td><td><b>${T.wd}</b></td><td class="r">${fmtN(T.wd*8)} h</td><td class="r">${fmtN(T.wd*7.5,1)} h</td><td><b>${T.sch}</b></td><td class="r">${fmtN(T.pl)} h</td><td class="r">−${fmtN(T.lost,1)} sálodnů</td></tr></tbody></table></div></section>`;}
/* ---------- Personál: průběh dne po hodinách, týdny roku, roční mapa ---------- */
const SX_T0=360,SX_T1=1200,SX_ST=15,SXN=(SX_T1-SX_T0)/SX_ST;
const sxHm=m=>`${Math.floor(m/60)}:${pad(m%60)}`;
function sxHourly(site,d){const key='sxh|'+site+'|'+d;if(SC.has(key))return SC.get(key);const A=Array.from({length:SXN},()=>({doc:0,nur:0,scr:0})),rows=[],N=D.staff.norm;
  const rooms=siteGroups(site).flatMap(g=>sortRooms(D.rooms.filter(r=>r.active&&r.site===site&&gOf(r)===g)));
  for(const room of rooms){const r=R(room,d),H=hoursFor(room,d),iv=[[tmin(H.from),tmin(H.split)],[tmin(H.split),tmin(H.to)]],segs=[];
    iv.forEach(([a,b],i)=>{let q=null,lab='',c='#999',an='';
      if(room.kind==='pacu'){if(r.open&&r.open[i]){const sf=room.staff||{};q={doc:+sf.doc||0,nur:+sf.nurse||0,scr:0};lab='DP';c='#7FBFB1';}}
      else{const h=r.h[i];if(h&&!h.released){const qq=N[h.an]||{};q={doc:+qq.doc||0,nur:+qq.nurse||0,scr:+qq.scrub||0};lab=h.s;an=h.an;c=(SPEC[h.s]||{}).c||'#999';}}
      if(!q)return;const l=segs[segs.length-1];if(l&&l.b===a&&l.lab===lab&&l.an===an&&l.q.doc===q.doc&&l.q.nur===q.nur&&l.q.scr===q.scr)l.b=b;else segs.push({a,b,q,lab,c,an});
      for(let k=0;k<SXN;k++){const t=SX_T0+k*SX_ST;if(t>=a&&t<b){A[k].doc+=q.doc;A[k].nur+=q.nur;A[k].scr+=q.scr;}}});
    const blk=r.block&&!segs.length?r.block.type:'';rows.push({room,segs,blk});}
  const v={A:A.map(x=>({doc:Math.ceil(x.doc-1e-9),nur:Math.ceil(x.nur-1e-9),scr:Math.ceil(x.scr-1e-9)})),rows};SC.set(key,v);return v;}
function sxChart(A,v,d){const W=1000,L=58,R=14,PH=92,G=26,TOP=8,n=A.length,X=k=>L+(W-L-R)*k/n,H=TOP+SR.length*(PH+G)+8;let s='';
  for(let m=SX_T0;m<=SX_T1;m+=60){const x=X((m-SX_T0)/SX_ST).toFixed(1);s+=`<line x1="${x}" x2="${x}" y1="${TOP}" y2="${H-22}" class="sx-gl${m%120?'':' b'}"/>`;if(m%120===0)s+=`<text x="${x}" y="${H-6}" class="sx-ax" text-anchor="middle">${m/60}:00</text>`;}
  SR.forEach(([k,ck,lab,c,ab],p)=>{const y0=TOP+p*(PH+G),cap=v[ck],peak=Math.max(0,...A.map(a=>a[k])),mx=Math.max(cap,peak,2)*1.18,Y=val=>(y0+PH-val/mx*PH).toFixed(1),id=`sxg${p}`;
    let d1=`M${X(0)},${y0+PH}`;A.forEach((a,i)=>{d1+=` L${X(i).toFixed(1)},${Y(a[k])} L${X(i+1).toFixed(1)},${Y(a[k])}`;});d1+=` L${X(n)},${y0+PH} Z`;
    let ln='';A.forEach((a,i)=>{ln+=`${i?'L':'M'}${X(i).toFixed(1)},${Y(a[k])} L${X(i+1).toFixed(1)},${Y(a[k])} `;});
    let over='';A.forEach((a,i)=>{if(a[k]>cap)over+=`<rect x="${X(i).toFixed(1)}" width="${(X(i+1)-X(i)+.3).toFixed(1)}" y="${Y(a[k])}" height="${(Y(cap)-Y(a[k])).toFixed(1)}" class="sx-over"/>`;});
    const pk=A.findIndex(a=>a[k]===peak);
    s+=`<defs><linearGradient id="${id}" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1"><stop offset="0" stop-color="${c}" stop-opacity=".55"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="${c}" stop-opacity=".06"/></linearGradient></defs>
      <rect x="${L}" y="${y0}" width="${W-L-R}" height="${PH}" rx="8" class="sx-pan"/>
      <path d="${d1}" fill="url(#${id})"/>${over}<path d="${ln}" fill="none" stroke="${c}" stroke-width="2.2" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
      <line x1="${L}" x2="${W-R}" y1="${Y(cap)}" y2="${Y(cap)}" class="sx-cap"/><text x="${W-R-6}" y="${(+Y(cap)-5).toFixed(1)}" class="sx-capt" text-anchor="end">kapacita ${cap}</text>
      <text x="${L-10}" y="${y0+18}" class="sx-lab" text-anchor="end">${ab}</text><text x="${L-10}" y="${y0+34}" class="sx-sub" text-anchor="end">max ${peak}</text>
      ${peak&&pk>=0?`<circle cx="${((X(pk)+X(pk+1))/2).toFixed(1)}" cy="${Y(peak)}" r="4" fill="${peak>cap?'var(--bad)':c}" class="sx-pk"/><text x="${((X(pk)+X(pk+1))/2+8).toFixed(1)}" y="${(+Y(peak)+(Math.abs(+Y(peak)-+Y(cap))<16?17:-9)).toFixed(1)}" class="sx-pkt" text-anchor="start">${peak} · ${sxHm(SX_T0+pk*SX_ST)}</text>`:''}`;});
  if(d===today){const nm=new Date(),t=nm.getHours()*60+nm.getMinutes();if(t>=SX_T0&&t<=SX_T1){const x=X((t-SX_T0)/SX_ST).toFixed(1);s+=`<line x1="${x}" x2="${x}" y1="${TOP}" y2="${H-22}" class="sx-now"/><text x="${x}" y="${TOP+2}" class="sx-nowt" text-anchor="middle">teď</text>`;}}
  return `<svg class="sx-chart" viewBox="0 0 ${W} ${H}" role="img" aria-label="Průběh potřeby personálu během dne">${s}</svg>`;}
function sxGantt(rows){const P=m=>clampN((m-SX_T0)/(SX_T1-SX_T0)*100,0,100),tk=[];for(let m=SX_T0;m<=SX_T1;m+=120)tk.push(`<span style="left:${P(m)}%${m===SX_T1?';transform:translateX(-100%)':m===SX_T0?';transform:none':''}">${m/60}:00</span>`);
  const f1=v=>fmtN(v,v%1?1:0);
  return `<div class="sx-g"><div class="sx-gh"><span></span><div>${tk.join('')}</div></div>${rows.map(({room,segs,blk})=>`<div class="sx-gr${room.kind==='pacu'?' pacu':''}"><span><code>${esc(room.code)}</code>${esc(room.name)}</span><div class="sx-gt">${segs.map(g=>`<i style="left:${P(g.a)}%;width:${P(g.b)-P(g.a)}%;--c:${g.c}" title="${esc(room.code)} · ${esc(g.lab)} ${g.an} · ${sxHm(g.a)}–${sxHm(g.b)} · AL ${f1(g.q.doc)} · AS ${f1(g.q.nur)} · SS ${f1(g.q.scr)}"><b>${esc(g.lab)}${g.an?` <small>${g.an}</small>`:''}</b><em>${f1(g.q.doc)} · ${f1(g.q.nur)} · ${f1(g.q.scr)}</em></i>`).join('')}${!segs.length?`<em class="sx-free">${blk?esc(blk):'volno'}</em>`:''}</div></div>`).join('')}</div>`;}
function sxHoursHTML(){const o=stInit(),d=o.day,di=DI(d),sites=scopeSites(S.staffSite);
  let h=`<section class="card"><div class="wt-top" style="padding:0 0 6px"><div class="wt-nav"><button class="icon-btn" data-act="stDayNav" data-v="-1" aria-label="Předchozí pracovní den">${ico('cl')}</button><div class="wt-ttl">Průběh dne · ${DOW_L[di.iso]} ${+d.slice(8)}. ${MONTHS_G[+d.slice(5,7)-1]}<small>Potřeba personálu po čtvrthodinách podle pracovní doby každého sálu${di.school?' · '+esc(di.school.name)+' – prázdninová kapacita':''}</small></div><button class="icon-btn" data-act="stDayNav" data-v="1" aria-label="Další pracovní den">${ico('cr')}</button></div><span class="sp"></span><button class="btn sm" data-act="stTab" data-v="day">Tabulka po sálech</button></div>`;
  if(di.weekend||di.holiday)return h+`<p class="hint" style="margin:10px 0">${di.holiday?esc(di.holiday):'Víkend'} – bez plánované operativy.</p></section>`;
  for(const st of sites){const v=SD(st,d),x=sxHourly(st,d),pk=SR.map(([k,ck])=>{const m=Math.max(0,...x.A.map(a=>a[k]));return {m,c:v[ck],t:x.A.filter(a=>a[k]>v[ck]).length*SX_ST};});
    h+=`<div class="sx-site"><div class="sx-sh"><h3>${SITE[st].name}</h3><div class="sx-kp">${SR.map(([k,ck,lab,c,ab],i)=>`<span class="${pk[i].m>pk[i].c?'bad':pk[i].c&&pk[i].m/pk[i].c>=.9?'tight':''}" style="--c:${c}"><i></i>${ab} <b>${pk[i].m}</b>/${pk[i].c}${pk[i].t?` <em>chybí ${Math.round(pk[i].t/60*10)/10} h</em>`:''}</span>`).join('')}</div></div>
      <div class="sx-cw">${sxChart(x.A,v,d)}</div>
      <div class="m3-cap sx-cap2">Obsazení sálů · AL · AS · SS na sál</div><div class="ovx">${sxGantt(x.rows)}</div></div>`;}
  return h+`<div class="wt-lg" style="padding:14px 0 0;border:0"><span><i style="background:linear-gradient(var(--ink-3),transparent)"></i>potřeba v čase</span><span><i style="background:none;border-top:2px dashed var(--ink-2);height:0"></i>kapacita pracoviště</span><span><i style="background:var(--bad)"></i>nedostatek</span><span class="wt-lgs">Půlden sálu zabírá personál po celou dobu svého bloku podle normativu.</span></div></section>`;}
function sxWeeks(){const y=S.year;let d=addD(`${y}-01-04`,-(isoDow(`${y}-01-04`)-1));const out=[];while(+addD(d,3).slice(0,4)===y){out.push(d);d=addD(d,7);}return out;}
function sxWeeksHTML(){const sites=scopeSites(S.staffSite),wk=stInit().wk;let h='',lastM=-1,tot={def:0,tight:0,ok:0};
  for(const mon of sxWeeks()){const days=[0,1,2,3,4].map(i=>addD(mon,i)),work=days.filter(d=>{const di=DI(d);return !di.weekend&&!di.holiday;}),m=+addD(mon,3).slice(5,7)-1;
    if(m!==lastM){lastM=m;h+=`<tr class="sx-mo"><th colspan="7">${cap(MONTHS[m])}</th></tr>`;}
    let ops=0;work.forEach(d=>sites.forEach(st=>{const q=stOps(st,d);ops+=q.ca+q.la;}));let defD=0,worst=0;
    const cells=SR.map(([k,ck,lab,c])=>{let best=null;const bars=days.map(d=>{const di=DI(d);if(di.weekend||di.holiday)return '<i class="nx"></i>';let r=0,bad=false,n=0,cp=0;sites.forEach(st=>{const v=SD(st,d);n+=v[k];cp+=v[ck];const rr=v[ck]?v[k]/v[ck]:v[k]?9:0;if(rr>r)r=rr;if(v[k]>v[ck])bad=true;});if(r>worst)worst=r;if(!best||r>best.r)best={r,n,cp,d};return `<i class="${bad?'bad':r>=.9?'tight':''}" style="height:${Math.max(6,Math.min(1.25,r)/1.25*100).toFixed(0)}%" title="${DOW[isoDow(d)]} ${fmt(d)} · ${lab} ${n} / ${cp}"></i>`;}).join('');
      return `<td class="sx-wc"><div class="sx-sp" style="--c:${c}">${bars}</div><span>${best&&best.n?`${best.n}<small>/${best.cp}</small>`:'—'}</span></td>`;}).join('');
    work.forEach(d=>{if(sites.some(st=>SD(st,d).def))defD++;});const stt=defD?'def':worst>=.9?'tight':'ok';tot[stt]++;
    h+=`<tr class="sx-wr${mon===wk?' on':''}${mon<=today&&today<=addD(mon,6)?' cur':''}" data-act="stWkGo" data-d="${mon}" tabindex="0" role="button"><th><b>${isoWeek(mon)}</b><span>${fmt(mon)}–${fmt(days[4])}</span><em class="par">${PAR_N[parity(mon)]}</em></th><td class="r"><b>${work.length}</b><small> dnů</small></td><td class="r sx-ops"><b>${ops}</b><small> půlbloků</small></td>${cells}<td>${stt==='def'?`<span class="tag bad">chybí · ${defD} ${plural(defD,'den','dny','dnů')}</span>`:stt==='tight'?'<span class="tag sx-t">těsně</span>':work.length?'<span class="tag ok">pokryto</span>':'<span class="tag">bez provozu</span>'}</td></tr>`;}
  return `<div class="gg sx-sum" style="margin-bottom:16px">${[['ok','Týdny bez problému','var(--ok)','personál pokryje celý týden'],['tight','Těsné týdny','var(--warn)','vytížení nad 90 % alespoň v jednom dni'],['def','Týdny s nedostatkem','var(--bad)','v některém dni chybí personál']].map(([k,t,c,s])=>`<div class="sx-kpi" style="--c:${c}"><span>${t}</span><b>${tot[k]}</b><small>${s}</small><u><i style="width:${(tot[k]/Math.max(1,tot.ok+tot.tight+tot.def)*100).toFixed(1)}%"></i></u></div>`).join('')}</div>
  <section class="card"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Týdny roku ${S.year} · vytížení po dnech</div><span class="hint">Sloupec = den (po–pá), výška = potřeba / kapacita · klepnutím otevřete týden</span></div>
  <div class="ovx"><table class="tb sx-wt"><thead><tr><th>Týden</th><th class="r">Provoz</th><th class="r">Půlbloky</th>${SR.map(([,,lab,c])=>`<th><span class="sx-th" style="--c:${c}"><i></i>${lab}</span></th>`).join('')}<th>Stav</th></tr></thead><tbody>${h}</tbody></table></div></section>`;}
function sxHeatHTML(){const sites=scopeSites(S.staffSite),y=S.year,hp=S.st.hp||'all',ks=hp==='all'?SR:SR.filter(x=>x[0]===hp);let h='';
  h+=`<div class="sx-hm"><div></div>${Array.from({length:31},(_,i)=>`<div class="sx-hd">${i+1}</div>`).join('')}<div class="sx-hd r">nedostatek · Ø vytížení</div>`;
