  for(let m=0;m<12;m++){const n=new Date(Date.UTC(y,m+1,0)).getUTCDate();let bad=0,sum=0,cnt=0;h+=`<div class="sx-ml" title="${cap(MONTHS[m])}">${['Led','Úno','Bře','Dub','Kvě','Čvn','Čvc','Srp','Zář','Říj','Lis','Pro'][m]}</div>`;
    for(let i=1;i<=31;i++){if(i>n){h+='<span class="sx-c na"></span>';continue;}const d=`${y}-${pad(m+1)}-${pad(i)}`,di=DI(d);
      if(di.holiday){h+=`<span class="sx-c hol" title="${esc(di.holiday)}"></span>`;continue;}if(di.weekend){h+='<span class="sx-c we"></span>';continue;}
      let r=0,isBad=false,tip=[];sites.forEach(st=>{const v=SD(st,d);ks.forEach(([k,ck,lab,c,ab])=>{const rr=v[ck]?v[k]/v[ck]:v[k]?9:0;if(rr>r)r=rr;if(v[k]>v[ck])isBad=true;tip.push(`${sites.length>1?SITE[st].name+' ':''}${ab} ${v[k]}/${v[ck]}`);});});
      if(isBad)bad++;if(r){sum+=Math.min(r,1.5);cnt++;}
      h+=`<button class="sx-c ${!r?'z':isBad?'r4':r>=.9?'r3':r>=.7?'r2':'r1'}${di.school?' sch':''}${d===today?' td':''}" data-act="stDay" data-d="${d}" title="${DOW[di.iso]} ${fmtY(d)}${di.school?' · '+esc(di.school.short||di.school.name):''} · ${esc(tip.join(' · '))}"></button>`;}
    const av=cnt?sum/cnt:0;h+=`<div class="sx-ms"><span class="${bad?'bad':''}">${bad?`${bad}×`:'—'}</span><u><i class="${av>1?'bad':av>=.9?'tight':''}" style="width:${Math.min(100,av*100).toFixed(0)}%"></i></u><em>${cnt?Math.round(av*100)+' %':'—'}</em></div>`;}
  return `<section class="card" style="margin-bottom:16px"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Roční mapa vytížení personálu · ${y}</div><div class="seg">${[['all','Všechny profese'],...SR.map(([k,,lab,,ab])=>[k,ab])].map(([v,t])=>`<button data-act="stHp" data-v="${v}" class="${hp===v?'on':''}">${t}</button>`).join('')}</div></div>
    <div class="ovx">${h}</div></div>
    <div class="wt-lg" style="padding:14px 0 0;border:0"><span><i style="background:color-mix(in srgb,var(--ok) 18%,var(--raised))"></i>do 70 %</span><span><i style="background:color-mix(in srgb,var(--ok) 42%,var(--raised))"></i>70–90 %</span><span><i style="background:color-mix(in srgb,var(--warn) 50%,var(--raised))"></i>90–100 %</span><span><i style="background:var(--bad)"></i>nedostatek</span><span><i style="box-shadow:inset 0 -3px 0 var(--sch)"></i>školní prázdniny</span><span><i style="background:color-mix(in srgb,var(--hol) 22%,transparent)"></i>svátek</span><span class="wt-lgs">Barva ukazuje nejvytíženější profesi v den. Klepnutím otevřete rozpis dne.</span></div></section>`;}

function capCmpHTML(){const c=D.staff.cap,sum=f=>SITES.reduce((a,x)=>a+(+(c[x.id]||{})[f]||0),0),R3=[['doc','docH','Anesteziologové','#76AFAA'],['nurse','nurseH','Anest. sestry','#A496CB'],['scrub','scrubH','Sálové sestry','#CF95A6']],mx=Math.max(1,...R3.map(([a,b])=>Math.max(sum(a),sum(b))));
  return `<div class="cc">${R3.map(([a,b,lab,col])=>{const x=sum(a),z=sum(b);return `<div class="cc-r"><span>${lab}</span><div class="cc-b" title="běžná kapacita ${x}, prázdninová ${z}"><i style="width:${(x/mx*100).toFixed(1)}%;background:color-mix(in srgb,${col} 30%,var(--raised))"></i><i style="width:${(z/mx*100).toFixed(1)}%;background:${col}"></i></div><b>${x} → ${z}</b></div>`;}).join('')}<p class="hint" style="margin:2px 0 0">Kapacita celé KNL: běžný provoz → školní prázdniny</p></div>`;}

/* =========================================================
   Společné: zatížení dne
   ========================================================= */
const MONTHS_G=['ledna','února','března','dubna','května','června','července','srpna','září','října','listopadu','prosince'];
const scopeOf=site=>site==='all'||!SITE[site]?SITES.map(x=>x.id):[site];
const missTxt=m=>[m.doc&&`${m.doc} lék.`,m.nur&&`${m.nur} an. ses.`,m.scr&&`${m.scr} sál. ses.`].filter(Boolean).join(' · ');
function calDay(d,site){site=site||S.bc.site;const key=`cd|${d}|${site}`;if(KC.has(key))return KC.get(key);
  const di=DI(d),work=!di.weekend&&!di.holiday,sites=scopeOf(site);let cap=0,pl=0,free=0;const sp={},blk=new Set();
  for(const room of D.rooms){if(!room.active||!sites.includes(room.site))continue;const b=blockFor(room.id,d);if(b)blk.add(b);if(room.kind==='pacu')continue;
    const r=R(room,d),H=hoursFor(room,d);if(work&&(room.kind!=='ext'||r.h[0]||r.h[1]))cap+=(tmin(H.to)-tmin(H.from))/60;
    for(let i=0;i<2;i++){const h=r.h[i];if(h&&!h.released){const v=halfMin(H,i)/60;pl+=v;sp[h.s]=(sp[h.s]||0)+v;}}
    if(work&&room.kind!=='ext'){const fa=freeAt(room,d);free+=(fa[0]?1:0)+(fa[1]?1:0);}}
  const rq=D.requests.filter(q=>q.status!=='rejected'&&d>=q.from&&d<=q.to&&(q.site==='all'||sites.includes(q.site)));
  let miss=null;if(work){const m={doc:0,nur:0,scr:0};sites.forEach(st=>{const v=SD(st,d);m.doc+=Math.max(0,v.doc-v.capDoc);m.nur+=Math.max(0,v.nur-v.capNur);m.scr+=Math.max(0,v.scr-v.capScr);});if(m.doc||m.nur||m.scr)miss=m;}
  const v={d,di,work,cap,pl,u:cap?pl/cap:0,sp,free,blk:[...blk].sort((a,b)=>a.from.localeCompare(b.from)),rq,miss};KC.set(key,v);return v;}

/* =========================================================
   Přehled: týdenní rozpis sálů
   ========================================================= */
function wtWeek(){const a=S.wt.anchor&&+addD(S.wt.anchor,3).slice(0,4)===S.year?S.wt.anchor:focusDay();return addD(a,-(isoDow(a)-1));}
function wtCell(room,d){const r=R(room,d),di=r.di,H=hoursFor(room,d),blk=r.block&&!(r.src==='override'&&(r.h[0]||r.h[1]));let inner='';
  const one=(h,i,full)=>{const span=full?' f':'';if(!h)return `<span class="wt-b free${span}">volno</span>`;const sp=SPEC[h.s]||{c:'#999',name:h.s};
    if(h.released)return `<span class="wt-b rel${span}" style="--c:${sp.c}"><b>${esc(h.s)}</b><small>uvolněno · ${esc(h.req.type.toLowerCase())}</small></span>`;
    return `<span class="wt-b ${h.an}${span}" style="--c:${sp.c}"><b>${full?esc(sp.name):esc(h.s)}</b><small>${full?fH(H):halfT(H,i)} · ${h.an}</small></span>`;};
  if(blk)inner=`<span class="wt-b f blk" style="--c:${BT_C[r.block.type]||'#888'}"><b>${esc(r.block.type)}</b><small>${fmtR(r.block.from,r.block.to)}</small></span>`;
  else if(r.h[0]||r.h[1]){const a=r.h[0],b=r.h[1];inner=a&&b&&a.s===b.s&&a.an===b.an&&!!a.released===!!b.released?one(a,0,true):one(a,0)+one(b,1);}
  else if(!di.weekend&&!di.holiday)inner=one(null,0,true);
  const tip=[room.name,`${cap(DOW_L[di.iso])} ${fmtY(d)}`,...r.issues.map(x=>x.t),r.note].filter(Boolean).join(' · ');
  return `<div class="wt-c${d===today?' td':''}${di.holiday?' hol':''}${di.weekend?' we':''}" role="button" tabindex="0" data-act="openDay" data-r="${room.id}" data-d="${d}" title="${esc(tip)}">${inner}${r.issues.length?'<i class="wt-x"></i>':''}</div>`;}
function wtStaff(st,d){const di=DI(d);if(di.weekend||di.holiday)return '<div class="wt-sf"></div>';const v=SD(st,d),m=(lab,full,n,c)=>`<span class="${n>c?'bad':''}" title="${full}: potřeba ${n}, k dispozici ${c}"><b>${n}<small>/${c}</small></b>${lab}<i><u style="width:${c?Math.min(100,n/c*100):100}%"></u></i></span>`;
  return `<div class="wt-sf">${m('AL','Anesteziologové',v.doc,v.capDoc)}${m('AS','Anesteziologické sestry',v.nur,v.capNur)}${m('SS','Sálové sestry',v.scr,v.capScr)}</div>`;}
function wtHTML(mon,days){const o=S.wt,sites=scopeOf(o.site);
  let h=`<div class="wt" style="--nd:${days.length}"><div class="wt-hd corner"><b>Sál</b></div>`;
  days.forEach(d=>{const di=DI(d),c=calDay(d,o.site),up=c.cap?Math.round(c.u*100):0,segs=Object.entries(c.sp).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]);
    h+=`<div class="wt-hd${d===today?' td':''}${di.holiday?' hol':''}${di.weekend?' we':''}"><b>${DOW_L[di.iso]}<span>${+d.slice(8)}. ${+d.slice(5,7)}.</span></b>
      <div class="wt-tags">${d===today?'<span class="tag ok">Dnes</span>':''}${di.holiday?`<span class="tag hol">${esc(di.holiday)}</span>`:''}${di.school?`<span class="tag sch">${esc(di.school.short)}</span>`:''}</div>
      ${c.work?`<div class="wt-u" title="Obsazenost ${up} %">${segs.map(([sp,v])=>`<i style="width:${(v/c.cap*100).toFixed(1)}%;--c:${(SPEC[sp]||{}).c||'#999'}"></i>`).join('')}</div><div class="wt-hm"><span>${up} % · ${fmtN(c.pl)} h</span>${c.miss?`<span class="bad" title="Chybí: ${missTxt(c.miss)}">${ico('warn')}personál</span>`:'<span class="ok">personál ✓</span>'}</div>`:`<div class="wt-hm"><span>${di.holiday?'státní svátek':'víkend'} · bez plánu</span></div>`}
      <div class="wt-sub"><span>dop</span><span>odp</span></div></div>`;});
  for(const st of sites){const rooms=D.rooms.filter(r=>r.active&&r.site===st&&r.kind!=='pacu');if(!rooms.length)continue;
    h+=`<div class="wt-site"><b>${SITE[st].name}</b><small>${roomCounts(rooms)}</small></div>`;
    for(const g of siteGroups(st)){const rs=sortRooms(rooms.filter(r=>gOf(r)===g));if(!rs.length)continue;
      if(g)h+=`<div class="wt-grp"><span>${esc(gName(g))}</span></div>`;
      for(const room of rs)h+=`<div class="wt-lab"><i style="--c:${roomColor(room)}"></i><div><b>${esc(room.name)}</b><code>${esc(room.code)}</code></div><em>${room.kind==='ext'?'EXT':room.an==='CA+LA'?'CA·LA':room.an}</em></div>${days.map(d=>wtCell(room,d)).join('')}`;}
    h+=`<div class="wt-lab wt-sl"><i style="--c:var(--ink-3)"></i><div><b>Personál</b><small>potřeba / k dispozici</small></div></div>${days.map(d=>wtStaff(st,d)).join('')}`;}
  return h+'</div>';}
function wtCard(){const o=S.wt,mon=wtWeek(),nd=o.we?7:5,days=Array.from({length:nd},(_,i)=>addD(mon,i)),specs=new Set();
  days.forEach(d=>Object.keys(calDay(d,o.site).sp).forEach(x=>specs.add(x)));
  return `<section class="card wt-card"><div class="wt-top"><div class="wt-nav"><button class="icon-btn" data-act="wtNav" data-v="-7" aria-label="Předchozí týden">${ico('cl')}</button><div class="wt-ttl">Týdenní rozpis sálů<small>Týden ${isoWeek(mon)} · ${PAR_N[parity(mon)]} · ${fmtR(mon,days[days.length-1])}</small></div><button class="icon-btn" data-act="wtNav" data-v="7" aria-label="Další týden">${ico('cr')}</button></div>
    <button class="btn sm" data-act="wtToday">Tento týden</button><span class="sp"></span>
    <div class="seg">${[['all','Vše'],...SITES.map(x=>[x.id,x.name])].map(([v,t])=>`<button data-act="wtSite" data-v="${v}" class="${o.site===v?'on':''}">${t}</button>`).join('')}</div>
    <label class="cbx"><input type="checkbox" data-ch="wtWe"${o.we?' checked':''}><span>Víkend</span></label></div>
   <div class="ovx">${wtHTML(mon,days)}</div>
   <div class="wt-lg">${[...specs].sort((a,b)=>specName(a).localeCompare(specName(b),'cs')).map(x=>{const sp=SPEC[x]||{c:'#999',name:x};return `<span><i style="background:${sp.c}"></i>${esc(sp.name)}</span>`;}).join('')}<span class="wt-lgs"><i class="la"></i>lokální anestezie</span><span class="wt-lgs"><i class="rel"></i>uvolněno</span><span class="wt-lgs"><i class="blk"></i>omezení sálu</span><span class="wt-lgs"><i class="fr"></i>volno</span><span class="wt-lgs">AL anesteziologové · AS anest. sestry · SS sálové sestry</span></div></section>`;}

/* =========================================================
   Velký kalendář provozu
   ========================================================= */
const BC_L=[['occ','Obsazenost'],['blk','Omezení sálů'],['as','Asanace – návrh'],['req','Požadavky oborů'],['staff','Personál'],['free','Volné sály']];
function bcInit(){const o=S.bc;if(!o.m||+o.m.slice(0,4)!==S.year)o.m=(S.year===todayY?today:`${S.year}-01-01`).slice(0,7)+'-01';if(o.sel&&o.sel.slice(0,7)!==o.m.slice(0,7)&&+o.sel.slice(0,4)!==S.year)o.sel=null;return o;}
function bcAsDays(){if(!S.bc.layers.as)return new Set();try{if(!asTarget().length)return new Set();const x=new Set();asPlan().forEach(w=>{if(w.status==='open'&&w.best)w.best.days.forEach(d=>x.add(d));});return x;}catch(e){return new Set();}}
function bcLanes(days,sites){const a=days[0],z=days[6];
  const L=D.blocks.filter(b=>b.to>=a&&b.from<=z&&b.roomIds.some(id=>ROOM[id]&&sites.includes(ROOM[id].site))).sort((x,y)=>x.from.localeCompare(y.from)||diffD(y.from,y.to)-diffD(x.from,x.to));
  const lanes=[];L.forEach(b=>{let k=lanes.findIndex(ln=>ln.every(o=>o.to<b.from||o.from>b.to));if(k<0){lanes.push([]);k=lanes.length-1;}lanes[k].push(b);});return lanes;}
function bcMine(d,spec,sites){const out=[];for(const room of sortRooms(D.rooms.filter(r=>r.active&&r.kind!=='pacu'&&sites.includes(r.site)))){const r=R(room,d),hs=[0,1].filter(i=>r.h[i]&&!r.h[i].released&&r.h[i].s===spec);if(hs.length)out.push({code:room.code,part:hs.length===2?'celý den':hs[0]===0?'dop':'odp'});}return out;}
function bcCell(d,mon,lanes,days,asD){const o=S.bc,L=o.layers,c=calDay(d),di=c.di,out=d.slice(0,7)!==mon.slice(0,7),sites=scopeOf(o.site),up=c.cap?Math.round(c.u*100):null;
  const sch=di.school,schL=sch&&(DI(addD(d,-1)).school!==sch||d===days[0]);
  let h=`<div class="bc-d"><b>${+d.slice(8)}</b>${di.holiday?`<small>${esc(di.holiday)}</small>`:schL?`<small class="bc-scl">${esc(sch.name)}</small>`:''}</div>${sch?`<i class="bc-sch" title="${esc(sch.name)}"></i>`:''}`;
  if(L.blk&&lanes.length){h+=lanes.slice(0,3).map(ln=>{const b=ln.find(x=>d>=x.from&&d<=x.to);if(!b)return '<div class="bc-gap"></div>';const st=d===b.from,en=d===b.to,codes=b.roomIds.map(id=>ROOM[id]&&sites.includes(ROOM[id].site)?ROOM[id].code:null).filter(Boolean);
      return `<div class="bc-bar${st?' s':''}${en?' e':''}" style="--c:${BT_C[b.type]||'#888'}" title="${esc(b.type)} · ${esc(codes.join(', '))} · ${fmtR(b.from,b.to)}">${st||d===days[0]?`<b>${BT_A[b.type]||'?'}</b><span>${esc(codes.join(' '))}</span>`:''}</div>`;}).join('');
    const hid=lanes.slice(3).filter(ln=>ln.some(x=>d>=x.from&&d<=x.to)).length;if(hid)h+=`<span class="bc-more">+${hid} ${plural(hid,'omezení','omezení','omezení')}</span>`;}
  if(L.as&&asD.has(d))h+=`<div class="bc-as" title="Doporučený asanační den · ${esc(asLabel(asTarget()))}">${ico('spark')}<span>Asanace</span></div>`;
  if(o.spec&&c.work){const mine=bcMine(d,o.spec,sites),sc=(SPEC[o.spec]||{}).c||'#999';h+=mine.slice(0,3).map(x=>`<div class="bc-mine" style="--c:${sc}"><b>${esc(x.code)}</b>${x.part}</div>`).join('')+(mine.length>3?`<span class="bc-more">+${mine.length-3} ${plural(mine.length-3,'sál','sály','sálů')}</span>`:'');}
  if(L.req&&c.rq.length){h+=c.rq.slice(0,2).map(q=>{const sp=SPEC[q.specId]||{c:'#999'};return `<div class="bc-rq${q.status==='pending'?' pend':''}" style="--c:${sp.c}" title="${esc(specName(q.specId))} · ${esc(q.type)} · ${RQ_ST[q.status||'ok']}"><b>${esc(q.specId)}</b>${esc(q.type.toLowerCase())}</div>`;}).join('');if(c.rq.length>2)h+=`<span class="bc-more">+${c.rq.length-2} ${plural(c.rq.length-2,'požadavek','požadavky','požadavků')}</span>`;}
  let bt='';
  if(L.occ&&c.work&&c.cap){const segs=Object.entries(c.sp).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]);bt+=`<div class="bc-ow"><em class="${up>=90?'hi':up<60?'lo':''}" title="Obsazenost sálů">${up} %</em><div class="bc-occ" title="${segs.map(([x,v])=>`${specName(x)} ${fmtN(v,1)} h`).join(' · ')}">${segs.map(([x,v])=>`<i style="width:${(v/c.cap*100).toFixed(1)}%;--c:${o.spec&&o.spec!==x?'var(--line-2)':(SPEC[x]||{}).c||'#999'}"></i>`).join('')}</div></div>`;}
  const ft=[L.staff&&c.miss?`<span class="bad" title="Chybí: ${missTxt(c.miss)}">${ico('warn')}${missTxt(c.miss)}</span>`:'',L.free&&c.free?`<span title="Volné půlbloky">${ico('free')}${c.free} ${plural(c.free,'volný','volné','volných')}</span>`:''].filter(Boolean).join('');
  if(ft)bt+=`<div class="bc-ft">${ft}</div>`;if(bt)h+=`<div class="bc-bt">${bt}</div>`;
  const dim=o.spec&&c.work&&!(c.sp[o.spec]>0);
  return `<div class="bc-c${out?' out':''}${di.weekend?' we':''}${di.holiday?' hol':''}${d===today?' td':''}${o.sel===d?' sel':''}${dim?' dim':''}" role="button" tabindex="0" data-act="bcSel" data-d="${d}" aria-label="${cap(DOW_L[di.iso])} ${fmtY(d)}${di.holiday?', '+esc(di.holiday):''}">${h}</div>`;}
function bcMonthHTML(){const o=S.bc,m=o.m,e=mEnd(m),start=addD(m,-(isoDow(m)-1)),asD=bcAsDays(),sites=scopeOf(o.site);
  let h=`<div class="bc-grid"><div class="bc-h"></div>${['Po','Út','St','Čt','Pá','So','Ne'].map((x,i)=>`<div class="bc-h${i>4?' we':''}">${x}</div>`).join('')}`;
  for(let w=0;w<6;w++){const days=Array.from({length:7},(_,i)=>addD(start,w*7+i));if(days[0]>e)break;const lanes=o.layers.blk?bcLanes(days,sites):[];
    h+=`<div class="bc-wk" title="Týden ${isoWeek(days[0])} · ${PAR_N[parity(days[0])]}"><b>${isoWeek(days[0])}</b><i>${parity(days[0]).toUpperCase()}</i></div>${days.map(d=>bcCell(d,m,lanes,days,asD)).join('')}`;}
  return h+'</div>';}
function bcYearHTML(){const y=S.year,asD=bcAsDays();let h='<div class="by">';
  for(let mo=0;mo<12;mo++){const m=`${y}-${pad(mo+1)}-01`,e=mEnd(m);let g=['Po','Út','St','Čt','Pá','So','Ne'].map(x=>`<span>${x}</span>`).join('')+'<i></i>'.repeat(isoDow(m)-1),pl=0,cp=0;
    for(let d=m;d<=e;d=addD(d,1)){const c=calDay(d),di=c.di,b=c.blk[0];pl+=c.pl;cp+=c.cap;const bg=c.work&&c.cap?`color-mix(in srgb,var(--dot) ${Math.round(12+Math.min(1,c.u)*76)}%,var(--sunk))`:'';
      g+=`<button class="by-d${di.weekend?' we':''}${di.holiday?' hol':''}${b?' blk':''}${di.school?' sch':''}${d===today?' td':''}${asD.has(d)?' asr':''}${c.miss?' miss':''}" style="${bg?`--bg-d:${bg};${c.u>.62?'color:#06122A;':''}`:''}${b?`--bc:${BT_C[b.type]||'#888'};`:''}" data-act="bcPick" data-d="${d}" title="${esc(`${cap(DOW_L[di.iso])} ${fmtY(d)} · ${di.holiday||(c.work?`obsazenost ${Math.round(c.u*100)} %`:'víkend')}${di.school?' · '+di.school.name:''}${b?' · '+b.type:''}${asD.has(d)?' · doporučená asanace':''}${c.miss?' · chybí personál':''}`)}">${+d.slice(8)}</button>`;}
    h+=`<div class="by-m"><button class="by-mh" data-act="bcMonth" data-v="${m}"><b>${MONTHS[mo]}</b><span>${cp?Math.round(pl/cp*100):0} % obsazenost</span></button><div class="by-g">${g}</div></div>`;}
  return h+'</div>';}
function bcLegend(isY){if(isY)return `<div class="bc-lg"><span>Obsazenost</span><span class="sc">${[10,40,70,100].map(p=>`<i style="background:color-mix(in srgb,var(--dot) ${Math.round(12+p*.76)}%,var(--sunk))"></i>`).join('')}</span><span>nízká → plná</span><span><i class="hol"></i>státní svátek</span><span><i class="sch"></i>prázdniny</span><span><i style="--c:#C46A66"></i>omezení sálu</span><span><i class="as"></i>doporučená asanace</span><span><i style="--c:var(--bad);width:8px;height:8px;border-radius:50%"></i>chybí personál</span></div>`;
  return `<div class="bc-lg">${BLOCK_TYPES.map(t=>`<span><i style="--c:${BT_C[t]}"></i>${esc(t)}</span>`).join('')}<span><i class="as"></i>asanace – návrh</span><span><i class="sch"></i>školní prázdniny</span><span><i class="rq"></i>požadavek čeká na schválení</span></div>`;}
function bcRoomRow(room,d){const r=R(room,d),blk=r.block&&!(r.src==='override'&&(r.h[0]||r.h[1]));let hs;
  if(blk)hs=`<span class="bc-h2 blk" style="--c:${BT_C[r.block.type]||'#888'}">${esc(r.block.type)}</span>`;
  else{const one=(x,lab)=>{if(!x)return `<span class="bc-h2 free">${lab} · volno</span>`;const sp=SPEC[x.s]||{c:'#999'};if(x.released)return `<span class="bc-h2 rel" title="${esc(x.req.type)}">${esc(x.s)} · uvolněno</span>`;return `<span class="bc-h2 ${x.an}" style="--c:${sp.c}"><b>${esc(x.s)}</b>${lab}<em>${x.an}</em></span>`;};
    const a=r.h[0],b=r.h[1];hs=a&&b&&a.s===b.s&&a.an===b.an&&!a.released&&!b.released?one(a,'celý den'):one(a,'dop')+one(b,'odp');}
  return `<div class="bc-room" role="button" tabindex="0" data-act="openDay" data-r="${room.id}" data-d="${d}" title="${esc(room.name)}${r.note?' · '+esc(r.note):''}"><code>${esc(room.code)}</code><div class="bc-hs">${hs}</div></div>`;}
function bcDayHTML(d){const o=S.bc,c=calDay(d),di=c.di,sites=scopeOf(o.site),up=c.cap?Math.round(c.u*100):0;
  let h=`<section class="card"><div class="card-h"><div><div class="eyebrow"><i></i>${cap(DOW_L[di.iso])} · týden ${isoWeek(d)}, ${PAR_N[parity(d)]}</div><h3 class="bc-st-h">${+d.slice(8)}. ${MONTHS_G[+d.slice(5,7)-1]} ${d.slice(0,4)}</h3></div><button class="icon-btn sm" data-act="bcSel" data-d="" aria-label="Zavřít detail dne">${ico('x')}</button></div>
    <div class="tags">${di.holiday?`<span class="tag hol">${esc(di.holiday)}</span>`:''}${di.school?`<span class="tag sch">${esc(di.school.name)}</span>`:''}${seasonFor(d)?`<span class="tag">${esc(seasonFor(d).name)}</span>`:''}${di.weekend?'<span class="tag">Víkend</span>':''}${c.blk.map(b=>`<span class="tag" style="background:color-mix(in srgb,${BT_C[b.type]||'#888'} 22%,var(--raised))">${esc(b.type)}</span>`).join('')}</div>
    ${c.work?`<div class="bc-st"><div><span>Plánováno</span><b>${fmtN(c.pl,1)} h</b></div><div><span>Obsazenost</span><b>${up} %</b></div><div><span>Volné půlbloky</span><b>${c.free}</b></div></div>`:''}`;
  if(c.work)h+=sites.map(st=>{const v=SD(st,d),cell=(lab,n,cp)=>`<div class="${n>cp?'bad':''}"><span>${lab}</span><b>${n}<small> / ${cp}</small></b></div>`;return `<div class="bc-site">Personál · ${SITE[st].name}</div><div class="bc-st" style="margin-top:0">${cell('Anest. lékaři',v.doc,v.capDoc)}${cell('Anest. sestry',v.nur,v.capNur)}${cell('Sálové sestry',v.scr,v.capScr)}</div>`;}).join('');
  h+=sites.map(st=>{const rooms=siteGroups(st).flatMap(g=>sortRooms(D.rooms.filter(r=>r.active&&r.site===st&&r.kind!=='pacu'&&gOf(r)===g)));return rooms.length?`<div class="bc-site">Sály · ${SITE[st].name}</div>${rooms.map(room=>bcRoomRow(room,d)).join('')}`:'';}).join('');
  if(c.rq.length)h+=`<div class="bc-site">Požadavky oborů</div><div style="display:grid;gap:4px">${c.rq.map(q=>`<div class="bc-rq${q.status==='pending'?' pend':''}" style="--c:${(SPEC[q.specId]||{}).c||'#999'}"><b>${esc(q.specId)}</b>${esc(q.type)} · ${PARTS[q.part].toLowerCase()}${q.status==='pending'?' · čeká na schválení':''}</div>`).join('')}</div>`;
  h+=`<div class="bc-acts"><button class="btn sm" data-act="jump" data-d="${d}" data-z="week">${ico('tl')}Na časové ose</button>${isCoord()&&c.work?`<button class="btn sm" data-act="bcBlock" data-d="${d}">${ico('ban')}Omezit sál</button>`:''}</div></section>`;
  return h;}
function bcMonthSumHTML(){const o=S.bc,m=o.m,e=mEnd(m),sites=scopeOf(o.site);let cp=0,pl=0,wd=0,miss=0,free=0;const sp={},hol=[],sch=new Map();
  for(let d=m;d<=e;d=addD(d,1)){const c=calDay(d);if(c.work)wd++;cp+=c.cap;pl+=c.pl;free+=c.free;if(c.miss)miss++;for(const k in c.sp)sp[k]=(sp[k]||0)+c.sp[k];if(c.di.holiday)hol.push([d,c.di.holiday]);if(c.di.school&&!sch.has(c.di.school))sch.set(c.di.school,d);}
  const bl=D.blocks.filter(b=>b.to>=m&&b.from<=e&&b.roomIds.some(id=>ROOM[id]&&sites.includes(ROOM[id].site))).sort((a,b)=>a.from.localeCompare(b.from));
  const top=Object.entries(sp).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]).slice(0,7),mx=top.length?top[0][1]:1;
  return `<section class="card"><div class="eyebrow"><i></i>Souhrn měsíce</div><h3 class="bc-st-h">${cap(MONTHS[+m.slice(5,7)-1])} ${m.slice(0,4)}</h3>
    <div class="bc-st"><div><span>Plánováno</span><b>${fmtN(pl)} h</b></div><div><span>Obsazenost</span><b>${cp?Math.round(pl/cp*100):0} %</b></div><div><span>Pracovní dny</span><b>${wd}</b></div><div><span>Volné půlbloky</span><b>${free}</b></div><div class="${miss?'bad':''}"><span>Dny bez personálu</span><b>${miss}</b></div><div><span>Omezení</span><b>${bl.length}</b></div></div>
    <p class="hint" style="margin:12px 0 0">Klepnutím na den zobrazíte jeho rozpis po sálech a personál.</p></section>
  ${top.length?`<section class="card"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Obory v měsíci</div></div><div class="bc-tops">${top.map(([x,v])=>`<div class="bc-top-r"><span><i style="background:${(SPEC[x]||{}).c}"></i>${esc(specName(x))}</span><b>${fmtN(v)} h</b><u><em style="width:${(v/mx*100).toFixed(1)}%;background:${(SPEC[x]||{}).c}"></em></u></div>`).join('')}</div></section>`:''}
  ${bl.length||hol.length||sch.size?`<section class="card"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Události měsíce</div></div><div class="bc-ev">${hol.map(([d,t])=>`<div><span class="bc-dot" style="--c:var(--hol)"></span><span><b>${+d.slice(8)}. ${+d.slice(5,7)}.</b> ${esc(t)}</span></div>`).join('')}${[...sch.keys()].map(x=>`<div><span class="bc-dot" style="--c:var(--sch)"></span><span><b>${fmtR(x.from,x.to)}</b> ${esc(x.name)}</span></div>`).join('')}${bl.map(b=>`<div><span class="bc-dot sq" style="--c:${BT_C[b.type]||'#888'}"></span><span><b>${fmtR(b.from,b.to)}</b> ${esc(b.type)} · ${esc(b.roomIds.map(id=>(ROOM[id]||{}).code||id).join(', '))}</span></div>`).join('')}</div></section>`:''}`;}
function vKal(){const o=bcInit(),L=o.layers,isY=o.mode==='year',mi=+o.m.slice(5,7)-1;let wd=0,hc=0;for(let d=o.m;d<=mEnd(o.m);d=addD(d,1)){const di=DI(d);if(!di.weekend&&!di.holiday)wd++;else if(di.holiday&&!di.weekend)hc++;}
  const top=`<div class="bc-top">${isY?`<div class="bc-mon">${S.year}<small>celý rok · klepnutím na den otevřete měsíc</small></div>`:`<div class="bc-navw"><button class="icon-btn" data-act="bcNav" data-v="-1" aria-label="Předchozí měsíc">${ico('cl')}</button><div class="bc-mon">${MONTHS[mi]} ${o.m.slice(0,4)}<small>${wd} ${plural(wd,'pracovní den','pracovní dny','pracovních dnů')}${hc?` · ${hc} ${plural(hc,'svátek','svátky','svátků')} v týdnu`:''}</small></div><button class="icon-btn" data-act="bcNav" data-v="1" aria-label="Další měsíc">${ico('cr')}</button></div>`}
    <button class="btn sm" data-act="bcToday">Dnes</button>
    <div class="seg">${[['month','Měsíc'],['year','Rok']].map(([v,t])=>`<button data-act="bcMode" data-v="${v}" class="${o.mode===v?'on':''}">${t}</button>`).join('')}</div>
    <span class="sp"></span>
    <div class="seg">${[['all','Vše'],...SITES.map(x=>[x.id,x.name])].map(([v,t])=>`<button data-act="bcSite" data-v="${v}" class="${o.site===v?'on':''}">${t}</button>`).join('')}</div>
    <select class="in" data-ch="bcSpec" aria-label="Zvýraznit obor" style="width:auto"><option value="">Zvýraznit obor…</option>${opt(D.specs.map(x=>[x.id,x.name]),o.spec||'')}</select></div>
    ${isY?'':`<div class="bc-layers">${BC_L.map(([k,t])=>`<button class="chip sm${L[k]?' on':''}" data-act="bcLayer" data-v="${k}" aria-pressed="${!!L[k]}">${t}</button>`).join('')}</div>`}`;
  return ph('Provoz','Kalendář provozu')+`<div class="bc-wrap${isY?' y':''}"><section class="card bc-card">${top}${isY?bcYearHTML():bcMonthHTML()}${bcLegend(isY)}</section>${isY?'':`<aside class="bc-side">${o.sel?bcDayHTML(o.sel):bcMonthSumHTML()}</aside>`}</div>`;}

/* =========================================================
   Jarní prázdniny v celé ČR
   ========================================================= */
function springHTML(y,rg){const W=springWeeks(y);if(!W.length)return '';const wi={};W.forEach(w=>wi[w.g]=w.i);const my=OKRES_G[rg.okres];
  const tiles=KRAJE.map(([name,,oks])=>{const [c,r]=KRAJ_POS[name]||[1,1],cnt={};oks.forEach(o=>{const g=OKRES_G[o];cnt[g]=(cnt[g]||0)+1;});const dom=Object.entries(cnt).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1])[0][0];
    return `<button class="rg-t${name===rg.kraj?' on':''}" style="grid-column:${c};grid-row:${r};--c:${SPW_C[wi[dom]]}" data-act="rgPick" data-v="${esc(name)}" title="${esc(name)} – ${oks.map(o=>`${o}: ${fmtR(...(SPRING[y][OKRES_G[o]]))}`).join('; ')}"><b>${esc(KRAJ_SHY[krajShort(name)]||krajShort(name))}</b><span>${oks.map(o=>`<i style="background:${SPW_C[wi[OKRES_G[o]]]}"></i>`).join('')}</span></button>`;}).join('');
  return `<section class="card" style="margin-top:16px"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Jarní prázdniny v celé ČR · ${y}</div><span class="hint">Termín se řídí okresem školy · zdroj MŠMT · klepnutím na kraj ho vyberete</span></div>
    <div class="rg-wrap"><div class="rg-map">${tiles}</div><div class="rg-wk">${W.map(w=>`<div class="rg-w${w.g===my?' on':''}" style="--c:${SPW_C[w.i]}"><div class="rg-wh"><i>${w.i+1}</i><b>${fmtR(w.a,w.b)}</b>${w.g===my?`<span class="tag ok">okres ${esc(rg.okres)}</span>`:''}</div><div class="rg-ok">${KRAJE.map(([name,,oks])=>{const l=oks.filter(o=>OKRES_G[o]===w.g);return l.length?`<span><em>${esc(krajShort(name))}:</em> ${l.map(o=>o===rg.okres?`<b>${esc(o)}</b>`:esc(o)).join(', ')}</span>`:'';}).join('')}</div></div>`).join('')}</div></div></section>`;}

/* =========================================================
   Stahování souborů a tisk mimo náhled
   ========================================================= */
let DL=null;
function blobSave(name,data){try{if(window.top!==window)return false;const t=/\.json$/.test(name)?'application/json':/\.csv$/.test(name)?'text/csv':/\.html$/.test(name)?'text/html':'text/plain',u=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([data],{type:t+';charset=utf-8'})),a=document.createElement('a');a.href=u;a.download=name;document.body.appendChild(a);a.click();a.remove();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(u),4000);toast(`Soubor ${name} se stahuje`);return true;}catch(e){return false;}}
async function saveFile(name,data,fallback){if(!DL){if(blobSave(name,data))return;if(fallback)fallback();return;}
  try{await DL.save({filename:name,data});toast(`Soubor ${name} je připravený`);}
  catch(e){const c=e&&e.code;if(c==='declined')return;if(c==='rate_limited'){toast('Okno pro uložení je už otevřené');return;}if(fallback)fallback();}}
function viewPrintHTML(){const sc=$('#tlscroll'),left=sc?sc.scrollLeft:null;let h='';PRINTING=true;try{if(S.view==='timeline')drawTimeline();if(S.view==='map')m3PrintImg(true);h=$('#view').innerHTML;}finally{PRINTING=false;if(S.view==='timeline')drawTimeline(left);if(S.view==='map')m3PrintImg(false);}return h;}
function printDocHTML(title,inner){const css=[...document.querySelectorAll('style')].map(x=>x.textContent).join('\n');
  return `<!doctype html><html lang="cs" data-ui="light"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"><title>${esc(title)}</title><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Geist:wght@300;400;500;600&display=swap"><style>${css}</style><style>
body{background:#fff!important;background-image:none!important;padding:24px}.app{display:block}.panel{border:0!important;box-shadow:none!important;background:#fff!important;backdrop-filter:none!important;-webkit-backdrop-filter:none!important;padding:0!important}
.print-h{display:flex!important;justify-content:space-between;align-items:baseline;gap:16px;padding-bottom:8px;margin-bottom:10px;border-bottom:1.5px solid #14212A;font-size:11px;color:#14212A}
.btn,.icon-btn,.linkbtn,.filters,.ph-r,.newspec,#rq-form,#bl-form,#bp-card,.collapse,.pop,.cpop{display:none!important}
.m3-print{display:block!important;width:100%;border-radius:12px}.m3-wrap,.m3-tools,.m3-help,.m3-load{display:none!important}.m3-host{height:auto!important;min-height:0!important}
.prt-bar{position:sticky;top:0;z-index:5;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;gap:12px;margin:-24px -24px 20px;padding:12px 24px;background:#14212A;color:#fff;font:500 13px Geist,system-ui,sans-serif}
.prt-bar button{all:unset;cursor:pointer;background:#fff;color:#14212A;border-radius:999px;padding:7px 18px;font-weight:500}
@media print{.prt-bar{display:none!important}body{padding:0}}
</style></head><body><div class="prt-bar"><span>${esc(title)}</span><button onclick="print()">Tisknout</button></div><div class="app"><main class="main"><section class="panel" id="view">${inner}</section></main></div><script>addEventListener('load',function(){setTimeout(function(){print()},600)})<\/script></body></html>`;}
async function printOut(title,inner,fname){
  if(window.self===window.top){if(inner==null){window.print();return;}const h=$('#prt');h.innerHTML=inner;document.body.classList.add('prt-on');const done=()=>{document.body.classList.remove('prt-on');h.innerHTML='';removeEventListener('afterprint',done);};addEventListener('afterprint',done);window.print();return;}
  if(!DL){S.printInfo=true;renderModal();return;}
  const doc=printDocHTML(title,inner==null?viewPrintHTML():inner);
  try{await DL.save({filename:fname,data:doc});toast('Soubor je připravený – otevřete ho v prohlížeči, tisk se spustí sám');}
  catch(e){const c=e&&e.code;if(c==='declined')return;if(c==='rate_limited'){toast('Okno pro uložení je už otevřené');return;}S.printInfo=true;renderModal();}}

/* =========================================================
   Náhradní sál za omezení
   ========================================================= */
function relocPlan(b){const out=[],taken=new Set(),y0=`${S.year}-01-01`,y1=`${S.year}-12-31`,tm=addD(today,1);let from=b.from>y0?b.from:y0;if(from<tm)from=tm;const to=b.to<y1?b.to:y1;
  for(let d=from;d<=to;d=addD(d,1)){const di=DI(d);if(di.weekend||di.holiday)continue;
    for(const id of b.roomIds){const room=ROOM[id];if(!room||!room.active||room.kind==='pacu')continue;const r=R(room,d);if(!r.block||!r.lost||!r.slot)continue;
      const sl=r.slot,hs=[sl.a||null,sl.split?(sl.b||null):(sl.a||null)];let prevTo=null;
      for(let i=0;i<2;i++){const h=hs[i];if(!h||!h.s||reqFor(h.s,d,i,room.site,room.id))continue;let best=null,bs=-1;
        for(const c of D.rooms){if(!c.active||c.kind==='pacu'||c.site!==room.site||c.id===room.id||b.roomIds.includes(c.id)||(c.kind==='ext'&&room.kind!=='ext')||!anOk(c,h.an))continue;
          const fa=freeAt(c,d);if(!fa[i]||taken.has(c.id+'|'+d+'|'+i))continue;
          let sc=0;if(gOf(c)&&gOf(c)===gOf(room))sc+=4;if(prevTo===c)sc+=3;if(mainSpec(c)===h.s)sc+=2;if(fa[i].src!=='rel')sc+=1;if(sc>bs){bs=sc;best=c;}}
        if(best){taken.add(best.id+'|'+d+'|'+i);prevTo=best;}
        out.push({d,room,i,s:h.s,an:h.an,to:best,why:best?(gOf(best)&&gOf(best)===gOf(room)?'stejná skupina':'stejné pracoviště'):''});}}}
  return out;}
function relocHTML(){const b=D.blocks.find(x=>x.id===(S.reloc||{}).id);if(!b){S.reloc=null;return '';}const L=relocPlan(b),ok=L.filter(a=>a.to).length,rows=[];
  L.forEach(a=>{const p=rows[rows.length-1];if(p&&p.d===a.d&&p.room===a.room&&p.s===a.s&&p.an===a.an&&p.to===a.to&&p.i===0&&a.i===1)p.i='full';else rows.push({...a});});
  const by=new Map();rows.forEach(a=>{if(!by.has(a.d))by.set(a.d,[]);by.get(a.d).push(a);});
  return `<div class="mo-scrim" data-act="relocClose"></div><div class="mo" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="rl-t" style="width:min(760px,100%)">
    <div class="mo-h"><div><div class="eyebrow"><i></i>Náhradní sál · ${esc(b.type)}</div><h2 id="rl-t">${b.roomIds.map(id=>ROOM[id]?esc(ROOM[id].code):esc(id)).join(', ')} · ${fmtR(b.from,b.to)}</h2></div><button class="icon-btn" data-act="relocClose" aria-label="Zavřít">${ico('x')}</button></div>
    <p class="hint">Pro každý budoucí půlblok zrušený omezením hledáme ve stejný den volný sál na stejném pracovišti – přednostně ve stejné skupině, s vybavením pro potřebnou anestezii a tam, kde obor běžně operuje. Uvolněné bloky z požadavků oborů se nepřesouvají.</p>
    ${rows.length?[...by].map(([d,as])=>`<div class="fx-d"><div class="fx-h"><b>${cap(DOW_L[isoDow(d)])} ${fmtY(d)}</b></div><div class="fx-l">${as.map(a=>{const sp=SPEC[a.s]||{c:'#999',name:a.s};return `<div class="fx-a"><span class="hp ${a.an}" style="--c:${sp.c}">${esc(a.s)}</span><span><b>${HALF_N[a.i]} · ${esc(a.room.name)}</b> <code>${esc(a.room.code)}</code><small>${a.to?`→ ${esc(a.to.name)} (${esc(a.to.code)}) · ${a.why}`:'bez náhrady – ve stejný den není na pracovišti volný vhodný sál'}</small></span><em class="${a.to?'mv':'no'}">${a.to?'přesunout':'—'}</em></div>`;}).join('')}</div></div>`).join(''):empty('Omezení neruší žádné budoucí bloky, které by šlo přesunout.')}
    <div class="mo-f"><span class="hint">${L.length?`Náhrada nalezena pro ${ok} z ${L.length} ${plural(L.length,'půlbloku','půlbloků','půlbloků')}. Změnu lze vrátit tlačítkem Zpět.`:''}</span><div class="btns"><button class="btn ghost" data-act="relocClose">Zavřít</button>${ok?`<button class="btn pri" data-act="relocApply">${ico('check')}Přesunout ${ok} ${plural(ok,'půlblok','půlbloky','půlbloků')}</button>`:''}</div></div></div>`;}

/* =========================================================
   Tisk omezení sálů a asanací za období
   ========================================================= */
const BT_A={'Asanace':'A','Servis / údržba':'S','Validace / revize':'V','Rekonstrukce':'R','Útlum provozu':'U','Jiné':'J'};
const BT_C={'Asanace':'#4E9A8C','Servis / údržba':'#C98A4E','Validace / revize':'#6F8DB8','Rekonstrukce':'#C46A66','Útlum provozu':'#8C989E','Jiné':'#9C83B8'};
const mEnd=ds=>{const y=+ds.slice(0,4),m=+ds.slice(5,7);return `${y}-${pad(m)}-${pad(new Date(Date.UTC(y,m,0)).getUTCDate())}`;};
const mAdd=(ds,k)=>{let y=+ds.slice(0,4),m=+ds.slice(5,7)-1+k;y+=Math.floor(m/12);m=((m%12)+12)%12;return `${y}-${pad(m+1)}-01`;};
function bpPresetRange(v){const an=S.year===todayY?today:`${S.year}-01-01`,m0=an.slice(0,7)+'-01';
  if(v==='m1'){const m=mAdd(m0,1);return [m,mEnd(m)];}
  if(v==='q'){const q=Math.floor((+an.slice(5,7)-1)/3)*3,a=`${an.slice(0,4)}-${pad(q+1)}-01`;return [a,mEnd(mAdd(a,2))];}
  if(v==='y')return [`${S.year}-01-01`,`${S.year}-12-31`];
  return [m0,mEnd(m0)];}
function bpRange(){const o=S.bp;if(!o.from||!o.to){const [a,b]=bpPresetRange('m');return [o.from||a,o.to&&o.to>=(o.from||a)?o.to:b];}return [o.from,o.to];}
function bpData(){const o=S.bp,[from,to]=bpRange(),types=o.types.length?new Set(o.types):null,siteOk=r=>r&&(o.site==='all'||r.site===o.site);
  const L=D.blocks.filter(b=>b.to>=from&&b.from<=to&&(!types||types.has(b.type))&&b.roomIds.some(id=>siteOk(ROOM[id]))).sort((a,b)=>a.from.localeCompare(b.from)||a.type.localeCompare(b.type));
  const items=L.map(b=>{const f=b.from>from?b.from:from,t=b.to<to?b.to:to,rooms=sortRooms(b.roomIds.map(id=>ROOM[id]).filter(siteOk));let wd=0,lost=0;const sp={};
    for(let d=f;d<=t;d=addD(d,1)){const di=DI(d);if(!di.weekend&&!di.holiday)wd++;
      for(const room of rooms){if(room.kind==='pacu')continue;const r=R(room,d);if(r.block!==b||!r.lost||!r.slot)continue;lost+=r.lost;const hs=[r.slot.a,r.slot.split?r.slot.b:r.slot.a],H=hoursFor(room,d);hs.forEach((h,i)=>{if(h&&h.s)sp[h.s]=(sp[h.s]||0)+halfMin(H,i)/60;});}}
    return {b,f,t,rooms,wd,lost,sp,clipped:f!==b.from||t!==b.to};});
  return {from,to,items};}
function bpMatrixHTML(M){const {from,to,items}=M,cell=new Map(),rset=new Set();
  items.forEach(x=>x.rooms.forEach(r=>{for(let d=x.f;d<=x.t;d=addD(d,1)){const k=r.id+'|'+d;if(!cell.has(k))cell.set(k,x.b.type);rset.add(r.id);}}));
  const rooms=SITES.flatMap(st=>siteGroups(st.id).flatMap(g=>sortRooms(D.rooms.filter(r=>r.site===st.id&&gOf(r)===g&&rset.has(r.id)))));
  let out='<h2 class="bpr-h">Kalendář omezení</h2>';
  for(let m=from.slice(0,7)+'-01';m<=to;m=mAdd(m,1)){const a=m<from?from:m,e=mEnd(m)>to?to:mEnd(m),days=[];for(let d=a;d<=e;d=addD(d,1))days.push(d);
    const rs=rooms.filter(r=>days.some(d=>cell.has(r.id+'|'+d)));if(!rs.length)continue;
    out+=`<div class="bpr-m"><div class="bpr-mh">${cap(MONTHS[+m.slice(5,7)-1])} ${m.slice(0,4)}</div><table class="bpr-cal"><thead><tr><th></th>${days.map(d=>{const di=DI(d);return `<th class="${di.weekend||di.holiday?'we':''}">${+d.slice(8)}<small>${DOW[di.iso]}</small></th>`;}).join('')}</tr></thead><tbody>${rs.map(r=>`<tr><th><code>${esc(r.code)}</code> ${esc(r.name)}</th>${days.map(d=>{const t=cell.get(r.id+'|'+d),di=DI(d);return `<td class="${di.weekend||di.holiday?'we':''}">${t?`<span style="--c:${BT_C[t]||'#999'}" title="${esc(t)}">${BT_A[t]||'?'}</span>`:''}</td>`;}).join('')}</tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table></div>`;}
  return out+`<div class="bpr-lg">${BLOCK_TYPES.map(t=>`<span><i style="--c:${BT_C[t]}">${BT_A[t]}</i>${esc(t)}</span>`).join('')}<span><i class="we"></i>víkend / svátek</span></div>`;}
function bpAsHTML(from,to){let P;try{if(!asTarget().length)return '';P=asPlan();}catch(e){return '';}const L=P.filter(w=>w.status==='open'&&w.best&&w.best.from<=to&&w.best.to>=from);if(!L.length)return '';
  return `<h2 class="bpr-h">Doporučené asanační dny<small>návrh, dosud nezapsaný do omezení · ${esc(asLabel(asTarget()))}</small></h2><table class="bpr-tb"><thead><tr><th>Doporučený termín</th><th>Plánovací okno</th><th>Sály</th><th class="r">Zrušilo by</th></tr></thead><tbody>${L.map(w=>`<tr><td><b>${w.best.from===w.best.to?`${cap(DOW_L[isoDow(w.best.from)])} ${fmtY(w.best.from)}`:fmtR(w.best.from,w.best.to)}</b></td><td>${fmtR(w.from,w.to)}</td><td>${w.remaining.map(id=>ROOM[id]?esc(ROOM[id].code):'').filter(Boolean).join(', ')}</td><td class="r">${w.best.lost} ${plural(w.best.lost,'půlblok','půlbloky','půlbloků')}</td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table>`;}
function bpReportHTML(){const o=S.bp,M=bpData(),{from,to,items}=M,site=o.site==='all'?'všechna pracoviště':SITE[o.site].name,typ=o.types.length?o.types.join(', '):'všechny důvody';
  const nOR=x=>x.rooms.filter(r=>r.kind!=='pacu').length,rd=items.reduce((a,x)=>a+x.wd*nOR(x),0),lost=items.reduce((a,x)=>a+x.lost,0),asn=items.filter(x=>x.b.type==='Asanace').length;
  return `<div class="bpr">
    <div class="print-h"><div><b>Rozpis operativy · Krajská nemocnice Liberec</b><span>Omezení operačních sálů</span></div><div>${fmtR(from,to)} · ${esc(site)} · vytištěno ${fmtY(today)}</div></div>
    <h1 class="bpr-t">Omezení operačních sálů</h1><p class="bpr-s">${fmtR(from,to)} · ${esc(site)} · ${esc(typ)}</p>
    <div class="bpr-k"><div><span>Omezení</span><b>${items.length}</b></div><div><span>Z toho asanace</span><b>${asn}</b></div><div><span>Sálové pracovní dny</span><b>${rd}</b></div><div><span>Zrušené půlbloky</span><b>${lost}</b></div></div>
    <h2 class="bpr-h">Seznam omezení</h2>
    ${items.length?`<table class="bpr-tb"><thead><tr><th>Období</th><th>Prac. dny</th><th>Důvod</th><th>Sály</th><th>Poznámka</th><th>Dotčené obory</th><th class="r">Zrušeno</th></tr></thead><tbody>${items.map(x=>`<tr><td><b>${x.f===x.t?`${DOW[isoDow(x.f)]} ${fmtY(x.f)}`:fmtR(x.f,x.t)}</b>${x.clipped?`<small>celé omezení ${fmtR(x.b.from,x.b.to)}</small>`:''}</td><td>${x.wd}</td><td><span class="bpr-ty" style="--c:${BT_C[x.b.type]||'#999'}">${BT_A[x.b.type]||'?'}</span>${esc(x.b.type)}</td><td>${x.rooms.map(r=>`<code>${esc(r.code)}</code> ${esc(r.name)}<small>${SITE[r.site].name}${gOf(r)?' · '+esc(gName(r.group)):''}</small>`).join('')}</td><td>${esc(x.b.note||'—')}</td><td>${Object.entries(x.sp).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]).map(([sp,h])=>`${esc(specName(sp))} ${fmtN(h,1)} h`).join('<br>')||'—'}</td><td class="r">${x.lost} ${plural(x.lost,'půlblok','půlbloky','půlbloků')}</td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table>`:'<p class="bpr-e">V tomto období nejsou podle výběru evidována žádná omezení sálů.</p>'}
    ${o.days&&items.length?bpMatrixHTML(M):''}
    ${o.as?bpAsHTML(from,to):''}
    ${o.sign?`<div class="bpr-sig"><div>Vypracoval(a)<span></span></div><div>Datum<span></span></div><div>Schválil(a)<span></span></div></div>`:''}
  </div>`;}
function bpGoBtn(){return S.role==='spec'?'':`<button class="btn sm" data-act="bpGo" title="Tisk omezení a asanací za období">${ico('printer')}Tisk omezení za období</button>`;}
function bpCard(){const o=S.bp,[from,to]=bpRange(),M=bpData(),lost=M.items.reduce((a,x)=>a+x.lost,0),asn=M.items.filter(x=>x.b.type==='Asanace').length;
  let asOpen=0;try{if(o.as&&asTarget().length)asOpen=asPlan().filter(w=>w.status==='open'&&w.best&&w.best.from<=to&&w.best.to>=from).length;}catch(e){}
  return `<section class="card" id="bp-card"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Tisk omezení za období</div><button class="btn sm ghost" data-act="bpPrev">${S.bpPrev?'Skrýt náhled':'Zobrazit náhled'}</button></div>
    <div class="filters" style="padding:0;border:0;background:none;box-shadow:none">
      <div class="frow"><label class="flab" for="bp-from">Od</label>${dateField('bp-from',from,'data-bp="from" data-role="from" data-pair="bp-to"')}<label class="flab" for="bp-to">Do</label>${dateField('bp-to',to,'data-bp="to" data-role="to" data-pair="bp-from"')}
        <div class="seg">${[['m','Tento měsíc'],['m1','Příští měsíc'],['q','Čtvrtletí'],['y','Celý rok']].map(([v,t])=>{const [a,b]=bpPresetRange(v);return `<button data-act="bpPreset" data-v="${v}" class="${a===from&&b===to?'on':''}">${t}</button>`;}).join('')}</div></div>
      <div class="frow"><span class="flab">Pracoviště</span><div class="seg">${[['all','Vše'],...SITES.map(x=>[x.id,x.name])].map(([v,t])=>`<button data-act="bpSite" data-v="${v}" class="${o.site===v?'on':''}">${t}</button>`).join('')}</div>
        <span class="flab">Důvod</span><button class="chip sm${o.types.length?'':' on'}" data-act="bpType" data-v="all">Vše</button>${BLOCK_TYPES.map(t=>`<button class="chip sm${o.types.includes(t)?' on':''}" data-act="bpType" data-v="${esc(t)}" style="--c:${BT_C[t]}"><i></i>${esc(t)}</button>`).join('')}</div>
      <div class="frow"><label class="cbx"><input type="checkbox" data-ch="bpOpt" data-k="days"${o.days?' checked':''}><span>Kalendář po dnech</span></label><label class="cbx"><input type="checkbox" data-ch="bpOpt" data-k="as"${o.as?' checked':''}><span>Doporučené asanační dny (návrh)</span></label><label class="cbx"><input type="checkbox" data-ch="bpOpt" data-k="sign"${o.sign?' checked':''}><span>Řádky pro podpis</span></label></div>
    </div>
    <div class="frm-foot"><p class="bp-sum">${fmtR(from,to)}: <b>${M.items.length}</b> ${plural(M.items.length,'omezení','omezení','omezení')}${asn?`, z toho <b>${asn}</b> ${plural(asn,'asanace','asanace','asanací')}`:''} · zrušeno <b>${lost}</b> ${plural(lost,'půlblok','půlbloky','půlbloků')}${asOpen?` · <b>${asOpen}</b> ${plural(asOpen,'doporučený asanační termín','doporučené asanační termíny','doporučených asanačních termínů')}`:''}</p><button class="btn pri" data-act="bpPrint">${ico('printer')}Tisk omezení</button></div>
    ${S.bpPrev?`<div class="bp-prev">${bpReportHTML()}</div>`:''}
  </section>`;}

/* =========================================================
   Čekací listiny a prognóza
   ========================================================= */
function waitStart(){return S.year===todayY?today:`${S.year}-01-04`;}
function waitHours(){const key='wh|'+S.year;if(KC.has(key))return KC.get(key);const st=waitStart(),hrs={},WK=12;
  for(let i=0;i<WK*7;i++){const d=addD(st,i);for(const room of D.rooms){if(!room.active||room.kind==='pacu')continue;const r=R(room,d);let H=null;for(let j=0;j<2;j++){const h=r.h[j];if(!h||h.released)continue;H=H||hoursFor(room,d);hrs[h.s]=(hrs[h.s]||0)+halfMin(H,j)/60;}}}
  Object.keys(hrs).forEach(k=>hrs[k]/=WK);KC.set(key,hrs);return hrs;}
function waitData(){if(!D.flags.wait){D.flags.wait=true;const hrs=waitHours(),u=.85,seed={CHI:[95,.9,3],ORT:[130,1.05,4],URO:[70,.8,2],GYN:[60,.85,1.5],ORL:[55,.95,2],OCN:[25,.7,4],TRA:[90,.97,1],PLA:[80,1.02,2.5]};
    Object.entries(seed).forEach(([k,[min,ratio,wk]])=>{if(!D.specs.some(x=>x.id===k)||D.wait[k]||!hrs[k])return;const cap=hrs[k]*60*u/min;D.wait[k]={n:Math.round(cap*wk),min,inflow:Math.round(cap*ratio)};});save();}
  return D.wait;}
function waitModel(){const key=`wait|${S.year}|${S.wlUtil}|${S.wlTarget}`;if(KC.has(key))return KC.get(key);const W=waitData(),hrs=waitHours(),u=S.wlUtil/100,T=S.wlTarget;
  const rows=D.specs.map(s=>{const w=W[s.id];if(!w)return null;const hw=hrs[s.id]||0,min=Math.max(5,+w.min||60),cap=hw*60*u/min,inflow=Math.max(0,+w.inflow||0),n=Math.max(0,+w.n||0),net=cap-inflow,wk=n<=0?0:net>0?n/net:Infinity;
    const needH=(inflow+n/T)*min/(60*u),extra=Math.max(0,needH-hw);return {s,w,hw,cap,inflow,n,net,wk,extra,min,st:wk<=T?'ok':net>0?'warn':'bad'};}).filter(Boolean);
  const v={rows,start:waitStart()};KC.set(key,v);return v;}
function waitSpark(r,T){const N=52,pts=[];let mx=1;for(let t=0;t<=N;t++){const v=Math.max(0,r.n-r.net*t);pts.push(v);mx=Math.max(mx,v);}const W=220,H=40,x=t=>t/N*W,y=v=>H-3-(v/mx)*(H-8);
  return `<svg class="wl-sp" viewBox="0 0 ${W} ${H}" preserveAspectRatio="none" role="img" aria-label="Vývoj čekací listiny za 52 týdnů"><line x1="${x(T)}" x2="${x(T)}" y1="0" y2="${H}" class="wl-t"/><path d="M${pts.map((v,t)=>`${x(t).toFixed(1)} ${y(v).toFixed(1)}`).join('L')}" class="wl-p ${r.st}"/></svg>`;}
function wlRow(r,ed,T){const inp=(k,v,lab)=>`<div class="wl-in" data-l="${lab}">${ed?`<input class="in" type="number" min="0" step="1" inputmode="numeric" value="${v}" data-ch="wl" data-id="${r.s.id}" data-k="${k}" aria-label="${lab} – ${esc(r.s.name)}">`:`<b>${fmtN(v)}</b>`}</div>`;
  const wk=r.n===0?'bez čekajících':r.wk===Infinity?'listina roste':`${fmtN(r.wk,r.wk<10?1:0)} týdnů`,date=r.n>0&&isFinite(r.wk)?`do ${fmtY(addD(waitStart(),Math.ceil(r.wk*7)))}`:r.wk===Infinity?`+${fmtN(-r.net,1)} pacientů týdně`:'';
  return `<div class="wl-r"><div class="wl-n" style="--c:${r.s.c}"><i></i><span>${esc(r.s.name)}</span></div>${inp('n',r.n,'Čekající')}${inp('min',r.min,'Výkon (min)')}${inp('inflow',r.inflow,'Nově / týden')}
    <div class="wl-m"><b>${fmtN(r.cap)}</b> výkonů/týden<small>${fmtN(r.hw,1)} h/týden v plánu</small></div>
    <div class="wl-gr">${waitSpark(r,T)}</div>
    <div class="wl-st ${r.st}"><b>${wk}</b><small>${date}${r.extra>.05?`${date?' · ':''}chybí ${fmtN(r.extra,1)} h/týden`:''}</small>${r.extra>.05&&S.role!=='mgmt'?`<button class="linkbtn" data-act="wlFree" data-v="${r.s.id}"><span>Volné kapacity</span></button>`:''}</div>
    ${isCoord()?`<button class="icon-btn sm" data-act="wlDel" data-id="${r.s.id}" aria-label="Odebrat čekací listinu ${esc(r.s.name)}">${ico('trash')}</button>`:'<span></span>'}</div>`;}
function vWait(){const M=waitModel(),T=S.wlTarget,rows=M.rows,ed=id=>isCoord()||(S.role==='spec'&&id===S.roleSpec);
  const tot=rows.reduce((a,r)=>a+r.n,0),grow=rows.filter(r=>r.st==='bad').length,over=rows.filter(r=>r.st!=='ok').length,extra=rows.reduce((a,r)=>a+r.extra,0);
  const fin=rows.filter(r=>isFinite(r.wk)&&r.n>0),fn=fin.reduce((a,r)=>a+r.n,0),avg=fn?fin.reduce((a,r)=>a+r.wk*r.n,0)/fn:0,missing=D.specs.filter(s=>!D.wait[s.id]);
  const sorted=[...rows].sort((a,b)=>({bad:0,warn:1,ok:2}[a.st]-{bad:0,warn:1,ok:2}[b.st])||(b.wk===Infinity?1:0)-(a.wk===Infinity?1:0)||b.wk-a.wk);
  return ph('Přehledy','Čekací listiny')+`
  <div class="kpis k4">
    ${kpi('Čekající pacienti',fmtN(tot),'',`${rows.length} ${plural(rows.length,'obor','obory','oborů')} se zadanou listinou`,'var(--dot)')}
    ${kpi('Průměrné vyřízení',fmtN(avg,1),'týdne',`vážený průměr · cíl ${T} týdnů`,'#86A0C2')}
    ${kpi('Nad cílem',over,`z ${rows.length}`,grow?`${grow} ${plural(grow,'listina roste','listiny rostou','listin roste')}`:'žádná listina neroste',over?'var(--warn)':'var(--ok)')}
    ${kpi('Chybí sálového času',fmtN(extra),'h/týden','pro splnění cíle u všech oborů','#D6A06E')}
  </div>
  <div class="card filters">
    <div class="frow"><span class="flab">Využití sálového času</span><div class="seg">${[75,80,85,90].map(v=>`<button data-act="wlUtil" data-v="${v}" class="${S.wlUtil===v?'on':''}">${v} %</button>`).join('')}</div>
      <span class="flab">Cíl vyřízení</span><div class="seg">${[13,26,52].map(v=>`<button data-act="wlTarget" data-v="${v}" class="${T===v?'on':''}">${v} týdnů</button>`).join('')}</div>
      ${isCoord()&&missing.length?`<span class="sp"></span><select class="in" id="wl-add" data-ch="wlAdd" style="width:auto" aria-label="Přidat obor"><option value="">Přidat obor…</option>${opt(missing.map(x=>[x.id,x.name]),'')}</select>`:''}</div>
    <p class="hint" style="margin:0">Kapacita = plánovaný operační čas příštích 12 týdnů × využití ÷ průměrná délka výkonu včetně přípravy. Vyřízení = čekající ÷ (kapacita − nově zařazení za týden). Svislá čárka v grafu je cíl.</p>
  </div>
  <section class="card" style="margin-top:16px"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Prognóza po oborech · od ${fmt(M.start)}<span class="cnt">${rows.length}</span></div><div class="q-leg"><span><i style="background:var(--ok)"></i>do cíle</span><span><i style="background:var(--warn)"></i>nad cílem</span><span><i style="background:var(--bad)"></i>listina roste</span></div></div>
    ${rows.length?`<div class="ovx"><div class="wl"><div class="wl-r wl-h"><span>Obor</span><span>Čekající</span><span>Výkon (min)</span><span>Nově / týden</span><span>Kapacita</span><span>Vývoj · 52 týdnů</span><span>Vyřízení</span><span></span></div>${sorted.map(r=>wlRow(r,ed(r.s.id),T)).join('')}</div></div>`:empty('Zatím není zadána žádná čekací listina.')}
    ${S.role==='spec'?'<p class="hint" style="margin-top:10px">Údaje svého oboru můžete upravit přímo v řádku.</p>':''}
  </section>`;}

/* =========================================================
   Role a oprávnění (ukázka)
   ========================================================= */
const ROLE_N={coord:'Koordinátor operativy',spec:'Primář oboru',mgmt:'Vedení nemocnice'};
const SPEC_HIDE=new Set(['rooms','blocks','asanace','data','pub','staff']);
const VIEW_ACTS=new Set('go year zoom tyWeek nav toToday site anf spec specAll pop roomsAll roomsNone jump closeDw tplSite tplEdit theme side help helpClose board rqFilter fixOpen fixClose fixStaff cmd openDay qSite verCmp verCmpClose bkCopy xCopy staffWeeks asOn asRoom asFreq asLen asDow asAlt asSelDay asFromBl occZoom occNav occToday occPlay occSite occGrp occGrpAll occFocus occSort plH plSite plF plSrc plPick plShowSkip plGo print closeInfo colorBy kindf staffSite staffMode closeBulk rolePop role mineWeeks goReqSpec frWeeks frSite frSrc frAn frQuota frMore pubCopy pubMore relocClose bpPreset bpSite bpType bpPrev bpPrint bpGo wlUtil wlTarget wlFree bkSave xSave pubSave bcNav bcToday bcMode bcSite bcLayer bcSel bcPick bcMonth wtNav wtToday wtSite stTab stWkGo stHp stWk stWkToday stMon stDay stDayNav mpTab mpSite kmB kmF kmHit kmCam kmGo3d mpBld mpCampus mpCampusGo mpCSel mpCTool mpCx mpCxPlay mpMode mpFloor mpCam mpPlay mpFollow mpHalf mpSel mpEdFloor mpTool mpOpen mpReg mpTime mpFull mpFocus mpEmTo'.split(' '));
const SPEC_ACTS=new Set(['newReq','rqSave','rqCancel','rqEdit','rqDel','rqScope','rqCnt','rqRoom','frClaim','claimDel']);
const RO_CH=new Set(['hideRoom','hideGroup','xMonth','xSite','impFile','impText','asOnCh','roleSpec','mySpec','frSpec','frExt','bpOpt','bcSpec','wtWe','mpFrom','mpTo','mpEx','mpEmFrom','kmBSel','kmFSel']);
function isCoord(){return (S.role||'coord')==='coord';}
const navOk=v=>S.role!=='spec'||!SPEC_HIDE.has(v);
function navList(){return NAV.map(([l,it])=>[l,it.filter(([v])=>navOk(v))]).filter(([,it])=>it.length);}
function canAct(a,t){if(isCoord()||VIEW_ACTS.has(a))return true;
  if(S.role==='spec'&&SPEC_ACTS.has(a)){
    if(a==='rqEdit'||a==='rqDel'){const q=D.requests.find(x=>x.id===t.dataset.id);return !!q&&q.specId===S.roleSpec&&q.status==='pending';}
    if(a==='claimDel'){const c=(D.claims||[]).find(x=>x.id===t.dataset.id);return !!c&&c.specId===S.roleSpec&&c.status==='pending';}
    return true;}
  return false;}
function denied(){toast(S.role==='mgmt'?'Role Vedení nemocnice má přístup jen pro čtení':'Tuto úpravu může provést jen koordinátor operativy');}
function roleLabel(){return S.role==='spec'?`Primář · ${specName(S.roleSpec)}`:ROLE_N[S.role||'coord'];}
function rolePopHTML(){return `<div class="pop rolep" role="dialog" aria-label="Přepnout roli"><div class="pop-h"><b>Přepnout roli</b></div><p class="hint" style="margin:0 0 8px">Ukázka oprávnění. V ostré verzi se role určí podle přihlášení.</p>
  ${[['coord','Koordinátor operativy','plánuje a upravuje vše'],['spec','Primář oboru','vidí plán, podává požadavky a žádosti'],['mgmt','Vedení nemocnice','přehledy, jen čtení']].map(([v,t,d])=>`<button class="role-o${(S.role||'coord')===v?' on':''}" data-act="role" data-v="${v}"><b>${t}</b><small>${d}</small></button>`).join('')}
  <div class="fld" style="margin-top:8px"><label for="role-spec">Obor primáře</label><select class="in" id="role-spec" data-ch="roleSpec">${opt(D.specs.map(x=>[x.id,x.name]),S.roleSpec||(D.specs[0]||{}).id)}</select></div></div>`;}

/* =========================================================
   Volné kapacity a žádosti oborů
   ========================================================= */
const HALF_N={0:'Dopoledne',1:'Odpoledne',full:'Celý den'};
function frFrom(){const f=S.fr.from;if(f&&YEARS.includes(+f.slice(0,4)))return f;return S.year===todayY?today:`${S.year}-01-01`;}
function freeAt(room,d){const r=R(room,d),di=r.di;if(di.weekend||di.holiday)return [null,null];if(r.block&&!(r.src==='override'&&(r.h[0]||r.h[1])))return [null,null];
  return [0,1].map(i=>{const h=r.h[i];if(!h)return {src:r.src==='override'?'ov':'tpl'};if(h.released)return {src:'rel',q:h.req,s:h.s};return null;});}
function claimFor(rid,d,half){return (D.claims||[]).filter(c=>c.status==='pending'&&c.roomId===rid&&c.d===d&&(c.half==='full'||half==='full'||c.half===half));}
function freeSlots(opts){const o={...S.fr,...(opts||{})},from=o.from||frFrom(),to=addD(from,o.weeks*7-1),out=[];
  for(let d=from;d<=to;d=addD(d,1)){const di=DI(d);if(di.weekend||di.holiday)continue;
    for(const st of SITES){if(o.site!=='all'&&o.site!==st.id)continue;
      for(const gid of siteGroups(st.id))for(const room of sortRooms(D.rooms.filter(r=>r.active&&r.site===st.id&&gOf(r)===gid&&r.kind!=='pacu'&&(o.ext||r.kind!=='ext')))){
        if(o.rooms&&!o.rooms.has(room.id))continue;
        const fa=freeAt(room,d),ok=x=>x&&(o.src==='all'||(o.src==='rel'?x.src==='rel':x.src!=='rel')),a=ok(fa[0])?fa[0]:null,b=ok(fa[1])?fa[1]:null;if(!a&&!b)continue;
        const H=hoursFor(room,d);
        if(a&&b&&a.src===b.src&&(a.q||null)===(b.q||null))out.push({d,room,half:'full',x:a,h:(tmin(H.to)-tmin(H.from))/60,t:fH(H)});
        else{if(a)out.push({d,room,half:0,x:a,h:halfMin(H,0)/60,t:halfT(H,0)});if(b)out.push({d,room,half:1,x:b,h:halfMin(H,1)/60,t:halfT(H,1)});}}}}
  return out;}
function srcTag(x){return x.src==='rel'?`<span class="tag sch">Uvolněno · ${esc(specName(x.s))} – ${esc(x.q.type.toLowerCase())}</span>`:x.src==='ov'?'<span class="tag">Zrušeno v plánu</span>':'<span class="tag">Volno ve šabloně</span>';}
function frAn(room){return room.an==='LA'?'LA':room.an==='CA'?'CA':(S.fr.an||'CA');}
function assignFree(room,ds,half,spec,an){let sl=slotAt(room,ds)||{split:true,a:null,b:null};if(!sl.split)sl={split:true,a:sl.a?{...sl.a}:null,b:sl.a?{...sl.a}:null};const x={s:spec,an};
  if(half==='full'||half===0)sl.a={...x};if(half==='full'||half===1)sl.b={...x};if(sl.a&&sl.b&&sl.a.s===sl.b.s&&sl.a.an===sl.b.an)sl={split:false,a:sl.a};setSlot(room,ds,sl);}
function frSpecDefault(){if(S.role==='spec')return S.roleSpec;if(S.fr.spec&&SPEC[S.fr.spec])return S.fr.spec;const under=quotaModel(S.fr.site).rows.filter(r=>r.p!=null).sort((a,b)=>a.p-b.p)[0];return under?under.s.id:(D.specs[0]||{}).id;}
function slotRow(x,spec){const room=x.room,cl=claimFor(room.id,x.d,x.half),mine=S.role==='spec'&&cl.some(c=>c.specId===S.roleSpec);
  const act=S.role==='mgmt'?'':S.role==='spec'?(mine?'<span class="tag ok">Žádost odeslána</span>':`<button class="btn sm pri" data-act="frClaim" data-r="${room.id}" data-d="${x.d}" data-h="${x.half}">Požádat</button>`):`<button class="btn sm pri" data-act="frAssign" data-r="${room.id}" data-d="${x.d}" data-h="${x.half}" data-s="${esc(spec)}">Přidělit ${esc(spec)}</button>`;
  return `<div class="li"><div class="when">${cap(DOW_L[isoDow(x.d)])}<small>${fmtY(x.d)}</small></div>
    <div><div class="l1"><i class="rdot" style="--c:${roomColor(room)}"></i><b>${esc(room.name)}</b><span class="tag"><code>${esc(room.code)}</code> ${SITE[room.site].name}${gOf(room)?' · '+esc(gName(room.group)):''}</span>${srcTag(x.x)}</div>
    <div class="l2">${HALF_N[x.half]} ${x.t} · ${fmtN(x.h,1)} h · ${anLabel(room.an)}${cl.length?` · <b>žádá: ${cl.map(c=>esc(specName(c.specId))).join(', ')}</b>`:''}</div></div>
    <div class="act">${act}<button class="btn sm" data-act="jump" data-d="${x.d}" data-z="week" data-site="${room.site}">Na ose</button></div></div>`;}
function claimRow(c){const room=ROOM[c.roomId],sp=SPEC[c.specId]||{c:'#999',name:c.specId};if(!room)return '';const fa=freeAt(room,c.d),still=c.half==='full'?(fa[0]&&fa[1]):fa[c.half];
  return `<div class="li"><div class="when">${cap(DOW_L[isoDow(c.d)])}<small>${fmtY(c.d)}</small></div><div><div class="l1"><span class="chip st" style="--c:${sp.c}"><i></i>${esc(sp.name)}</span><b>${esc(room.name)}</b><span class="tag"><code>${esc(room.code)}</code> ${SITE[room.site].name}</span><span class="tag">${HALF_N[c.half]}</span>${still?'':'<span class="tag bad">Už není volné</span>'}</div><div class="l2">Žádost o volnou kapacitu${c.note?' · '+esc(c.note):''}</div></div>
    <div class="act">${isCoord()?`${still?`<button class="btn sm pri" data-act="claimOk" data-id="${c.id}">${ico('check')}Přidělit</button>`:''}<button class="btn sm" data-act="claimNo" data-id="${c.id}">Zamítnout</button>`:(S.role==='spec'&&c.specId===S.roleSpec?`<button class="btn sm" data-act="claimDel" data-id="${c.id}">Stáhnout žádost</button>`:'')}<button class="btn sm" data-act="jump" data-d="${c.d}" data-z="week" data-site="${room.site}">Na ose</button></div></div>`;}
function vFree(){const o=S.fr,from=frFrom(),L=freeSlots(),spec=frSpecDefault(),lim=o.limit||160;
  const hrs=L.reduce((a,x)=>a+x.h,0),rel=L.filter(x=>x.x.src==='rel').reduce((a,x)=>a+x.h,0),halves=L.reduce((a,x)=>a+(x.half==='full'?2:1),0);
  const claims=(D.claims||[]).filter(c=>c.status==='pending'&&(S.role!=='spec'||c.specId===S.roleSpec));
  const qm=quotaModel(o.site).rows.filter(r=>r.p!=null&&r.p<95).sort((a,b)=>a.p-b.p).slice(0,5);
  let wk='',list='';L.slice(0,lim).forEach(x=>{const m=addD(x.d,-(isoDow(x.d)-1));if(m!==wk){wk=m;list+=`<div class="rb-g">Týden ${isoWeek(m)} · ${fmtR(m,addD(m,4))}</div>`;}list+=slotRow(x,spec);});
  return ph('Plánování','Volné kapacity')+`
  <div class="kpis k4">
    ${kpi('Volné půlbloky',halves,'',`${o.weeks} ${plural(o.weeks,'týden','týdny','týdnů')} od ${fmt(from)}`,'var(--dot)')}
    ${kpi('Volný sálový čas',fmtN(hrs),'h','k přidělení jiným oborům','#86A0C2')}
    ${kpi('Uvolněno požadavky',fmtN(rel),'h','obory se svého času vzdaly','#D6A06E')}
    ${kpi('Žádosti oborů',claims.length,'',claims.length?'čekají na rozhodnutí':'žádné nevyřízené','#CF95A6')}
  </div>
  ${claims.length?`<section class="card" style="margin-bottom:16px"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>${S.role==='spec'?'Vaše žádosti o volnou kapacitu':'Žádosti oborů o volnou kapacitu'}<span class="cnt">${claims.length}</span></div></div><div class="list">${claims.map(claimRow).join('')}</div></section>`:''}
  <div class="card filters">
    <div class="frow"><label class="flab" for="fr-from">Od</label>${dateField('fr-from',from,'data-fr="from"')}<div class="seg">${[2,4,8,12].map(w=>`<button data-act="frWeeks" data-v="${w}" class="${o.weeks===w?'on':''}">${w} týd.</button>`).join('')}</div><span class="sp"></span>
      <span class="flab">Pracoviště</span><div class="seg">${[['all','Vše'],...SITES.map(x=>[x.id,x.name])].map(([v,t])=>`<button data-act="frSite" data-v="${v}" class="${o.site===v?'on':''}">${t}</button>`).join('')}</div></div>
    <div class="frow"><span class="flab">Zdroj</span><div class="seg">${[['all','Vše'],['rel','Uvolněno požadavky'],['tpl','Volno v plánu']].map(([v,t])=>`<button data-act="frSrc" data-v="${v}" class="${o.src===v?'on':''}">${t}</button>`).join('')}</div>
      <label class="cbx"><input type="checkbox" id="fr-ext" data-ch="frExt"${o.ext?' checked':''}><span>Včetně externích sálů</span></label>
      ${isCoord()?`<span class="sp"></span><label class="flab" for="fr-spec">Přidělit oboru</label><select class="in" id="fr-spec" data-ch="frSpec" style="width:auto">${opt(D.specs.map(x=>[x.id,x.name]),spec)}</select><div class="seg sm" role="group" aria-label="Anestezie u sálů CA + LA">${['CA','LA'].map(v=>`<button data-act="frAn" data-v="${v}" class="${(o.an||'CA')===v?'on':''}">${v}</button>`).join('')}</div>`:''}</div>
    ${isCoord()&&qm.length?`<div class="frow"><span class="flab">Pod kvótou</span>${qm.map(r=>`<button class="chip sm${spec===r.s.id?' on':''}" data-act="frQuota" data-v="${r.s.id}" style="--c:${r.s.c}"><i></i>${esc(r.s.name)} · ${fmtN(r.p)} %</button>`).join('')}</div>`:''}
  </div>
  <section class="card" style="margin-top:16px"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Volné půlbloky<span class="cnt">${L.length}</span></div><span class="hint">${S.role==='spec'?'O volný sál požádáte jedním klepnutím, rozhodne koordinátor.':S.role==='mgmt'?'Přehled volného sálového času.':'Přidělením vznikne ruční úprava dne, lze ji vrátit tlačítkem Zpět.'}</span></div>
    ${L.length?`<div class="list">${list}</div>${L.length>lim?`<button class="btn" data-act="frMore" style="margin-top:12px">Zobrazit dalších ${Math.min(160,L.length-lim)}</button>`:''}`:empty('V tomto období nejsou podle výběru žádné volné kapacity.')}
  </section>`;}

/* =========================================================
   Pohled oboru / primáře
   ========================================================= */
function mineSpec(){if(S.role==='spec'&&SPEC[S.roleSpec])return S.roleSpec;if(!S.mySpec||!SPEC[S.mySpec])S.mySpec=(D.specs[0]||{}).id;return S.mySpec;}
function mineCell(spec,d){const out=[];
  for(const room of D.rooms){if(!room.active||room.kind==='pacu')continue;const r=R(room,d),base=baseSlot(room,d),bh=base?[base.a,base.split?base.b:base.a]:[null,null],H=hoursFor(room,d),hs=[],blk=r.block&&!(r.src==='override'&&(r.h[0]||r.h[1]));
    for(let i=0;i<2;i++){const h=r.h[i],b=bh[i],isM=h&&h.s===spec,was=b&&b.s===spec;let st=null;
      if(isM)st=h.released?'rel':(was?'ok':'extra');else if(was)st=blk?'blk':'lost';
      if(st)hs.push({i,st,an:isM?h.an:b.an,why:st==='rel'?h.req.type.toLowerCase():st==='blk'?r.block.type.toLowerCase():st==='lost'?(h?`přiděleno ${specName(h.s)}`:'zrušeno'):''});}
    if(!hs.length)continue;
    if(hs.length===2&&hs[0].st===hs[1].st)out.push({room,part:'full',st:hs[0].st,an:hs[0].an,why:hs[0].why,t:fH(H),h:(tmin(H.to)-tmin(H.from))/60});
    else hs.forEach(x=>out.push({room,part:x.i,st:x.st,an:x.an,why:x.why,t:halfT(H,x.i),h:halfMin(H,x.i)/60}));}
  return out;}
function mineChip(x,sp){const lab=x.part==='full'?'celý den':x.part===0?'dop':'odp',cls={ok:'',extra:' extra',rel:' off',lost:' off',blk:' off'}[x.st],t={ok:'',extra:'navíc',rel:'uvolněno',lost:'zrušeno',blk:x.why}[x.st];
  return `<span class="mc${cls}" style="--c:${sp.c}" title="${esc(x.room.name)} · ${x.t}${x.why?' · '+esc(x.why):''}"><b>${esc(x.room.code)}</b>${lab}${t?` · ${esc(t)}`:''}${x.st==='ok'||x.st==='extra'?`<em>${x.an}</em>`:''}</span>`;}
function vMine(){const spec=mineSpec(),sp=SPEC[spec]||{c:'#999',name:spec||'—'},weeks=S.mineWeeks||4;
  const st0=S.year===todayY?today:`${S.year}-01-04`,mon=addD(st0,-(isoDow(st0)-1));let hrs=0,rel=0,lost=0,extra=0;const W=[];
  for(let w=0;w<weeks;w++){const m=addD(mon,w*7);W.push({m,days:[0,1,2,3,4].map(i=>{const d=addD(m,i),e=DI(d).holiday?[]:mineCell(spec,d);e.forEach(x=>{if(x.st==='ok'||x.st==='extra')hrs+=x.h;if(x.st==='extra')extra+=x.h;if(x.st==='rel')rel+=x.h;if(x.st==='lost'||x.st==='blk')lost+=x.h;});return {d,e};})});}
  const qr=quotaModel('all').rows.find(r=>r.s.id===spec),reqs=D.requests.filter(q=>q.specId===spec&&q.to>=`${S.year}-01-01`&&q.from<=`${S.year}-12-31`).sort((a,b)=>a.from.localeCompare(b.from)),pend=reqs.filter(q=>q.status==='pending').length;
  const per=pubDiff(),pc=per&&per[spec];
  const aff=D.rooms.filter(r=>tplFlat(D.template[r.id]).some(sl=>sl&&((sl.a&&sl.a.s===spec)||(sl.b&&sl.b.s===spec))));
  const grp=new Set(D.rooms.filter(r=>r.active&&r.kind!=='pacu'&&aff.some(a=>(gOf(a)&&gOf(a)===gOf(r))||a.id===r.id)).map(r=>r.id));
  const fr=grp.size?freeSlots({from:S.year===todayY?today:`${S.year}-01-01`,weeks:8,site:'all',src:'all',ext:true,rooms:grp}).slice(0,6):[];
  const sel=(S.role==='spec'?'<span class="tag dark">Váš obor</span>':`<select class="in" id="my-spec" data-ch="mySpec" style="width:auto" aria-label="Obor">${opt(D.specs.map(x=>[x.id,x.name]),spec)}</select>`)+`<div class="seg">${[4,8].map(w=>`<button data-act="mineWeeks" data-v="${w}" class="${weeks===w?'on':''}">${w} týdnů</button>`).join('')}</div>`;
  return ph(S.role==='spec'?'Pohled primáře':'Pohled oboru',esc(sp.name),sel)+`
  <div class="kpis k4">
    ${kpi('Plánovaný čas',fmtN(hrs),'h',`příštích ${weeks} týdnů${extra?` · ${fmtN(extra)} h navíc`:''}`,sp.c)}
    ${kpi('Plnění kvóty',qr&&qr.p!=null?fmtN(qr.p):'—',qr&&qr.p!=null?'%':'',qr&&qr.t?`kvóta ${fmtN(qr.t,1)} h/týden · rok ${S.year}`:'bez kvóty','#86A0C2')}
    ${kpi('Uvolněno a zrušeno',fmtN(rel+lost),'h',`požadavky ${fmtN(rel)} h · omezení a změny ${fmtN(lost)} h`,'#D6A06E')}
    ${kpi('Požadavky',reqs.length,'',`${pend} ${plural(pend,'čeká','čekají','čeká')} na schválení`,pend?'var(--warn)':'var(--ok)')}
  </div>
  <section class="card"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Rozpis oboru · ${weeks} týdnů od ${fmt(mon)}</div><div class="q-leg"><span><i style="background:${sp.c}"></i>plánováno</span><span><i style="box-shadow:inset 0 0 0 1.5px ${sp.c}"></i>navíc proti šabloně</span><span><i style="background:var(--sunk)"></i>uvolněno / zrušeno</span></div></div>
    <div class="ovx"><div class="mw">${['','Po','Út','St','Čt','Pá'].map(x=>`<span class="mw-h">${x}</span>`).join('')}${W.map(w=>`<span class="mw-w">T${isoWeek(w.m)}<small>${fmt(w.m)}</small></span>${w.days.map(({d,e})=>{const di=DI(d);return `<div class="mw-c${di.holiday?' hol':''}${d===today?' td':''}"><span class="mw-d">${+d.slice(8)}. ${+d.slice(5,7)}.</span>${di.holiday?`<span class="mw-hol">${esc(di.holiday)}</span>`:e.length?e.map(x=>mineChip(x,sp)).join(''):'<span class="mw-n">—</span>'}</div>`;}).join('')}`).join('')}</div></div>
  </section>
  <div class="two" style="margin-top:16px">
    <section class="card"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Požadavky oboru · ${S.year}<span class="cnt">${reqs.length}</span></div>${S.role!=='mgmt'?`<button class="btn sm pri" data-act="goReqSpec" data-v="${spec}">${ico('plus')}Nový požadavek</button>`:''}</div>
      ${reqs.length?`<div class="list">${reqs.map(q=>`<div class="li"><div class="when">${fmtR(q.from,q.to)}<small>${PARTS[q.part].toLowerCase()}</small></div><div><div class="l1"><span class="tag">${esc(q.type)}</span><span class="tag ${(q.status||'ok')==='ok'?'ok':q.status==='pending'?'sch':'bad'}">${RQ_ST[q.status||'ok']}</span></div>${q.note?`<div class="l2">${esc(q.note)}</div>`:''}</div><div class="act"><button class="btn sm" data-act="jump" data-d="${q.from}">Na ose</button></div></div>`).join('')}</div>`:empty('Obor zatím nemá v tomto roce žádné požadavky.')}
    </section>
    <div class="stack">
      ${(()=>{const wr=waitModel().rows.find(r=>r.s.id===spec);return wr?`<section class="card"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Čekací listina</div><button class="btn sm" data-act="go" data-v="wait">Detail</button></div><div class="wl-mini"><div class="wl-st ${wr.st}"><b>${fmtN(wr.n)} ${plural(wr.n,'pacient','pacienti','pacientů')}</b><small>${wr.wk===Infinity?'listina roste':wr.n?`vyřízení za ${fmtN(wr.wk,1)} týdnů`:'bez čekajících'}</small></div>${waitSpark(wr,S.wlTarget)}</div></section>`:'';})()}
      <section class="card"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Volné kapacity ve skupinách oboru</div><button class="btn sm" data-act="go" data-v="free">Vše</button></div>
        ${fr.length?`<div class="list">${fr.map(x=>slotRow(x,spec)).join('')}</div>`:empty('V příštích 8 týdnech nejsou ve skupinách oboru volné kapacity.')}</section>
      <section class="card"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Změny od zveřejnění plánu</div>${pc?`<button class="btn sm" data-act="pubCopy" data-v="${spec}">${ico('copy')}Kopírovat oznámení</button>`:''}</div>
        ${pc?pubSpecLines(spec,pc,6):'<p class="hint">Od posledního zveřejnění se plán oboru nezměnil.</p>'}</section>
    </div></div>`;}

/* =========================================================
   Zveřejnění plánu a oznámení změn oborům
   ========================================================= */
function pubData(){const o={};['v','specs','groups','rooms','template','seasons','overrides','requests','blocks','staff','hours','dayHours','flags','claims'].forEach(k=>{if(D[k]!==undefined)o[k]=D[k];});return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(o));}
function pubRange(){const y0=`${S.year}-01-01`,y1=`${S.year}-12-31`;return [today>y0?(today>y1?y1:today):y0,y1];}
function assignMap(from,to){const m=new Map();for(let d=from;d<=to;d=addD(d,1))for(const room of D.rooms){if(!room.active||room.kind==='pacu')continue;const r=R(room,d);r.h.forEach((h,i)=>{if(h&&!h.released)m.set(room.id+'|'+d+'|'+i,h.s);});}return m;}
function lostWhy(k){const [rid,d,i]=k.split('|'),room=ROOM[rid];if(!room)return 'místnost odebrána z plánu';if(!room.active)return 'místnost mimo provoz';const r=R(room,d);
  if(r.block&&!(r.src==='override'&&(r.h[0]||r.h[1])))return `omezení sálu: ${r.block.type.toLowerCase()}`;const h=r.h[+i];if(h&&h.released)return `požadavek oboru: ${h.req.type.toLowerCase()}`;if(h)return `přiděleno oboru ${specName(h.s)}`;return 'zrušeno v plánu';}
function pubDiff(){if(!D.pub||!D.pub.data)return null;const key='pub|'+S.year;if(KC.has(key))return KC.get(key);
  const [from,to]=pubRange(),cur=assignMap(from,to),old=withData(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(D.pub.data)),()=>assignMap(from,to)),per={};
  const add=(sp,kind,k,why)=>{(per[sp]=per[sp]||{gain:[],lost:[]})[kind].push({k,why});};
  new Set([...cur.keys(),...old.keys()]).forEach(k=>{const a=old.get(k),b=cur.get(k);if(a===b)return;if(a)add(a,'lost',k,lostWhy(k));if(b)add(b,'gain',k,'');});
  KC.set(key,per);return per;}
function pubLines(list){const m=new Map();list.forEach(x=>{const [rid,d,i]=x.k.split('|'),kk=rid+'|'+d,e=m.get(kk)||{rid,d,hs:[],why:x.why};e.hs.push(+i);m.set(kk,e);});return [...m.values()].sort((a,b)=>a.d.localeCompare(b.d)||a.rid.localeCompare(b.rid));}
function lineHrs(e){const room=ROOM[e.rid];if(!room)return 0;const H=hoursFor(room,e.d);return e.hs.reduce((a,i)=>a+halfMin(H,i)/60,0);}
function lineTxt(e){const room=ROOM[e.rid]||{name:e.rid,code:e.rid},part=e.hs.length===2?'celý den':e.hs[0]===0?'dopoledne':'odpoledne';return `${DOW[isoDow(e.d)].toLowerCase()} ${fmtY(e.d)} · ${room.name} (${room.code}) · ${part}${e.why?' – '+e.why:''}`;}
function pubSpecLines(spec,pc,max){const L=[...pubLines(pc.lost).map(e=>['−',e]),...pubLines(pc.gain).map(e=>['+',e])].sort((a,b)=>a[1].d.localeCompare(b[1].d)),open=S.pubOpen===spec,show=open?L:L.slice(0,max);
  return `<div class="pl">${show.map(([t,e])=>`<div class="pl-r ${t==='+'?'g':'l'}"><b>${t}</b><span>${esc(lineTxt(e))}</span></div>`).join('')}</div>${L.length>max?`<button class="linkbtn" data-act="pubMore" data-v="${spec}" style="margin-top:6px"><span>${open?'Zobrazit méně':`Zobrazit všech ${L.length} změn`}</span></button>`:''}`;}
function pubMsg(spec){const per=pubDiff(),pc=per&&per[spec];if(!pc)return '';const sp=specName(spec),lost=pubLines(pc.lost),gain=pubLines(pc.gain),hl=lost.reduce((a,e)=>a+lineHrs(e),0),hg=gain.reduce((a,e)=>a+lineHrs(e),0),d=new Date(D.pub.ts);
  return [`Předmět: Změny operačního plánu – ${sp} (stav k ${fmtY(today)})`,'','Dobrý den,','',`v operačním plánu oboru ${sp} došlo od zveřejnění ${d.getDate()}. ${d.getMonth()+1}. ${d.getFullYear()} k těmto změnám:`,'',
    ...(lost.length?['Zrušeno:',...lost.map(e=>'– '+lineTxt(e)),'']:[]),...(gain.length?['Nově přiděleno:',...gain.map(e=>'– '+lineTxt(e)),'']:[]),
    `Celkem: ${[lost.length?'−'+fmtN(hl,1)+' h':'',gain.length?'+'+fmtN(hg,1)+' h':''].filter(Boolean).join(', ')} operačního času.`,'','S pozdravem','Koordinátor operativy, COS Liberec'].join('\n');}
function vPub(){const P=D.pub,per=P?pubDiff():null,specs=per?D.specs.filter(x=>per[x.id]):[],[from,to]=pubRange();
  const n=specs.reduce((a,x)=>a+per[x.id].gain.length+per[x.id].lost.length,0);let hl=0,hg=0;specs.forEach(x=>{hl+=pubLines(per[x.id].lost).reduce((a,e)=>a+lineHrs(e),0);hg+=pubLines(per[x.id].gain).reduce((a,e)=>a+lineHrs(e),0);});
  return ph('Systém','Zveřejnění plánu')+`
  <div class="kpis k4">
    ${kpi('Změněné půlbloky',n,'',`proti zveřejněné verzi · ${fmtR(from,to)}`,n?'var(--warn)':'var(--ok)')}
    ${kpi('Dotčené obory',specs.length,`z ${D.specs.length}`,specs.length?'čekají na oznámení':'všechny mají aktuální stav','#CF95A6')}
    ${kpi('Ubráno / přidáno',fmtN(hl),'h',`přidáno ${fmtN(hg)} h operačního času`,'#D6A06E')}
    ${kpi('Zveřejněno',P?relTime(P.ts):'—','',P?esc([P.by,P.label].filter(Boolean).join(' · ')):'plán nebyl zveřejněn','#86A0C2')}
  </div>
  <section class="card"><div class="card-h"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Zveřejněná verze plánu</div>${isCoord()?`<button class="btn${S.confirm==='pub'?' warn':' pri'}" data-act="pubNow">${ico('send')}${S.confirm==='pub'?'Opravdu zveřejnit? Změny se označí jako oznámené':'Zveřejnit aktuální plán'}</button>`:''}</div>
    <p class="hint">Zveřejněním potvrdíte oborům aktuální rozpis. Další změny (požadavky, omezení, přesuny, sezónní šablony, přidělení volných kapacit) se porovnávají s touto verzí a pro každý dotčený obor se připraví oznámení k odeslání e-mailem.</p></section>
  ${specs.length?`<div class="card-h" style="margin:20px 2px 10px"><div class="card-t"><i></i>Oznámení pro obory</div><div class="btns">${DL?`<button class="btn sm" data-act="pubSave">${ico('save')}Stáhnout (.txt)</button>`:''}<button class="btn sm" data-act="pubCopy" data-v="all">${ico('copy')}Kopírovat oznámení pro všechny</button></div></div>
    <div class="pub-grid">${specs.map(x=>{const pc=per[x.id],l=pubLines(pc.lost).reduce((a,e)=>a+lineHrs(e),0),g=pubLines(pc.gain).reduce((a,e)=>a+lineHrs(e),0);
      return `<section class="card"><div class="card-h"><div class="sbr-l" style="font-size:14px;font-weight:500"><i style="background:${x.c}"></i>${esc(x.name)}</div><span class="hint">${l?`<b class="dt-neg">−${fmtN(l,1)} h</b>`:''} ${g?`<b class="dt-pos">+${fmtN(g,1)} h</b>`:''}</span></div>${pubSpecLines(x.id,pc,6)}<div style="margin-top:12px"><button class="btn sm" data-act="pubCopy" data-v="${x.id}">${ico('copy')}Kopírovat oznámení</button></div></section>`;}).join('')}</div>`
  :`<section class="card" style="margin-top:16px">${empty(P?'Od posledního zveřejnění nedošlo v plánu ke změnám.':'Plán zatím nebyl zveřejněn.')}</section>`}
  ${S.copyFallback?`<section class="card" style="margin-top:16px"><div class="fld"><label for="copy-fb">Text ke zkopírování</label><textarea class="in" id="copy-fb" rows="8" readonly>${esc(S.copyFallback)}</textarea></div></section>`:''}`;}

/* =========================================================
   Render
   ========================================================= */
const VIEWS={plan:vPlan,home:vHome,kal:vKal,map:vMap,wait:vWait,mine:vMine,free:vFree,pub:vPub,quota:vQuota,data:vData,timeline:vTimeline,rooms:vRooms,requests:vRequests,blocks:vBlocks,asanace:vAsanace,occ:vOcc,staff:vStaff,calendar:vCalendar,alerts:vAlerts};
function render(){
  if(!VIEWS[S.view])S.view='timeline';
  if(S.role==='spec'&&SPEC_HIDE.has(S.view))S.view='mine';
  document.body.classList.toggle('ro',!isCoord());document.body.classList.toggle('mg',S.role==='mgmt');
  if(S.view!=='occ'&&PLAY)stopPlay();
  if(!$('#dp').hidden)closeDP();
  const sc=$('#tlscroll'),left=sc?sc.scrollLeft:null;
  $('.app').classList.toggle('mini',!!S.mini);
  $('#side').innerHTML=sideHTML();
  $('#top').innerHTML=topHTML();
  const view=$('#view'),vkey=S.view+'|'+S.year,kkey=vkey+'|'+S.site+'|'+S.staffSite,entering=vkey!==lastViewKey&&!RM()&&!['timeline','kal','map'].includes(S.view);
  view.innerHTML=VIEWS[S.view]();
  if(S.view==='timeline')drawTimeline(left);
  if(S.view==='map'&&S.mp.tab==='3d')m3Mount();else m3Stop();
  if(S.view==='map'&&S.mp.tab==='map')kmMount();else kmStop();
  if(entering){view.classList.remove('view-in');void view.offsetWidth;view.classList.add('view-in');clearTimeout(render._t);render._t=setTimeout(()=>view.classList.remove('view-in'),700);const ch=view.querySelector('.chart');if(ch){ch.classList.add('anim');}}
  if(['timeline','kal','map'].includes(S.view)){clearTimeout(render._t);view.classList.remove('view-in');}
  if(kkey!==lastKpiKey&&!['timeline','kal','map'].includes(S.view)) countUp(view);
  if(S.view==='occ'&&entering){const r=$('#occ-root');if(r){r.classList.add('occ-anim');setTimeout(()=>r.classList.remove('occ-anim'),1400);r.querySelectorAll('[data-num]').forEach(el=>tween(el,0,+el.dataset.num||0,+el.dataset.dec||0));}}
  lastViewKey=vkey;lastKpiKey=kkey;
  const n=ISSUES().length;if(lastIss>=0&&n!==lastIss)document.querySelectorAll('.ni .badge,.top .icon-btn b').forEach(b=>b.classList.add('bump'));lastIss=n;
  savePrefs();
}
addEventListener('beforeprint',()=>{if(S.view==='map')m3PrintImg(true);PRINTING=true;document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-ui','light');if(S.view==='timeline')drawTimeline();});
addEventListener('afterprint',()=>{m3PrintImg(false);PRINTING=false;applyTheme();if(S.view==='timeline'){const sc=$('#tlscroll');drawTimeline(sc?sc.scrollLeft:null);}});
$('.app').addEventListener('transitionend',e=>{if(e.target===e.currentTarget&&S.view==='timeline'){const sc=$('#tlscroll');drawTimeline(sc?sc.scrollLeft:null);}});
let rzT;addEventListener('resize',()=>{clearTimeout(rzT);rzT=setTimeout(()=>{if(S.view==='timeline'){const sc=$('#tlscroll');drawTimeline(sc?sc.scrollLeft:null);}},140);});
function uiState(){return {view:S.view,year:S.year,zoom:S.zoom,anchor:S.anchor,site:S.site,an:S.an,kind:S.kind,colorBy:S.colorBy,specs:[...S.specs],hidden:[...S.hidden],theme:S.theme,mini:S.mini,staffMode:S.staffMode,staffSite:S.staffSite,tplSite:S.tplSite};}
function applyUi(u){if(!u||typeof u!=='object')return;
  ['view','zoom','anchor','site','an','kind','colorBy','staffMode','staffSite','tplSite'].forEach(k=>{if(typeof u[k]==='string')S[k]=u[k];});
  if(YEARS.includes(u.year))S.year=u.year; if(u.theme==='dark'||u.theme==='light')S.theme=u.theme; S.mini=!!u.mini;
  if(Array.isArray(u.specs))S.specs=new Set(u.specs); if(Array.isArray(u.hidden))S.hidden=new Set(u.hidden);
  if(!ZL[S.zoom])S.zoom='year'; if(+String(S.anchor).slice(0,4)!==S.year)S.anchor=`${S.year}-01-04`; applyTheme();}
let booted=false;
function boot(data){try{if(data&&data.ui)applyUi(data.ui);}catch(e){} booted=true; if(!D.pub){D.pub={ts:Date.now(),by:'Výchozí stav',label:'při zavedení aplikace',data:pubData()};save();} LASTJ=snap(); render();}
try{if(window.claude&&typeof window.claude.use==='function')window.claude.use('downloads').then(x=>{DL=x||null;if(DL&&booted)render();},()=>{});}catch(e){}
const HOT=window.claude&&window.claude.hot;
try{if(HOT&&typeof HOT.snapshot==='function')HOT.snapshot(()=>({ui:uiState()}));}catch(e){}
if(HOT&&typeof HOT.ready==='function'){try{HOT.ready(boot);}catch(e){boot({});} setTimeout(()=>{if(!booted)boot({});},1200);}
else boot((HOT&&HOT.data)||{});
</script>

</body>
</html>
